These results demonstrate that region-aware editing offers an accurate and scalable mechanism for continual adaptation in dynamic recommendation scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/fengaogao/RAIE.
摘要 (中文)
大语言模型（LLMs）正日益被采用为推荐系统的骨干。然而，真实场景中的用户-item 交互是非平稳的，使得偏好随时间漂移不可避免。现有模型更新策略主要依赖全局微调或逐点编辑，但它们面临两个根本性挑战：(i) 更新粒度不均衡，全局更新会扰动与目标无关的行为，而逐点编辑无法捕捉更广泛的偏好漂移；(ii) 增量更新不稳定，重复编辑会干扰此前的适配，导致灾难性遗忘和不一致的推荐。为解决这些问题，我们提出 Region-Aware Incremental Editing（RAIE），一个冻结骨干模型并执行区域级更新的插件式框架。RAIE 首先通过表示空间中的 spherical k-means 构建语义一致的偏好区域。随后它通过置信度感知门控将到来的序列分配到各区域，并执行三种局部编辑操作——Update、Expand 和 Add——以动态修正受影响的区域。每个区域配备一个专用的 Low-Rank Adaptation（LoRA）模块，仅在该区域的更新数据上训练。推理时，RAIE 将每个用户序列路由到其对应区域，并激活该区域专属的 adapter 进行预测。在时间切片协议（将数据划分为 Set-up (S)、Finetune (F) 和 Test (T)）下于两个基准数据集上的实验表明，RAIE 显著优于 SOTA baseline，同时有效缓解遗忘。这些结果表明，区域感知编辑为动态推荐场景中的持续适配提供了一种准确且可扩展的机制。我们的代码见 https://github.com/fengaogao/RAIE。
FeDecider: An LLM-Based Framework for Federated Cross-Domain Recommendation
UIUC2026推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐federated cross-domain recommendation, LLM-based recommendation, low-rank update disentanglement, domain-specific adapters, personalized aggregationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsXinrui He, Ting-Wei Li, Tianxin Wei, Xuying Ning, Xinyu He, Wenxuan Bao, Hanghang Tong, Jingrui He
Abstract (EN)
Federated cross-domain recommendation (Federated CDR) aims to collaboratively learn personalized recommendation models across heterogeneous domains while preserving data privacy. Recently, large language model (LLM)-based recommendation models have demonstrated impressive performance by leveraging LLMs' strong reasoning capabilities and broad knowledge. However, adopting LLM-based recommendation models in Federated CDR scenarios introduces new challenges. First, there exists a risk of overfitting with domain-specific local adapters. The magnitudes of locally optimized parameter updates often vary across domains, causing biased aggregation and overfitting toward domain-specific distributions. Second, unlike traditional recommendation models (e.g., collaborative filtering, bipartite graph-based methods) that learn explicit and comparable user/item representations, LLMs encode knowledge implicitly through autoregressive text generation training. This poses additional challenges for effectively measuring the cross-domain similarities under heterogeneity. To address these challenges, we propose an LLM-based framework for federated cross-domain recommendation, FeDecider. Specifically, FeDecider tackles the challenge of scale-specific noise by disentangling each client's low-rank updates and sharing only their directional components. To handle the need for flexible and effective integration, each client further learns personalized weights that achieve the data-aware integration of updates from other domains. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets validate the effectiveness of our proposed FeDecider.
摘要 (中文)
联邦跨域推荐（Federated CDR）旨在保护数据隐私的同时，跨异构域协同学习个性化推荐模型。近来，基于大语言模型（LLM）的推荐模型通过利用 LLMs 强大的推理能力和广博知识，展现出令人印象深刻的性能。然而，在 Federated CDR 场景中采用基于 LLM 的推荐模型带来了新的挑战。第一，域特定的本地 adapter 存在过拟合风险。各域间局部优化参数更新的幅度往往不同，导致有偏的聚合以及对域特定分布的过拟合。第二，不同于学习显式且可比较的用户/item 表示的传统推荐模型（例如协同过滤、基于二部图的方法），LLMs 通过自回归文本生成训练隐式地编码知识。这为在异构条件下有效度量跨域相似性带来了额外挑战。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 FeDecider，一个基于 LLM 的联邦跨域推荐框架。具体而言，FeDecider 通过解耦每个 client 的低秩更新并仅共享其方向分量，来应对尺度特定噪声的挑战。为满足灵活且有效整合的需求，每个 client 进一步学习个性化权重，实现来自其他域更新的数据感知整合。在多样数据集上的大量实验验证了我们提出的 FeDecider 的有效性。
Token-level Collaborative Alignment for LLM-based Generative Recommendation
USTC / Rutgers2026推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐generative recommendation, collaborative filtering, token-level alignment, soft label alignment, next-token predictionPDF · arXiv
AuthorsFake Lin, Binbin Hu, Zhi Zheng, Xi Zhu, Ziqi Liu, Zhiqiang Zhang, Jun Zhou, Tong Xu
Abstract (EN)
Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong potential for generative recommendation by leveraging rich semantic knowledge. However, existing LLM-based recommender systems struggle to effectively incorporate collaborative filtering (CF) signals, due to a fundamental mismatch between item-level preference modeling in CF and token-level next-token prediction (NTP) optimization in LLMs. Prior approaches typically treat CF as contextual hints or representation bias, and resort to multi-stage training to reduce behavioral semantic space discrepancies, leaving CF unable to explicitly regulate LLM generation. In this work, we propose Token-level Collaborative Alignment for Recommendation (TCA4Rec), a model-agnostic and plug-and-play framework that establishes an explicit optimization-level interface between CF supervision and LLM generation. TCA4Rec consists of (i) Collaborative Tokenizer, which projects raw item-level CF logits into token-level distributions aligned with the LLM token space, and (ii) Soft Label Alignment, which integrates these CF-informed distributions with one-hot supervision to optimize a soft NTP objective. This design preserves the generative nature of LLM training while enabling collaborative alignment with essential user preference of CF models. We highlight TCA4Rec is compatible with arbitrary traditional CF models and generalizes across a wide range of decoder-based LLM recommender architectures. Moreover, it provides an explicit mechanism to balance behavioral alignment and semantic fluency, yielding generative recommendations that are both accurate and controllable. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TCA4Rec consistently improves recommendation performance across a broad spectrum of CF models and LLM-based recommender systems.
摘要 (中文)
大语言模型（LLMs）通过利用丰富的语义知识，在生成式推荐方面展现出强大潜力。然而，现有基于 LLM 的推荐系统难以有效融入协同过滤（CF）信号，原因在于 CF 中 item 级的偏好建模与 LLMs 中 token 级的 next-token prediction（NTP）优化之间存在根本性不匹配。此前的方法通常将 CF 视为上下文提示或表示偏置，并求助于多阶段训练来减小行为语义空间差异，使 CF 无法显式地调控 LLM 生成。在本工作中，我们提出 Token-level Collaborative Alignment for Recommendation（TCA4Rec），一个与模型无关、即插即用的框架，它在 CF 监督与 LLM 生成之间建立起显式的优化级接口。TCA4Rec 包含 (i) Collaborative Tokenizer，将原始的 item 级 CF logits 投影为与 LLM token 空间对齐的 token 级分布，以及 (ii) Soft Label Alignment，将这些蕴含 CF 信息的分布与 one-hot 监督相结合，以优化一个软 NTP 目标。该设计在保留 LLM 训练生成式特性的同时，实现了与 CF 模型所刻画的关键用户偏好的协同对齐。我们强调 TCA4Rec 与任意传统 CF 模型兼容，并能泛化到广泛的基于 decoder 的 LLM 推荐器架构。此外，它提供了一种显式机制来平衡行为对齐与语义流畅性，产生既准确又可控的生成式推荐。大量实验表明，TCA4Rec 在广泛的 CF 模型和基于 LLM 的推荐系统上持续提升推荐性能。
Every Preference Has Its Strength: Injecting Ordinal Semantics into LLM-Based Recommenders
KAIST2026推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐ordinal preference, semantic anchors, collaborative filtering signals, hybrid CF-LLM, preference strengthPDF · arXiv
AuthorsJiwon Jeong, Donghee Han, Sungrae Hong, Woosung Kang, Mun Yong Yi
Abstract (EN)
Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can enhance recommender systems by integrating collaborative filtering (CF) signals through hybrid prompting. However, most existing CF-LLM frameworks collapse explicit ratings into implicit or positive-only feedback, discarding the ordinal structure that conveys fine-grained preference strength. As a result, these models struggle to exploit graded semantics and nuanced preference distinctions. We propose Ordinal Semantic Anchoring (OSA), a hybrid CF-LLM framework that explicitly incorporates preference strength by modeling interaction-level user feedback. OSA represents ordinal preference levels as numeric textual tokens and uses their token embeddings as semantic anchors to align user-item interaction representations in the LLM latent space. Through strength-aware alignment across ordinal levels, OSA preserves preference semantics when integrating collaborative signals with LLMs. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that OSA consistently outperforms existing baselines, particularly in pairwise preference evaluation, highlighting its effectiveness in modeling fine-grained user preferences over prior CF-LLM methods.
摘要 (中文)
近期研究表明，大语言模型（LLMs）可以通过混合 prompting 整合协同过滤（CF）信号来增强推荐系统。然而，大多数现有 CF-LLM 框架将显式评分坍缩为隐式或仅正向反馈，丢弃了传达细粒度偏好强度的序数结构。结果，这些模型难以利用分级语义和细微的偏好区分。我们提出 Ordinal Semantic Anchoring（OSA），一个混合 CF-LLM 框架，通过建模交互级用户反馈来显式纳入偏好强度。OSA 将序数偏好等级表示为数值文本 token，并使用其 token embedding 作为语义锚点，在 LLM 潜空间中对齐用户-item 交互表示。通过跨序数等级的强度感知对齐，OSA 在将协同信号与 LLMs 整合时保留了偏好语义。在多个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，OSA 持续优于现有 baseline，尤其在成对偏好评估中，凸显了其相较此前 CF-LLM 方法在建模细粒度用户偏好方面的有效性。
Beyond Static Best-of-N: Bayesian List-wise Alignment for LLM-based Recommendation
USTC / 浙大2026推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐list-wise alignment, Best-of-N, Bayesian framework, LLM4Rec, ranking metricsPDF · arXiv
AuthorsRuijun Chen, Chongming Gao, Jiawei Chen, Weiqin Yang, Xiangnan He
Abstract (EN)
Large Language Models have revolutionized recommender systems (LLM4Rec) by leveraging their generative capabilities to model complex user preferences. However, existing LLM4Rec methods primarily rely on token-level objectives, making it difficult to optimize list-level and non-differentiable metrics (e.g., NDCG, fairness) that define actual recommendation quality. While Best-of-N (BoN) directly optimizes these metrics during inference, its high computational cost hinders real-world deployment. To address this, BoN Alignment aims to distill the search capability into the model itself, yet current approaches suffer from two critical limitations: (1) Indiscriminate Supervision, where the static reference fails to distinguish the relative quality of candidates exceeding its empirical range, leading to a loss of ranking guidance; and (2) Gradient Decay, where the effective supervision signal rapidly diminishes as the evolving policy improves, resulting in inefficient optimization. To overcome these challenges, we propose BLADE (Bayesian List-wise Alignment via Dynamic Estimation). Unlike static approaches, BLADE introduces a Bayesian framework that continuously updates the target distribution by fusing historical priors with dynamic evidence from the model's current rollouts. This mechanism constructs a self-evolving target that adapts to the model's growing capabilities, ensuring the training signal remains informative throughout the learning process. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that BLADE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Crucially, it breaks the static performance upper bound, achieving sustained gains in both ranking accuracy (Recall, NDCG) and complex list-wise metrics (Fairness, Diversity). The code is available via https://github.com/RegionCh/BLADE.
摘要 (中文)
大语言模型通过其生成能力建模复杂用户偏好，彻底改变了推荐系统（LLM4Rec）。然而，现有 LLM4Rec 方法主要依赖 token 级目标，难以优化定义实际推荐质量的列表级且不可微的指标（例如 NDCG、公平性）。虽然 Best-of-N（BoN）在推理时直接优化这些指标，但其高昂的计算成本阻碍了实际部署。为此，BoN Alignment 旨在将搜索能力蒸馏进模型本身，但当前方法存在两个关键局限：(1) 无差别监督（Indiscriminate Supervision），静态参考无法区分超出其经验范围的候选之间的相对质量，导致 ranking 指导的丢失；(2) 梯度衰减（Gradient Decay），随着演化中的策略不断改进，有效监督信号迅速减弱，导致优化效率低下。为克服这些挑战，我们提出 BLADE（Bayesian List-wise Alignment via Dynamic Estimation）。与静态方法不同，BLADE 引入一个贝叶斯框架，通过融合历史先验与来自模型当前 rollout 的动态证据来持续更新目标分布。该机制构建了一个随模型能力增长而自适应演化的自演化目标，确保训练信号在整个学习过程中保持信息量。在三个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，BLADE 显著优于 SOTA baseline。至关重要的是，它打破了静态性能上界，在 ranking 准确性（Recall、NDCG）和复杂列表级指标（Fairness、Diversity）上均取得持续收益。代码见 https://github.com/RegionCh/BLADE。
ProMax: Exploring the Potential of LLM-derived Profiles with Distribution Shaping for Recommender Systems
安徽大学 / 电子科大2026推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐LLM-derived profiles, distribution shaping, dense retrieval, user profiles, implicit feedbackPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYi Zhang, Yiwen Zhang, Kai Zheng, Tong Chen, Hongzhi Yin
Abstract (EN)
The remarkable text understanding and generation capabilities of large language models (LLMs) have revitalized the field of general recommendation based on implicit user feedback. Rather than deploying LLMs directly as recommendation models, a more flexible paradigm leverages their ability to interpret users' historical interactions and semantic contexts to extract structured profiles that characterize user preferences. These profiles can be further transformed into actionable high-dimensional representations, serving as powerful signals to augment and strengthen recommendation models. However, the mechanism by which such profiles enhance recommendation performance within the feature space remains insufficiently understood. Moreover, existing studies predominantly rely on nonlinear alignment and fusion strategies to incorporate these profiles, which often lead to semantic loss and fail to fully exploit their potential. To address these limitations, we revisit profiles from a retrieval perspective and propose a simple yet effective recommendation framework built upon distribution shaping (ProMax) in this paper. We begin by employing dense retrieval to uncover the collaborative relationships between user and item profiles within the feature space. Based on this insight, we introduce a dual distribution-reshaping process, in which the profile distribution acts as a guiding signal to steer the recommendation model toward learning user preferences for unseen items beyond the scope of observed interactions. We apply ProMax to four classic recommendation methods on three public datasets. The results indicate that ProMax substantially improves base model performance and outperforms existing LLM-based recommendation approaches.
摘要 (中文)
大语言模型（LLMs）卓越的文本理解与生成能力，重振了基于隐式用户反馈的通用推荐领域。与其将 LLMs 直接用作推荐模型，一种更灵活的范式利用其解读用户历史交互和语义上下文的能力，来提取刻画用户偏好的结构化 profile。这些 profile 可进一步转化为可操作的高维表示，作为强大信号来增强并强化推荐模型。然而，此类 profile 在特征空间内提升推荐性能的机制仍未得到充分理解。此外，现有研究主要依赖非线性对齐与融合策略来纳入这些 profile，这往往导致语义损失，未能充分发挥其潜力。为弥补这些局限，我们从 retrieval 的视角重新审视 profile，并在本文中提出一个简单而有效的、建立在分布塑造（distribution shaping）之上的推荐框架（ProMax）。我们首先采用稠密检索来揭示特征空间中用户 profile 与 item profile 之间的协同关系。基于这一洞见，我们引入一个双重分布重塑过程，其中 profile 分布作为引导信号，推动推荐模型学习用户对观测交互范围之外的未见 item 的偏好。我们将 ProMax 应用于三个公开数据集上的四种经典推荐方法。结果表明，ProMax 大幅提升了基础模型性能，并优于现有的基于 LLM 的推荐方法。
Filling the Gaps: Selective Knowledge Augmentation for LLM Recommenders
POSTECH / Korea University2026推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐knowledge gap problem, selective knowledge augmentation, training-free recommender, context efficiency, knowledge probingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsJaehyun Lee, Sanghwan Jang, SeongKu Kang, Hwanjo Yu
Abstract (EN)
Large language models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful training-free recommenders. However, their knowledge of individual items is inevitably uneven due to imbalanced information exposure during pretraining, a phenomenon we refer to as knowledge gap problem. To address this, most prior methods have employed a naive uniform augmentation that appends external information for every item in the input prompt. However, this approach not only wastes limited context budget on redundant augmentation for well-known items but can also hinder the model's effective reasoning. To this end, we propose KnowSA_CKP (Knowledge-aware Selective Augmentation with Comparative Knowledge Probing) to mitigate the knowledge gap problem. KnowSA_CKP estimates the LLM's internal knowledge by evaluating its capability to capture collaborative relationships and selectively injects additional information only where it is most needed. By avoiding unnecessary augmentation for well-known items, KnowSA_CKP focuses on items that benefit most from knowledge supplementation, thereby making more effective use of the context budget. KnowSA_CKP requires no fine-tuning step, and consistently improves both recommendation accuracy and context efficiency across four real-world datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/nowhyun/KnowSA\_CKP.
摘要 (中文)
大语言模型（LLMs）近来作为强大的免训练推荐器崭露头角。然而，由于预训练期间信息曝光不均衡，它们对单个 item 的知识不可避免是不均匀的，我们将这一现象称为知识鸿沟（knowledge gap）问题。为解决此问题，此前大多数方法采用了朴素的均匀增强，即为输入 prompt 中的每个 item 都附加外部信息。然而，这种做法不仅将有限的上下文预算浪费在对众所周知 item 的冗余增强上，还可能妨碍模型的有效推理。为此，我们提出 KnowSA_CKP（Knowledge-aware Selective Augmentation with Comparative Knowledge Probing）来缓解知识鸿沟问题。KnowSA_CKP 通过评估 LLM 捕捉协同关系的能力来估计其内部知识，并仅在最为需要处选择性地注入额外信息。通过对众所周知 item 避免不必要的增强，KnowSA_CKP 聚焦于那些最能从知识补充中获益的 item，从而更有效地利用上下文预算。KnowSA_CKP 无需微调步骤，并在四个真实世界数据集上持续提升推荐准确性和上下文效率。我们的代码见 https://github.com/nowhyun/KnowSA\_CKP。
Mixture-of-Experts Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Multi-Agent LLM-based Recommendation
港理工 / 新加坡国立2026推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐knowledge graph retrieval-augmented generation, Mixture-of-Experts, multi-agent, contrastive learning, policy optimizationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsShijie Wang, Chengyi Liu, Yujuan Ding, Shanru Lin, See-Kiong Ng, Xu Xin, Wenqi Fan
Abstract (EN)
Large language models (LLMs) have recently been adopted for recommendations due to their ability to understand user intent and item semantics. However, LLM-based recommender systems often rely on parametric knowledge and suffer from outdated knowledge, motivating knowledge graph retrieval-augmented generation (KG-RAG) to ground recommendations on structured, up-to-date KGs. Despite this promise, effective KG-RAG in recommendations faces great challenges. First, users' queries vary in complexity and require KG knowledge at different granularities, whereas existing methods adopt a one-size-fits-all retrieval strategy, leading to over-retrieval for simple queries and under-retrieval for complex ones. In addition, augmenting LLMs with KG knowledge requires translating graph-structured data into linear text, which may introduce noise and cause structural information loss. Moreover, the selection of retrieval granularity lacks direct supervision and must be inferred from the final recommendation after alignment and downstream utilization, making query-aware retrieval hard to learn end-to-end. To address these issues, we propose MixRAGRec, a cooperative multi-agent framework for KG-RAG recommendations. MixRAGRec integrates a Mixture-of-Experts Retrieval Agent that routes each query to a KG retrieval expert with different granularities, a Knowledge Preference Alignment Agent that converts structured knowledge into LLM-friendly natural language, and a Contrastive Learning-reinforced Recommendation Agent trained with contrastive preference feedback. Notably, we introduce Mixture-of-Experts Multi-Agent Policy Optimization (MMAPO) to train three agents under a unified objective. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.
摘要 (中文)
近来，由于能够理解用户意图与商品语义，Large Language Models（LLM）被用于推荐。然而，基于 LLM 的推荐系统往往依赖参数化知识，并受困于知识过时，这促使人们采用 knowledge graph retrieval-augmented generation（KG-RAG），将推荐建立在结构化、最新的 KG 之上。尽管前景可期，在推荐中有效实现 KG-RAG 仍面临巨大挑战。首先，用户 query 的复杂程度各异，需要不同粒度的 KG 知识，而现有方法采用一刀切的检索策略，导致对简单 query 过度检索、对复杂 query 检索不足。此外，用 KG 知识增强 LLM 需要把图结构数据转写为线性文本，这可能引入噪声并造成结构信息损失。再者，检索粒度的选择缺乏直接监督，必须在对齐与下游使用之后从最终推荐结果反推，使得 query 感知的检索难以端到端学习。为解决这些问题，我们提出 MixRAGRec，一个面向 KG-RAG 推荐的协同多智能体框架。MixRAGRec 集成了一个 Mixture-of-Experts Retrieval Agent，将每个 query 路由到具有不同粒度的 KG 检索专家；一个 Knowledge Preference Alignment Agent，将结构化知识转换为 LLM 友好的自然语言；以及一个以对比偏好反馈训练的 Contrastive Learning-reinforced Recommendation Agent。值得注意的是，我们提出 Mixture-of-Experts Multi-Agent Policy Optimization（MMAPO），在统一目标下训练这三个智能体。在真实数据集上的大量实验证明了我们框架的有效性。
Enriching Semantic Profiles into Knowledge Graph for Recommender Systems Using Large Language Models
Inha University / Sogang University2026推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐semantic profiles, knowledge graph, LLM, profile-aware KG aggregation, preference matchingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsSeokho Ahn, Sungbok Shin, Young-Duk Seo
Abstract (EN)
Rich and informative profiling to capture user preferences is essential for improving recommendation quality. However, there is still no consensus on how best to construct and utilize such profiles. To address this, we revisit recent profiling-based approaches in recommender systems along four dimensions: 1) knowledge base, 2) preference indicator, 3) impact range, and 4) subject. We argue that large language models (LLMs) are effective at extracting compressed rationales from diverse knowledge sources, while knowledge graphs (KGs) are better suited for propagating these profiles to extend their reach. Building on this insight, we propose a new recommendation model, called SPiKE. SPiKE consists of three core components: i) Entity profile generation, which uses LLMs to generate semantic profiles for all KG entities; ii) Profile-aware KG aggregation, which integrates these profiles into the KG; and iii) Pairwise profile preference matching, which aligns LLM- and KG-based representations during training. In experiments, we demonstrate that SPiKE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art KG- and LLM-based recommenders in real-world settings.
摘要 (中文)
丰富且有信息量的用户画像对于捕捉用户偏好、提升推荐质量至关重要。然而，如何最好地构建与利用此类画像，目前仍无共识。为此，我们从四个维度重新审视推荐系统中近期的画像类方法：1）知识库，2）偏好指示物，3）影响范围，4）主体。我们认为，Large Language Models（LLM）擅长从多样化的知识来源中抽取压缩后的理性依据（rationale），而 knowledge graphs（KG）更适合传播这些画像以扩展其触达范围。基于这一洞见，我们提出一个新的推荐模型 SPiKE。SPiKE 包含三个核心组件：i）Entity profile generation，使用 LLM 为所有 KG 实体生成语义画像；ii）Profile-aware KG aggregation，将这些画像整合进 KG；iii）Pairwise profile preference matching，在训练中对齐基于 LLM 与基于 KG 的表示。在实验中，我们证明 SPiKE 在真实场景下持续优于 state-of-the-art 的基于 KG 与基于 LLM 的推荐器。
ReRec: Reasoning-Augmented LLM-based Recommendation Assistant via Reinforcement Fine-tuning
港理工2026推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐reinforcement fine-tuning, reasoning-augmented recommendation, reward shaping, curriculum learning, recommendation assistantPDF · arXiv
AuthorsJiani Huang, Shijie Wang, Liangbo Ning, Wenqi Fan, Qing Li
Abstract (EN)
With the rise of LLMs, there is an increasing need for intelligent recommendation assistants that can handle complex queries and provide personalized, reasoning-driven recommendations. LLM-based recommenders show potential but face challenges in multi-step reasoning, underscoring the need for reasoning-augmented systems. To address this gap, we propose ReRec, a novel reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) framework designed to improve LLM reasoning in complex recommendation tasks. Our framework introduces three key components: (1) Dual-Graph Enhanced Reward Shaping, integrating recommendation metrics like NDCG@K with Query Alignment and Preference Alignment Scores to provide fine-grained reward signals for LLM optimization; (2) Reasoning-aware Advantage Estimation, which decomposes LLM outputs into reasoning segments and penalizes incorrect steps to enhance reasoning of recommendation; and (3) Online Curriculum Scheduler, dynamically assess query difficulty and organize training curriculum to ensure stable learning during RFT. Experiments demonstrate that ReRec outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and preserves core abilities like instruction-following and general knowledge. Our codes are available at https://github.com/jiani-huang/ReRec.
摘要 (中文)
随着 LLM 的兴起，对能够处理复杂 query 并提供个性化、推理驱动推荐的智能推荐助手的需求日益增长。基于 LLM 的推荐器展现出潜力，但在多步推理上面临挑战，凸显了推理增强系统的必要性。为填补这一空白，我们提出 ReRec，一个新颖的 reinforcement fine-tuning（RFT）框架，旨在提升 LLM 在复杂推荐任务中的推理能力。我们的框架引入三个关键组件：（1）Dual-Graph Enhanced Reward Shaping，将 NDCG@K 等推荐指标与 Query Alignment 和 Preference Alignment Scores 相结合，为 LLM 优化提供细粒度 reward 信号；（2）Reasoning-aware Advantage Estimation，将 LLM 输出分解为推理片段并对错误步骤施加惩罚，以增强推荐的推理；（3）Online Curriculum Scheduler，动态评估 query 难度并组织训练课程，确保 RFT 过程中的稳定学习。实验表明，ReRec 优于 state-of-the-art baseline，并保留了指令遵循与通用知识等核心能力。代码见 https://github.com/jiani-huang/ReRec。
Uncertainty-aware Generative Recommendation
USTC2026推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐generative recommendation, uncertainty, preference optimization, confidence alignment, risk-awarePDF · arXiv
AuthorsChenxiao Fan, Chongming Gao, Yaxin Gong, Haoyan Liu, Fuli Feng, Xiangnan He
Abstract (EN)
Generative Recommendation has emerged as a transformative paradigm, reformulating recommendation as an end-to-end autoregressive sequence generation task. Despite its promise, existing preference optimization methods typically rely on binary outcome correctness, suffering from a systemic limitation we term uncertainty blindness. This issue manifests in the neglect of the model's intrinsic generation confidence, the variation in sample learning difficulty, and the lack of explicit confidence expression, directly leading to unstable training dynamics and unquantifiable decision risks. In this paper, we propose Uncertainty-aware Generative Recommendation (UGR), a unified framework that leverages uncertainty as a critical signal for adaptive optimization. UGR synergizes three mechanisms: (1) an uncertainty-weighted reward to penalize confident errors; (2) difficulty-aware optimization dynamics to prevent premature convergence; and (3) explicit confidence alignment to empower the model with confidence expression capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UGR not only yields superior recommendation performance but also fundamentally stabilizes training, preventing the performance degradation often observed in standard methods. Furthermore, the learned confidence enables reliable downstream risk-aware applications. Our project repository is available at: https://github.com/cxfann/UGR.
摘要 (中文)
生成式推荐已成为一种变革性范式，它将推荐重新表述为端到端的自回归序列生成任务。尽管前景广阔，但现有的偏好优化方法通常依赖二元的结果正确性，并因此受困于一种我们称之为不确定性盲（uncertainty blindness）的系统性局限。该问题表现为：忽视模型内在的生成置信度、样本学习难度的差异，以及缺乏显式的置信度表达，直接导致训练动态不稳定和决策风险无法量化。在本文中，我们提出了不确定性感知生成式推荐（Uncertainty-aware Generative Recommendation, UGR），一个统一的框架，将不确定性作为自适应优化的关键信号加以利用。UGR 协同了三种机制：（1）不确定性加权奖励，用以惩罚自信的错误；（2）难度感知的优化动态，以防止过早收敛；（3）显式的置信度对齐，赋予模型置信度表达能力。大量实验表明，UGR 不仅带来更优的推荐性能，还从根本上稳定了训练，避免了标准方法中常见的性能退化。此外，学习到的置信度使可靠的下游风险感知应用成为可能。我们的项目仓库可在 https://github.com/cxfann/UGR 获取。
Mixture of Sequence: Theme-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Sequence Recommendation
Meta2026推荐工业长序列建模mixture-of-experts, long-sequence recommendation, theme-aware routing, multi-scale fusion, session hoppingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsXiao Lin, Zhicheng Tang, Weilin Cong, Mengyue Hang, Kai Wang, Yajuan Wang, Zhichen Zeng, Ting-Wei Li, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Sequential recommendation has rapidly advanced in click-through rate prediction due to its ability to model dynamic user interests. A key challenge, however, lies in modeling long sequences: users often exhibit significant interest shifts, introducing substantial irrelevant or misleading information. Our empirical analysis corroborates this challenge and uncovers a recurring behavioral pattern in long sequences (\textit{session hopping}): user interests remain stable within short temporal spans (\textit{sessions}) but shift drastically across sessions and may reappear after multiple sessions. To address this challenge, we propose the Mixture of Sequence (MoS) framework, a model-agnostic MoE approach that achieves accurate predictions by extracting theme-specific and multi-scale subsequences from noisy raw user sequences. First, MoS employs a theme-aware routing mechanism to adaptively learn the latent themes of user sequences and organizes these sequences into multiple coherent subsequences. Each subsequence contains only sessions aligned with a specific theme, thereby effectively filtering out irrelevant or even misleading information introduced by user interest shifts in session hopping. In addition, to alleviate potential information loss, we introduce a multi-scale fusion mechanism, which leverages three types of experts to capture global sequence characteristics, short-term user behaviors, and theme-specific semantic patterns. Together, these two mechanisms endow MoS with the ability to deliver accurate recommendations from multi-faceted and multi-scale perspectives. Experimental results demonstrate that MoS consistently achieves the SOTA performance while introducing fewer FLOPs compared with other MoE counterparts, providing strong evidence of its excellent balance between utility and efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/xiaolin-cs/MoS.
摘要 (中文)
序列推荐因其对动态用户兴趣的建模能力，在点击率预测领域发展迅速。然而，一个关键挑战在于长序列建模：用户往往表现出显著的兴趣漂移，引入了大量无关或误导性信息。我们的实证分析印证了这一挑战，并揭示出长序列中一种反复出现的行为模式（会话跳转，session hopping）：用户兴趣在短时间跨度（会话，session）内保持稳定，但在会话之间会发生剧烈转移，并可能在多个会话之后再次出现。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了 Mixture of Sequence（MoS）框架，一种模型无关的 MoE 方法，通过从含噪的原始用户序列中抽取主题专属且多尺度的子序列来实现准确预测。首先，MoS 采用主题感知路由机制，自适应地学习用户序列的潜在主题，并将这些序列组织为多个连贯的子序列。每个子序列仅包含与特定主题对齐的会话，从而有效滤除会话跳转中用户兴趣转移所引入的无关甚至误导性信息。此外，为缓解潜在的信息损失，我们引入了多尺度融合机制，利用三类 expert 分别捕捉全局序列特征、短期用户行为和主题专属的语义模式。这两个机制共同赋予 MoS 从多面向、多尺度视角提供准确推荐的能力。实验结果表明，MoS 在持续取得 SOTA 性能的同时，相比其他 MoE 同类方法引入了更少的 FLOPs，有力证明了其在效用与效率之间出色的平衡。代码可在 https://github.com/xiaolin-cs/MoS 获取。
MTFM: A Scalable and Alignment-free Foundation Model for Industrial Recommendation in Meituan
美团2026推荐工业工业落地系统industrial recommendation foundation model, multi-scenario, cross-domain, alignment-free, heterogeneous tokensPDF
AuthorsXin Song, Zhilin Guan, Ruidong Han, Binghao Tang, Tianwen Chen, Bing Li, Zihao Li, Han Zhang, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Industrial recommendation systems typically involve multiple scenarios, yet existing cross-domain (CDR) and multi-scenario (MSR) methods often require prohibitive resources and strict input alignment, limiting their extensibility. We propose MTFM (Meituan Foundation Model for Recommendation), a transformer-based framework that addresses these challenges. Instead of pre-aligning inputs, MTFM transforms cross-domain data into heterogeneous tokens, capturing multi-scenario knowledge in an alignment-free manner. To enhance efficiency, we first introduce a multi-scenario user-level sample aggregation that significantly enhances training throughput by reducing the total number of instances. We further integrate Grouped-Query Attention and a customized Hybrid Target Attention to minimize memory usage and computational complexity. Furthermore, we implement various system-level optimizations, such as kernel fusion and the elimination of CPU-GPU blocking, to further enhance both training and inference throughput. Offline and online experiments validate the effectiveness of MTFM, demonstrating that significant performance gains are achieved by scaling both model capacity and multi-scenario training data.
摘要 (中文)
工业推荐系统通常涉及多个场景，然而现有的跨域（CDR）和多场景（MSR）方法往往需要高昂的资源投入和严格的输入对齐，限制了其可扩展性。我们提出 MTFM（Meituan Foundation Model for Recommendation），一个基于 Transformer 的框架来应对这些挑战。MTFM 不对输入进行预对齐，而是将跨域数据转换为异构 token，以 alignment-free 的方式捕捉多场景知识。为提升效率，我们首先引入多场景用户级样本聚合，通过减少实例总数显著提高训练吞吐。我们进一步集成 Grouped-Query Attention 和定制的 Hybrid Target Attention，以降低内存占用和计算复杂度。此外，我们实施了多种系统级优化，例如 kernel fusion 和消除 CPU-GPU 阻塞，进一步提升训练和推理吞吐。离线和在线实验验证了 MTFM 的有效性，表明通过同时扩展模型容量和多场景训练数据可获得显著的性能提升。
The Tencent Advertising Algorithm Challenge 2025: All-Modality Generative Recommendation
腾讯2026推荐工业工业落地系统generative recommendation, all-modality, benchmark dataset, multi-modal sequence generation, advertisingPDF
AuthorsJunwei Pan, Wei Xue, Chao Zhou, Xing Zhou, Lunan Fan, Yanbo Wang, Haoran Xin, Zhiyu Hu, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Generative recommender systems are rapidly emerging as a new paradigm for recommendation, where collaborative identifiers and/or multi-modal content are mapped into discrete token spaces and user behavior is modelled with autoregressive sequence models. Despite progress on multi-modal recommendation datasets, there is still a lack of public benchmarks that jointly offer large-scale, realistic and fully all-modality data designed specifically for generative recommendation (GR) in industrial advertising. To foster research in this direction, we organised the Tencent Advertising Algorithm Challenge 2025, a global competition built on top of two all-modality datasets for GR: TencentGR-1M and TencentGR-10M. Both datasets are constructed from real de-identified Tencent Ads logs and contain rich collaborative IDs and multi-modal representations extracted with state-of-the-art embedding models. The preliminary track (TencentGR-1M) provides 1 million user sequences with up to 100 interacted items each, where each interaction is labeled with exposure and click signals, while the final track (TencentGR-10M) scales this to 10 million users and explicitly distinguishes between click and conversion events at both the sequence and target level. This paper presents the task definition, data construction process, feature schema, baseline GR model, evaluation protocol, and key findings from top-ranked and award-winning solutions. Our datasets focus on multi-modal sequence generation in an advertising setting and introduce weighted evaluation for high-value conversion events. We release our datasets at this https URL and baseline implementations at this https URL to enable future research on all-modality generative recommendation at an industrial scale. The official website is this https URL.
摘要 (中文)
生成式推荐系统正迅速成为推荐的一种新范式，其中协同标识符和/或多模态内容被映射到离散 token 空间，用户行为则用自回归序列模型建模。尽管在多模态推荐数据集方面已有进展，但仍缺乏同时提供大规模、真实且完全 all-modality 的、专为工业广告中的生成式推荐（GR）设计的公开 benchmark。为推动这一方向的研究，我们组织了 Tencent Advertising Algorithm Challenge 2025，这是一个基于两个面向 GR 的 all-modality 数据集 TencentGR-1M 和 TencentGR-10M 的全球竞赛。这两个数据集均由真实的脱敏 Tencent Ads 日志构建，包含丰富的协同 ID 以及用 state-of-the-art embedding 模型提取的多模态表示。初赛赛道（TencentGR-1M）提供 100 万条用户序列，每条最多包含 100 个交互物品，其中每次交互都标注了曝光和点击信号；决赛赛道（TencentGR-10M）将其扩展到 1000 万用户，并在序列层面和目标层面明确区分点击与转化事件。本文介绍了任务定义、数据构建过程、特征 schema、baseline GR 模型、评测协议，以及排名靠前和获奖方案的主要发现。我们的数据集聚焦于广告场景下的多模态序列生成，并针对高价值转化事件引入了加权评测。我们在 this https URL 发布了数据集，在 this https URL 发布了 baseline 实现，以支持未来面向工业规模 all-modality 生成式推荐的研究。官方网站为 this https URL。
DaV-Gen: End-to-End Generative Retrieval via Draft-and-Verify
阿里2026推荐工业工业落地系统generative retrieval, Draft-and-Verify, multi-stage cascade architectures, end-to-end generation, contrastive lossPDF · arXiv
AuthorsMeng Zhao, Chunmei Liu, Qinyong Wang
Abstract (EN)
Mainstream industrial information retrieval systems (e.g., search and recommendation) are usually built upon Multi-Stage Cascade Architectures (MCAs), which balance effectiveness and efficiency through a coarse-to-fine ``retrieval-ranking'' pipeline. However, the optimization objectives across different stages are substantially inconsistent, propagating or even amplifying the early-stage errors that ultimately degrade the quality of final results. While emerging end-to-end generative models offer a potential solution by unifying the pipeline, their online serving performance is severely hindered by the auto-regressive process inherited from the standard decoder-only structure. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{DaV-Gen}, a novel unified solution designed to fundamentally refactor the paradigm for both search and recommendation via a ``Draft-and-Verify'' mechanism. Inspired by the process used by speculative decoding, our framework redesigns the generation task into two synergistic operations within a single model. During training, the model is concurrently optimized for both candidate drafting and fine-grained verification. This is achieved by a composite loss function that jointly trains the model on two distinct but related objectives: 1) a contrastive loss that structures the embedding space for efficient drafting, and 2) a fusion loss that combines generative likelihood with vector similarity to produce a superior verification score. This integrated training strategy equips the model with dual capabilities. At inference time, it first performs highly efficient vector-based drafting to generate a candidate set, and then verifies these candidates using the more powerful fused scoring function, thereby achieving both the speed of sparse drafting and the precision of advanced generative models within a unified, end-to-end architecture.
摘要 (中文)
主流工业信息检索系统（如搜索与推荐）通常构建于多阶段级联架构（MCA）之上，通过由粗到细的“retrieval-ranking”流水线来平衡效果与效率。然而，不同阶段之间的优化目标存在显著不一致，会传播甚至放大早期阶段的错误，最终损害最终结果的质量。新兴的端到端生成式模型虽然通过统一流水线提供了潜在解决方案，但其在线服务性能严重受制于标准 decoder-only 结构所固有的自回归过程。为弥合这一差距，我们提出 DaV-Gen，一种全新的统一方案，旨在通过“Draft-and-Verify”机制从根本上重构搜索与推荐的范式。受 speculative decoding 所用过程的启发，我们的框架将生成任务重新设计为单一模型内的两种协同操作。训练时，模型同时针对候选草拟与细粒度验证进行优化，这通过一个复合损失函数实现，该函数以两个不同但相关的目标联合训练模型：1）一个对比损失，用于构建高效草拟的 embedding 空间；2）一个融合损失，将生成似然与向量相似度结合，以产生更优的验证得分。这种集成训练策略赋予模型双重能力。推理时，它首先执行高效的基于向量的草拟以生成候选集，然后用更强大的融合打分函数验证这些候选，从而在统一的端到端架构中同时实现稀疏草拟的速度与先进生成式模型的精度。
GenRec: A Preference-Oriented Generative Framework for Large-Scale Recommendation
京东 / Waseda University2026推荐工业工业落地系统generative retrieval, page-wise next-token prediction, semantic IDs, token merger, preference alignmentPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYanyan Zou, Junbo Qi, Lunsong Huang, Yu Li, Kewei Xu, Jiabao Gao, Binglei Zhao, Xuanhua Yang, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Generative Retrieval (GR) offers a promising paradigm for recommendation through next-token prediction (NTP). However, scaling it to large-scale industrial systems introduces three challenges: (i) within a single request, the identical model inputs may produce inconsistent outputs due to the pagination request mechanism; (ii) the prohibitive cost of encoding long user behavior sequences with multi-token item representations based on semantic IDs, and (iii) aligning the generative policy with nuanced user preference signals. We present GenRec, a preference-oriented generative framework deployed on the JD App that addresses above challenges within a single decoder-only architecture. For training objective, we propose Page-wise NTP task, which supervises over an entire interaction page rather than each interacted item individually, providing denser gradient signal and resolving the one-to-many ambiguity of point-wise training. On the prefilling side, an asymmetric linear Token Merger compresses multi-token Semantic IDs in the prompt while preserving full-resolution decoding, reducing input length by ~2X with negligible accuracy loss. To further align outputs with user satisfaction, we introduce GRPO-SR, a reinforcement learning method that pairs Group Relative Policy Optimization with NLL regularization for training stability, and employs Hybrid Rewards combining a dense reward model with a relevance gate to mitigate reward hacking. In month-long online A/B tests serving production traffic, GenRec achieves 9.5% improvement in click count and 8.7% in transaction count over the existing pipeline.
摘要 (中文)
Generative Retrieval（GR）通过下一 token 预测（NTP）为推荐提供了有前景的范式。然而，将其扩展至大规模工业系统会带来三个挑战：(i) 在单次请求内，相同的模型输入可能因分页请求机制而产生不一致的输出；(ii) 基于 semantic IDs 的多 token 物品表示编码长用户行为序列的成本过高；(iii) 使生成策略与细粒度的用户偏好信号对齐。我们提出 GenRec，一个部署于京东 App 的面向偏好的生成式框架，在单一 decoder-only 架构内解决上述挑战。在训练目标方面，我们提出 Page-wise NTP 任务，它监督整个交互页面而非单个交互物品，从而提供更稠密的梯度信号，并解决逐点训练的一对多歧义。在预填充侧，一个非对称线性 Token Merger 在 prompt 中压缩多 token Semantic IDs，同时保持全分辨率解码，将输入长度减少约 2 倍而精度损失可忽略。为进一步使输出与用户满意度对齐，我们引入 GRPO-SR，一种强化学习方法，将 Group Relative Policy Optimization 与 NLL 正则相结合以提升训练稳定性，并采用由稠密奖励模型与相关性门控组成的 Hybrid Rewards 来缓解奖励劫持。在服务生产流量的长达一个月的在线 A/B 测试中，GenRec 相比现有流水线在点击次数上提升 9.5%，在成交次数上提升 8.7%。
SIGMA: A Semantic-Grounded Instruction-Driven Generative Multi-Task Recommender at AliExpress
阿里国际2026推荐工业工业落地系统generative recommendation, instruction-following, multi-task recommendation, item tokenization, semantic groundingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYang Yu, Lei Kou, Huaikuan Yi, Bin Chen, Yayu Cao, Lei Shen, Chao Zhang, Bing Wang, et al.
Abstract (EN)
With the rapid evolution of Large Language Models (LLMs), generative recommendation is gradually reshaping the paradigm of recommender systems. However, most existing methods remain confined to the interaction-driven next-item prediction paradigm, struggling to keep pace with the latest evolving trends or address the diverse recommendation tasks along with business-specific requirements in real-world scenarios. To this end, we present SIGMA, a Semantic-Grounded Instruction-Driven Generative Multi-Task Recommender deployed at AliExpress. Specifically, we first ground item entities in a unified latent space capturing both general semantics and collaborative signals. Building upon this, we introduce a hybrid item tokenization method for both precise modeling and efficient generation. Moreover, we construct a large-scale multi-task supervised fine-tuning dataset empowering SIGMA to fulfill various recommendation demands via instruction-following. Finally, we design a three-step item generation procedure integrated with an adaptive probabilistic fusion mechanism to calibrate the output distributions based on task-specific requirements for recommendation accuracy and diversity. Extensive offline experiments and online A/B tests demonstrate the effectiveness of SIGMA across various real-world recommendation tasks.
摘要 (中文)
随着大语言模型（LLM）的快速演进，生成式推荐正逐步重塑推荐系统的范式。然而，大多数现有方法仍局限于交互驱动的下一物品预测范式，难以跟上最新的演进趋势，也难以应对现实场景中多样的推荐任务与业务特定需求。为此，我们提出 SIGMA，一个部署于 AliExpress 的语义锚定、指令驱动的生成式多任务推荐器。具体而言，我们首先将物品实体锚定在一个同时捕获通用语义与协同信号的统一潜在空间中。在此基础上，我们引入一种混合物品 tokenization 方法，以兼顾精确建模与高效生成。此外，我们构建了一个大规模多任务监督微调数据集，使 SIGMA 能通过指令遵循满足各种推荐需求。最后，我们设计了一个三步物品生成流程，并整合自适应概率融合机制，以根据任务特定的推荐准确性与多样性需求校准输出分布。大量离线实验与在线 A/B 测试证明了 SIGMA 在各种真实推荐任务上的有效性。
Beyond Item IDs: Scaling Short-Form-Video Recommendation via Semantic-Native Long Sequence Modeling
Google2026推荐工业工业落地系统semantic ID, long sequence modeling, short-form video recommendation, Global-Aware Compression Transformer, cold-startPDF · arXiv
AuthorsRuixiao Sun, Diego Uribe Mora, Zhimeng Jiang, Yuanzhen Lin, Jiarui Wang, Yuening Li, Danfeng Guo, Zhizhong Chen, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Capturing user interests across extensive watch histories is critical for short-form video recommendation, yet scaling sequence length is limited by two bottlenecks: the semantic sparsity of atomic Video IDs and the quadratic computational complexity of Transformers. Traditional orthogonal Video IDs fail to capture content relationships and demand large embedding tables, while the quadratic complexity of self-attention restricts the maximum sequence length under strict industrial latency and resource constraints. In this work, we present a production-deployed framework for modeling ultra-long user behavior sequences at a billion-user scale. We first address the representation bottleneck by adopting content-native Semantic IDs. By utilizing depth-truncated, coarse-grained Semantic IDs, we shrink the embedding table size from corpus cardinality. This compact representation naturally generalizes to cold-start content through shared semantic prefixes. Second, to overcome the sequence scaling barrier, we introduce a Global-Aware Compression Transformer that leverages non-parametric temporal folding and unified global query integration to effectively condense the sequence, alleviating both the memory and computational bottlenecks of standard self-attention. Offline profiling on our computing infrastructure demonstrates an order-of-magnitude reduction in peak memory footprint and a drastic decrease in computational overhead. This efficiency gain enables supporting longer sequence lengths at an affordable cost in production, yielding substantial online gains in satisfied user engagement and satisfied content consumption in large-scale online A/B tests.
摘要 (中文)
从漫长的观看历史中捕捉用户兴趣对短视频推荐至关重要，然而扩展序列长度受制于两个瓶颈：原子化 Video ID 的语义稀疏性，以及 Transformer 的二次计算复杂度。传统的正交 Video ID 无法刻画内容之间的关系，且需要庞大的 embedding 表；而 self-attention 的二次复杂度在严格的工业级时延与资源约束下限制了最大序列长度。在本工作中，我们提出了一个已在生产环境部署的框架，用于在十亿用户规模下建模超长用户行为序列。我们首先通过采用内容原生的 Semantic ID 来解决表示瓶颈。通过使用深度截断的粗粒度 Semantic ID，我们将 embedding 表规模从语料基数缩减下来。这种紧凑表示借助共享的语义前缀，能够自然地泛化到冷启动内容。其次，为克服序列扩展的障碍，我们引入了 Global-Aware Compression Transformer，它利用非参数化的时序折叠与统一的全局 query 集成来有效压缩序列，从而缓解标准 self-attention 的内存与计算瓶颈。在我们计算基础设施上的离线性能剖析表明，峰值内存占用降低了一个数量级，计算开销也大幅下降。这一效率收益使得在生产中以可承受的成本支持更长的序列长度，并在大规模在线 A/B 测试中于满意的用户互动与满意的内容消费上取得了可观的线上收益。
From Modularity to Unity: Towards Industrial-Scale Generative Recommendation
京东2026推荐工业工业落地系统generative recommendation, unified ranking framework, causal behavior multi-task attention, gradient interference, dual-encoder tokenizationPDF
AuthorsXiaofeng Liu, Wenliang Li, Tao Yu, Guanliang Song, Zhen Chen, Jing Zhou, Dongyue Wang, Xiwei Zhao, Sulong Xu
Abstract (EN)
Traditional deep learning recommendation models (DLRMs) rely on modular architectures composed of specialized, hand-designed sub-networks to process heterogeneous data. While effective, such designs introduce strong inductive biases that limit performance and scalability. Generative recommendation represents a promising path from modularity to unity by consolidating these architectures within a single framework, yet still faces critical challenges: an incompatibility between generative architectures and discriminative tasks, gradient interference across objectives in a shared backbone, and misaligned heterogeneous sequences. To address these challenges, we present UniGenR, a unified ranking framework that fundamentally rethinks generative recommendation models (GRM) design. First, to better integrate discriminative tasks from DLRMs into generative modeling, UniGenR employs an encoder-decoder architecture named Unified Multi-Task Backbone, alongside a novel Causal Behavior Multi-task Attention mechanism that mitigates potential conflicts across different objectives. Second, for effective unified sequence modeling in the generative framework, UniGenR adopts a dual-encoder structure comprising a global token encoder for heterogeneous sequence alignment and local token encoders for dynamic tokenization and sequence adaptation. Comprehensive offline experiments and online A/B testing validate that UniGenR achieves state-of-the-art performance while establishing a new paradigm for scalable and conflict-friendly generative recommendation.
摘要 (中文)
传统深度学习推荐模型（DLRM）依赖由专门的、人工设计的子网络构成的模块化架构来处理异构数据。这类设计虽然有效，却引入了较强的归纳偏置，限制了性能与可扩展性。生成式推荐是一条从"模块化"走向"统一"的有前景路径——把上述架构整合进单一框架，但仍面临关键挑战：生成式架构与判别式任务之间的不兼容、共享骨干上多目标引发的梯度干扰，以及异构序列之间未对齐。为解决这些问题，本文提出 UniGenR，一个统一排序框架，从根本上重新思考生成式推荐模型（GRM）的设计。第一，为把 DLRM 的判别式任务更好地融入生成式建模，UniGenR 采用名为"统一多任务骨干"（Unified Multi-Task Backbone）的 encoder-decoder 架构，并提出"因果行为多任务注意力"（Causal Behavior Multi-task Attention）机制以缓解不同目标之间的冲突。第二，为实现生成式框架下有效的统一序列建模，UniGenR 采用双 encoder 结构：全局 token encoder 负责异构序列对齐，局部 token encoder 负责动态 tokenization 与序列适配。大量离线实验与线上 A/B 测试验证：UniGenR 取得 SOTA 性能，并为可扩展、冲突友好的生成式推荐树立了新范式。
SynGR: Unleashing the Potential of Cross-Modal Synergy for Generative Recommendation
北航2026推荐学术多模态推荐generative recommendation, cross-modal synergy, multimodal, synergistic information, item semanticsPDF · arXiv
AuthorsWei Chen, Xingyu Guo, Shuang Li, Fuwei Zhang, Meng Yuan, Jing Fan, Zhao Zhang, Deqing Wang, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Generative Recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm by formulating item recommendation as a sequence-to-sequence generation task over item identifiers. Recent studies have incorporated multimodal signals to provide richer token-level evidence for generation. However, existing approaches largely rely on alignment-centric fusion and underexplore synergistic information across modalities. In practice, synergistic information plays a critical role in capturing emergent item properties that cannot be inferred from any single modality alone. Such properties encode intrinsic item semantics and guide user preferences, enabling models to move beyond surface-level feature matching. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{SynGR}, a synergistic generative recommendation framework that explicitly encourages the exploitation of cross-modal dependencies during generation. By constraining overreliance on dominant modalities, SynGR enables the model to capture emergent item semantics beyond shared or modality-specific signals. Extensive experiments across three benchmark datasets demonstrate that SynGR achieves superior performance.
摘要 (中文)
生成式推荐（GR）通过将物品推荐表述为基于物品标识符的序列到序列生成任务，已成为一种有前景的范式。近期研究引入了多模态信号，为生成提供更丰富的 token 级证据。然而，现有方法大多依赖以对齐为中心的融合，对跨模态的协同信息探索不足。在实践中，协同信息对于捕捉无法从任何单一模态推断出的涌现性物品属性起着关键作用。这类属性编码了物品的内在语义并引导用户偏好，使模型能够超越表层的特征匹配。为弥补这一局限，我们提出了 \textbf{SynGR}，一个协同式生成推荐框架，在生成过程中显式地鼓励对跨模态依赖的利用。通过约束对主导模态的过度依赖，SynGR 使模型能够捕捉超越共享信号或模态专属信号的涌现性物品语义。在三个 benchmark 数据集上的大量实验表明，SynGR 取得了优越的性能。
Gated Bidirectional Linear Attention for Generative Retrieval
Yandex2026通用学术基础设施 / 基础模型gated bidirectional linear attention, generative retrieval, linear-time attention, hybrid encoder, long user historiesPDF · arXiv
AuthorsArtem Matveev, Vladislav Tytskiy, Sergei Makeev, Sergei Liamaev
Abstract (EN)
In recommender systems, generative retrieval typically uses an encoder-decoder setup: an encoder processes a user interaction history, and an autoregressive decoder then generates recommended items. In large-scale streaming services, active users accumulate very long histories over time. As histories grow, the encoder becomes a major latency bottleneck because softmax attention scales quadratically with sequence length. In our experiments, using bidirectional attention in the encoder substantially improves quality. However, most sub-quadratic attention methods focus on causal attention. We propose Gated Bidirectional Linear Attention (GBLA), a linear-time bidirectional attention layer that extends kernelized linear attention with three lightweight components: local causal mixing (Conv1D), sequence-level key gating for soft forgetting, and a gated RMSNorm output. On a large-scale Yandex Music dataset, a hybrid encoder that interleaves self-attention (SA) and GBLA in a 1:2 ratio (one SA block followed by two GBLA blocks) matches bidirectional self-attention quality. On H100 GPUs, GBLA reaches up to an $8.2\times$ single-layer speedup at a history length of 32768, compared to FlashAttention-v3. Finally, we show that the same hybrid design generalizes beyond our proprietary setting, consistently preserving self-attention retrieval quality on public Amazon benchmarks.
摘要 (中文)
在推荐系统中，生成式检索通常采用 encoder-decoder 设置：encoder 处理用户交互历史，自回归 decoder 随后生成推荐物品。在大规模流媒体服务中，活跃用户随时间累积非常长的历史。随着历史增长，encoder 成为主要的延迟瓶颈，因为 softmax attention 随序列长度呈二次增长。在我们的实验中，在 encoder 中使用双向注意力能显著提升质量。然而，大多数亚二次注意力方法聚焦于因果注意力。我们提出 Gated Bidirectional Linear Attention（GBLA），一种线性时间的双向注意力层，它在核化线性注意力基础上扩展了三个轻量组件：局部因果混合（Conv1D）、用于软遗忘的序列级 key 门控，以及门控 RMSNorm 输出。在大规模 Yandex Music 数据集上，以 1:2 比例交错自注意力（SA）与 GBLA 的混合 encoder（一个 SA 块后接两个 GBLA 块）达到了与双向自注意力相当的质量。在 H100 GPU 上，当历史长度为 32768 时，GBLA 相比 FlashAttention-v3 实现最高 8.2 倍的单层加速。最后，我们表明相同的混合设计可推广至我们的专有场景之外，在公开的 Amazon 基准上持续保持自注意力的检索质量。
Vectorizing the Trie: Efficient Constrained Decoding for LLM-based Generative Retrieval on Accelerators
Google / Yale University2026通用工业基础设施 / 基础模型constrained decoding, generative retrieval, trie, compressed sparse row, cold-startPDF · arXiv
AuthorsZhengyang Su, Isay Katsman, Yueqi Wang, Ruining He, Lukasz Heldt, Raghunandan Keshavan, Shao-Chuan Wang, Xinyang Yi, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Generative retrieval has emerged as a powerful paradigm for LLM-based recommendation. However, industrial recommender systems often benefit from restricting the output space to a constrained subset of items based on business logic (e.g. enforcing content freshness or product category), which standard autoregressive decoding cannot natively support. Moreover, existing constrained decoding methods that make use of prefix trees (Tries) incur severe latency penalties on hardware accelerators (TPUs/GPUs). In this work, we introduce STATIC (Sparse Transition Matrix-Accelerated Trie Index for Constrained Decoding), an efficient and scalable constrained decoding technique designed specifically for high-throughput LLM-based generative retrieval on TPUs/GPUs. By flattening the prefix tree into a static Compressed Sparse Row (CSR) matrix, we transform irregular tree traversals into fully vectorized sparse matrix operations, unlocking massive efficiency gains on hardware accelerators. We deploy STATIC on a large-scale industrial video recommendation platform serving billions of users. STATIC produces significant product metric impact with minimal latency overhead (0.033 ms per step and 0.25% of inference time), achieving a 948x speedup over a CPU trie implementation and a 47-1033x speedup over a hardware-accelerated binary-search baseline. Furthermore, the runtime overhead of STATIC remains extremely low across a wide range of practical configurations. To the best of our knowledge, STATIC enables the first production-scale deployment of strictly constrained generative retrieval. In addition, evaluation on academic benchmarks demonstrates that STATIC can considerably improve cold-start performance for generative retrieval. Our code is available at https://github.com/youtube/static-constraint-decoding.
摘要 (中文)
生成式检索已成为基于 LLM 的推荐的一种强大范式。然而，工业推荐系统常常受益于根据业务逻辑（例如强制内容新鲜度或商品类目）将输出空间限制到物品的受限子集，而标准的自回归解码无法原生支持这一点。此外，现有利用前缀树（Trie）的受限解码方法在硬件加速器（TPU/GPU）上会带来严重的延迟代价。在本工作中，我们提出 STATIC（Sparse Transition Matrix-Accelerated Trie Index for Constrained Decoding），一种高效且可扩展的受限解码技术，专为 TPU/GPU 上高吞吐的基于 LLM 的生成式检索而设计。通过将前缀树展平为静态的压缩稀疏行（CSR）矩阵，我们将不规则的树遍历转化为完全向量化的稀疏矩阵运算，从而在硬件加速器上释放巨大的效率收益。我们在服务数十亿用户的大规模工业视频推荐平台上部署了 STATIC。STATIC 以极小的延迟开销（每步 0.033 ms，占推理时间 0.25%）产生了显著的产品指标收益，相比 CPU trie 实现实现 948 倍加速，相比硬件加速的二分查找 baseline 实现 47–1033 倍加速。此外，STATIC 的运行时开销在广泛的实用配置下都保持极低。据我们所知，STATIC 使严格受限的生成式检索首次实现生产规模部署。此外，在学术基准上的评估表明，STATIC 能显著改善生成式检索的冷启动性能。我们的代码见 https://github.com/youtube/static-constraint-decoding。
Unleashing the Potential of Sparse Attention on Long-term Behaviors for CTR Prediction
中科院 / 国科大2026通用学术基础设施 / 基础模型CTR prediction, sparse self-attention, long-term behaviors, scaling law, relative temporal encodingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsWeijiang Lai, Beihong Jin, Di Zhang, Siru Chen, Jiongyan Zhang, Yuhang Gou, Jian Dong, Xingxing Wang
Abstract (EN)
In recent years, the success of large language models (LLMs) has driven the exploration of scaling laws in recommender systems. However, models that demonstrate scaling laws are actually challenging to deploy in industrial settings for modeling long sequences of user behaviors, due to the high computational complexity of the standard self-attention mechanism. Despite various sparse self-attention mechanisms proposed in other fields, they are not fully suited for recommendation scenarios. This is because user behaviors exhibit personalization and temporal characteristics: different users have distinct behavior patterns, and these patterns change over time, with data from these users differing significantly from data in other fields in terms of distribution. To address these challenges, we propose SparseCTR, an efficient and effective model specifically designed for long-term behaviors of users. To be precise, we first segment behavior sequences into chunks in a personalized manner to avoid separating continuous behaviors and enable parallel processing of sequences. Based on these chunks, we propose a three-branch sparse self-attention mechanism to jointly identify users' global interests, interest transitions, and short-term interests. Furthermore, we design a composite relative temporal encoding via learnable, head-specific bias coefficients, better capturing sequential and periodic relationships among user behaviors. Extensive experimental results show that SparseCTR not only improves efficiency but also outperforms state-of-the-art methods. More importantly, it exhibits an obvious scaling law phenomenon, maintaining performance improvements across three orders of magnitude in FLOPs. In online A/B testing, SparseCTR increased CTR by 1.72\% and CPM by 1.41\%. Our source code is available at https://github.com/laiweijiang/SparseCTR.
摘要 (中文)
近年来，大语言模型（LLM）的成功推动了推荐系统中 scaling law 的探索。然而，由于标准自注意力机制的高计算复杂度，展现出 scaling law 的模型实际上很难在工业环境中部署以对长序列用户行为建模。尽管其他领域已提出各种稀疏自注意力机制，但它们并不完全适用于推荐场景。这是因为用户行为具有个性化和时序特性：不同用户有不同的行为模式，且这些模式随时间变化，这些用户的数据在分布上与其他领域的数据存在显著差异。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了 SparseCTR，一个专为用户长期行为设计的高效且有效的模型。具体而言，我们首先以个性化的方式将行为序列切分为 chunk，以避免割裂连续行为，并支持序列的并行处理。基于这些 chunk，我们提出了一种三分支稀疏自注意力机制，共同识别用户的全局兴趣、兴趣迁移和短期兴趣。此外，我们通过可学习的、每个 head 专属的偏置系数设计了复合相对时间编码，更好地捕捉用户行为之间的序列关系和周期性关系。大量实验结果表明，SparseCTR 不仅提升了效率，还优于 SOTA 方法。更重要的是，它展现出明显的 scaling law 现象，在 FLOPs 跨越三个数量级的范围内持续保持性能提升。在线上 A/B 测试中，SparseCTR 将 CTR 提升了 1.72%，CPM 提升了 1.41%。我们的源代码可在 https://github.com/laiweijiang/SparseCTR 获取。
CollectiveKV: Decoupling and Sharing Collaborative Information in Sequential Recommendation
华为 / 清华2026通用工业基础设施 / 基础模型KV cache, sequential recommendation, cross-user KV sharing, collaborative signals, inference latencyPDF · arXiv
AuthorsJingyu Li, Zhaocheng Du, Qianhui Zhu, kaiyuan Li, Zhicheng Zhang, Song-Li Wu, Chaolang Li, Pengwen Dai
Abstract (EN)
Sequential recommendation models are widely used in applications, yet they face stringent latency requirements. Mainstream models leverage the Transformer attention mechanism to improve performance, but its computational complexity grows with the sequence length, leading to a latency challenge for long sequences. Consequently, KV cache technology has recently been explored in sequential recommendation systems to reduce inference latency. However, KV cache introduces substantial storage overhead in sequential recommendation systems, which often have a large user base with potentially very long user history sequences. In this work, we observe that KV sequences across different users exhibit significant similarities, indicating the existence of collaborative signals in KV. Furthermore, we analyze the KV using singular value decomposition (SVD) and find that the information in KV can be divided into two parts: the majority of the information is shareable across users, while a small portion is user-specific. Motivated by this, we propose CollectiveKV, a cross-user KV sharing mechanism. It captures the information shared across users through a learnable global KV pool. During inference, each user retrieves high-dimensional shared KV from the pool and concatenates them with low-dimensional user-specific KV to obtain the final KV. Experiments on five sequential recommendation models and three datasets show that our method can compress the KV cache to only 0.8% of its original size, while maintaining or even enhancing model performance.
摘要 (中文)
序列推荐模型在应用中被广泛使用，但它们面临严格的延迟要求。主流模型利用 Transformer 注意力机制来提升性能，但其计算复杂度随序列长度增长，导致长序列面临延迟挑战。因此，近期序列推荐系统开始探索 KV 缓存技术以降低推理延迟。然而，KV 缓存在序列推荐系统中引入了巨大的存储开销，因为这类系统往往拥有庞大的用户群，且用户历史序列可能非常长。在本工作中，我们观察到不同用户的 KV 序列之间存在显著的相似性，表明 KV 中存在协同信号。进一步地，我们使用奇异值分解（SVD）分析 KV，发现其中的信息可分为两部分：大部分信息可在用户之间共享，小部分信息则是用户专属的。受此启发，我们提出了 CollectiveKV，一种跨用户的 KV 共享机制。它通过一个可学习的全局 KV 池捕捉用户间共享的信息。在推理时，每个用户从池中检索高维共享 KV，并将其与低维的用户专属 KV 拼接，得到最终的 KV。在五个序列推荐模型和三个数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法可以将 KV 缓存压缩至原始大小的仅 0.8%，同时保持甚至提升模型性能。
Hyena Operator for Fast Sequential Recommendation
武汉理工 / 武汉纺织大学2026通用学术基础设施 / 基础模型sequential recommendation, Hyena operator, polynomial-based kernel parameterization, gated convolutions, Legendre orthogonal polynomialsPDF · arXiv
AuthorsJiahao Liu, Lin Li, Zhiyuan Li, Kaixi Hu, Kaize Shi, Jingling Yuan
Abstract (EN)
Sequential recommendation models, particularly those based on attention, achieve strong accuracy but incur quadratic complexity, making long user histories prohibitively expensive. Sub-quadratic operators such as Hyena provide efficient alternatives in language modeling, but their potential in recommendation remains underexplored. We argue that Hyena faces challenges in recommendation due to limited representation capacity on sparse, long user sequences. To address these challenges, we propose HyenaRec, a novel sequential recommender that integrates polynomial-based kernel parameterization with gated convolutions. Specifically, we design convolutional kernels using Legendre orthogonal polynomials, which provides a smooth and compact basis for modeling long-term temporal dependencies. A complementary gating mechanism captures fine-grained short-term behavioral bursts, yielding a hybrid architecture that balances global temporal evolution with localized user interests under sparse feedback. This construction enhances expressiveness while scaling linearly with sequence length. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that HyenaRec consistently outperforms Attention-, Recurrent-, and other baselines in ranking accuracy. Moreover, it trains significantly faster (up to 6x speedup), with particularly pronounced advantages on long-sequence scenarios where efficiency is maintained without sacrificing accuracy. These results highlight polynomial-based kernel parameterization as a principled and scalable alternative to attention for sequential recommendation.
摘要 (中文)
序列推荐模型，尤其是基于注意力的模型，能够取得很高的准确率，但其复杂度为二次方，使得长用户历史成本高得难以承受。诸如 Hyena 这类亚二次方（sub-quadratic）算子为语言建模提供了高效的替代方案，但它们在推荐中的潜力仍未得到充分探索。我们认为，由于在稀疏、长用户序列上表示能力有限，Hyena 在推荐中面临挑战。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了 HyenaRec，一个新颖的序列推荐器，将基于多项式的核参数化与门控卷积相结合。具体而言，我们使用 Legendre 正交多项式设计卷积核，为建模长期时间依赖提供了一个平滑且紧致的基。一个互补的门控机制捕捉细粒度的短期行为爆发，从而形成一个混合架构，在稀疏反馈下平衡全局时间演化与局部用户兴趣。这种构造增强了表达力，同时随序列长度线性扩展。在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，HyenaRec 在排序准确率上持续优于基于注意力、基于循环以及其他 baseline。此外，它的训练速度显著更快（最高 6 倍加速），在长序列场景下优势尤为明显，在保持效率的同时不牺牲准确率。这些结果凸显出基于多项式的核参数化是序列推荐中一个有原则且可扩展的注意力替代方案。
Sparse Meets Dense: Unified Generative Recommendations with Cascaded Sparse-Dense Representations
百度2025推荐工业Semantic IDgenerative retrieval, cascaded sparse-dense representations, Semantic ID, dense retrieval, BeamFusionPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYuhao Yang, Zhi Ji, Zhaopeng Li, Yi Li, Zhonglin Mo, Yue Ding, Kai Chen, Zijian Zhang, Jie Li, Shuanglong Li, Lin Liu
Abstract (EN)
Generative models have recently gained attention in recommendation systems by directly predicting item identifiers from user interaction sequences. However, existing methods suffer from significant information loss due to the separation of stages such as quantization and sequence modeling, hindering their ability to achieve the modeling precision and accuracy of sequential dense retrieval techniques. Integrating generative and dense retrieval methods remains a critical challenge. To address this, we introduce the Cascaded Organized Bi-Represented generAtive retrieval (COBRA) framework, which innovatively integrates sparse semantic IDs and dense vectors through a cascading process. Our method alternates between generating these representations by first generating sparse IDs, which serve as conditions to aid in the generation of dense vectors. End-to-end training enables dynamic refinement of dense representations, capturing both semantic insights and collaborative signals from user-item interactions. During inference, COBRA employs a coarse-to-fine strategy, starting with sparse ID generation and refining them into dense vectors via the generative model. We further propose BeamFusion, an innovative approach combining Permission to make digital or hard copies of all or part of this work for personal or classroom use is granted without fee provided that copies are not made or distributed for profit or commercial advantage and that copies bear this notice and the full citation on the first page. Extensive experiments on public datasets and offline tests validate our method’s robustness. Online A/B tests on a real-world advertising platform with over 200 million daily users demonstrate substantial improvements in key metrics, highlighting COBRA’s practical advantages.
摘要 (中文)
生成模型近来在推荐系统中受到关注，它直接从用户交互序列预测物品标识符。然而，现有方法由于量化与序列建模等阶段的分离而遭受严重的信息损失，阻碍了其达到序列稠密 retrieval 技术的建模精度与准确性。如何整合生成式与稠密 retrieval 方法仍是一个关键挑战。为此，我们提出级联组织双表示生成检索（Cascaded Organized Bi-Represented generAtive retrieval，COBRA）框架，它通过级联过程创新性地整合稀疏 semantic ID 与稠密向量。我们的方法交替生成这两种表示：首先生成稀疏 ID，并将其作为条件辅助稠密向量的生成。端到端训练使稠密表示能够动态细化，从而同时捕捉来自用户-物品交互的语义洞见与协同信号。在推理时，COBRA 采用由粗到细的策略，首先生成稀疏 ID，再通过生成模型将其细化为稠密向量。我们进一步提出 BeamFusion，一种将 beam search 与最近邻得分相结合的新方法，以增强推理的灵活性和推荐的多样性。在公开数据集和离线测试上的大量实验验证了我们方法的鲁棒性。在日活超 2 亿的真实广告平台上的在线 A/B 测试表明关键指标取得大幅提升，凸显了 COBRA 的实际优势。
Generative Recommender with End-to-End Learnable Item Tokenization
人大2025推荐学术Semantic IDgenerative recommendation, item tokenization, end-to-end learning, sequence-item alignment, preference-semantic alignmentPDF · arXiv
AuthorsEnze Liu, Bowen Zheng, Cheng Ling, Lantao Hu, Han Li, Wayne Xin Zhao
Abstract (EN)
Generative recommendation systems have gained increasing attention as an innovative approach that directly generates item identifiers for recommendation tasks. Despite their potential, a major challenge is the effective construction of item identifiers that align well with recommender systems. Current approaches often treat item tokenization and generative recommendation training as separate processes, which can lead to suboptimal performance. To overcome this issue, we introduce ETEGRec, a novel End-To-End Generative Recommender that unifies item tokenization and generative recommendation into a cohesive framework. Built on a dual encoder-decoder architecture, ETEGRec consists of an item tokenizer and a generative recommender. To enable synergistic interaction between these components, we propose a recommendation-oriented alignment strategy, which includes two key optimization objectives: sequence-item alignment and preference-semantic alignment. These objectives tightly couple the learning processes of the item tokenizer and the generative recommender, fostering mutual enhancement. Additionally, we develop an alternating optimization technique to ensure stable and efficient end-to-end training of the entire framework. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of our approach compared to traditional sequential recommendation models and existing generative recommendation baselines. Our code is available at this https URL.
