生成式推荐系统作为一种直接生成物品标识符（item identifiers）来完成推荐任务的创新方法，正受到越来越多的关注。尽管潜力巨大，但一个主要挑战在于如何有效构建与推荐系统良好对齐的物品标识符。现有方法通常将物品 tokenization 与生成式推荐训练视为相互独立的过程，这可能导致次优性能。为解决这一问题，我们提出 ETEGRec，一种新颖的端到端生成式推荐模型（End-To-End Generative Recommender），将物品 tokenization 与生成式推荐统一到一个连贯的框架中。ETEGRec 基于双编码器-解码器（dual encoder-decoder）架构，由物品 tokenizer 和生成式推荐器两部分组成。为实现这两个组件之间的协同交互，我们提出了一种面向推荐的 alignment 策略，包含两个关键优化目标：sequence-item alignment 与 preference-semantic alignment。这两个目标将物品 tokenizer 与生成式推荐器的学习过程紧密耦合，促进相互增强。此外，我们开发了一种交替优化（alternating optimization）技术，以确保整个框架稳定而高效的端到端训练。大量实验表明，我们的方法相比传统序列推荐模型和现有生成式推荐 baseline 具有更优的性能。我们的代码见 this https URL。
Semantic Convergence: Harmonizing Recommender Systems via Two-Stage Alignment and Behavioral Semantic Tokenization
美团2025推荐工业Semantic IDtwo-stage alignment, behavioral semantic tokenization, Alignment Tokenization, LLM, collaborative semanticsPDF · arXiv
AuthorsGuanghan Li, Xun Zhang, Yufei Zhang, Yifan Yin, Guojun Yin, Wei Lin
Abstract (EN)
Large language models (LLMs), endowed with exceptional reasoning capabilities, are adept at discerning profound user interests from historical behaviors, thereby presenting a promising avenue for the advancement of recommendation systems. However, a notable discrepancy persists between the sparse collaborative semantics typically found in recommendation systems and the dense token representations within LLMs. In our study, we propose a novel framework that harmoniously merges traditional recommendation models with the prowess of LLMs. We initiate this integration by transforming ItemIDs into sequences that align semantically with the LLMs’ space, through the proposed Alignment Tokenization module. Additionally, we design a series of specialized supervised learning tasks aimed at aligning collaborative signals with the subtleties of natural language semantics. To ensure practical applicability, we optimize online inference by pre-caching the top-K results for each user, reducing latency and improving efficiency. Extensive experimental evidence indicates that our model markedly improves recall metrics and displays remarkable scalability of recommendation systems.
摘要 (中文)
大语言模型（LLM）具备卓越的推理能力，擅长从历史行为中辨识深层的用户兴趣，因而为推荐系统的发展提供了有前景的方向。然而，推荐系统中常见的稀疏协同语义与 LLM 中稠密的 token 表示之间仍存在显著差异。在本研究中，我们提出一个新颖的框架，将传统推荐模型与 LLM 的强大能力和谐融合。我们通过所提出的 Alignment Tokenization 模块，将 ItemID 转换为与 LLM 空间语义对齐的序列，从而开启这一融合。此外，我们设计了一系列专门的监督学习任务，旨在将协同信号与自然语言语义的细微之处对齐。为确保实际可用性，我们通过预先缓存每个用户的 top-K 结果来优化在线推理，降低延迟并提升效率。大量实验证据表明，我们的模型显著提升了 recall 指标，并展现出推荐系统出色的可扩展性。
FORGE: Forming Semantic Identifiers for Generative Retrieval in Industrial Datasets
浙大 / 阿里2025推荐工业Semantic IDsemantic identifiers, generative retrieval, SID construction, SID evaluation metrics, benchmarkPDF · arXiv
AuthorsKairui Fu, Tao Zhang, Shuwen Xiao, Ziyang Wang, Xinming Zhang, Chenchi Zhang, Yuliang Yan, Junjun Zheng, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Semantic identifiers (SIDs) have gained increasing attention in generative retrieval (GR) for recommendation due to their meaningful semantic discriminability. However, current studies in this field primarily (1) offer limited investigation into the construction strategies for better SIDs, and (2) their SID assessment typically relies on costly GR training. To address these challenges, we propose FORGE, a comprehensive benchmark for FOrming semantic identifieRs for Generative rEtrieval. Specifically, FORGE provides a taxonomy of the SID construction process from several perspectives and validates their impact on downstream GR through offline experiments across diverse settings. Notably, these empirical findings have led to a 0.35% increase in transaction count via online A/B experiments in the Guess You Like section of Taobao. The corresponding SID construction strategies have since been deployed at full scale on Taobao, demonstrating their practical effectiveness. To avoid expensive SID assessment that requires full GR training, we propose two novel SID evaluation metrics that are highly correlated with recommendation performance, enabling convenient evaluations without any GR training. Furthermore, to facilitate the community, we release AL-GR, the industrial dataset used in our experiments, comprising 14 billion interactions and 250 million items with the corresponding multimodal features collected from Taobao. All the code and data are available at https://github.com/selous123/al_sid.
摘要 (中文)
Semantic identifiers（SIDs）因其有意义的语义区分性，在面向推荐的生成式检索（GR）中受到越来越多的关注。然而，该领域当前的研究主要 (1) 对更优 SID 的构建策略探索有限，(2) 其 SID 评估通常依赖昂贵的 GR 训练。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 FORGE，一个用于为生成式检索构建 semantic identifiers 的综合基准（FOrming semantic identifieRs for Generative rEtrieval）。具体而言，FORGE 从多个视角提供了 SID 构建过程的分类体系，并通过跨多种设置的离线实验验证其对下游 GR 的影响。值得注意的是，这些实证发现促成了淘宝“猜你喜欢”板块在线 A/B 实验中成交次数 0.35% 的提升。相应的 SID 构建策略此后已在淘宝全量部署，证明了其实际有效性。为避免需要完整 GR 训练的昂贵 SID 评估，我们提出两个与推荐性能高度相关的新型 SID 评估指标，使得无需任何 GR 训练即可便捷评估。此外，为便利社区，我们发布 AL-GR，即我们实验中使用的工业数据集，包含 140 亿次交互与 2.5 亿个物品及其从淘宝采集的多模态特征。所有代码与数据见 https://github.com/selous123/al_sid。
Multi-Aspect Cross-modal Quantization for Generative Recommendation
北航 / 美团2025推荐工业Semantic IDgenerative recommendation, cross-modal quantization, semantic IDs, multimodal alignment, codebookPDF · arXiv
AuthorsFuwei Zhang, Xiaoyu Liu, Dongbo Xi, Jishen Yin, Huan Chen, Peng Yan, Fuzhen Zhuang, Zhao Zhang
Abstract (EN)
Generative Recommendation (GR) has emerged as a new paradigm in recommender systems. This approach relies on quantized representations to discretize item features, modeling users' historical interactions as sequences of discrete tokens. Based on these tokenized sequences, GR predicts the next item by employing next-token prediction methods. The challenges of GR lie in constructing high-quality semantic identifiers (IDs) that are hierarchically organized, minimally conflicting, and conducive to effective generative model training. However, current approaches remain limited in their ability to harness multimodal information and to capture the deep and intricate interactions among diverse modalities, both of which are essential for learning high-quality semantic IDs and for effectively training GR models. To address this, we propose Multi-Aspect Cross-modal quantization for generative Recommendation (MACRec), which introduces multimodal information and incorporates it into both semantic ID learning and generative model training from different aspects. Specifically, we first introduce cross-modal quantization during the ID learning process, which effectively reduces conflict rates and thus improves codebook usability through the complementary integration of multimodal information. In addition, to further enhance the generative ability of our GR model, we incorporate multi-aspect cross-modal alignments, including the implicit and explicit alignments. Finally, we conduct extensive experiments on three well-known recommendation datasets to demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method.
摘要 (中文)
Generative Recommendation（GR）已成为推荐系统中的新范式。该方法依赖量化表示来离散化物品特征，将用户的历史交互建模为离散 token 序列。基于这些 token 化序列，GR 采用下一 token 预测方法预测下一个物品。GR 的挑战在于构建高质量 semantic identifiers（IDs），其应具备层级组织、最小冲突且有利于有效生成式模型训练。然而，当前方法在利用多模态信息以及捕获不同模态之间深层复杂交互方面仍能力有限，而这两者对于学习高质量 semantic IDs 与有效训练 GR 模型都必不可少。为此，我们提出 Multi-Aspect Cross-modal quantization for generative Recommendation（MACRec），它引入多模态信息，并从不同方面将其纳入 semantic ID 学习与生成式模型训练。具体而言，我们首先在 ID 学习过程中引入跨模态量化，通过多模态信息的互补整合有效降低冲突率，从而提升码本可用性。此外，为进一步增强 GR 模型的生成能力，我们引入多视角跨模态对齐，包括隐式与显式对齐。最后，我们在三个知名推荐数据集上进行大量实验，证明所提方法的有效性。
Tokenize Once, Recommend Anywhere: Unified Item Tokenization for Multi-domain LLM-based Recommendation
Yonsei University2025推荐学术Semantic IDitem tokenization, multi-domain recommendation, mixture-of-experts, codebook, LLM-based recommendationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYu Hou, Won-Yong Shin
Abstract (EN)
Large language model (LLM)-based recommender systems have achieved high-quality performance by bridging the discrepancy between the item space and the language space through item tokenization. However, existing item tokenization methods typically require training separate models for each item domain, limiting generalization. Moreover, the diverse distributions and semantics across item domains make it difficult to construct a unified tokenization that preserves domain-specific information. To address these challenges, we propose UniTok, a Unified item Tokenization framework that integrates our own mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture with a series of codebooks to convert items into discrete tokens, enabling scalable tokenization while preserving semantic information across multiple item domains. Specifically, items from different domains are first projected into a unified latent space through a shared encoder. They are then routed to domain-specific experts to capture the unique semantics, while a shared expert, which is always active, encodes common knowledge transferable across domains. Additionally, to mitigate semantic imbalance across domains, we present a mutual information calibration mechanism, which guides the model towards retaining similar levels of semantic information for each domain. Comprehensive experiments on wide-ranging real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed UniTok framework is (a) highly effective: achieving up to 51.89% improvements over strong benchmarks, (b) theoretically sound: showing the analytical validity of our architectural design and optimization; and (c) highly generalizable: demonstrating robust performance across diverse domains without requiring per-domain retraining, a capability not supported by existing baselines.
摘要 (中文)
基于大语言模型（LLM）的推荐系统通过物品 tokenization 弥合物品空间与语言空间之间的差异，实现了高质量的推荐性能。然而，现有物品 tokenization 方法通常需要为每个物品域训练单独的模型，限制了泛化能力。此外，不同物品域之间分布与语义的多样性，使得构建既能保留域特定信息的统一 tokenization 十分困难。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 UniTok，一个统一物品 tokenization 框架，它将我们自研的 mixture-of-experts（MoE）架构与一系列码本相结合，将物品转换为离散 token，在跨多个物品域保留语义信息的同时实现可扩展的 tokenization。具体而言，来自不同域的物品首先通过共享 encoder 投影到统一潜在空间。随后它们被路由到域特定的专家以捕获独特语义，而一个始终激活的共享专家则编码可跨域迁移的通用知识。此外，为缓解跨域语义不平衡，我们提出一种互信息校准机制，引导模型为每个域保留相近水平的语义信息。在广泛的真实数据集上的大量实验表明，所提 UniTok 框架 (a) 高度有效：相比强基准最高提升 51.89%；(b) 理论合理：展示了我们架构设计与优化在分析上的有效性；(c) 高度可泛化：在多样域上展现出稳健性能且无需按域重新训练，这是现有 baseline 不支持的能力。
Bi-Level Optimization for Generative Recommendation: Bridging Tokenization and Generation
USTC / 北航2025推荐学术Semantic IDgenerative recommendation, bi-level optimization, item tokenization, meta-learning, gradient surgeryPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYimeng Bai, Chang Liu, Yang Zhang, Dingxian Wang, Frank Yang, Andrew Rabinovich, Wenge Rong, Fuli Feng
Abstract (EN)
Generative recommendation is emerging as a transformative paradigm by directly generating recommended items, rather than relying on matching. Building such a system typically involves two key components: (1) optimizing the tokenizer to derive suitable item identifiers, and (2) training the recommender based on those identifiers. Existing approaches often treat these components separately--either sequentially or in alternation--overlooking their interdependence. This separation can lead to misalignment: the tokenizer is trained without direct guidance from the recommendation objective, potentially yielding suboptimal identifiers that degrade recommendation performance. To address this, we propose BLOGER, a Bi-Level Optimization for GEnerative Recommendation framework, which explicitly models the interdependence between the tokenizer and the recommender in a unified optimization process. The lower level trains the recommender using tokenized sequences, while the upper level optimizes the tokenizer based on both the tokenization loss and recommendation loss. We adopt a meta-learning approach to solve this bi-level optimization efficiently, and introduce gradient surgery to mitigate gradient conflicts in the upper-level updates, thereby ensuring that item identifiers are both informative and recommendation-aligned. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that BLOGER consistently outperforms state-of-the-art generative recommendation methods while maintaining practical efficiency with no significant additional computational overhead, effectively bridging the gap between item tokenization and autoregressive generation. We release our code at https://github.com/Ten-Mao/BLOGER.
摘要 (中文)
生成式推荐正作为一种变革性范式兴起，它直接生成推荐物品而非依赖匹配。构建这样一个系统通常涉及两个关键组件：(1) 优化 tokenizer 以导出合适的物品标识符，(2) 基于这些标识符训练推荐器。现有方法往往将这两个组件分开处理——要么串行、要么交替——忽视了它们之间的相互依赖。这种分离可能导致错位：tokenizer 在训练时未得到推荐目标的直接指导，可能产生次优标识符，从而降低推荐性能。为此，我们提出 BLOGER，一个生成式推荐的双层优化（Bi-Level Optimization for GEnerative Recommendation）框架，它在统一的优化过程中显式建模 tokenizer 与推荐器之间的相互依赖。下层使用 token 化序列训练推荐器，上层则基于 tokenization 损失与推荐损失优化 tokenizer。我们采用元学习方法高效求解该双层优化，并引入梯度手术以缓解上层更新中的梯度冲突，从而确保物品标识符既信息丰富又与推荐对齐。在多个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，BLOGER 持续优于最先进的生成式推荐方法，同时以实用的效率保持无显著额外计算开销，有效弥合了物品 tokenization 与自回归生成之间的差距。我们开源代码于 https://github.com/Ten-Mao/BLOGER。
From IDs to Semantics: A Generative Framework for Cross-Domain Recommendation with Adaptive Semantic Tokenization
西交利物浦大学 / University of Liverpool2025推荐学术Semantic IDcross-domain recommendation, generative recommendation, semantic IDs, domain-adaptive tokenization, prefix-tree decodingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsPeiyu Hu, Wayne Lu, Jia Wang
Abstract (EN)
Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) is crucial for improving recommendation accuracy and generalization, yet traditional methods are often hindered by the reliance on shared user/item IDs, which are unavailable in most real-world scenarios. Consequently, many efforts have focused on learning disentangled representations through multi-domain joint training to bridge the domain gaps. Recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based approaches show promise, they still face critical challenges, including: (1) the \textbf{item ID tokenization dilemma}, which leads to vocabulary explosion and fails to capture high-order collaborative knowledge; and (2) \textbf{insufficient domain-specific modeling} for the complex evolution of user interests and item semantics. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{GenCDR}, a novel \textbf{Gen}erative \textbf{C}ross-\textbf{D}omain \textbf{R}ecommendation framework. GenCDR first employs a \textbf{Domain-adaptive Tokenization} module, which generates disentangled semantic IDs for items by dynamically routing between a universal encoder and domain-specific adapters. Symmetrically, a \textbf{Cross-domain Autoregressive Recommendation} module models user preferences by fusing universal and domain-specific interests. Finally, a \textbf{Domain-aware Prefix-tree} enables efficient and accurate generation. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that GenCDR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available in the supplementary materials.
摘要 (中文)
跨域推荐（CDR）对于提升推荐准确性与泛化性至关重要，但传统方法常常受制于对共享用户/物品 ID 的依赖，而这在多数现实场景中并不可得。因此，许多工作聚焦于通过多域联合训练学习解耦表示以弥合域间差距。近期基于大语言模型（LLM）的方法展现出前景，但仍面临关键挑战，包括：(1) 物品 ID tokenization 困境，导致词表爆炸且无法捕获高阶协同知识；(2) 对用户兴趣与物品语义复杂演化的域特定建模不足。为应对这些局限，我们提出 GenCDR，一个新颖的生成式跨域推荐（Generative Cross-Domain Recommendation）框架。GenCDR 首先采用一个域自适应 Tokenization 模块，通过在通用 encoder 与域特定 adapter 之间动态路由，为物品生成解耦的 semantic IDs。对称地，一个跨域自回归推荐模块通过融合通用与域特定兴趣来建模用户偏好。最后，一个域感知前缀树实现高效且准确的生成。在多个真实数据集上的大量实验表明 GenCDR 显著优于最先进的 baseline。我们的代码见补充材料。
Generative Next POI Recommendation with Semantic ID
电子科大2025推荐学术Semantic IDnext POI recommendation, semantic IDs, generative recommendation, residual quantized VAE, LLM-based recommendationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsDongsheng Wang, Yuxi Huang, Shen Gao, Yifan Wang, Chengrui Huang, Shuo Shang
Abstract (EN)
Point-of-interest (POI) recommendation systems aim to predict the next destinations of user based on their preferences and historical check-ins. Existing generative POI recommendation methods usually employ random numeric IDs for POIs, limiting the ability to model semantic relationships between similar locations. In this paper, we propose Generative Next POI Recommendation with Semantic ID (GNPR-SID), an LLM-based POI recommendation model with a novel semantic POI ID (SID) representation method that enhances the semantic understanding of POI modeling. There are two key components in our GNPR-SID: (1) a Semantic ID Construction module that generates semantically rich POI IDs based on semantic and collaborative features, and (2) a Generative POI Recommendation module that fine-tunes LLMs to predict the next POI using these semantic IDs. By incorporating user interaction patterns and POI semantic features into the semantic ID generation, our method improves the recommendation accuracy and generalization of the model. To construct semantically related SIDs, we propose a POI quantization method based on residual quantized variational autoencoder, which maps POIs into a discrete semantic space. We also propose a diversity loss to ensure that SIDs are uniformly distributed across the semantic space. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that GNPR-SID substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving up to 16% improvement in recommendation accuracy.
摘要 (中文)
兴趣点（POI）推荐系统旨在基于用户偏好和历史签到预测用户的下一个目的地。现有生成式 POI 推荐方法通常为 POIs 采用随机数字 ID，限制了建模相似地点之间语义关系的能力。本文提出 Generative Next POI Recommendation with Semantic ID（GNPR-SID），一种基于 LLM 的 POI 推荐模型，采用新颖的语义 POI ID（SID）表示方法，增强 POI 建模的语义理解。我们的 GNPR-SID 有两个关键组件：(1) Semantic ID Construction 模块，基于语义与协同特征生成语义丰富的 POI ID；(2) Generative POI Recommendation 模块，微调 LLMs 以使用这些语义 ID 预测下一个 POI。通过将用户交互模式和 POI 语义特征纳入语义 ID 生成，我们的方法提升了模型的推荐准确性和泛化能力。为构建语义相关的 SIDs，我们提出一种基于 residual quantized variational autoencoder 的 POI 量化方法，将 POIs 映射到离散语义空间。我们还提出了一种多样性损失，以确保 SIDs 在语义空间中均匀分布。在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，GNPR-SID 大幅超越 SOTA 方法，推荐准确性最高提升 16%。
Bridging Textual-Collaborative Gap through Semantic Codes for Sequential Recommendation
人大2025推荐学术Semantic IDsequential recommendation, semantic codes, vector quantization, textual-collaborative fusion, code maskingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsEnze Liu, Bowen Zheng, Wayne Xin Zhao, Ji-Rong Wen
Abstract (EN)
In recent years, substantial research efforts have been devoted to enhancing sequential recommender systems by integrating abundant side information with ID-based collaborative information. This study specifically focuses on leveraging the textual metadata (e.g., titles and brands) associated with items. While existing methods have achieved notable success by combining text and ID representations, they often struggle to strike a balance between textual information embedded in text representations and collaborative information from sequential patterns of user behavior. In light of this, we propose CCFRec, a novel Code-based textual and Collaborative semantic Fusion method for sequential Recommendation. The key idea behind our approach is to bridge the gap between textual and collaborative information using semantic codes. Specifically, we generate fine-grained semantic codes from multi-view text embeddings through vector quantization techniques. Subsequently, we develop a code-guided semantic-fusion module based on the cross-attention mechanism to flexibly extract and integrate relevant information from text representations. In order to further enhance the fusion of textual and collaborative semantics, we introduce an optimization strategy that employs code masking with two specific objectives: masked code modeling and masked sequence alignment. The merit of these objectives lies in leveraging mask prediction tasks and augmented item representations to capture code correlations within individual items and enhance the sequence modeling of the recommendation backbone. Extensive experiments conducted on four public datasets demonstrate the superiority of CCFRec, showing significant improvements over various sequential recommendation models. Our code is available at https://github.com/RUCAIBox/CCFRec.
摘要 (中文)
近年来，大量研究致力于通过将丰富的侧信息与基于 ID 的协同信息相结合来增强序列推荐系统。本研究特别关注利用与 item 相关的文本元数据（例如标题和品牌）。尽管现有方法通过结合文本与 ID 表示取得了显著成功，但它们往往难以在文本表示所蕴含的文本信息与来自用户行为序列模式的协同信息之间取得平衡。有鉴于此，我们提出 CCFRec，一种新颖的基于 Code 的文本与协同语义融合方法（Code-based textual and Collaborative semantic Fusion method）用于序列推荐。我们方法的核心思想是利用语义 code 弥合文本信息与协同信息之间的差距。具体而言，我们通过向量量化技术从多视角文本 embedding 中生成细粒度语义 code。随后，我们开发了一个基于 cross-attention 机制的 code 引导语义融合模块，以灵活地从文本表示中提取并整合相关信息。为了进一步增强文本与协同语义的融合，我们引入了一种采用 code masking 的优化策略，包含两个具体目标：masked code modeling 和 masked sequence alignment。这些目标的价值在于利用掩码预测任务和增强的 item 表示来捕捉单个 item 内的 code 关联性，并增强推荐主干的序列建模。在四个公开数据集上的大量实验证明了 CCFRec 的优越性，相较各类序列推荐模型均有显著提升。我们的代码见 https://github.com/RUCAIBox/CCFRec。
ActionPiece: Contextually Tokenizing Action Sequences for Generative Recommendation
UCSD / Google2025推荐工业Semantic IDgenerative recommendation, contextual tokenization, action sequences, item features, set permutation regularizationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYupeng Hou, Jianmo Ni, Zhankui He, Noveen Sachdeva, Wang-Cheng Kang, Ed H. Chi, Julian McAuley, Derek Zhiyuan Cheng
Abstract (EN)
Generative recommendation (GR) is an emerging paradigm where user actions are tokenized into discrete token patterns and autoregressively generated as predictions. However, existing GR models tokenize each action independently, assigning the same fixed tokens to identical actions across all sequences without considering contextual relationships. This lack of context-awareness can lead to suboptimal performance, as the same action may hold different meanings depending on its surrounding context. To address this issue, we propose ActionPiece to explicitly incorporate context when tokenizing action sequences. In ActionPiece, each action is represented as a set of item features. Given the action sequence corpora, we construct the vocabulary by merging feature patterns as new tokens, based on their co-occurrence frequency both within individual sets and across adjacent sets. Considering the unordered nature of feature sets, we further introduce set permutation regularization, which produces multiple segmentations of action sequences with the same semantics. Our code is available at: https://github.com/google-deepmind/action_piece.
摘要 (中文)
生成式推荐（GR）是一种新兴范式，其中用户动作被 tokenize 为离散 token 模式，并自回归生成作为预测。然而，现有 GR 模型对每个动作独立进行 tokenize，在所有序列中为相同动作分配相同的固定 token，而不考虑上下文关系。这种缺乏上下文感知的做法可能导致次优性能，因为同一动作可能因其周围上下文不同而具有不同含义。为解决此问题，我们提出 ActionPiece，在对动作序列进行 tokenize 时显式纳入上下文。在 ActionPiece 中，每个动作表示为一组 item 特征。给定动作序列语料，我们基于特征模式在单个集合内部以及相邻集合之间的共现频率，通过合并特征模式为新 token 来构建词表。考虑到特征集合的无序性，我们进一步引入集合置换正则化（set permutation regularization），它会产生同一动作序列的多个语义相同的切分。我们的代码见：https://github.com/google-deepmind/action_piece。
Diffusion Generative Recommendation with Continuous Tokens
香港城市大学 / 国防科大2025推荐学术Semantic IDgenerative recommendation, continuous tokens, diffusion model, sigma-VAE tokenizer, item tokenizationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsHaohao Qu, Shanru Lin, Yujuan Ding, Yiqi Wang, Wenqi Fan
Abstract (EN)
Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence, particularly large language models (LLMs), have opened new opportunities for enhancing recommender systems (RecSys). Most existing LLM-based RecSys approaches operate in a discrete space, using vector-quantized tokenizers to align with the inherent discrete nature of language models. However, these quantization methods often result in lossy tokenization and suboptimal learning, primarily due to inaccurate gradient propagation caused by the non-differentiable argmin operation in standard vector quantization. Inspired by the emerging trend of embracing continuous tokens in language models, we propose ContRec, a novel framework that seamlessly integrates continuous tokens into LLM-based RecSys. Specifically, ContRec consists of two key modules: a sigma-VAE Tokenizer, which encodes users/items with continuous tokens; and a Dispersive Diffusion module, which captures implicit user preference. The tokenizer is trained with a continuous Variational Auto-Encoder (VAE) objective, where three effective techniques are adopted to avoid representation collapse. By conditioning on the previously generated tokens of the LLM backbone during user modeling, the Dispersive Diffusion module performs a conditional diffusion process with a novel Dispersive Loss, enabling high-quality user preference generation through next-token diffusion. Finally, ContRec leverages both the textual reasoning output from the LLM and the latent representations produced by the diffusion model for Top-K item retrieval, thereby delivering comprehensive recommendation results. Extensive experiments on four datasets demonstrate that ContRec consistently outperforms both traditional and SOTA LLM-based recommender systems. Our results highlight the potential of continuous tokenization and generative modeling for advancing the next generation of recommender systems.
摘要 (中文)
生成式人工智能，特别是大语言模型（LLMs）的最新进展，为增强推荐系统（RecSys）开辟了新机遇。大多数现有的基于 LLM 的 RecSys 方法在离散空间中运作，使用向量量化 tokenizer 来对齐语言模型固有的离散特性。然而，这些量化方法往往导致有损的 tokenization 和次优的学习，主要原因是标准向量量化中不可微的 argmin 操作导致梯度传播不准确。受语言模型中拥抱连续 token 这一新兴趋势的启发，我们提出 ContRec，一个将连续 token 无缝集成到基于 LLM 的 RecSys 中的新颖框架。具体而言，ContRec 包含两个关键模块：sigma-VAE Tokenizer，用连续 token 编码用户/item；以及 Dispersive Diffusion 模块，捕捉隐式用户偏好。该 tokenizer 以连续 Variational Auto-Encoder（VAE）目标训练，并采用三种有效技术来避免表示坍缩。通过在用户建模期间以 LLM 骨干此前生成的 token 为条件，Dispersive Diffusion 模块执行带有新颖 Dispersive Loss 的条件扩散过程，通过 next-token diffusion 实现高质量的用户偏好生成。最后，ContRec 同时利用来自 LLM 的文本推理输出和扩散模型产生的隐表示进行 Top-K item retrieval，从而给出全面的推荐结果。在四个数据集上的大量实验表明，ContRec 持续优于传统以及 SOTA 的基于 LLM 的推荐系统。我们的结果凸显了连续 tokenization 和生成式建模在推进下一代推荐系统方面的潜力。
UNGER: Generative Recommendation with A Unified Code via Semantic and Collaborative Integration
华为 / 华科2025推荐工业Semantic IDgenerative recommendation, unified code, semantic and collaborative integration, quantization, knowledge distillationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsLongtao Xiao, Haozhao Wang, Cheng Wang, Linfei Ji, Yifan Wang, Jieming Zhu, Zhenhua Dong, Rui Zhang, et al.
Abstract (EN)
With the rise of generative paradigms, generative recommendation has garnered increasing attention. The core component is the item code, generally derived by quantizing collaborative or semantic representations to serve as candidate items identifiers in the context. However, existing methods typically construct separate codes for each modality, leading to higher computational and storage costs and hindering the integration of their complementary strengths. Considering this limitation, we seek to integrate two different modalities into a unified code, fully unleashing the potential of complementary nature among modalities. Nevertheless, the integration remains challenging: the integrated embedding obtained by the common concatenation method would lead to underutilization of collaborative knowledge, thereby resulting in limited effectiveness. To address this, we propose a novel method, named UNGER, which integrates semantic and collaborative knowledge into a unified code for generative recommendation. Specifically, we propose to adaptively learn an integrated embedding through the joint optimization of cross-modality knowledge alignment and next item prediction tasks. Subsequently, to mitigate the information loss caused by the quantization process, we introduce an intra-modality knowledge distillation task, using the integrated embeddings as supervised signals to compensate. Extensive experiments on three widely used benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our approach compared to existing methods.
摘要 (中文)
随着生成式范式的兴起，生成式推荐受到越来越多的关注。其核心组件是 item code，通常通过对协同或语义表示进行量化得到，用作上下文中候选 item 的标识符。然而，现有方法通常为每种模态分别构建 code，导致更高的计算与存储成本，并阻碍了它们互补优势的融合。考虑到这一局限，我们试图将两种不同模态整合为一个统一 code，充分释放模态间互补性的潜力。然而，这种整合仍具挑战：通过常见拼接方式得到的整合 embedding 会导致协同知识利用不足，从而效果有限。为解决这一问题，我们提出一种新方法 UNGER，将语义与协同知识整合为统一 code 用于生成式推荐。具体而言，我们提出通过跨模态知识对齐与下一 item 预测任务的联合优化来自适应地学习一个整合 embedding。随后，为缓解量化过程造成的信息损失，我们引入模态内知识蒸馏任务，以整合 embedding 作为监督信号进行补偿。在三个广泛使用的基准上的大量实验表明，我们的方法优于现有方法。
Towards Distribution Matching between Collaborative and Language Spaces for Generative Recommendation
安徽大学 / The University of Queensland2025推荐学术Semantic IDgenerative recommendation, distribution matching, pre-trained language models, probabilistic meta-network, cross-space alignmentPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYi Zhang, Yiwen Zhang, Yu Wang, Tong Chen, Hongzhi Yin
Abstract (EN)
Generative recommendation aims to learn the underlying generative process over the entire item set to produce recommendations for users. Although it leverages non-linear probabilistic models to surpass the limited modeling capacity of linear factor models, it is often constrained by a trade-off between representation ability and tractability. With the rise of a new generation of generative methods based on pre-trained language models (LMs), incorporating LMs into general recommendation with implicit feedback has gained considerable attention. However, adapting them to generative recommendation remains challenging. The core reason lies in the mismatch between the input-output formats and semantics of generative models and LMs, making it challenging to achieve optimal alignment in the feature space. This work addresses this issue by proposing a model-agnostic generative recommendation framework called DMRec, which introduces a probabilistic meta-network to bridge the outputs of LMs with user interactions, thereby enabling an equivalent probabilistic modeling process. Subsequently, we design three cross-space distribution matching processes aimed at maximizing shared information while preserving the unique semantics of each space and filtering out irrelevant information. We apply DMRec to three different types of generative recommendation methods and conduct extensive experiments on three public datasets. The experimental results demonstrate that DMRec can effectively enhance the recommendation performance of these generative models, and it shows significant advantages over mainstream LM-enhanced recommendation methods.
摘要 (中文)
生成式推荐旨在学习整个 item 集合上的潜在生成过程，从而为用户生成推荐。尽管它利用非线性概率模型超越了线性因子模型有限的建模能力，但往往受限于表示能力与可处理性之间的权衡。随着基于预训练语言模型（LM）的新一代生成方法的兴起，将 LM 融入具有隐式反馈的通用推荐已获得相当多的关注。然而，将其适配到生成式推荐仍具挑战。核心原因在于生成模型与 LM 在输入输出格式和语义上的不匹配，使得在特征空间中实现最优对齐变得困难。本工作通过提出一种模型无关的生成式推荐框架 DMRec 来解决该问题，该框架引入概率元网络将 LM 的输出与用户交互桥接起来，从而实现等价的概率建模过程。随后，我们设计了三个跨空间分布匹配过程，旨在最大化共享信息，同时保留每个空间独有的语义并过滤掉无关信息。我们将 DMRec 应用于三种不同类型的生成式推荐方法，并在三个公开数据集上进行了大量实验。实验结果表明，DMRec 能有效提升这些生成模型的推荐性能，且相较于主流的 LM 增强推荐方法具有显著优势。
GRAM: Generative Recommendation via Semantic-aware Multi-granular Late Fusion
Samsung2025推荐学术Semantic IDgenerative recommendation, multi-granular late fusion, semantic-to-lexical translation, LLM, item relationshipsPDF · arXiv
AuthorsSunkyung Lee, Minjin Choi, Eunseong Choi, Hye-young Kim, Jongwuk Lee
Abstract (EN)
Generative recommendation is an emerging paradigm that leverages the extensive knowledge of large language models by formulating recommendations into a text-to-text generation task. However, existing studies face two key limitations in (i) incorporating implicit item relationships and (ii) utilizing rich yet lengthy item information. To address these challenges, we propose a Generative Recommender via semantic-Aware Multi-granular late fusion (GRAM), introducing two synergistic innovations. First, we design semantic-to-lexical translation to encode implicit hierarchical and collaborative item relationships into the vocabulary space of LLMs. Second, we present multi-granular late fusion to integrate rich semantics efficiently with minimal information loss. It employs separate encoders for multi-granular prompts, delaying the fusion until the decoding stage. Experiments on four benchmark datasets show that GRAM outperforms eight state-of-the-art generative recommendation models, achieving significant improvements of 11.5-16.0% in Recall@5 and 5.3-13.6% in NDCG@5. The source code is available at https://github.com/skleee/GRAM.
摘要 (中文)
生成式推荐是一种新兴范式，它将推荐表述为 text-to-text 生成任务，从而利用大语言模型的广泛知识。然而，现有研究面临两个关键局限：（i）难以融入隐式 item 关系；（ii）难以利用丰富但冗长的 item 信息。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 Generative Recommender via semantic-Aware Multi-granular late fusion（GRAM），引入了两项协同创新。首先，我们设计了 semantic-to-lexical translation，将隐式的层次化与协同 item 关系编码进 LLM 的词表空间。其次，我们提出 multi-granular late fusion，以最小的信息损失高效整合丰富的语义。它为多粒度 prompt 使用独立的编码器，将融合推迟到解码阶段。在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，GRAM 优于八个 state-of-the-art 生成式推荐模型，在 Recall@5 上取得 11.5-16.0% 的显著提升，在 NDCG@5 上取得 5.3-13.6% 的提升。源代码见 https://github.com/skleee/GRAM。
Climber: Toward Efficient Scaling Laws for Large Recommendation Models
网易2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalingscaling law, multi-scale sequence extraction, dynamic temperature modulation, KV caching, inference accelerationPDF
AuthorsSongpei Xu, Shijia Wang, Da Guo, Xianwen Guo, Qiang Xiao, Bin Huang, Guanlin Wu, Chuanjiang Luo
Abstract (EN)
Transformer-based generative models have achieved remarkable success across domains with various scaling law manifestations. However, our extensive experiments reveal persistent challenges when applying Transformer to recommendation systems: (1) Transformer scaling is not ideal with increased computational resources, due to structural incompatibilities with recommendation-specific features such as multi-source data heterogeneity; (2) critical online inference latency constraints (tens of milliseconds) that intensify with longer user behavior sequences and growing computational demands. We propose Climber, an efficient recommendation framework comprising two synergistic components: the model architecture for efficient scaling and the co-designed acceleration techniques. Our proposed model adopts two core innovations: (1) multi-scale sequence extraction that achieves a time complexity reduction by a constant factor, enabling more efficient scaling with sequence length; (2) dynamic temperature modulation adapting attention distributions to the multi-scenario and multi-behavior patterns. Complemented by acceleration techniques, Climber achieves a 5.15× throughput gain without performance degradation by adopting a "single user, multiple item" batched processing and memoryefficient Key-Value caching. Comprehensive offline experiments on multiple datasets validate that Climber exhibits a more ideal scaling curve. To our knowledge, this is the first publicly documented framework where controlled ∗ Corresponding author. † Corresponding author. 摘要 (中文)
基于 Transformer 的生成式模型已在各领域取得显著成功，并展现出多种 scaling law 形式。然而，我们的大量实验揭示了将 Transformer 应用于推荐系统时持续存在的挑战：(1) 由于与多源数据异构性等推荐特有特征在结构上不兼容，Transformer 在计算资源增加时扩展并不理想；(2) 关键的在线推理时延约束（数十毫秒级）会随用户行为序列变长和计算需求增长而愈发严峻。我们提出 Climber，一个高效的推荐框架，包含两个协同组件：用于高效扩展的模型架构，以及与之协同设计的加速技术。我们提出的模型采用两项核心创新：(1) 多尺度序列抽取（multi-scale sequence extraction），将时间复杂度降低一个常数因子，从而支持随序列长度更高效地扩展；(2) 动态温度调制（dynamic temperature modulation），使注意力分布自适应多场景和多行为模式。配合加速技术，Climber 通过采用“single user, multiple item”的批处理以及内存高效的 Key-Value 缓存，在不损失性能的情况下实现 5.15 倍吞吐提升。在多个数据集上的全面离线实验验证了 Climber 表现出更理想的 scaling 曲线。据我们所知，这是首个公开记录的、受控 ∗ 通讯作者。† 通讯作者。允许出于个人或课堂教学目的，对本作品的全部或部分内容进行数字或硬拷贝复制，无需付费，前提是复制件不得用于盈利或商业优势，且复制件须在首页保留本声明以及完整的引用信息。本作品中由作者以外他人拥有的版权必须予以尊重。允许在注明出处的前提下进行摘要引用。以其他方式复制、重新发布、发布到服务器或转发至列表，需事先获得特别许可和/或支付费用。许可申请请联系 permissions@acm.org。CIKM '25, November 10–14, 2025, Seoul, Republic of Korea. © 2025 版权归作者/所有者持有。出版权已授权给 ACM。ACM ISBN 979-8-4007-2040-6/2025/11. . . $15.00 https://doi.org/10.1145/3746252.3761561 的模型扩展能够驱动在线指标持续增长（整体提升 12.19%）且资源成本不具惩罚性的框架。Climber 已成功部署在网易云音乐——中国最大的音乐流媒体平台之一——每天服务数千万用户。CCS CONCEPTS • Information systems → Recommender systems。
INFNet: A Task-aware Information Flow Network for Large-Scale Recommendation Systems
快手2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalingfeature interaction, task-aware modeling, multi-task learning, categorical tokens, cross attention with proxyPDF
AuthorsKaiyuan Li, Dongdong Mao, Yongxiang Tang, Yanhua Cheng, Yanxiang Zeng, Chao Wang, Xialong Liu, Peng Jiang
Abstract (EN)
Feature interaction has long been a cornerstone of ranking models in large-scale recommender systems due to its proven effectiveness in capturing complex dependencies among features. However, existing feature interaction strategies face two critical challenges in industrial applications: (1) The vast number of categorical and sequential features makes exhaustive interaction computationally prohibitive, often resulting in optimization difficulties. (2) Realworld recommender systems typically involve multiple prediction objectives, yet most current approaches apply feature interaction modules prior to the multi-task learning layers. This late-fusion design overlooks task-specific feature dependencies and inherently limits the capacity of multi-task modeling. To address these limitations, we propose the Information Flow Network (INFNet), a task-aware architecture designed for largescale recommendation scenarios. INFNet distinguishes features into three token types, categorical tokens, sequence tokens, and task tokens, and introduces a novel dual-flow design comprising heterogeneous and homogeneous alternating information blocks. For heterogeneous information flow, we employ a cross attention mechanism with proxy that facilitates efficient cross-modal token interaction with balanced computational cost. For homogeneous flow, we design type-specific Proxy Gated Units (PGUs) to enable fine-grained intra-type feature processing. Extensive experiments on multiple offline benchmarks confirm that INFNet achieves state-of-the-art performance. Moreover, INFNet has been successfully deployed in a commercial online advertising Permission to make digital or hard copies of all or part of this work for personal or classroom use is granted without fee provided that copies are not made or distributed for profit or commercial advantage and that copies bear this notice and the full citation on the first page. Copyrights for components of this work owned by others than the author(s) must be honored. Abstracting with credit is permitted. ACM ISBN 978-1-4503-XXXX-X/18/06 https://doi.org/XXXXXXX.XXXXXXX system, yielding significant gains of +1.587% in Revenue (REV) and +1.155% in Click-Through Rate (CTR).
摘要 (中文)
特征交互长期以来一直是大规模推荐系统中排序模型的基石，因为它在捕捉特征间复杂依赖方面已被证明有效。然而，现有特征交互策略在工业应用中面临两个关键挑战：(1) 类别特征和序列特征数量庞大，使得穷尽式交互在计算上不可行，往往导致优化困难。(2) 现实推荐系统通常涉及多个预测目标，而当前大多数方法在多任务学习层之前就应用特征交互模块。这种后期融合（late-fusion）设计忽视了任务特定的特征依赖，从根本上限制了多任务建模的能力。为解决这些局限，我们提出 Information Flow Network（INFNet），一种面向大规模推荐场景的任务感知架构。INFNet 将特征区分为三种 token 类型——类别 token、序列 token 和任务 token——并引入一种新颖的双流设计，由异质与同质交替的信息块组成。对于异质信息流，我们采用带 proxy 的 cross attention 机制，以均衡的计算开销促进高效的跨模态 token 交互。对于同质流，我们设计按类型独立的 Proxy Gated Unit（PGU），以实现细粒度的类型内特征处理。在多个离线基准上的大量实验证实，INFNet 达到 state-of-the-art 性能。此外，INFNet 已成功部署于一个商业在线广告系统，取得显著收益：收入（Revenue, REV）提升 +1.587%，点击率（CTR）提升 +1.155%。（允许出于个人或课堂教学目的，在无费用前提下制作本作品全部或部分的数字或纸质副本，前提是副本不得用于盈利或商业优势，且副本须在首页保留本声明。本作品中由非作者第三方拥有的版权组件必须予以尊重。允许带署名的摘要引用。除此以外的复制、再发表、在服务器上发布或向列表分发，均需事先获得具体许可和/或支付费用。请向 permissions@acm.org 申请许可。Conference acronym ’XX, Woodstock, NY © 2018 Copyright held by the owner/author(s). HHFT: Hierarchical Heterogeneous Feature Transformer for Recommendation Systems
阿里2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 ScalingCTR prediction, heterogeneous feature Transformer, semantic feature partitioning, high-order feature interaction, HiformerPDF
AuthorsLiren Yu, Wenming Zhang, Silu Zhou, Zhixuan Zhang, Dan Ou
Abstract (EN)
We propose HHFT (Hierarchical Heterogeneous Feature Transformer), a Transformer-based architecture tailored for industrial CTR prediction. HHFT addresses the limitations of DNN through three key designs: (1) Semantic Feature Partitioning: Grouping heterogeneous features (e.g. user profile, item information, behaviour sequennce) into semantically coherent blocks to preserve domain-specific information; (2) Heterogeneous Transformer Encoder: Adopting block-specific QKV projections and FFNs to avoid semantic confusion between distinct feature types; (3) Hiformer Layer: Capturing high-order interactions across features. Our findings reveal that Transformers significantly outperform DNN baselines, achieving a +0.4% improvement in CTR AUC at scale. We have successfully deployed the model on Taobao’s production platform, observing a significant uplift in key business metrics, including a +0.6% increase in Gross Merchandise Value (GMV).
摘要 (中文)
我们提出 HHFT（Hierarchical Heterogeneous Feature Transformer），一种为工业 CTR 预测量身定制的基于 Transformer 的架构。HHFT 通过三项关键设计解决了 DNN 的局限：(1) 语义特征划分（Semantic Feature Partitioning）：将异质特征（如用户画像、物品信息、行为序列）分组为语义一致的块，以保留领域特定信息；(2) 异质 Transformer 编码器（Heterogeneous Transformer Encoder）：采用按块独立的 QKV 投影和 FFN，避免不同特征类型之间的语义混淆；(3) Hiformer 层（Hiformer Layer）：捕捉特征之间的高阶交互。我们的发现表明，Transformer 显著优于 DNN baseline，在大规模下将 CTR AUC 提升 +0.4%。我们已将该模型成功部署到淘宝生产平台，观察到关键业务指标的显著提升，包括商品交易总额（Gross Merchandise Value, GMV）增长 +0.6%。
One Model to Rank Them All: Unifying Online Advertising with End-to-End Learning
美团2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalingend-to-end generative ranking, multistage cascading architecture, advertising externalities, cluster attention, RecFormerPDF
AuthorsJunyan Qiu, Ze Wang, Fan Zhang, Zuowu Zheng, Jile Zhu, Jiangke Fan
Abstract (EN)
Modern industrial advertising systems commonly employ Multistage Cascading Architectures (MCA) to balance computational efficiency with ranking accuracy. However, this approach presents two fundamental challenges: (1) performance inconsistencies arising from divergent optimization targets and capability differences between stages, and (2) failure to account for advertisement externalities - the complex interactions between candidate ads during ranking. These limitations ultimately compromise system effectiveness and reduce platform profitability. In this paper, we present UniROM, an end-to-end generative architecture that Unifies online advertising Ranking as One Model. UniROM replaces cascaded stages with a single model to directly generate optimal ad sequences from the full candidate ad corpus in location-based services (LBS). The primary challenges associated with this approach stem from high costs of feature processing and computational bottlenecks in modeling externalities of large-scale candidate pools. To address these challenges, UniROM introduces an algorithm and engine codesigned hybrid feature service to decouple user and ad feature processing, reducing latency while preserving expressiveness. To efficiently extract intra- and cross-sequence mutual information, we propose RecFormer with an innovative cluster-attention mechanism as its core architectural component. Furthermore, we propose ∗ Equal contribution. † Corresponding authors. Extensive offline evaluations on public benchmarks and large-scale online A/B testing on industrial advertising platform have demonstrated the superior performance of UniROM over state-of-the-art MCAs.

现代工业广告系统通常采用多阶段级联架构（MCA）以在计算效率与排序准确率之间取得平衡。然而，这种做法带来两个根本性挑战：（1）由各阶段优化目标不同与能力差异所导致的性能不一致；（2）未能考虑广告外部性——即排序过程中候选广告之间复杂的相互作用。这些局限最终损害了系统效果并降低了平台盈利能力。本文提出 UniROM，一种端到端生成式架构，将在线广告排序统一为 One Model。UniROM 用单一模型取代级联的各阶段，在基于位置的服务（LBS）中直接从全量候选广告语料生成最优广告序列。该方法面临的主要挑战源于特征处理的高成本以及在大规模候选池上建模外部性时的计算瓶颈。为应对这些挑战，UniROM 引入算法与引擎协同设计的混合特征服务，将用户特征与广告特征的处理解耦，在保持表达能力的同时降低延迟。为高效提取序列内与跨序列的互信息，我们提出以创新的 cluster-attention 机制作为核心架构组件的 RecFormer。此外，我们提出 ∗ 贡献相同。† 通讯作者。允许出于个人或课堂教学目的，对本作品的全部或部分内容进行数字或纸质复制，无需付费，前提是复制件不得用于营利或商业目的，且复制件须在首页保留本声明及完整引用信息。本作品中他人拥有的版权必须得到尊重。允许在注明出处的前提下摘录。以其他方式复制、重新发布、在服务器上发布或向邮件列表分发，均需事先获得特别许可和/或支付费用。许可申请请联系 permissions@acm.org。Conference’17, Washington, DC, USA © 2025 版权归作者/所有者持有。出版权由 ACM 授权。ACM ISBN 978-x-xxxx-xxxx-x/YYYY/MM https://doi.org/10.1145/nnnnnnn.nnnnnnn 一种双阶段训练策略，将预训练与基于强化学习的后训练相结合，以满足复杂的平台与广告目标。在公开 benchmark 上的大量离线评估以及在工业广告平台上的大规模在线 A/B 测试表明，UniROM 相比 state-of-the-art 的 MCA 具有更优的表现。
Exploring Scaling Laws of CTR Model for Online Performance Improvement
中科院2025推荐学术生成式骨干 与 ScalingCTR prediction, scaling law, unified attention block, online distillation, sparse self-attentionPDF
AuthorsWeijiang Lai, Beihong Jin, Jiongyan Zhang, Yiyuan Zheng, Jian Dong, Jia Cheng, Jun Lei, Xingxing Wang
Abstract (EN)
Click-Through Rate (CTR) models play a vital role in improving user experience and boosting business revenue in many online personalized services. However, current CTR models generally encounter bottlenecks in performance improvement. Inspired by the scaling law phenomenon of Large Language Models (LLMs), we propose a new paradigm for improving CTR predictions: first, constructing a CTR model with accuracy scalable to the model grade and data size, and then distilling the knowledge implied in this model into its lightweight model that can serve online users. To put it into practice, we construct a CTR model named SUAN (Stacked Unified Attention Network). In SUAN, we propose the unified attention block (UAB) as a behavior sequence encoder. A single UAB unifies the modeling of the sequential and non-sequential features and also measures the importance of each user behavior feature from multiple perspectives. Stacked UABs elevate the configuration to a high grade, paving the way for performance improvement. In order to benefit from the high performance of the high-grade SUAN and avoid the disadvantage of its long inference time, we modify the SUAN with sparse self-attention and parallel inference strategies to form LightSUAN, and then adopt online distillation to train the low-grade LightSUAN, taking a high-grade SUAN as a teacher. The distilled LightSUAN has superior performance but the same inference time as the LightSUAN, making it well-suited for † Corresponding author. Please use nonacm option or ACM Engage class to enable CC licenses This work is licensed under a Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International License. RecSys ’25, September 22–26, 2025, Prague, Czech Republic © 2025 Copyright held by the owner/author(s). ACM ISBN 979-8-4007-1364-4/2025/09 https://doi.org/10.1145/3705328.3748046 online deployment. Experimental results show that SUAN performs exceptionally well and holds the scaling laws spanning three orders of magnitude in model grade and data size, and the distilled LightSUAN outperforms the SUAN configured with one grade higher. More importantly, the distilled LightSUAN has been integrated into an online service, increasing the CTR by 2.81% and CPM by 1.69% while keeping the average inference time acceptable. Our source code is available at https://github.com/laiweijiang/SUAN.
摘要 (中文)
点击率（CTR）模型在许多在线个性化服务中，对提升用户体验和增加商业收入起着至关重要的作用。然而，当前的 CTR 模型普遍在性能提升上遇到瓶颈。受大语言模型（LLM）scaling law 现象的启发，我们提出了一种改进 CTR 预测的新范式：首先构建一个精度可随模型档位（model grade）和数据规模扩展的 CTR 模型，然后将该模型中蕴含的知识蒸馏到其轻量级模型中，以服务于线上用户。为落地实践，我们构建了一个名为 SUAN（Stacked Unified Attention Network）的 CTR 模型。在 SUAN 中，我们提出统一注意力块（unified attention block, UAB）作为行为序列编码器。单个 UAB 统一了序列特征与非序列特征的建模，并从多个视角度量每个用户行为特征的重要性。堆叠 UAB 可将配置提升到更高档位，为性能提升铺平道路。为了既获得高档位 SUAN 的高性能，又避免其推理时间长的缺点，我们用稀疏自注意力和平行推理策略改造 SUAN，形成 LightSUAN，然后采用在线蒸馏，以高档位 SUAN 作为 teacher 来训练低档位 LightSUAN。蒸馏后的 LightSUAN 性能更优，但推理时间与 LightSUAN 相同，因而非常适合在线部署。实验结果表明，SUAN 表现极为出色，在模型档位和数据规模上跨越三个数量级仍保持 scaling law，且蒸馏后的 LightSUAN 优于高一个档位配置的 SUAN。更重要的是，蒸馏后的 LightSUAN 已集成到一项在线服务中，在保持平均推理时间可接受的前提下，将 CTR 提升 2.81%、CPM 提升 1.69%。我们的源码开源于 https://github.com/laiweijiang/SUAN。（† 通讯作者。请使用 nonacm 选项或 ACM Engage 类以启用 CC 许可。本作品依据 Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International License 授权。RecSys ’25, September 22–26, 2025, Prague, Czech Republic © 2025 Copyright held by the owner/author(s). ACM ISBN 979-8-4007-1364-4/2025/09 https://doi.org/10.1145/3705328.3748046）
Scaling Transformers for Discriminative Recommendation via Generative Pretraining
阿里国际2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalinggenerative pretraining, discriminative recommendation, sparse parameter freezing, overfitting, scaling lawPDF · arXiv
AuthorsChunqi Wang, Bingchao Wu, Zheng Chen, Lei Shen, Bing Wang, Xiaoyi Zeng
Abstract (EN)
Discriminative recommendation tasks, such as CTR (click-through rate) and CVR (conversion rate) prediction, play critical roles in the ranking stage of large-scale industrial recommender systems. However, training a discriminative model encounters a significant overfitting issue induced by data sparsity. Moreover, this overfitting issue worsens with larger models, causing them to underperform smaller ones. To address the overfitting issue and enhance model scalability, we propose a framework named GPSD (\textbf{G}enerative \textbf{P}retraining for \textbf{S}calable \textbf{D}iscriminative Recommendation), drawing inspiration from generative training, which exhibits no evident signs of overfitting. GPSD leverages the parameters learned from a pretrained generative model to initialize a discriminative model, and subsequently applies a sparse parameter freezing strategy. Extensive experiments conducted on both industrial-scale and publicly available datasets demonstrate the superior performance of GPSD. Moreover, it delivers remarkable improvements in online A/B tests. GPSD offers two primary advantages: 1) it substantially narrows the generalization gap in model training, resulting in better test performance; and 2) it leverages the scalability of Transformers, delivering consistent performance gains as models are scaled up. Specifically, we observe consistent performance improvements as the model dense parameters scale from 13K to 0.3B, closely adhering to power laws. These findings pave the way for unifying the architectures of recommendation models and language models, enabling the direct application of techniques well-established in large language models to recommendation models. The code is available at this https URL.
摘要 (中文)
判别式推荐任务，例如 CTR（click-through rate）和 CVR（conversion rate）预测，在大规模工业推荐系统的 ranking 阶段发挥着关键作用。然而，训练判别式模型会因数据稀疏而遭遇严重的过拟合问题。而且，该过拟合问题随模型增大而加剧，导致大模型反而不如小模型。为解决过拟合并提升模型可扩展性，我们提出名为 GPSD（Generative Pretraining for Scalable Discriminative Recommendation）的框架，其灵感来自没有明显过拟合迹象的生成式训练。GPSD 利用从预训练生成式模型中学到的参数来初始化判别式模型，随后应用稀疏参数冻结策略。在工业规模和公开数据集上的大量实验表明 GPSD 性能优越。此外，它在在线 A/B test 中也带来了显著提升。GPSD 有两大优势：1) 大幅缩小模型训练中的泛化差距，从而获得更好的测试性能；2) 利用 Transformer 的可扩展性，在模型扩展时带来持续的性能提升。具体而言，我们观察到当模型稠密参数从 13K 扩展到 0.3B 时性能持续提升，且紧密遵循 power law。这些发现为统一推荐模型与语言模型的架构铺平了道路，使在大语言模型中成熟的技术能够直接应用于推荐模型。代码见 this https URL。
Understanding Generative Recommendation with Semantic IDs from a Model-scaling View
Michigan State University / Snap2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalinggenerative recommendation, semantic IDs, scaling laws, LLM-as-recommender, quantization tokenizerPDF · arXiv
AuthorsJingzhe Liu, Liam Collins, Jiliang Tang, Tong Zhao, Neil Shah, Clark Mingxuan Ju
Abstract (EN)
Recent advancements in generative models have allowed the emergence of a promising paradigm for recommender systems (RS), known as Generative Recommendation (GR), which tries to unify rich item semantics and collaborative filtering signals. One popular modern approach is to use semantic IDs (SIDs), which are discrete codes quantized from the embeddings of modality encoders (e.g., large language or vision models), to represent items in an autoregressive user interaction sequence modeling setup (henceforth, SID-based GR). While generative models in other domains exhibit well-established scaling laws, our work reveals that SID-based GR shows significant bottlenecks while scaling up the model. In particular, the performance of SID-based GR quickly saturates as we enlarge each component: the modality encoder, the quantization tokenizer, and the RS itself. In this work, we identify the limited capacity of SIDs to encode item semantic information as one of the fundamental bottlenecks. Motivated by this observation, as an initial effort to obtain GR models with better scaling behaviors, we revisit another GR paradigm that directly uses large language models (LLMs) as recommenders (henceforth, LLM-as-RS). Our experiments show that the LLM-as-RS paradigm has superior model scaling properties and achieves up to 20 percent improvement over the best achievable performance of SID-based GR through scaling. We also challenge the prevailing belief that LLMs struggle to capture collaborative filtering information, showing that their ability to model user-item interactions improves as LLMs scale up. Our analyses on both SID-based GR and LLMs across model sizes from 44M to 14B parameters underscore the intrinsic scaling limits of SID-based GR and position LLM-as-RS as a promising path toward foundation models for GR.
摘要 (中文)
生成模型的最新进展催生了推荐系统（RS）中一种颇具前景的新范式，即生成式推荐（GR），它试图统一丰富的 item 语义与协同过滤信号。一种流行的现代做法是使用 semantic IDs（SIDs），即从模态编码器（例如大语言模型或视觉模型）的 embedding 量化而来的离散 code，在自回归用户交互序列建模框架下表示 item（下称基于 SID 的 GR）。尽管其他领域的生成模型展现出成熟的 scaling law，我们的工作却揭示出基于 SID 的 GR 在扩大模型规模时存在显著瓶颈。具体而言，随着我们扩大各个组件——模态编码器、量化 tokenizer 以及 RS 本身——基于 SID 的 GR 的性能会迅速饱和。在本工作中，我们将 SIDs 编码 item 语义信息能力有限这一点认定为根本瓶颈之一。受此观察启发，作为获得具有更好 scaling 行为的 GR 模型的初步努力，我们重新审视了另一种 GR 范式，即直接使用大语言模型（LLMs）作为推荐器（下称 LLM-as-RS）。我们的实验表明，LLM-as-RS 范式具有更优的模型 scaling 特性，并且通过 scaling 相比基于 SID 的 GR 所能达到的最佳性能提升了最高 20%。我们还挑战了 LLMs 难以捕捉协同过滤信息的普遍看法，表明其建模用户-item 交互的能力会随着 LLMs 规模扩大而提升。我们在从 44M 到 14B 参数的各模型规模上对基于 SID 的 GR 和 LLMs 的分析，凸显了基于 SID 的 GR 的固有 scaling 局限，并将 LLM-as-RS 定位为通往 GR 基础模型的一条有前景的路径。
DiffGRM: Diffusion-based Generative Recommendation Model
快手2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalinggenerative recommendation, discrete diffusion, semantic IDs, parallel decoding, item tokenizationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsZhao Liu, Yichen Zhu, Yiqing Yang, Xiao Lv, Guoping Tang, Rui Huang, Qiang Luo, Ruiming Tang, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Generative recommendation (GR) is an emerging paradigm that represents each item via a tokenizer as an n-digit semantic ID (SID) and predicts the next item by autoregressively generating its SID conditioned on the user's history. However, two structural properties of SIDs make ARMs ill-suited. First, intra-item consistency: the n digits jointly specify one item, yet the left-to-right causality trains each digit only under its prefix and blocks bidirectional cross-digit evidence, collapsing supervision to a single causal path. Second, inter-digit heterogeneity: digits differ in semantic granularity and predictability, while the uniform next-token objective assigns equal weight to all digits, overtraining easy digits and undertraining hard digits. To address these two issues, we propose DiffGRM, a diffusion-based GR model that replaces the autoregressive decoder with a masked discrete diffusion model (MDM), thereby enabling bidirectional context and any-order parallel generation of SID digits for recommendation. Specifically, we tailor DiffGRM in three aspects: (1) tokenization with Parallel Semantic Encoding (PSE) to decouple digits and balance per-digit information; (2) training with On-policy Coherent Noising (OCN) that prioritizes uncertain digits via coherent masking to concentrate supervision on high-value signals; and (3) inference with Confidence-guided Parallel Denoising (CPD) that fills higher-confidence digits first and generates diverse Top-K candidates. Experiments show consistent gains over strong generative and discriminative recommendation baselines on multiple datasets, improving NDCG@10 by 6.9%-15.5%. Code is available at https://github.com/liuzhao09/DiffGRM.
摘要 (中文)
生成式推荐（GR）是一种新兴范式，它通过 tokenizer 将每个 item 表示为 n 位 semantic ID（SID），并以用户历史为条件，通过自回归生成其 SID 来预测下一个 item。然而，SIDs 的两个结构特性使 ARMs 难以胜任。第一，item 内一致性（intra-item consistency）：这 n 位数字共同指定一个 item，但从左到右的因果性只在其前缀条件下训练每一位，阻断了双向的跨位证据，将监督坍缩为单一因果路径。第二，位间异质性（inter-digit heterogeneity）：各位数字在语义粒度和可预测性上不同，而统一的 next-token 目标对所有位赋予相同权重，导致易预测的位被过度训练、难预测的位训练不足。为解决这两个问题，我们提出 DiffGRM，一种基于 diffusion 的 GR 模型，用 masked discrete diffusion model（MDM）取代自回归解码器，从而为推荐启用 SID 各位的双向上下文和任意顺序的并行生成。具体而言，我们在三个方面对 DiffGRM 进行了定制：(1) 采用 Parallel Semantic Encoding（PSE）的 tokenization 来解耦各位并平衡每位的信息；(2) 采用 On-policy Coherent Noising（OCN）进行训练，通过一致性掩码优先处理不确定的位，将监督集中于高价值信号；(3) 采用 Confidence-guided Parallel Denoising（CPD）进行推理，优先填充置信度更高的位并生成多样化的 Top-K 候选。实验表明，在多个数据集上相较强生成式和判别式推荐 baseline 均取得一致的提升，NDCG@10 提升 6.9%-15.5%。代码见 https://github.com/liuzhao09/DiffGRM。
Massive Memorization with Hundreds of Trillions of Parameters for Sequential Transducer Generative Recommenders
Meta / Yale University2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalinggenerative recommender, sequential transducer, virtual sequential target attention, user history summarization, long-sequence modelingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsZhimin Chen, Chenyu Zhao, Ka Chun Mo, Yunjiang Jiang, Jane H. Lee, Khushhall Chandra Mahajan, Ning Jiang, Kai Ren, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Modern large-scale recommendation systems rely heavily on user interaction history sequences to enhance the model performance. The advent of large language models and sequential modeling techniques, particularly transformer-like architectures, has led to significant advancements recently (e.g., HSTU, SIM, and TWIN models). While scaling to ultra-long user histories (10k to 100k items) generally improves model performance, it also creates significant challenges on latency, queries per second (QPS) and GPU cost in industry-scale recommendation systems. Existing models do not adequately address these industrial scalability issues. In this paper, we propose a novel two-stage modeling framework, namely VIrtual Sequential Target Attention (VISTA), which decomposes traditional target attention from a candidate item to user history items into two distinct stages: (1) user history summarization into a few hundred tokens; followed by (2) candidate item attention to those tokens. These summarization token embeddings are then cached in storage system and then utilized as sequence features for downstream model training and inference. This novel design for scalability enables VISTA to scale to lifelong user histories (up to one million items) while keeping downstream training and inference costs fixed, which is essential in industry. Our approach achieves significant improvements in offline and online metrics and has been successfully deployed on an industry leading recommendation platform serving billions of users.
摘要 (中文)
现代大规模推荐系统高度依赖用户交互历史序列来提升模型性能。大语言模型和序列建模技术，特别是 transformer 类架构的出现，近年来带来了显著进展（例如 HSTU、SIM 和 TWIN 模型）。虽然扩展到超长用户历史（1 万到 10 万个 item）通常能提升模型性能，但在工业规模推荐系统中也会对延迟、每秒查询数（QPS）和 GPU 成本带来重大挑战。现有模型未能充分解决这些工业可扩展性问题。本文提出一种新颖的两阶段建模框架，即 VIrtual Sequential Target Attention（VISTA），它将传统的从候选 item 到用户历史 item 的 target attention 分解为两个不同阶段：(1) 将用户历史摘要为数百个 token；(2) 候选 item 对这些 token 的注意力。这些摘要 token embedding 随后被缓存在存储系统中，并作为序列特征用于下游模型训练和推理。这种面向可扩展性的新颖设计使 VISTA 能够扩展到终生用户历史（最多一百万个 item），同时保持下游训练和推理成本固定，这在工业界至关重要。我们的方法在离线与在线指标上均取得显著提升，并已成功部署于一个服务数十亿用户的行业领先推荐平台。
Equip Pre-ranking with Target Attention by Residual Quantization
阿里 / 上海交大2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalingpre-ranking, target attention, residual quantization, industrial recommendation, efficiencyPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYutong Li, Yu Zhu, Yichen Qiao, Ziyu Guan, Lv Shao, Tong Liu, Bo Zheng
Abstract (EN)
The pre-ranking stage in industrial recommendation systems faces a fundamental conflict between efficiency and effectiveness. While powerful models like Target Attention (TA) excel at capturing complex feature interactions in the ranking stage, their high computational cost makes them infeasible for pre-ranking, which often relies on simplistic vector-product models. This disparity creates a significant performance bottleneck for the entire system. To bridge this gap, we propose TARQ, a novel pre-ranking framework. Inspired by generative models, TARQ's key innovation is to equip pre-ranking with an architecture approximate to TA by Residual Quantization. This allows us to bring the modeling power of TA into the latency-critical pre-ranking stage for the first time, establishing a new state-of-the-art trade-off between accuracy and efficiency. Extensive offline experiments and large-scale online A/B tests at Taobao demonstrate TARQ's significant improvements in ranking performance. Consequently, our model has been fully deployed in production, serving tens of millions of daily active users and yielding substantial business improvements. The code and data are available at https://github.com/zyody/tarq_sigir2026.
摘要 (中文)
工业推荐系统中的粗排阶段面临着效率与效果之间的根本性冲突。虽然像 Target Attention（TA）这样强大的模型在精排阶段擅长捕捉复杂的特征交互，但其高昂的计算成本使其无法应用于粗排，而粗排往往依赖简单的向量内积模型。这种差距对整个系统造成了显著的性能瓶颈。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了 TARQ，一个新颖的粗排框架。受生成式模型启发，TARQ 的核心创新在于通过残差量化（Residual Quantization）为粗排装备一个近似 TA 的架构。这使我们首次能够将 TA 的建模能力引入延迟敏感的粗排阶段，在准确率与效率之间确立了新的 SOTA 权衡。大量离线实验以及在淘宝进行的大规模线上 A/B 测试，证明了 TARQ 在排序性能上的显著提升。最终，我们的模型已全面部署到生产环境，服务数千万日活跃用户，并带来了可观的业务收益。代码和数据可在 https://github.com/zyody/tarq_sigir2026 获取。
OneTrans: Unified Feature Interaction and Sequence Modeling with One Transformer in Industrial Recommender
NTU / 字节2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalingunified Transformer, feature interaction, sequence modeling, causal attention, KV cachingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsZhaoqi Zhang, Haolei Pei, Jun Guo, Tianyu Wang, Yufei Feng, Hui Sun, Shaowei Liu, Aixin Sun
Abstract (EN)
In recommendation systems, scaling up feature-interaction modules (e.g., Wukong, RankMixer) or user-behavior sequence modules (e.g., LONGER) has achieved notable success. However, these efforts typically proceed on separate tracks, which not only hinders bidirectional information exchange but also prevents unified optimization and scaling. In this paper, we propose OneTrans, a unified Transformer backbone that simultaneously performs user-behavior sequence modeling and feature interaction. OneTrans employs a unified tokenizer to convert both sequential and non-sequential attributes into a single token sequence. The stacked OneTrans blocks share parameters across similar sequential tokens while assigning token-specific parameters to non-sequential tokens. Through causal attention and cross-request KV caching, OneTrans enables precomputation and caching of intermediate representations, significantly reducing computational costs during both training and inference. Experimental results on industrial-scale datasets demonstrate that OneTrans scales efficiently with increasing parameters, consistently outperforms strong baselines, and yields a 5.68% lift in per-user GMV in online A/B tests.
摘要 (中文)
在推荐系统中，扩大特征交互模块（如 Wukong、RankMixer）或用户行为序列模块（如 LONGER）的规模已取得了显著成效。然而，这些工作通常沿着彼此独立的路线推进，这不仅阻碍了双向信息交流，也妨碍了统一优化与扩展。在本文中，我们提出了 OneTrans，一个统一的 Transformer 主干，同时执行用户行为序列建模和特征交互。OneTrans 采用统一的 tokenizer，将序列属性和非序列属性转换为单一的 token 序列。堆叠的 OneTrans block 在相似的序列 token 之间共享参数，同时为非序列 token 分配各自专属的参数。通过 causal attention 和跨请求 KV 缓存，OneTrans 能够对中间表示进行预计算与缓存，显著降低训练和推理阶段的计算成本。在工业规模数据集上的实验结果表明，OneTrans 能随参数增加高效地扩展，持续优于强大的 baseline，并在线上 A/B 测试中实现单用户 GMV 提升 5.68%。
From Scaling to Structured Expressivity: Rethinking Transformers for CTR Prediction
阿里2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalingstructured expressivity, CTR prediction, Field-Aware Transformer, Basis-Composed Hypernetwork, scaling lawPDF · arXiv
AuthorsBencheng Yan, Yuejie Lei, Zhiyuan Zeng, Zheye Deng, Di Wang, Kaiyi Lin, Pengjie Wang, Chuan Yu, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Despite massive investments in scale, deep models for click-through rate (CTR) prediction often exhibit rapidly diminishing returns -- a stark contrast to the {predictable scaling laws} seen in large language models (LLMs). We identify the root cause as a {fundamental} \textit{structural misalignment}: {standard} Transformers assume sequential compositionality, whereas CTR data demand combinatorial reasoning over {heterogeneous} fields. To restore alignment, we introduce the \textbf{Field-Aware Transformer (FAT)}. {By reconstructing the standard Transformer block with field-centric parameters, FAT achieves \textit{structured expressivity}, {fundamentally shifting the model complexity dependence from the total vocabulary size $n$ with the number of fields $F$ ($n \gg F$).}} Crucially, to decouple model capacity from field cardinality, FAT employs a {Basis-Composed Hypernetwork} to synthesize field-specific parameters from shared bases, further reducing parameter complexity. {Theoretically, we ground this scaling behavior through a formal scaling law based on Rademacher complexity. Empirically, FAT outperforms exisiting state-of-the-art methods with up to \textbf{+4.38\%} AUC improvement, and delivers \textbf{+2.33\%} CTR and \textbf{+0.66\%} RPM in live production.} Our work establishes that scalable recommendation arises not from size alone, but from \textit{structured expressivity} -- architectural coherence with data semantics.
摘要 (中文)
尽管在规模上投入巨大，用于点击率（CTR）预测的深度模型往往表现出收益迅速递减——这与大语言模型（LLM）中可预测的 scaling law 形成鲜明对比。我们将根本原因归结为一种根本性的结构错配：标准 Transformer 假设序列式组合性，而 CTR 数据则要求对异构字段进行组合式推理。为恢复这种对齐，我们提出了 Field-Aware Transformer（FAT）。通过以字段为中心的参数重建标准 Transformer block，FAT 实现了结构化表达力，从根本上将模型复杂度对总词表规模 $n$ 的依赖转变为对字段数量 $F$ 的依赖（$n \gg F$）。关键在于，为使模型容量与字段基数解耦，FAT 采用 Basis-Composed Hypernetwork，从共享基底合成字段专属参数，进一步降低参数复杂度。在理论上，我们通过基于 Rademacher 复杂度的形式化 scaling law 为这种扩展行为奠定基础。在实证上，FAT 优于现有 SOTA 方法，AUC 提升最高达 +4.38%，并在线上生产环境中带来 +2.33% 的 CTR 和 +0.66% 的 RPM。我们的工作表明，可扩展的推荐并非仅来自规模，而是来自结构化表达力——即架构与数据语义的协调一致。
FuXi-$γ$: Efficient Sequential Recommendation with Exponential-Power Temporal Encoder and Diagonal-Sparse Positional Mechanism
南开 / 华为2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalingsequential recommendation, exponential-power temporal encoder, diagonal-sparse positional mechanism, long-sequence recommendation, Ebbinghaus forgetting curvePDF · arXiv
AuthorsDezhi Yi, Wei Guo, Wenyang Cui, Wenxuan He, Huifeng Guo, Yong Liu, Zhenhua Dong, Ye Lu
Abstract (EN)
Sequential recommendation aims to model users' evolving preferences based on their historical interactions. Recent advances leverage Transformer-based architectures to capture global dependencies, but existing methods often suffer from high computational overhead, primarily due to discontinuous memory access in temporal encoding and dense attention over long sequences. To address these limitations, we propose FuXi-$γ$, a novel sequential recommendation framework that improves both effectiveness and efficiency through principled architectural design. FuXi-$γ$ adopts a decoder-only Transformer structure and introduces two key innovations: (1) An exponential-power temporal encoder that encodes relative temporal intervals using a tunable exponential decay function inspired by the Ebbinghaus forgetting curve. This encoder enables flexible modeling of both short-term and long-term preferences while maintaining high efficiency through continuous memory access and pure matrix operations. (2) A diagonal-sparse positional mechanism that prunes low-contribution attention blocks using a diagonal-sliding strategy guided by the persymmetry of Toeplitz matrix. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that FuXi-$γ$ achieves state-of-the-art performance in recommendation quality, while accelerating training by up to 4.74$\times$ and inference by up to 6.18$\times$, making it a practical and scalable solution for long-sequence recommendation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Yeedzhi/FuXi-gamma.
摘要 (中文)
序列推荐旨在基于用户的历史交互对其不断演变的偏好进行建模。近期的进展利用基于 Transformer 的架构来捕捉全局依赖，但现有方法往往面临高昂的计算开销，主要原因是时间编码中的非连续内存访问以及长序列上的稠密注意力。为应对这些局限，我们提出了 FuXi-$γ$，一个新颖的序列推荐框架，通过有原则的架构设计同时提升效果与效率。FuXi-$γ$ 采用 decoder-only 的 Transformer 结构，并引入了两项关键创新：（1）指数幂时间编码器（exponential-power temporal encoder），它受艾宾浩斯遗忘曲线启发，使用可调的指数衰减函数对相对时间间隔进行编码。该编码器能够灵活地对短期和长期偏好进行建模，同时通过连续内存访问和纯矩阵运算保持高效率。（2）对角稀疏位置机制（diagonal-sparse positional mechanism），它借助 Toeplitz 矩阵的 persymmetry 特性，通过对角滑动策略剪除低贡献的注意力块。在四个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，FuXi-$γ$ 在推荐质量上达到 SOTA 水平，同时将训练加速最高 4.74$\times$、推理加速最高 6.18$\times$，使其成为长序列推荐中实用且可扩展的解决方案。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/Yeedzhi/FuXi-gamma 获取。
FuXi-$α$: Scaling Recommendation Model with Feature Interaction Enhanced Transformer
USTC / 华为2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalingscaling law, feature interaction, Adaptive Multi-channel Self-attention, Multi-stage FFN, sequential recommendationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYufei Ye, Wei Guo, Jin Yao Chin, Hao Wang, Hong Zhu, Xi Lin, Yuyang Ye, Yong Liu, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Inspired by scaling laws and large language models, research on large-scale recommendation models has gained significant attention. Recent advancements have shown that expanding sequential recommendation models to large-scale recommendation models can be an effective strategy. Current state-of-the-art sequential recommendation models primarily use self-attention mechanisms for explicit feature interactions among items, while implicit interactions are managed through Feed-Forward Networks (FFNs). However, these models often inadequately integrate temporal and positional information, either by adding them to attention weights or by blending them with latent representations, which limits their expressive power. A recent model, HSTU, further reduces the focus on implicit feature interactions, constraining its performance. We propose a new model called FuXi-$α$ to address these issues. This model introduces an Adaptive Multi-channel Self-attention mechanism that distinctly models temporal, positional, and semantic features, along with a Multi-stage FFN to enhance implicit feature interactions. Our offline experiments demonstrate that our model outperforms existing models, with its performance continuously improving as the model size increases. Additionally, we conducted an online A/B test within the Huawei Music app, which showed a $4.76\%$ increase in the average number of songs played per user and a $5.10\%$ increase in the average listening duration per user. Our code has been released at https://github.com/USTC-StarTeam/FuXi-alpha.
摘要 (中文)
受 scaling laws 和大语言模型的启发，大规模推荐模型的研究获得了广泛关注。近期进展表明，将序列推荐模型扩展为大规模推荐模型是一种有效策略。当前 state-of-the-art 的序列推荐模型主要使用 self-attention 机制对 item 之间的显式特征交互进行建模，而隐式交互则通过 Feed-Forward Networks（FFN）来处理。然而，这些模型往往未能充分整合时间与位置信息，或是将其加到 attention 权重上，或是与隐表示混合，从而限制了其表达能力。近期的模型 HSTU 进一步弱化了对隐式特征交互的关注，制约了其性能。我们提出名为 FuXi-$α$ 的新模型来解决这些问题。该模型引入了 Adaptive Multi-channel Self-attention 机制，分别对时间、位置和语义特征进行建模，并配合 Multi-stage FFN 来增强隐式特征交互。我们的离线实验表明，该模型优于现有模型，且随着模型规模增大其性能持续提升。此外，我们在华为音乐 App 内进行了线上 A/B 测试，结果显示人均播放歌曲数提升 $4.76\%$，人均收听时长提升 $5.10\%$。我们的代码已发布于 https://github.com/USTC-StarTeam/FuXi-alpha。
Killing Two Birds with One Stone: Unifying Retrieval and Ranking with a Single Generative Recommendation Model
USTC / 华为2025推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalinggenerative recommendation, unifying retrieval and ranking, ranking-driven enhancer, gradient-guided adaptive weighter, sequence generationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsLuankang Zhang, Kenan Song, Yi Quan Lee, Wei Guo, Hao Wang, Yawen Li, Huifeng Guo, Yong Liu, et al.
Abstract (EN)
In recommendation systems, the traditional multi-stage paradigm, which includes retrieval and ranking, often suffers from information loss between stages and diminishes performance. Recent advances in generative models, inspired by natural language processing, suggest the potential for unifying these stages to mitigate such loss. This paper presents the Unified Generative Recommendation Framework (UniGRF), a novel approach that integrates retrieval and ranking into a single generative model. By treating both stages as sequence generation tasks, UniGRF enables sufficient information sharing without additional computational costs, while remaining model-agnostic. To enhance inter-stage collaboration, UniGRF introduces a ranking-driven enhancer module that leverages the precision of the ranking stage to refine retrieval processes, creating an enhancement loop. Besides, a gradient-guided adaptive weighter is incorporated to dynamically balance the optimization of retrieval and ranking, ensuring synchronized performance improvements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UniGRF significantly outperforms existing models on benchmark datasets, confirming its effectiveness in facilitating information transfer. Ablation studies and further experiments reveal that UniGRF not only promotes efficient collaboration between stages but also achieves synchronized optimization. UniGRF provides an effective, scalable, and compatible framework for generative recommendation systems.
摘要 (中文)
在推荐系统中，包含 retrieval 与 ranking 的传统多阶段范式往往存在阶段间的信息损失并降低性能。受自然语言处理启发的近期生成模型进展表明，统一这些阶段以缓解此类损失具有潜力。本文提出 Unified Generative Recommendation Framework（UniGRF），一种将 retrieval 与 ranking 整合到单一生成模型中的新方法。通过将两个阶段都视为序列生成任务，UniGRF 能够在无额外计算成本的情况下实现充分的信息共享，同时保持模型无关。为增强阶段间协作，UniGRF 引入了一个 ranking 驱动的增强器模块，利用 ranking 阶段的精确性来改进 retrieval 过程，形成增强回路。此外，还引入了一个梯度引导的自适应加权器，以动态平衡 retrieval 与 ranking 的优化，确保性能同步提升。大量实验表明，UniGRF 在基准数据集上显著优于现有模型，验证了其在促进信息传递方面的有效性。消融研究与进一步实验表明，UniGRF 不仅促进了阶段间的高效协作，还实现了同步优化。UniGRF 为生成式推荐系统提供了一个有效、可扩展且兼容的框架。
STAR-Rec: Making Peace with Length Variance and Pattern Diversity in Sequential Recommendation
香港城市大学 / 南科大2025推荐学术生成式骨干 与 Scalingsequential recommendation, state-space modeling, mixture-of-experts, preference-aware attention, length variancePDF · arXiv
AuthorsMaolin Wang, Sheng Zhang, Ruocheng Guo, Wanyu Wang, Xuetao Wei, Zitao Liu, Hongzhi Yin, Yi Chang, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Recent deep sequential recommendation models often struggle to effectively model key characteristics of user behaviors, particularly in handling sequence length variations and capturing diverse interaction patterns. We propose STAR-Rec, a novel architecture that synergistically combines preference-aware attention and state-space modeling through a sequence-level mixture-of-experts framework. STAR-Rec addresses these challenges by: (1) employing preference-aware attention to capture both inherently similar item relationships and diverse preferences, (2) utilizing state-space modeling to efficiently process variable-length sequences with linear complexity, and (3) incorporating a mixture-of-experts component that adaptively routes different behavioral patterns to specialized experts, handling both focused category-specific browsing and diverse category exploration patterns. We theoretically demonstrate how the state space model and attention mechanisms can be naturally unified in recommendation scenarios, where SSM captures temporal dynamics through state compression while attention models both similar and diverse item relationships. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that STAR-Rec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art sequential recommendation methods, particularly in scenarios involving diverse user behaviors and varying sequence lengths.
摘要 (中文)
近期的深度序列推荐模型往往难以有效建模用户行为的关键特征，尤其是在处理序列长度变化和捕捉多样交互模式方面。我们提出 STAR-Rec，一种通过序列级 mixture-of-experts 框架将偏好感知 attention 与状态空间建模协同结合的新架构。STAR-Rec 通过以下方式应对这些挑战：（1）采用偏好感知 attention 同时捕捉内在相似的 item 关系和多样偏好，（2）利用状态空间建模以线性复杂度高效处理变长序列，（3）引入 mixture-of-experts 组件，将不同的行为模式自适应地路由到专门的专家，从而同时处理聚焦的类目内浏览和多样的类目探索模式。我们从理论上证明了状态空间模型与 attention 机制如何在推荐场景中自然统一，其中 SSM 通过状态压缩捕捉时间动态，而 attention 对相似与多样的 item 关系进行建模。在四个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，STAR-Rec 始终优于 state-of-the-art 的序列推荐方法，尤其是在涉及多样用户行为和不同序列长度的场景中。
Towards Large-scale Generative Ranking
小红书2025推荐工业生成式检索 / 排序方法generative ranking, generative architecture, industrial-scale recommendation, GenRankPDF
AuthorsYanhua Huang, Yuqi Chen, Xiong Cao, Rui Yang, Mingliang Qi, Yinghao Zhu, Qingchang Han, Yaowei Liu, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Generative recommendation has recently emerged as a promising paradigm in information retrieval. However, generative ranking systems are still understudied, particularly with respect to their effectiveness and feasibility in large-scale industrial settings. This paper investigates this topic at the ranking stage of Xiaohongshu’s Explore Feed, a recommender system that serves hundreds of millions of users. Specifically, we first examine how generative ranking outperforms current industrial recommenders. Through theoretical and empirical analyses, we find that the primary improvement in effectiveness stems from the generative architecture, rather than the training paradigm. To facilitate efficient deployment of generative ranking, we introduce GenRank, a novel generative architecture for ranking. We validate the effectiveness and efficiency of our solution through online A/B experiments. The results show that GenRank achieves significant improvements in user satisfaction with nearly equivalent computational resources compared to the existing production system.
