生成式推荐近来已成为信息检索中一个有前景的范式。然而，生成式 ranking 系统仍研究不足，尤其是其在大规模工业场景中的有效性与可行性。本文在小红书探索信息流的 ranking 阶段研究这一课题，该推荐系统服务数亿用户。具体而言，我们首先考察生成式 ranking 如何优于当前的工业推荐器。通过理论与实证分析，我们发现有效性的主要提升源自生成式架构本身，而非训练范式。为促进生成式 ranking 的高效部署，我们提出 GenRank，一种新颖的面向 ranking 的生成式架构。我们通过在线 A/B 实验验证了方案的有效性与效率。结果表明，与现有生产系统相比，GenRank 在几乎同等的计算资源下实现了用户满意度的显著提升。
Action is All You Need: Dual-Flow Generative Ranking Network for Recommendation
美团2025推荐工业生成式检索 / 排序方法generative ranking, dual-flow mechanism, HSTU, user behavior sequences, scaling lawPDF
AuthorsHao Guo, Erpeng Xue, Lei Huang, Shichao Wang, Xiaolei Wang, Lei Wang, Jinpeng Wang, Sheng Chen
Abstract (EN)
Deep Learning Recommendation Models (DLRMs) often rely on extensive manual feature engineering to improve accuracy and user experience, which increases system complexity and limits scalability of model performance with respect to computational resources. Recently, Meta introduced a generative ranking paradigm based on HSTU block that enables end-to-end learning from raw user behavior sequences and demonstrates scaling law on large datasets that can be regarded as the state-of-the-art (SOTA). However, splitting user behaviors into interleaved item and action information significantly increases the input sequence length, which adversely affects both training and inference efficiency. To address this issue, we propose the Dual-Flow Generative Ranking Network (DFGR), that employs a dual-flow mechanism to optimize interaction modeling, ensuring efficient training and inference through end-to-end token processing. DFGR duplicates the original user behavior sequence into a real flow and a fake flow based on the authenticity of the action information, and then defines a novel interaction method between the real flow and the fake flow within the QKV module of the self-attention mechanism. This design reduces computational overhead and improves both training efficiency and inference performance compared to Meta's HSTU-based model. Experiments on both open-source and real industrial datasets show that DFGR outperforms DLRM, which serves as the industrial online baseline with extensive feature engineering, as well as Meta's HSTU and other common recommendation models such as DIN, DCN, DIEN, and DeepFM. Furthermore, we investigate optimal parameter allocation strategies under computational constraints, establishing DFGR as an efficient and effective next-generation generative ranking paradigm.
摘要 (中文)
Deep Learning Recommendation Models (DLRMs) 通常依赖大量人工特征工程来提升准确率和用户体验，这增加了系统复杂度，并限制了模型性能随计算资源扩展的能力。最近，Meta 提出了基于 HSTU block 的生成式排序范式，能够从原始用户行为序列进行端到端学习，并在大规模数据集上展现出 scaling law，可视为 state-of-the-art (SOTA)。然而，将用户行为拆分为交错的物品信息和行为信息会显著增加输入序列长度，从而对训练和推理效率造成不利影响。为解决这一问题，我们提出双流生成式排序网络（Dual-Flow Generative Ranking Network, DFGR），采用双流机制优化交互建模，通过端到端 token 处理确保高效的训练与推理。DFGR 根据行为信息的真实性将原始用户行为序列复制为 real flow 和 fake flow，并在 self-attention 机制的 QKV 模块中定义了 real flow 与 fake flow 之间一种新颖的交互方式。与 Meta 基于 HSTU 的模型相比，该设计降低了计算开销，并提升了训练效率和推理性能。在开源数据集和真实工业数据集上的实验表明，DFGR 优于作为工业在线 baseline（带有大量特征工程）的 DLRM，也优于 Meta 的 HSTU 以及 DIN、DCN、DIEN、DeepFM 等其他常见推荐模型。此外，我们研究了计算约束下的最优参数分配策略，确立了 DFGR 作为一种高效且有效的下一代生成式排序范式。
Leveraging Passage Embeddings for Efficient Listwise Reranking with Large Language Models
人大2025搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法passage reranking, listwise reranking, LLM, passage embeddings, context compressionPDF · arXiv
AuthorsQi Liu, Bo Wang, Nan Wang, Jiaxin Mao
Abstract (EN)
Recent studies have demonstrated the effectiveness of using large language language models (LLMs) in passage ranking. The listwise approaches, such as RankGPT, have become new state-of-the-art in this task. However, the efficiency of RankGPT models is limited by the maximum context length and relatively high latency of LLM inference. To address these issues, in this paper, we propose PERank, leveraging the single passage embedding as a good context compression for efficient listwise passage reranking. By treating each passage as a special token, we can directly input passage embeddings into LLMs, thereby reducing input length. Additionally, we introduce an inference method that dynamically constrains the decoding space to these special tokens, accelerating the decoding process. For adapting the model to reranking, we employ listwise learning to rank loss for training. Evaluation results on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that PE-Rank significantly improves efficiency in both prefilling and decoding, while maintaining competitive ranking effectiveness.1
摘要 (中文)
近期研究已表明，将大语言模型（LLM）用于段落排序是有效的。诸如 RankGPT 之类的 listwise 方法已成为该任务上新的 state-of-the-art。然而，RankGPT 模型的效率受限于最大上下文长度以及 LLM 推理相对较高的延迟。为解决这些问题，本文提出 PE-Rank，利用单个段落 embedding 作为一种良好的上下文压缩，以实现高效的 listwise 段落 reranking。通过将每个段落视为一个特殊 token，我们可以直接把段落 embedding 输入 LLM，从而缩短输入长度。此外，我们提出一种推理方法，将解码空间动态约束到这些特殊 token 上，从而加速解码过程。为使模型适配 reranking，我们采用 listwise learning to rank 损失进行训练。在多个 benchmark 上的评估结果表明，PE-Rank 在 prefilling 与 decoding 两个阶段都显著提升了效率，同时保持了有竞争力的排序效果。
Towards Context-aware Reasoning-enhanced Generative Searching in E-commerce
USTC / 快手2025搜索工业生成式检索 / 排序方法generative search, context-aware recommendation, reasoning-enhanced recommendation, self-evolving post-training, debiased GRPOPDF · arXiv
AuthorsZhiding Liu, Ben Chen, Mingyue Cheng, Enhong Chen, Li Li, Chenyi Lei, Wenwu Ou, Han Li, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Search-based recommendation is one of the most critical application scenarios in e-commerce platforms. Users' complex search contexts--such as spatiotemporal factors, historical interactions, and current query's information--constitute an essential part of their decision-making, reflecting implicit preferences that complement explicit query terms. Modeling such rich contextual signals and their intricate associations with candidate items remains a key challenge. Although numerous efforts have been devoted to building more effective search methods, existing approaches still show limitations in integrating contextual information, which hinders their ability to fully capture user intent. To address these challenges, we propose a context-aware reasoning-enhanced generative search framework for better \textbf{understanding the complicated context}. Specifically, the framework first unifies heterogeneous user and item contexts into textual representations or text-based semantic identifiers and aligns them. To overcome the lack of explicit reasoning trajectories, we introduce a self-evolving post-training paradigm that iteratively combines supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning to progressively enhance the model's reasoning capability. In addition, we identify potential biases in existing RL algorithms when applied to search scenarios and present a debiased variant of GRPO to improve ranking performance. Extensive experiments on search log data collected from a real-world e-commerce platform demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance compared with strong baselines, validating its effectiveness for search-based recommendation.

Constrained Auto-Regressive Decoding Constrains Generative Retrieval
山东大学 / Leiden University2025搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, constrained auto-regressive decoding, beam search, generalization, out-of-distribution corporaPDF · arXiv
AuthorsShiguang Wu, Zhaochun Ren, Xin Xin, Jiyuan Yang, Mengqi Zhang, Zhumin Chen, Maarten de Rijke, Pengjie Ren
Abstract (EN)
Generative retrieval seeks to replace traditional search index data structures with a single large-scale neural network, offering the potential for improved efficiency and seamless integration with generative large language models. As an end-to-end paradigm, generative retrieval adopts a learned differentiable search index to conduct retrieval by directly generating document identifiers through corpus-specific constrained decoding. The generalization capabilities of generative retrieval on out-of-distribution corpora have gathered significant attention. In this paper, we examine the inherent limitations of constrained auto-regressive generation from two essential perspectives: constraints and beam search. We begin with the Bayes-optimal setting where the generative retrieval model exactly captures the underlying relevance distribution of all possible documents. Then we apply the model to specific corpora by simply adding corpus-specific constraints. Our main findings are two-fold: (i) For the effect of constraints, we derive a lower bound of the error, in terms of the KL divergence between the ground-truth and the model-predicted step-wise marginal distributions. (ii) For the beam search algorithm used during generation, we reveal that the usage of marginal distributions may not be an ideal approach. This paper aims to improve our theoretical understanding of the generalization capabilities of the auto-regressive decoding retrieval paradigm, laying a foundation for its limitations and inspiring future advancements toward more robust and generalizable generative retrieval.
摘要 (中文)
生成式检索试图用单个大规模神经网络取代传统搜索索引数据结构，有望提升效率并与生成式大语言模型无缝集成。作为一种端到端范式，生成式检索采用可学习的可微搜索索引，通过语料特定的受限解码直接生成文档标识符来执行检索。生成式检索在分布外语料上的泛化能力已引起广泛关注。本文从两个基本视角考察受限自回归生成的固有局限：约束与 beam search。我们首先从 Bayes 最优设定出发，即生成式检索模型精确捕获所有可能文档的底层相关性分布。随后我们通过简单添加语料特定约束将该模型应用于特定语料。我们的主要发现有两方面：(i) 就约束的影响而言，我们推导出误差下界，其以真实分布与模型预测的逐步边缘分布之间的 KL 散度表示。(ii) 就生成过程中使用的 beam search 算法而言，我们揭示使用边缘分布可能并非理想做法。本文旨在加深对自回归解码检索范式泛化能力的理论理解，为其局限奠定基础，并启发未来朝向更稳健、更可泛化的生成式检索的进展。
ZeroGR: A Generalizable and Scalable Framework for Zero-Shot Generative Retrieval
CMU / 山东大学2025搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法zero-shot generative retrieval, docid generation, instruction tuning, reverse annealing decoding, information retrievalPDF · arXiv
AuthorsWeiwei Sun, Keyi Kong, Xinyu Ma, Shuaiqiang Wang, Dawei Yin, Maarten de Rijke, Zhaochun Ren, Yiming Yang
Abstract (EN)
Generative retrieval (GR) reformulates information retrieval (IR) by framing it as the generation of document identifiers (docids), thereby enabling end-to-end optimization and seamless integration with generative language models (LMs). Despite notable progress under supervised training, GR still struggles to generalize to zero-shot IR scenarios, which are prevalent in real-world applications. To tackle this challenge, we propose ZeroGR, a zero-shot generative retrieval framework that uses natural language instructions to extend GR across a wide range of IR tasks. Specifically, ZeroGR is composed of three key components: (i) an LM-based docid generator that unifies heterogeneous documents (e.g., text, tables, code) into semantically meaningful docids; (ii) an instruction-tuned query generator that generates diverse types of queries from natural language task descriptions to enhance corpus indexing; and (iii) a reverse annealing decoding strategy to balance precision and recall during docid generation. Furthermore, we introduce OpenInstIR, the most diverse open-source instructed retrieval dataset. We investigate the impact of instruction fine-tuning scale and find that performance consistently improves as the number of IR tasks encountered during training increases. Extensive experiments on the BEIR and MAIR benchmarks demonstrate that ZeroGR achieves competitive performance across a wide range of retrieval tasks, establishing a new state-of-the-art among GR methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunnweiwei/ZeroGR.
摘要 (中文)
生成式检索（GR）通过将信息检索（IR）表述为文档标识符（docid）的生成来重构 IR，从而实现端到端优化并与生成式语言模型（LM）无缝集成。尽管在监督训练下取得了显著进展，GR 仍难以泛化到现实应用中普遍存在的零样本 IR 场景。为应对这一挑战，我们提出 ZeroGR，一个零样本生成式检索框架，它使用自然语言指令将 GR 扩展到广泛的 IR 任务。具体而言，ZeroGR 由三个关键组件构成：(i) 一个基于 LM 的 docid 生成器，将异构文档（如文本、表格、代码）统一为语义上有意义的 docid；(ii) 一个指令微调的 query 生成器，从自然语言任务描述生成多样类型的 query，以增强语料索引；(iii) 一种反向退火解码策略，在 docid 生成过程中平衡精度与召回。此外，我们引入 OpenInstIR，即最多样化的开源指令式检索数据集。我们研究了指令微调规模的影响，发现随着训练中遇到的 IR 任务数量增加，性能持续提升。在 BEIR 与 MAIR 基准上的大量实验表明，ZeroGR 在广泛的检索任务上取得有竞争力的性能，在 GR 方法中确立了新的 state-of-the-art。我们的代码见 https://github.com/sunnweiwei/ZeroGR。
Lightweight and Direct Document Relevance Optimization for Generative Information Retrieval
Univ. of Amsterdam2025搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法generative information retrieval, document relevance optimization, pairwise ranking, token-level misalignment, docid generationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsKidist Amde Mekonnen, Yubao Tang, Maarten de Rijke
Abstract (EN)
Generative information retrieval (GenIR) is a promising neural retrieval paradigm that formulates document retrieval as a document identifier (docid) generation task, allowing for end-to-end optimization toward a unified global retrieval objective. However, existing GenIR models suffer from token-level misalignment, where models trained to predict the next token often fail to capture document-level relevance effectively. While reinforcement learning-based methods, such as reinforcement learning from relevance feedback (RLRF), aim to address this misalignment through reward modeling, they introduce significant complexity, requiring the optimization of an auxiliary reward function followed by reinforcement fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive and often unstable. To address these challenges, we propose direct document relevance optimization (DDRO), which aligns token-level docid generation with document-level relevance estimation through direct optimization via pairwise ranking, eliminating the need for explicit reward modeling and reinforcement learning. Experimental results on benchmark datasets, including MS MARCO document and Natural Questions, show that DDRO outperforms reinforcement learning-based methods, achieving a 7.4% improvement in MRR@10 for MS MARCO and a 19.9% improvement for Natural Questions. These findings highlight DDRO's potential to enhance retrieval effectiveness with a simplified optimization approach. By framing alignment as a direct optimization problem, DDRO simplifies the ranking optimization pipeline of GenIR models while offering a viable alternative to reinforcement learning-based methods.
摘要 (中文)
生成式信息检索（GenIR）是一种有前景的神经检索范式，它将文档检索表述为文档标识符（docid）生成任务，从而能够朝统一的全局检索目标进行端到端优化。然而，现有 GenIR 模型存在 token 级错位问题，即被训练用于预测下一 token 的模型往往无法有效捕获文档级相关性。基于强化学习的方法，如 reinforcement learning from relevance feedback（RLRF），旨在通过奖励建模来解决这种错位，但它们引入了显著的复杂性，需要先优化辅助奖励函数再进行强化微调，这计算昂贵且常常不稳定。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 direct document relevance optimization（DDRO），它通过成对排序的直接优化，使 token 级 docid 生成与文档级相关性估计对齐，从而无需显式的奖励建模与强化学习。在包括 MS MARCO document 与 Natural Questions 在内的基准数据集上的实验结果表明，DDRO 优于基于强化学习的方法，在 MS MARCO 上 MRR@10 提升 7.4%，在 Natural Questions 上提升 19.9%。这些发现凸显了 DDRO 以简化优化方法提升检索效果的潜力。通过将对齐表述为直接优化问题，DDRO 简化了 GenIR 模型的排序优化流水线，同时提供了基于强化学习方法的可行替代方案。
Replication and Exploration of Generative Retrieval over Dynamic Corpora
山东大学 / 百度2025搜索工业生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, dynamic corpora, docid design, text-based docids, multi-docidPDF · arXiv
AuthorsZhen Zhang, Xinyu Ma, Weiwei Sun, Pengjie Ren, Zhumin Chen, Shuaiqiang Wang, Dawei Yin, Maarten de Rijke, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Generative retrieval (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm in information retrieval (IR). However, most existing GR models are developed and evaluated using a static document collection, and their performance in dynamic corpora where document collections evolve continuously is rarely studied. In this paper, we first reproduce and systematically evaluate various representative GR approaches over dynamic corpora. Through extensive experiments, we reveal that existing GR models with \textit{text-based} docids show superior generalization to unseen documents. We observe that the more fine-grained the docid design in the GR model, the better its performance over dynamic corpora, surpassing BM25 and even being comparable to dense retrieval methods. While GR models with \textit{numeric-based} docids show high efficiency, their performance drops significantly over dynamic corpora. Furthermore, our experiments find that the underperformance of numeric-based docids is partly due to their excessive tendency toward the initial document set, which likely results from overfitting on the training set. We then conduct an in-depth analysis of the best-performing GR methods. We identify three critical advantages of text-based docids in dynamic corpora: (i) Semantic alignment with language models' pretrained knowledge, (ii) Fine-grained docid design, and (iii) High lexical diversity. Building on these insights, we finally propose a novel multi-docid design that leverages both the efficiency of numeric-based docids and the effectiveness of text-based docids, achieving improved performance in dynamic corpus without requiring additional retraining. Our work offers empirical evidence for advancing GR methods over dynamic corpora and paves the way for developing more generalized yet efficient GR models in real-world search engines.
摘要 (中文)
生成式检索（GR）已成为信息检索（IR）中一个颇具前景的范式。然而，大多数现有 GR 模型是在静态文档集合上开发和评估的，其在文档集合持续演化的动态语料上的性能鲜有研究。本文中，我们首先在动态语料上复现并系统评估了各类有代表性的 GR 方法。通过大量实验，我们揭示出使用 \textit{基于文本的} docid 的现有 GR 模型对未见文档表现出更优的泛化能力。我们观察到，GR 模型中 docid 设计越细粒度，其在动态语料上的性能就越好，超越了 BM25，甚至可与稠密检索方法相媲美。而使用 \textit{基于数字的} docid 的 GR 模型虽然效率高，但在动态语料上性能显著下降。此外，我们的实验发现，基于数字的 docid 表现不佳部分源于其过度倾向于初始文档集合，这很可能由在训练集上的过拟合导致。随后，我们对表现最佳的 GR 方法进行了深入分析。我们识别出基于文本的 docid 在动态语料中的三个关键优势：(i) 与语言模型预训练知识的语义对齐，(ii) 细粒度的 docid 设计，以及 (iii) 高词法多样性。基于这些洞见，我们最终提出了一种新颖的多 docid 设计，兼顾基于数字的 docid 的效率与基于文本的 docid 的有效性，在无需额外重训的情况下于动态语料中取得更优性能。我们的工作为推动动态语料上的 GR 方法提供了实证依据，并为在真实搜索引擎中开发更泛化且更高效的 GR 模型铺平了道路。
ReasonRank: Empowering Passage Ranking with Strong Reasoning Ability
人大 / 百度2025搜索工业生成式检索 / 排序方法passage ranking, listwise ranking, reasoning-intensive, reinforcement learning, training data synthesisPDF · arXiv
AuthorsWenhan Liu, Xinyu Ma, Weiwei Sun, Yutao Zhu, Yuchen Li, Dawei Yin, Zhicheng Dou
Abstract (EN)
Large Language Model (LLM) based listwise ranking has shown superior performance in many passage ranking tasks. With the development of Large Reasoning Models (LRMs), many studies have demonstrated that step-by-step reasoning during test-time helps improve listwise ranking performance. However, due to the scarcity of reasoning-intensive training data, existing rerankers perform poorly in many complex ranking scenarios, and the ranking ability of reasoning-intensive rerankers remains largely underdeveloped. In this paper, we first propose an automated reasoning-intensive training data synthesis framework, which sources training queries and passages from diverse domains and applies DeepSeek-R1 to generate high-quality training labels. To empower the listwise reranker with strong reasoning ability, we further propose a two-stage training approach, which includes a cold-start supervised fine-tuning (SFT) stage and a reinforcement learning (RL) stage. During the RL stage, we design a novel multi-view ranking reward tailored to the multi-turn nature of listwise ranking. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our trained reasoning-intensive reranker \textbf{ReasonRank} outperforms existing baselines significantly and also achieves much lower latency than the pointwise reranker. Our codes are available at https://github.com/8421BCD/ReasonRank.
摘要 (中文)
基于大语言模型（LLM）的 listwise 排序在许多 passage 排序任务中展现出优越的性能。随着大推理模型（LRM）的发展，许多研究表明，在测试时进行逐步推理有助于提升 listwise 排序性能。然而，由于推理密集型训练数据的稀缺，现有 reranker 在许多复杂排序场景中表现不佳，推理密集型 reranker 的排序能力也仍未得到充分发展。在本文中，我们首先提出了一个自动化的推理密集型训练数据合成框架，它从多个领域采集训练 query 和 passage，并应用 DeepSeek-R1 生成高质量的训练标签。为使 listwise reranker 具备强大的推理能力，我们进一步提出了一种两阶段训练方法，包括冷启动监督微调（SFT）阶段和强化学习（RL）阶段。在 RL 阶段，我们针对 listwise 排序的多轮特性设计了一种新颖的多视角排序奖励。大量实验表明，我们训练出的推理密集型 reranker \textbf{ReasonRank} 显著优于现有 baseline，并且相比 pointwise reranker 实现了低得多的延迟。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/8421BCD/ReasonRank 获取。
Exploring Training and Inference Scaling Laws in Generative Retrieval
新加坡国立 / 港理工2025搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, scaling law, inference-time compute, contrastive entropy, n-gramPDF · arXiv
AuthorsHongru Cai, Yongqi Li, Ruifeng Yuan, Wenjie Wang, Zhen Zhang, Wenjie Li, Tat-Seng Chua
Abstract (EN)
Generative retrieval reformulates retrieval as an autoregressive generation task, where large language models (LLMs) generate target documents directly from a query. As a novel paradigm, the mechanisms that underpin its performance and scalability remain largely unexplored. We systematically investigate training and inference scaling laws in generative retrieval, exploring how model size, training data scale, and inference-time compute jointly influence performance. We propose a novel evaluation metric inspired by contrastive entropy and generation loss, providing a continuous performance signal that enables robust comparisons across diverse generative retrieval methods. Our experiments show that n-gram-based methods align strongly with training and inference scaling laws. We find that increasing model size, training data scale, and inference-time compute all contribute to improved performance, highlighting the complementary roles of these factors in enhancing generative retrieval. Across these settings, LLaMA models consistently outperform T5 models, suggesting a particular advantage for larger decoder-only models in generative retrieval. Our findings underscore that model sizes, data availability, and inference computation interact to unlock the full potential of generative retrieval, offering new insights for designing and optimizing future systems.
摘要 (中文)
生成式检索将检索重新表述为自回归生成任务，由大语言模型（LLM）直接根据 query 生成目标文档。作为一种新范式，支撑其性能与可扩展性的机制在很大程度上仍未被探索。我们系统地研究了生成式检索中的训练与推理 scaling laws，探究模型规模、训练数据规模以及推理时计算量如何共同影响性能。我们受对比熵与生成损失启发提出了一种新的评测指标，提供连续的性能信号，从而能够在多样的生成式检索方法之间进行稳健的比较。我们的实验表明，基于 n-gram 的方法与训练和推理 scaling laws 高度吻合。我们发现，增大模型规模、训练数据规模和推理时计算量都有助于提升性能，凸显了这些因素在增强生成式检索中的互补作用。在这些设定下，LLaMA 模型始终优于 T5 模型，表明更大的 decoder-only 模型在生成式检索中具有独特优势。我们的发现强调，模型规模、数据可用性与推理计算量相互作用，才能释放生成式检索的全部潜力，为设计和优化未来系统提供了新的洞见。
Alleviating LLM-based Generative Retrieval Hallucination in Alipay Search
上海交大 / 蚂蚁2025搜索工业生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, hallucination, knowledge distillation, decision agent, LLMPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYedan Shen, Kaixin Wu, Yuechen Ding, Jingyuan Wen, Hong Liu, Mingjie Zhong, Zhouhan Lin, Jia Xu, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Generative retrieval (GR) has revolutionized document retrieval with the advent of large language models (LLMs), and LLM-based GR is gradually being adopted by the industry. Despite its remarkable advantages and potential, LLM-based GR suffers from hallucination and generates documents that are irrelevant to the query in some instances, severely challenging its credibility in practical applications. We thereby propose an optimized GR framework designed to alleviate retrieval hallucination, which integrates knowledge distillation reasoning in model training and incorporate decision agent to further improve retrieval precision. Specifically, we employ LLMs to assess and reason GR retrieved query-document (q-d) pairs, and then distill the reasoning data as transferred knowledge to the GR model. Moreover, we utilize a decision agent as post-processing to extend the GR retrieved documents through retrieval model and select the most relevant ones from multi perspectives as the final generative retrieval result. Extensive offline experiments on real-world datasets and online A/B tests on Fund Search and Insurance Search in Alipay demonstrate our framework's superiority and effectiveness in improving search quality and conversion gains.
摘要 (中文)
随着大语言模型（LLM）的出现，生成式检索（GR）彻底改变了文档检索，基于 LLM 的 GR 正逐渐被工业界采用。尽管具有显著优势和潜力，基于 LLM 的 GR 存在幻觉问题，在某些情形下会生成与 query 无关的文档，严重挑战其在实际应用中的可信度。因此，我们提出一个优化的 GR 框架以缓解检索幻觉，该框架在模型训练中整合了知识蒸馏推理，并引入决策 agent 以进一步提升检索精度。具体而言，我们使用 LLM 对 GR 检索到的 query-document（q-d）对进行评估和推理，然后将推理数据作为迁移知识蒸馏到 GR 模型中。此外，我们利用决策 agent 作为后处理，通过检索模型扩展 GR 检索到的文档，并从多个视角选出最相关的文档作为最终的生成式检索结果。在真实数据集上的大量离线实验以及在支付宝基金搜索和保险搜索上的线上 A/B 测试，证明了我们的框架在提升搜索质量和转化收益方面的优越性与有效性。
Generative Retrieval and Alignment Model: A New Paradigm for E-commerce Retrieval
京东2025搜索工业生成式检索 / 排序方法e-commerce retrieval, generative retrieval, text identifier, co-alignment, query-product scoringPDF · arXiv
AuthorsMing Pang, Chunyuan Yuan, Xiaoyu He, Zheng Fang, Donghao Xie, Fanyi Qu, Xue Jiang, Changping Peng, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Traditional sparse and dense retrieval methods struggle to leverage general world knowledge and often fail to capture the nuanced features of queries and products. With the advent of large language models (LLMs), industrial search systems have started to employ LLMs to generate identifiers for product retrieval. Commonly used identifiers include (1) static/semantic IDs and (2) product term sets. The first approach requires creating a product ID system from scratch, missing out on the world knowledge embedded within LLMs. While the second approach leverages this general knowledge, the significant difference in word distribution between queries and products means that product-based identifiers often do not align well with user search queries, leading to missed product recalls. Furthermore, when queries contain numerous attributes, these algorithms generate a large number of identifiers, making it difficult to assess their quality, which results in low overall recall efficiency. To address these challenges, this paper introduces a novel e-commerce retrieval paradigm: the Generative Retrieval and Alignment Model (GRAM). GRAM employs joint training on text information from both queries and products to generate shared text identifier codes, effectively bridging the gap between queries and products. This approach not only enhances the connection between queries and products but also improves inference efficiency. The model uses a co-alignment strategy to generate codes optimized for maximizing retrieval efficiency. Additionally, it introduces a query-product scoring mechanism to compare product values across different codes, further boosting retrieval efficiency. Extensive offline and online A/B testing demonstrates that GRAM significantly outperforms traditional models and the latest generative retrieval models, confirming its effectiveness and practicality.
摘要 (中文)
传统的稀疏与稠密检索方法难以利用通用世界知识，且往往无法捕捉 query 与商品的细微特征。随着大语言模型（LLM）的出现，工业搜索系统开始采用 LLM 为商品检索生成标识符。常用的标识符包括（1）静态/语义 ID 和（2）商品词集合。第一种方法需要从零构建商品 ID 体系，从而错失了 LLM 内嵌的世界知识。而第二种方法虽利用了这种通用知识，但 query 与商品之间词分布差异显著，导致基于商品的标识符往往无法与用户搜索 query 良好对齐，造成商品漏召回。此外，当 query 包含大量属性时，这类算法会生成大量标识符，使其质量难以评估，从而导致整体召回效率低下。为应对这些挑战，本文提出一种新的电商检索范式：Generative Retrieval and Alignment Model（GRAM）。GRAM 对 query 与商品的文本信息进行联合训练以生成共享的文本标识符 code，有效弥合 query 与商品之间的鸿沟。该方法不仅增强了 query 与商品之间的关联，还提升了推理效率。模型采用共对齐策略生成以最大化检索效率为优化目标的 code。此外，它引入 query-product 打分机制，在不同 code 之间比较商品价值，进一步提升检索效率。大量离线与线上 A/B 测试表明，GRAM 显著优于传统模型以及最新的生成式检索模型，验证了其有效性与实用性。
AcuRank: Uncertainty-Aware Adaptive Computation for Listwise Reranking
Seoul National University / UCSB2025搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法listwise reranking, uncertainty-aware, adaptive computation, Bayesian TrueSkill, accuracy-efficiency trade-offPDF · arXiv
AuthorsSoyoung Yoon, Gyuwan Kim, Gyu-Hwung Cho, Seung-won Hwang
Abstract (EN)
Listwise reranking with large language models (LLMs) enhances top-ranked results in retrieval-based applications. Due to the limit in context size and high inference cost of long context, reranking is typically performed over a fixed size of small subsets, with the final ranking aggregated from these partial results. This fixed computation disregards query difficulty and document distribution, leading to inefficiencies. We propose AcuRank, an adaptive reranking framework that dynamically adjusts both the amount and target of computation based on uncertainty estimates over document relevance. Using a Bayesian TrueSkill model, we iteratively refine relevance estimates until reaching sufficient confidence levels, and our explicit modeling of ranking uncertainty enables principled control over reranking behavior and avoids unnecessary updates to confident predictions. Results on the TREC-DL and BEIR benchmarks show that our method consistently achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off and scales better with compute than fixed-computation baselines. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of our method across diverse retrieval tasks and LLM-based reranking models.
摘要 (中文)
基于大语言模型（LLM）的 listwise reranking 能提升基于检索应用的顶部排序结果。由于上下文长度限制和长上下文的高推理成本，reranking 通常在固定大小的小子集上进行，最终排序由这些局部结果聚合而成。这种固定计算量忽视了 query 难度和文档分布情况，导致效率低下。我们提出 AcuRank，一种自适应 reranking 框架，它基于对文档相关性的不确定性估计来动态调整计算量和计算目标。借助贝叶斯 TrueSkill 模型，我们迭代地细化相关性估计，直至达到足够的置信水平；我们对排序不确定性的显式建模使得能够对 reranking 行为进行有原则的控制，并避免对高置信预测进行不必要的更新。在 TREC-DL 和 BEIR 基准上的结果表明，我们的方法始终取得更优的精度-效率权衡，并且相比固定计算量的 baseline 具有更好的计算可扩展性。这些结果凸显了我们的方法在多样检索任务和基于 LLM 的 reranking 模型上的有效性与泛化性。
SLMRec: Distilling Large Language Models into Small for Sequential Recommendation
Rutgers2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐knowledge distillation, small language models, sequential recommendation, layer redundancy, model compressionPDF · arXiv
AuthorsWujiang Xu, Qitian Wu, Zujie Liang, Jiaojiao Han, Xuying Ning, Yunxiao Shi, Wenfang Lin, Yongfeng Zhang
Abstract (EN)
Sequential Recommendation (SR) task involves predicting the next item a user is likely to interact with, given their past interactions. The SR models examine the sequence of a user’s actions to discern more complex behavioral patterns and temporal dynamics. Recent research demonstrates the great impact of LLMs on sequential recommendation systems, either viewing sequential recommendation as language modeling or serving as the backbone for user representation. Although these methods deliver outstanding performance, there is scant evidence of the necessity of a large language model and how large the language model is needed, especially in the sequential recommendation scene. Meanwhile, due to the huge size of LLMs, it is inefficient and impractical to apply a LLM-based model in realworld platforms that often need to process billions of traffic logs daily. In this paper, we explore the influence of LLMs’ depth by conducting extensive experiments on large-scale industry datasets. Surprisingly, our motivational experiments reveal that most intermediate layers of LLMs are redundant, indicating that pruning the remaining layers can still maintain strong performance. Motivated by this insight, we empower small language models for SR, namely SLMR EC, which adopt a simple yet effective knowledge distillation method. Moreover, SLMR EC is orthogonal to other post-training efficiency techniques, such as quantization and pruning, so that they can be leveraged in combination. Comprehensive experimental results illustrate that the proposed SLMR EC model attains the best performance using only 13% of the parameters found in LLM-based recommendation models, while simultaneously achieving up to 6.6x and 8.0x speedups in training and inference time costs, respectively. Besides, we provide a theoretical justification for why small language models can perform comparably to large language models in SR. The source code and datasets are available at the URL 1 .
摘要 (中文)
序列推荐（SR）任务是指在给定用户过去交互的情况下，预测其可能交互的下一个物品。SR 模型通过考察用户的行为序列来识别更复杂的行为模式和时间动态。近期研究表明 LLM 对序列推荐系统有重大影响，无论是将序列推荐视为语言建模，还是将其作为用户表示的 backbone。尽管这些方法性能出色，但对于大语言模型的必要性以及需要多大的语言模型，尤其是在序列推荐场景中，尚缺乏充分证据。同时，由于 LLM 规模巨大，在通常需要每天处理数十亿条流量日志的真实平台中，应用基于 LLM 的模型既低效也不现实。本文通过在大规模工业数据集上进行大量实验，探究了 LLM 深度的影响。令人惊讶的是，我们的动机性实验揭示 LLM 的大多数中间层是冗余的，这表明剪除其余层仍能保持强劲性能。受此启发，我们为 SR 赋能小语言模型，即 SLMRec，采用一种简单却有效的知识蒸馏方法。此外，SLMRec 与其他训练后效率技术（如量化和剪枝）是正交的，因此可以结合使用。全面的实验结果表明，所提出的 SLMRec 模型仅使用基于 LLM 的推荐模型 13% 的参数就达到了最佳性能，同时在训练和推理时间成本上分别实现了最高 6.6 倍和 8.0 倍的加速。此外，我们从理论上论证了为什么小语言模型在 SR 中能够与大语言模型表现相当。源代码和数据集见文中所给 URL。
EAGER-LLM: Enhancing Large Language Models as Recommenders through Exogenous Behavior-Semantic Integration
浙大2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐generative recommendation, LLM-based recommender, dual-source item indices, multiscale alignment, annealing adapterPDF · arXiv
AuthorsMinjie Hong, Yan Xia, Zehan Wang, Jieming Zhu, Ye Wang, Sihang Cai, Xiaoda Yang, Quanyu Dai, Zhenhua Dong
Abstract (EN)
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly leveraged as foundational backbones in the development of advanced recommender systems, offering enhanced capabilities through their extensive knowledge and reasoning. Existing llm-based recommender systems (RSs) often face challenges due to the significant differences between the linguistic semantics of pre-trained LLMs and the collaborative semantics essential for RSs. These systems use pre-trained linguistic semantics but learn collaborative semantics from scratch via the llm-Backbone. However, LLMs are not designed for recommendations, leading to inefficient collaborative learning, weak result correlations, and poor integration of traditional RS features. To address these challenges, we propose EAGER-LLM, a decoder-only llm-based generative recommendation framework that integrates endogenous and exogenous behavioral and semantic information in a non-intrusive manner. Specifically, we propose 1) dual-source knowledge-rich item indices that integrates indexing sequences ∗ Both authors contributed equally to this research. † Project Lead. ‡ Corresponding Author. 摘要 (中文)
大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地被用作构建先进推荐系统的基础骨干，凭借其广泛的知识和推理能力提供更强的能力。现有基于 LLM 的推荐系统（RSs）常常面临挑战，原因在于预训练 LLMs 的语言语义与 RSs 所需的协同语义之间存在显著差异。这些系统使用预训练的语言语义，但通过 llm-Backbone 从零学习协同语义。然而，LLMs 并非为推荐而设计，导致协同学习效率低下、结果相关性弱，以及传统 RS 特征融合不佳。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 EAGER-LLM，一个 decoder-only 的基于 llm 的生成式推荐框架，以非侵入式（non-intrusive）方式整合内生与外生的行为及语义信息。具体而言，我们提出 1）双源知识丰富型 item indices，它整合索引序列 ∗ 两位作者对本研究贡献相同。† Project Lead。‡ 通讯作者。允许出于个人或课堂教学目的，对本作品的全部或部分内容进行数字或硬拷贝复制，无需付费，前提是复制件不得用于盈利或商业优势，且复制件须在首页保留本声明以及完整的引用信息。本作品中由作者以外他人拥有的版权必须予以尊重。允许在注明出处的前提下进行摘要引用。以其他方式复制、重新发布、发布到服务器或转发至列表，需事先获得特别许可和/或支付费用。许可申请请联系 permissions@acm.org。WWW '25, Sydney, NSW, Australia © 2025 版权归作者/所有者持有。出版权已授权给 ACM。ACM ISBN 979-8-4007-1274-6/25/04 https://doi.org/10.1145/3696410.3714933 与外生信号，从而实现高效的 link-wide 处理；2）非侵入式多尺度对齐重建任务，引导模型更深入地理解协同信号与语义信号；3）一个退火适配器（annealing adapter），用于精细平衡模型的推荐性能与理解能力。我们通过在三个公开基准上的严格测试证明了 EAGER-LLM 的有效性。
AlphaFuse: Learn ID Embeddings for Sequential Recommendation in Null Space of Language Embeddings
USTC / 新加坡国立2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐sequential recommendation, ID embeddings, language embeddings, null space, singular value decompositionPDF · arXiv
AuthorsGuoqing Hu, An Zhang, Shuo Liu, Zhibo Cai, Xun Yang, Xiang Wang
Abstract (EN)
Recent advancements in sequential recommendation have underscored the potential of Large Language Models (LLMs) for enhancing item embeddings. However, existing approaches face three key limitations: 1) the degradation of the semantic space when high-dimensional language embeddings are mapped to lower-dimensional ID embeddings, 2) the underutilization of language embeddings, and 3) the reliance on additional trainable parameters, such as an adapter, to bridge the gap between the semantic and behavior spaces. In this paper, we introduce AlphaFuse, a simple but effective language-guided learning strategy that addresses these challenges by learning ID embeddings within the null space of language embeddings. Specifically, we decompose the semantic space of language embeddings via Singular Value Decomposition (SVD), distinguishing it into a semantic-rich row space and a semantic-sparse null space. Collaborative signals are then injected into the null space, while preserving the rich semantics of the row space. AlphaFuse prevents degradation of the semantic space, integrates the retained language embeddings into the final item embeddings, and eliminates the need for auxiliary trainable modules, enabling seamless adaptation to any sequential recommendation framework. We validate the effectiveness and flexibility of AlphaFuse through extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets, including cold-start user and long-tail settings, showcasing significant improvements in both discriminative and diffusion-based generative sequential recommenders. Our codes and datasets are available at https://github.com/Hugo-Chinn/AlphaFuse.
摘要 (中文)
序列推荐的最新进展凸显了大语言模型（LLM）在增强物品 embedding 方面的潜力。然而，现有方法面临三个关键局限：1) 高维语言 embedding 映射到低维 ID embedding 时语义空间退化，2) 语言 embedding 利用不足，3) 依赖额外可训练参数（如 adapter）来弥合语义空间与行为空间之间的差距。在本文中，我们提出 AlphaFuse，一种简单而有效的语言引导学习策略，通过在语言 embedding 的零空间内学习 ID embedding 来应对这些挑战。具体而言，我们通过奇异值分解（SVD）分解语言 embedding 的语义空间，将其区分为语义丰富的行空间与语义稀疏的零空间。随后将协同信号注入零空间，同时保留行空间的丰富语义。AlphaFuse 防止语义空间退化，将保留的语言 embedding 整合进最终物品 embedding，并消除对辅助可训练模块的需求，从而能无缝适配任何序列推荐框架。我们通过在三个基准数据集上的大量实验（包括冷启动用户与长尾设置）验证了 AlphaFuse 的有效性与灵活性，在判别式与基于扩散的生成式序列推荐器中均展现出显著提升。我们的代码与数据集见 https://github.com/Hugo-Chinn/AlphaFuse。
R$^2$ec: Towards Large Recommender Models with Reasoning
港理工 / 新加坡国立2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐large recommender models, LLM reasoning, dual-head architecture, reinforcement learning, reasoning chainPDF · arXiv
AuthorsRunyang You, Yongqi Li, Xinyu Lin, Xin Zhang, Wenjie Wang, Wenjie Li, Liqiang Nie
Abstract (EN)
Large recommender models have extended LLMs as powerful recommenders via encoding or item generation, and recent breakthroughs in LLM reasoning synchronously motivate the exploration of reasoning in recommendation. In this work, we propose R$^2$ec, a unified large recommender model with intrinsic reasoning capability. R$^2$ec introduces a dual-head architecture that supports both reasoning chain generation and efficient item prediction in a single model, significantly reducing inference latency. To overcome the lack of annotated reasoning data, we design RecPO, a reinforcement learning framework that optimizes reasoning and recommendation jointly with a novel fused reward mechanism. Extensive experiments on three datasets demonstrate that R$^2$ec outperforms traditional, LLM-based, and reasoning-augmented recommender baselines, while further analyses validate its competitive efficiency among conventional LLM-based recommender baselines and strong adaptability to diverse recommendation scenarios. Code and checkpoints available at https://github.com/YRYangang/RRec.
摘要 (中文)
大型推荐模型通过编码或 item 生成将 LLMs 扩展为强大的推荐器，而近期 LLM 推理方面的突破同步激发了在推荐中探索推理的兴趣。在本工作中，我们提出 R$^2$ec，一个具备内在推理能力的统一大型推荐模型。R$^2$ec 引入双头（dual-head）架构，在单一模型中同时支持推理链生成和高效的 item 预测，显著降低推理延迟。为克服标注推理数据的缺乏，我们设计了 RecPO，一个以新颖融合奖励机制联合优化推理与推荐的强化学习框架。在三个数据集上的大量实验表明，R$^2$ec 优于传统、基于 LLM 以及推理增强的推荐 baseline，进一步的分析验证了其在常规基于 LLM 的推荐 baseline 中具有竞争力的效率，以及对多样推荐场景的强适应能力。代码与 checkpoint 见 https://github.com/YRYangang/RRec。
LLM Reasoning for Cold-Start Item Recommendation
UT Austin2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐cold-start item recommendation, LLM reasoning, supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning fine-tuning, hybrid fine-tuningPDF · arXiv
AuthorsShijun Li, Yu Wang, Jin Wang, Ying Li, Joydeep Ghosh, Anne Cocos
Abstract (EN)
Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown significant potential for improving recommendation systems through their inherent reasoning capabilities and extensive knowledge base. Yet, existing studies predominantly address warm-start scenarios with abundant user-item interaction data, leaving the more challenging cold-start scenarios, where sparse interactions hinder traditional collaborative filtering methods, underexplored. To address this limitation, we propose novel reasoning strategies designed for cold-start item recommendations within the Netflix domain. Our method utilizes the advanced reasoning capabilities of LLMs to effectively infer user preferences, particularly for newly introduced or rarely interacted items. We systematically evaluate supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning-based fine-tuning, and hybrid approaches that combine both methods to optimize recommendation performance. Extensive experiments on real-world data demonstrate significant improvements in both methodological efficacy and practical performance in cold-start recommendation contexts. Remarkably, our reasoning-based fine-tuned models outperform Netflix's production ranking model by up to 8% in certain cases.
摘要 (中文)
大语言模型（LLMs）凭借其固有的推理能力和广博的知识库，在改进推荐系统方面展现出巨大潜力。然而，现有研究主要针对用户-item 交互数据丰富的 warm-start 场景，而对更具挑战性的 cold-start 场景（交互稀疏阻碍了传统协同过滤方法）则探索不足。为弥补这一局限，我们提出了面向 Netflix 领域 cold-start item 推荐的新型推理策略。我们的方法利用 LLMs 的先进推理能力来有效推断用户偏好，尤其是针对新引入或很少被交互的 item。我们系统评估了监督微调、基于强化学习的微调，以及结合二者的混合方法，以优化推荐性能。在真实世界数据上的大量实验表明，在 cold-start 推荐场景中，方法有效性和实际性能均有显著提升。值得注意的是，我们基于推理的微调模型在某些情况下相较 Netflix 的生产 ranking 模型性能提升最高达 8%。
AgentDR: Dynamic Recommendation with Implicit Item-Item Relations via LLM-based Agents
Amazon / University of Michigan2025推荐工业LLM4Rec 与对齐LLM-based agents, item-item relations, substitute and complement relationships, full-catalog ranking, hallucination mitigationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsMingdai Yang, Nurendra Choudhary, Jiangshu Du, Edward W. Huang, Philip S. Yu, Karthik Subbian, Danai Koutra
Abstract (EN)
Recent agent-based recommendation frameworks aim to simulate user behaviors by incorporating memory mechanisms and prompting strategies, but they struggle with hallucinating non-existent items and full-catalog ranking. Besides, a largely underexplored opportunity lies in leveraging LLMs'commonsense reasoning to capture user intent through substitute and complement relationships between items, which are usually implicit in datasets and difficult for traditional ID-based recommenders to capture. In this work, we propose a novel LLM-agent framework, AgenDR, which bridges LLM reasoning with scalable recommendation tools. Our approach delegates full-ranking tasks to traditional models while utilizing LLMs to (i) integrate multiple recommendation outputs based on personalized tool suitability and (ii) reason over substitute and complement relationships grounded in user history. This design mitigates hallucination, scales to large catalogs, and enhances recommendation relevance through relational reasoning. Through extensive experiments on three public grocery datasets, we show that our framework achieves superior full-ranking performance, yielding on average a twofold improvement over its underlying tools. We also introduce a new LLM-based evaluation metric that jointly measures semantic alignment and ranking correctness.
摘要 (中文)
近期的基于 agent 的推荐框架旨在通过引入 memory 机制和 prompting 策略来模拟用户行为，但它们难以应对虚构不存在的 item 以及全目录 ranking 的问题。此外，一个很大程度上尚未被充分探索的机会在于利用 LLMs 的常识推理，通过 item 之间的替代（substitute）和互补（complement）关系来捕捉用户意图，而这些关系在数据集中通常是隐式的，传统基于 ID 的推荐器难以捕捉。在本工作中，我们提出一个新颖的 LLM-agent 框架 AgenDR，它将 LLM 推理与可扩展的推荐工具连接起来。我们的方法将全量 ranking 任务委托给传统模型，同时利用 LLMs 来 (i) 基于个性化工具适用性整合多个推荐输出，以及 (ii) 基于用户历史对替代与互补关系进行推理。该设计缓解了幻觉、可扩展到大目录，并通过关系推理增强了推荐相关性。通过在三个公开杂货数据集上的大量实验，我们表明该框架取得了优越的全量 ranking 性能，平均相较其底层工具提升两倍。我们还引入了一个新的基于 LLM 的评估指标，联合衡量语义对齐与 ranking 正确性。
ItemRAG: Item-Based Retrieval-Augmented Generation for LLM-Based Recommendation
Seoul National University2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐retrieval-augmented generation, item-level retrieval, cold-start recommendation, co-purchase, LLM-based recommendationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsSunwoo Kim, Geon Lee, Kyungho Kim, Jaemin Yoo, Kijung Shin
Abstract (EN)
Recently, large language models (LLMs) have been widely used as recommender systems, owing to their reasoning capability and effectiveness in handling cold-start items. A common approach prompts an LLM with a target user's purchase history to recommend items from a candidate set, often enhanced with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Most existing RAG approaches retrieve purchase histories of users similar to the target user; however, these histories often contain noisy or weakly relevant information and provide little or no useful information for candidate items. To address these limitations, we propose ItemRAG, a novel RAG approach that shifts focus from coarse user-history retrieval to fine-grained item-level retrieval. ItemRAG augments the description of each item in the target user's history or the candidate set by retrieving items relevant to each. To retrieve items not merely semantically similar but informative for recommendation, ItemRAG leverages co-purchase information alongside semantic information. Especially, through their careful combination, ItemRAG prioritizes more informative retrievals and also benefits cold-start items. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that ItemRAG consistently outperforms existing RAG approaches under both standard and cold-start item recommendation settings. Supplementary materials, code, and datasets are provided at https://github.com/kswoo97/ItemRAG.
摘要 (中文)
近来，得益于自身的推理能力以及在处理冷启动商品上的有效性，Large Language Models（LLM）被广泛用作推荐系统。一种常见做法是用目标用户的购买历史作为 prompt 提示 LLM，从候选集中推荐商品，并常辅以 retrieval-augmented generation（RAG）增强。现有大多数 RAG 方法检索的是与目标用户相似用户的购买历史；然而这些历史往往包含噪声或弱相关信息，对候选商品提供的有用信息很少甚至没有。为克服这些局限，我们提出 ItemRAG，一种新颖的 RAG 方法，将关注点从粗粒度的用户历史检索转向细粒度的 item 级检索。ItemRAG 通过为目标用户历史或候选集中的每个商品检索与其相关的商品，来增强该商品的描述。为了检索出不仅在语义上相似、而且对推荐有信息量的商品，ItemRAG 在语义信息之外还利用了共购买（co-purchase）信息。特别是通过对二者的精心结合，ItemRAG 优先选择信息量更大的检索结果，并同样使冷启动商品受益。通过大量实验，我们证明 ItemRAG 在标准与冷启动商品推荐两种设定下都持续优于现有 RAG 方法。补充材料、代码与数据集见 https://github.com/kswoo97/ItemRAG。
SPRINT: Scalable and Predictive Intent Refinement for LLM-Enhanced Session-based Recommendation
UIUC / Korea University2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐session-based recommendation, LLM profiling, intent prediction, scalability, hallucination mitigationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsGyuseok Lee, Wonbin Kweon, Zhenrui Yue, Yaokun Liu, Yifan Liu, Susik Yoon, Dong Wang, SeongKu Kang
Abstract (EN)
Large language models (LLMs) have enhanced conventional recommendation models via user profiling, which generates representative textual profiles from users' historical interactions. However, their direct application to session-based recommendation (SBR) remains challenging due to severe session context scarcity and poor scalability. In this paper, we propose SPRINT, a scalable SBR framework that incorporates reliable and informative intents while ensuring high efficiency in both training and inference. SPRINT constrains LLM-based profiling with a global intent pool and validates inferred intents based on recommendation performance to mitigate noise and hallucinations under limited context. To ensure scalability, LLMs are selectively invoked only for uncertain sessions during training, while a lightweight intent predictor generalizes intent prediction to all sessions without LLM dependency at inference time. Experiments on real-world datasets show that SPRINT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods while providing more explainable recommendations.
摘要 (中文)
Large Language Models（LLM）通过用户画像（user profiling）从用户历史交互中生成具有代表性的文本画像，从而增强了传统推荐模型。然而，由于会话上下文严重稀缺以及可扩展性差，将其直接应用于 session-based recommendation（SBR）仍具挑战。本文提出 SPRINT，一个可扩展的 SBR 框架，在保证训练与推理高效性的同时引入可靠且有信息量的 intent。SPRINT 借助全局 intent pool 约束基于 LLM 的画像生成，并依据推荐表现验证推断出的 intent，以缓解上下文有限情形下的噪声与幻觉。为保证可扩展性，训练时仅对不确定的 session 选择性地调用 LLM，而一个轻量的 intent predictor 将 intent 预测泛化到所有 session，在推理阶段不依赖 LLM。在真实数据集上的实验表明，SPRINT 持续优于 state-of-the-art 方法，同时提供更具可解释性的推荐。
Mirroring Users: Towards Building Preference-aligned User Simulator with User Feedback in Recommendation
NTU / 浙大2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐user simulation, preference alignment, user feedback, data distillation, uncertainty estimationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsTianjun Wei, Huizhong Guo, Yingpeng Du, Zhu Sun, Huang Chen, Dongxia Wang, Jie Zhang
Abstract (EN)
User simulation is increasingly vital to develop and evaluate recommender systems (RSs). While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer promising avenues to simulate user behavior, they often struggle with the absence of specific task alignment required for RSs and the efficiency demands of large-scale simulation. A vast yet underutilized resource for enhancing this alignment is the extensive user feedback inherent in RSs, but leveraging it is challenging due to its ambiguity, noise and massive volume, which hinders efficient preference alignment. To overcome these hurdles, we introduce a novel data construction framework that leverages user feedback in RSs with advanced LLM capabilities to generate high-quality simulation data. Our framework unfolds in two key phases: (1) using LLMs to generate decision-making processes as explanatory rationales on simulation samples, thereby reducing ambiguity; and (2) data distillation based on uncertainty estimation and behavior sampling to efficiently filter the most informative, denoised samples. Accordingly, we fine-tune lightweight LLMs, as user simulators, using such high-quality dataset with corresponding decision-making processes. Extensive experiments confirm that our framework significantly boosts the alignment with human preferences and the in-domain reasoning capabilities of the fine-tuned LLMs, providing more insightful and interpretable signals for RS interaction. We believe our work, together with publicly available developed framework, high-quality mixed-domain dataset, and fine-tuned LLM checkpoints, will advance the RS community and offer valuable insights for broader human-centric AI research. Our code is available at https://github.com/Joinn99/UserMirrorer.
摘要 (中文)
用户模拟对于开发和评估推荐系统（RS）日益重要。尽管 Large Language Models（LLM）为模拟用户行为提供了有前景的途径，但它们往往难以满足 RS 所需的特定任务对齐以及大规模模拟的效率要求。RS 中蕴含的大量用户反馈是增强这种对齐的一片广阔却未被充分利用的资源，但由于其模糊性、噪声与庞大体量，利用起来颇具挑战，阻碍了高效的偏好对齐。为克服这些障碍，我们引入一个新颖的数据构建框架，利用 RS 中的用户反馈并结合先进的 LLM 能力来生成高质量的模拟数据。我们的框架分为两个关键阶段：（1）使用 LLM 在模拟样本上生成作为解释性依据（rationale）的决策过程，从而降低模糊性；（2）基于不确定性估计与行为采样的数据蒸馏，以高效筛选出最有信息量、去噪后的样本。据此，我们使用这类带有相应决策过程的高质量数据集微调轻量 LLM，作为用户模拟器。大量实验证实，我们的框架显著提升了所微调 LLM 与人类偏好的对齐程度及其域内推理能力，为 RS 交互提供了更有洞见、更可解释的信号。我们相信，我们的工作连同公开的框架、高质量混合领域数据集以及微调后的 LLM checkpoint，将推动 RS 社区发展，并为更广泛的以人为中心的 AI 研究提供有价值的洞见。代码见 https://github.com/Joinn99/UserMirrorer。
What Makes LLMs Effective Sequential Recommenders? A Study on Preference Intensity and Temporal Context
Dartmouth College / 字节2025推荐工业LLM4Rec 与对齐preference intensity, temporal context, preference optimization, sequential recommendation, reward marginsPDF · arXiv
AuthorsZhongyu Ouyang, Qianlong Wen, Chunhui Zhang, Yanfang Ye, Soroush Vosoughi
Abstract (EN)
What enables large language models (LLMs) to effectively model user preferences in sequential recommendation? Our investigation reveals that existing preference-alignment approaches largely rely on binary pairwise comparisons, overlooking two critical factors: preference intensity (the structured strength of affinity or aversion) and temporal context (the extent to which recent interactions better reflect a user's current intent). Through controlled experiments, we show that leveraging comprehensive feedback with structured preference signals substantially improves recommendation performance, indicating that binary modeling discards essential information. Motivated by these findings, we propose RecPO, a unified preference optimization framework that maps both explicit and implicit feedback into a common preference signal and constructs adaptive reward margins that jointly account for preference intensity and interaction recency. Experiments across five datasets show that RecPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while exhibiting behavioral patterns aligned with human decision-making, including favoring immediate satisfaction, maintaining preference coherence, and avoiding dispreferred items. Our results highlight that preference intensity and temporal context are fundamental ingredients for effective LLM-based recommendation.
摘要 (中文)
是什么让 Large Language Models（LLM）能够有效建模序列推荐中的用户偏好？我们的研究表明，现有偏好对齐方法大多依赖二元成对比较，忽略了两个关键因素：偏好强度（preference intensity，即亲和或厌恶的结构化强度）与时间上下文（temporal context，即近期交互在多大程度上更能反映用户当前意图）。通过受控实验，我们表明利用带有结构化偏好信号的完整反馈能显著提升推荐性能，这说明二元建模丢弃了关键信息。受这些发现启发，我们提出 RecPO，一个统一的偏好优化框架，将显式与隐式反馈映射为共同的偏好信号，并构建能够同时考虑偏好强度与交互近因性的自适应 reward margin。在五个数据集上的实验表明，RecPO 持续优于 state-of-the-art baseline，并展现出与人类决策相一致的行为模式，包括偏好即时满足、保持偏好一致性以及回避不受偏好的商品。我们的结果凸显出，偏好强度与时间上下文是有效基于 LLM 的推荐的基本要素。
AgentSociety Challenge: Designing LLM Agents for User Modeling and Recommendation on Web Platforms
清华 / 香港科技大学（广州）2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐LLM agents, user modeling, agent benchmark, interactive environment simulator, web platformsPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYuwei Yan, Yu Shang, Qingbin Zeng, Yu Li, Keyu Zhao, Zhiheng Zheng, Xuefei Ning, Tianji Wu, et al.
Abstract (EN)
The AgentSociety Challenge is the first competition in the Web Conference that aims to explore the potential of Large Language Model (LLM) agents in modeling user behavior and enhancing recommender systems on web platforms. The Challenge consists of two tracks: the User Modeling Track and the Recommendation Track. Participants are tasked to utilize a combined dataset from Yelp, Amazon, and Goodreads, along with an interactive environment simulator, to develop innovative LLM agents. The Challenge has attracted 295 teams across the globe and received over 1,400 submissions in total over the course of 37 official competition days. The participants have achieved 21.9% and 20.3% performance improvement for Track 1 and Track 2 in the Development Phase, and 9.1% and 15.9% in the Final Phase, representing a significant accomplishment. This paper discusses the detailed designs of the Challenge, analyzes the outcomes, and highlights the most successful LLM agent designs. To support further research and development, we have open-sourced the benchmark environment at https://tsinghua-fib-lab.github.io/AgentSocietyChallenge.
摘要 (中文)
AgentSociety Challenge 是 Web Conference 上首个旨在探索 Large Language Model（LLM）智能体在建模用户行为与增强 web 平台推荐系统方面潜力的竞赛。该竞赛包含两个赛道：User Modeling Track 与 Recommendation Track。参赛者需利用来自 Yelp、Amazon 与 Goodreads 的合并数据集以及一个交互式环境模拟器，开发创新的 LLM 智能体。竞赛吸引了全球 295 支队伍，在 37 个正式比赛日内共收到 1400 多份提交。参赛者在 Development Phase 中于 Track 1 和 Track 2 分别取得 21.9% 和 20.3% 的性能提升，在 Final Phase 中分别取得 9.1% 和 15.9% 的提升，成绩斐然。本文讨论了竞赛的详细设计，分析了结果，并重点介绍了最成功的 LLM 智能体设计。为支持后续研究与开发，我们已在 https://tsinghua-fib-lab.github.io/AgentSocietyChallenge 开源该 benchmark 环境。
Uncertainty Quantification and Decomposition for LLM-based Recommendation
POSTECH / Korea University2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐uncertainty quantification, uncertainty decomposition, reliability, uncertainty-aware prompting, LLM-based recommendationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsWonbin Kweon, Sanghwan Jang, SeongKu Kang, Hwanjo Yu
Abstract (EN)
Despite the widespread adoption of large language models (LLMs) for recommendation, we demonstrate that LLMs often exhibit uncertainty in their recommendations. To ensure the trustworthy use of LLMs in generating recommendations, we emphasize the importance of assessing the reliability of recommendations generated by LLMs. We start by introducing a novel framework for estimating the predictive uncertainty to quantitatively measure the reliability of LLM-based recommendations. We further propose to decompose the predictive uncertainty into recommendation uncertainty and prompt uncertainty, enabling in-depth analyses of the primary source of uncertainty. Through extensive experiments, we (1) demonstrate predictive uncertainty effectively indicates the reliability of LLM-based recommendations, (2) investigate the origins of uncertainty with decomposed uncertainty measures, and (3) propose uncertainty-aware prompting for a lower predictive uncertainty and enhanced recommendation. Our source code and model weights are available at https://github.com/WonbinKweon/UNC_LLM_REC_WWW2025
摘要 (中文)
尽管 Large Language Models（LLM）在推荐中已被广泛采用，我们仍证明 LLM 的推荐往往存在不确定性。为确保在生成推荐时可信地使用 LLM，我们强调评估 LLM 所生成推荐可靠性的重要性。我们首先引入一个新颖的框架来估计预测不确定性，以定量衡量基于 LLM 推荐的可靠性。我们进一步提出将预测不确定性分解为 recommendation uncertainty 与 prompt uncertainty，从而能够深入分析不确定性的主要来源。通过大量实验，我们（1）证明预测不确定性能够有效指示基于 LLM 推荐的可靠性，（2）借助分解后的不确定性度量探究不确定性的来源，（3）提出 uncertainty-aware prompting，以实现更低的预测不确定性与更强的推荐。我们的源代码与模型权重见 https://github.com/WonbinKweon/UNC_LLM_REC_WWW2025
Reason4Rec: Deliberative User Preference Alignment of Large Language Models for Recommendation
USTC / 中关村学院2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐deliberative recommendation, preference alignment, reasoning, step-wise expertsPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYi Fang, Wenjie Wang, Yang Zhang, Fengbin Zhu, Qifan Wang, Fuli Feng, Xiangnan He
Abstract (EN)
Aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with recommendation tasks represents an emerging paradigm in recommendation domain, exhibiting promising performance overall. However, these aligned recommendation LLMs often struggle with complex scenarios due to limitations in the current alignment task formulation, which optimizes LLMs to directly generate user feedback without deliberation. To develop more reliable recommendation LLMs, we introduce a new \textit{Deliberative Recommendation} task, which enforces explicit reasoning about user preferences as an additional alignment objective. To address this task, we propose a \textit{Reasoning-powered Recommender} framework designed to enhance reasoning capabilities by leveraging verbalized user feedback in a step-wise manner. Specifically, this framework employs collaborative step-wise experts alongside specifically crafted expert-wise training strategies. Extensive experiments conducted on three real-world datasets demonstrate the rationality of the deliberative task formulation and the effectiveness of the proposed framework in improving both prediction accuracy and reasoning quality. Our implementation is publicly available on GitHub: https://github.com/Peter-Fy/Reason4Rec.
摘要 (中文)
将 Large Language Models（LLM）与推荐任务对齐是推荐领域一个新兴范式，整体上展现出良好性能。然而，由于当前对齐任务形式化的局限——其优化目标是让 LLM 直接生成用户反馈而不做深思（deliberation）——这些已对齐的推荐 LLM 在复杂场景下常常力不从心。为构建更可靠的推荐 LLM，我们引入一个新的 Deliberative Recommendation 任务，将对用户偏好的显式推理作为额外的对齐目标。为完成该任务，我们提出一个 Reasoning-powered Recommender 框架，通过以逐步（step-wise）方式利用语言化的用户反馈来增强推理能力。具体而言，该框架采用协同的 step-wise experts 以及专门设计的 expert-wise 训练策略。在三个真实数据集上的大量实验证明了该深思式任务形式化的合理性，以及所提框架在提升预测准确性与推理质量方面的有效性。我们的实现已在 GitHub 公开：https://github.com/Peter-Fy/Reason4Rec。
X-Cross: Dynamic Integration of Language Models for Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation
Ben-Gurion University2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐cross-domain sequential recommendation, LoRA, language model integration, low-rank adaptersPDF · arXiv
AuthorsGuy Hadad, Haggai Roitman, Yotam Eshel, Bracha Shapira, Lior Rokach
Abstract (EN)
As new products are emerging daily, recommendation systems are required to quickly adapt to possible new domains without needing extensive retraining. This work presents ``X-Cross'' -- a novel cross-domain sequential-recommendation model that recommends products in new domains by integrating several domain-specific language models; each model is fine-tuned with low-rank adapters (LoRA). Given a recommendation prompt, operating layer by layer, X-Cross dynamically refines the representation of each source language model by integrating knowledge from all other models. These refined representations are propagated from one layer to the next, leveraging the activations from each domain adapter to ensure domain-specific nuances are preserved while enabling adaptability across domains. Using Amazon datasets for sequential recommendation, X-Cross achieves performance comparable to a model that is fine-tuned with LoRA, while using only 25% of the additional parameters. In cross-domain tasks, such as adapting from Toys domain to Tools, Electronics or Sports, X-Cross demonstrates robust performance, while requiring about 50%-75% less fine-tuning data than LoRA to make fine-tuning effective. Furthermore, X-Cross achieves significant improvement in accuracy over alternative cross-domain baselines. Overall, X-Cross enables scalable and adaptive cross-domain recommendations, reducing computational overhead and providing an efficient solution for data-constrained environments.
摘要 (中文)
由于新产品层出不穷，推荐系统需要在不进行大规模重训练的情况下快速适应可能出现的新领域。本工作提出 X-Cross——一个新颖的跨域序列推荐模型，通过集成多个领域特定的语言模型来推荐新领域中的商品；每个模型都使用低秩适配器（LoRA）微调。给定一个推荐 prompt，X-Cross 逐层运作，通过整合来自所有其他模型的知识来动态精炼每个源语言模型的表示。这些精炼后的表示逐层传播，利用每个领域适配器的激活值，在保持领域特定细微差异的同时实现跨领域的适应性。在 Amazon 数据集上进行序列推荐，X-Cross 取得了与使用 LoRA 微调的模型相当的性能，而额外参数仅为其 25%。在跨域任务中，例如从 Toys 领域迁移到 Tools、Electronics 或 Sports，X-Cross 展现出鲁棒的性能，且使微调生效所需的数据比 LoRA 少约 50%-75%。此外，X-Cross 在准确性上相较其他跨域 baseline 取得显著提升。总体而言，X-Cross 实现了可扩展且自适应的跨域推荐，降低了计算开销，为数据受限环境提供了高效解决方案。
Bridge the Domains: Large Language Models Enhanced Cross-domain Sequential Recommendation
西安交大 / 香港城市大学2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐cross-domain sequential recommendation, LLM, contrastive regularization, hierarchical profiling, overlap dilemmaPDF · arXiv
AuthorsQidong Liu, Xiangyu Zhao, Yejing Wang, Zijian Zhang, Howard Zhong, Chong Chen, Xiang Li, Wei Huang, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Cross-domain Sequential Recommendation (CDSR) aims to extract the preference from the user's historical interactions across various domains. Despite some progress in CDSR, two problems set the barrier for further advancements, i.e., overlap dilemma and transition complexity. The former means existing CDSR methods severely rely on users who own interactions on all domains to learn cross-domain item relationships, compromising the practicability. The latter refers to the difficulties in learning the complex transition patterns from the mixed behavior sequences. With powerful representation and reasoning abilities, Large Language Models (LLMs) are promising to address these two problems by bridging the items and capturing the user's preferences from a semantic view. Therefore, we propose an LLMs Enhanced Cross-domain Sequential Recommendation model (LLM4CDSR). To obtain the semantic item relationships, we first propose an LLM-based unified representation module to represent items. Then, a trainable adapter with contrastive regularization is designed to adapt the CDSR task. Besides, a hierarchical LLMs profiling module is designed to summarize user cross-domain preferences. Finally, these two modules are integrated into the proposed tri-thread framework to derive recommendations. We have conducted extensive experiments on three public cross-domain datasets, validating the effectiveness of LLM4CDSR. We have released the code online.
摘要 (中文)
跨域序列推荐（CDSR）旨在从用户跨多个领域的历史交互中提取偏好。尽管 CDSR 已取得一些进展，但有两个问题阻碍了其进一步发展，即 overlap dilemma 与 transition complexity。前者指现有 CDSR 方法严重依赖在所有领域都有交互的用户来学习跨域商品关系，损害了实用性。后者指从混合行为序列中学习复杂迁移模式的困难。凭借强大的表示与推理能力，Large Language Models（LLM）有望通过桥接商品并从语义视角捕捉用户偏好来解决这两个问题。因此，我们提出 LLM 增强的跨域序列推荐模型（LLM4CDSR）。为获得语义层面的商品关系，我们首先提出一个基于 LLM 的统一表示模块来表示商品。随后，设计了一个带对比正则化的可训练 adapter，以适配 CDSR 任务。此外，设计了一个分层的 LLM 画像模块来总结用户的跨域偏好。最后，将这两个模块整合进所提出的三线（tri-thread）框架以生成推荐。我们在三个公开跨域数据集上进行了大量实验，验证了 LLM4CDSR 的有效性。代码已在线发布。
Process-Supervised LLM Recommenders via Flow-guided Tuning
USTC / 香港科技大学2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐GFlowNet, process supervision, token-level reward propagation, popularity bias, diversityPDF · arXiv
AuthorsChongming Gao, Mengyao Gao, Chenxiao Fan, Shuai Yuan, Wentao Shi, Xiangnan He
Abstract (EN)
While large language models (LLMs) are increasingly adapted for recommendation systems via supervised fine-tuning (SFT), this approach amplifies popularity bias due to its likelihood maximization objective, compromising recommendation diversity and fairness. To address this, we present Flow-guided fine-tuning recommender (Flower), which replaces SFT with a Generative Flow Network (GFlowNet) framework that enacts process supervision through token-level reward propagation. Flower's key innovation lies in decomposing item-level rewards into constituent token rewards, enabling direct alignment between token generation probabilities and their reward signals. This mechanism achieves three critical advancements: (1) popularity bias mitigation and fairness enhancement through empirical distribution matching, (2) preservation of diversity through GFlowNet's proportional sampling, and (3) flexible integration of personalized preferences via adaptable token rewards. Experiments demonstrate Flower's superior distribution-fitting capability and its significant advantages over traditional SFT in terms of accuracy, fairness, and diversity, highlighting its potential to improve LLM-based recommendation systems. The implementation is available via https://github.com/MrPeach0301/Flower
摘要 (中文)
尽管 Large Language Models（LLM）正越来越多地通过监督微调（SFT）适配于推荐系统，但该方法的似然最大化目标会放大流行度偏差，损害推荐多样性与公平性。为解决这一问题，我们提出 Flow-guided fine-tuning recommender（Flower），用 Generative Flow Network（GFlowNet）框架替代 SFT，通过 token 级 reward 传播实现过程监督（process supervision）。Flower 的关键创新在于将 item 级 reward 分解为组成它的 token reward，使 token 生成概率与其 reward 信号能够直接对齐。该机制实现了三项关键进展：（1）通过经验分布匹配缓解流行度偏差并提升公平性；（2）通过 GFlowNet 的比例采样保持多样性；（3）通过可适配的 token reward 灵活集成个性化偏好。实验证明 Flower 具有更优的分布拟合能力，并在准确性、公平性与多样性方面相较传统 SFT 具有显著优势，凸显了其改进基于 LLM 推荐系统的潜力。实现见 https://github.com/MrPeach0301/Flower
Can LLMs Outshine Conventional Recommenders? A Comparative Evaluation
港理工 / 华为2025推荐工业LLM4Rec 与对齐benchmark, item representation, CTR prediction, sequential recommendation, semantic identifierPDF · arXiv
AuthorsQijiong Liu, Jieming Zhu, Lu Fan, Kun Wang, Hengchang Hu, Wei Guo, Yong Liu, Xiao-Ming Wu
Abstract (EN)
In recent years, integrating large language models (LLMs) into recommender systems has created new opportunities for improving recommendation quality. However, a comprehensive benchmark is needed to thoroughly evaluate and compare the recommendation capabilities of LLMs with traditional recommender systems. In this paper, we introduce RecBench, which systematically investigates various item representation forms (including unique identifier, text, semantic embedding, and semantic identifier) and evaluates two primary recommendation tasks, i.e., click-through rate prediction (CTR) and sequential recommendation (SeqRec). Our extensive experiments cover up to 17 large models and are conducted across five diverse datasets from fashion, news, video, books, and music domains. Our findings indicate that LLM-based recommenders outperform conventional recommenders, achieving up to a 5% AUC improvement in the CTR scenario and up to a 170% NDCG@10 improvement in the SeqRec scenario. However, these substantial performance gains come at the expense of significantly reduced inference efficiency, rendering the LLM-as-RS paradigm impractical for real-time recommendation environments. We aim for our findings to inspire future research, including recommendation-specific model acceleration methods. We will release our code, data, configurations, and platform to enable other researchers to reproduce and build upon our experimental results.
摘要 (中文)
近年来，将 Large Language Models（LLM）集成进推荐系统为提升推荐质量创造了新机遇。然而，需要一个全面的 benchmark 来深入评估并比较 LLM 与传统推荐系统的推荐能力。本文提出 RecBench，系统性地考察了多种商品表示形式（包括 unique identifier、text、semantic embedding 与 semantic identifier），并评估了两类主要推荐任务，即 click-through rate prediction（CTR）与 sequential recommendation（SeqRec）。我们的实验覆盖多达 17 个大模型，并在来自时尚、新闻、视频、图书与音乐领域的五个多样化数据集上进行。我们的发现表明，基于 LLM 的推荐器优于传统推荐器，在 CTR 场景中取得最高 5% 的 AUC 提升，在 SeqRec 场景中取得最高 170% 的 NDCG@10 提升。然而，这些可观的性能增益以推理效率大幅下降为代价，使得 LLM-as-RS 范式在实时推荐环境中并不实用。我们希望这些发现能启发未来研究，包括面向推荐的专用模型加速方法。我们将发布代码、数据、配置与平台，以便其他研究者复现并基于我们的实验结果继续构建。
LLM2Rec: Large Language Models Are Powerful Embedding Models for Sequential Recommendation
新加坡国立 / USTC2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐sequential recommendation, embedding model, collaborative filtering, supervised fine-tuning, item embeddingsPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYingzhi He, Xiaohao Liu, An Zhang, Yunshan Ma, Tat-Seng Chua
Abstract (EN)
Sequential recommendation aims to predict users' future interactions by modeling collaborative filtering (CF) signals from historical behaviors of similar users or items. Traditional sequential recommenders predominantly rely on ID-based embeddings, which capture CF signals through high-order co-occurrence patterns. However, these embeddings depend solely on past interactions, lacking transferable knowledge to generalize to unseen domains. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have motivated text-based recommendation approaches that derive item representations from textual descriptions. While these methods enhance generalization, they fail to encode CF signals-i.e., latent item correlations and preference patterns-crucial for effective recommendation. We argue that an ideal embedding model should seamlessly integrate CF signals with rich semantic representations to improve both in-domain and out-of-domain recommendation performance. To this end, we propose LLM2Rec, a novel embedding model tailored for sequential recommendation, integrating the rich semantic understanding of LLMs with CF awareness. Our approach follows a two-stage training framework: (1) Collaborative Supervised Fine-tuning, which adapts LLMs to infer item relationships based on historical interactions, and (2) Item-level Embedding Modeling, which refines these specialized LLMs into structured item embedding models that encode both semantic and collaborative information. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LLM2Rec effectively improves recommendation quality across both in-domain and out-of-domain settings. Our findings highlight the potential of leveraging LLMs to build more robust, generalizable embedding models for sequential recommendation. Our codes are available at https://github.com/HappyPointer/LLM2Rec.
摘要 (中文)
序列推荐旨在通过建模相似用户或商品历史行为中的协同过滤（CF）信号来预测用户未来的交互。传统序列推荐器主要依赖基于 ID 的 embedding，通过高阶共现模式捕捉 CF 信号。然而，这些 embedding 仅依赖过往交互，缺乏可迁移知识，难以泛化到未见领域。Large Language Models（LLM）的近期进展推动了基于文本的推荐方法，从文本描述中导出商品表示。尽管这些方法增强了泛化能力，却无法编码对有效推荐至关重要的 CF 信号，即潜在的商品相关性与偏好模式。我们认为，理想的 embedding 模型应将 CF 信号与丰富的语义表示无缝结合，以同时提升域内与域外推荐性能。为此，我们提出 LLM2Rec，一个为序列推荐量身定制的新颖 embedding 模型，将 LLM 丰富的语义理解与 CF 感知能力相融合。我们的方法遵循两阶段训练框架：（1）Collaborative Supervised Fine-tuning，使 LLM 能够基于历史交互推断商品关系；（2）Item-level Embedding Modeling，将这些专门化的 LLM 精炼为结构化的商品 embedding 模型，编码语义与协同信息。在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，LLM2Rec 在域内与域外设定下均有效提升了推荐质量。我们的发现凸显了利用 LLM 为序列推荐构建更鲁棒、更可泛化 embedding 模型的潜力。代码见 https://github.com/HappyPointer/LLM2Rec。
GORACS: Group-level Optimal Transport-guided Coreset Selection for LLM-based Recommender Systems
复旦2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐coreset selection, optimal transport, data selection, fine-tuning efficiencyPDF · arXiv
AuthorsTiehua Mei, Hengrui Chen, Peng Yu, Jiaqing Liang, Deqing Yang
Abstract (EN)
Although large language models (LLMs) have shown great potential in recommender systems, the prohibitive computational costs for fine-tuning LLMs on entire datasets hinder their successful deployment in real-world scenarios. To develop affordable and effective LLM-based recommender systems, we focus on the task of coreset selection which identifies a small subset of fine-tuning data to optimize the test loss, thereby facilitating efficient LLMs' fine-tuning. Although there exist some intuitive solutions of subset selection, including distribution-based and importance-based approaches, they often lead to suboptimal performance due to the misalignment with downstream fine-tuning objectives or weak generalization ability caused by individual-level sample selection. To overcome these challenges, we propose GORACS, which is a novel Group-level Optimal tRAnsport-guided Coreset Selection framework for LLM-based recommender systems. GORACS is designed based on two key principles for coreset selection: 1) selecting the subsets that minimize the test loss to align with fine-tuning objectives, and 2) enhancing model generalization through group-level data selection. Corresponding to these two principles, GORACS has two key components: 1) a Proxy Optimization Objective (POO) leveraging optimal transport and gradient information to bound the intractable test loss, thus reducing computational costs by avoiding repeated LLM retraining, and 2) a two-stage Initialization-Then-Refinement Algorithm (ITRA) for efficient group-level selection. Our extensive experiments across diverse recommendation datasets and tasks validate that GORACS significantly reduces fine-tuning costs of LLMs while achieving superior performance over the state-of-the-art baselines and full data training. The source code of GORACS are available at https://github.com/Mithas-114/GORACS.
摘要 (中文)
尽管 Large Language Models（LLM）在推荐系统中展现出巨大潜力，但在全量数据集上微调 LLM 的高昂计算成本阻碍了其在真实场景中的成功部署。为构建负担得起且有效的基于 LLM 的推荐系统，我们聚焦于 coreset selection 任务，即识别一小部分微调数据子集来优化测试损失，从而促进 LLM 的高效微调。尽管已有一些直观的子集选择方案，包括基于分布与基于重要性的方法，但由于与下游微调目标不一致，或个体级样本选择导致泛化能力较弱，它们往往性能次优。为克服这些挑战，我们提出 GORACS，一个面向基于 LLM 推荐系统的新颖 Group-level Optimal tRAnsport-guided Coreset Selection 框架。GORACS 基于 coreset selection 的两条关键原则设计：1）选择能最小化测试损失的子集，以与微调目标对齐；2）通过组级数据选择增强模型泛化能力。对应这两条原则，GORACS 有两个关键组件：1）Proxy Optimization Objective（POO），利用最优传输与梯度信息来界定难以处理的测试损失，从而通过避免重复的 LLM 重训练降低计算成本；2）两阶段的 Initialization-Then-Refinement Algorithm（ITRA），用于高效的组级选择。我们在多种推荐数据集与任务上的大量实验验证了 GORACS 在显著降低 LLM 微调成本的同时，性能优于 state-of-the-art baseline 与全量数据训练。GORACS 的源代码见 https://github.com/Mithas-114/GORACS。
Lost in Sequence: Do Large Language Models Understand Sequential Recommendation?
NAVER / UCSD2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐sequential recommendation, sequential information, knowledge distillation, CF-SRec, LLM4RecPDF · arXiv
AuthorsSein Kim, Hongseok Kang, Kibum Kim, Jiwan Kim, Donghyun Kim, Minchul Yang, Kwangjin Oh, Julian McAuley, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as promising tools for recommendation thanks to their advanced textual understanding ability and context-awareness. Despite the current practice of training and evaluating LLM-based recommendation (LLM4Rec) models under a sequential recommendation scenario, we found that whether these models understand the sequential information inherent in users' item interaction sequences has been largely overlooked. In this paper, we first demonstrate through a series of experiments that existing LLM4Rec models do not fully capture sequential information both during training and inference. Then, we propose a simple yet effective LLM-based sequential recommender, called LLM-SRec, a method that enhances the integration of sequential information into LLMs by distilling the user representations extracted from a pre-trained CF-SRec model into LLMs. Our extensive experiments show that LLM-SRec enhances LLMs' ability to understand users' item interaction sequences, ultimately leading to improved recommendation performance. Furthermore, unlike existing LLM4Rec models that require fine-tuning of LLMs, LLM-SRec achieves state-of-the-art performance by training only a few lightweight MLPs, highlighting its practicality in real-world applications. Our code is available at https://github.com/Sein-Kim/LLM-SRec.
摘要 (中文)

Knowledge Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for LLM-based Recommendation
港理工 / University of Birmingham2025推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐knowledge graph, retrieval-augmented generation, structure information, hallucination, K-RagRecPDF · arXiv
AuthorsShijie Wang, Wenqi Fan, Yue Feng, Shanru Lin, Xinyu Ma, Shuaiqiang Wang, Dawei Yin
Abstract (EN)
Recommender systems have become increasingly vital in our daily lives, helping to alleviate the problem of information overload across various user-oriented online services. The emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) has yielded remarkable achievements, demonstrating their potential for the development of next-generation recommender systems. Despite these advancements, LLM-based recommender systems face inherent limitations stemming from their LLM backbones, particularly issues of hallucinations and the lack of up-to-date and domain-specific knowledge. Recently, Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has garnered significant attention for addressing these limitations by leveraging external knowledge sources to enhance the understanding and generation of LLMs. However, vanilla RAG methods often introduce noise and neglect structural relationships in knowledge, limiting their effectiveness in LLM-based recommendations. To address these limitations, we propose to retrieve high-quality and up-to-date structure information from the knowledge graph (KG) to augment recommendations. Specifically, our approach develops a retrieval-augmented framework, termed K-RagRec, that facilitates the recommendation generation process by incorporating structure information from the external KG. Extensive experiments have been conducted to demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method.
摘要 (中文)
推荐系统在我们的日常生活中日益重要，有助于缓解各类面向用户的在线服务中的信息过载问题。Large Language Models（LLM）的出现带来了显著成就，展现了其在构建下一代推荐系统方面的潜力。尽管取得了这些进展，基于 LLM 的推荐系统仍面临源于其 LLM backbone 的固有限制，尤其是幻觉以及缺乏最新且领域特定知识的问题。近来，Retrieval-Augmented Generation（RAG）因能利用外部知识源增强 LLM 的理解与生成，在应对这些限制上受到广泛关注。然而，朴素 RAG 方法往往引入噪声并忽略知识中的结构关系，限制了其在基于 LLM 推荐中的有效性。为克服这些局限，我们提出从 knowledge graph（KG）中检索高质量且最新的结构信息来增强推荐。具体而言，我们的方法构建了一个检索增强框架，称为 K-RagRec，通过引入外部 KG 的结构信息来促进推荐生成过程。我们进行了大量实验以证明所提方法的有效性。
GoalRank: Group-Relative Optimization for a Large Ranking Model
快手 / 国科大2025推荐工业LLM4Rec 与对齐group-relative optimization, generator-only ranking, one-stage ranking, scaling law, reward modelPDF · arXiv
AuthorsKaike Zhang, Xiaobei Wang, Shuchang Liu, Hailan Yang, Xiang Li, Lantao Hu, Han Li, Qi Cao, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Mainstream ranking approaches typically follow a Generator-Evaluator two-stage paradigm, where a generator produces candidate lists and an evaluator selects the best one. Recent work has attempted to enhance performance by expanding the number of candidate lists, for example, through multi-generator settings. However, ranking involves selecting a recommendation list from a combinatorially large space. Simply enlarging the candidate set remains ineffective, and performance gains quickly saturate. At the same time, recent advances in large recommendation models have shown that end-to-end one-stage models can achieve promising performance with the expectation of scaling laws. Motivated by this, we revisit ranking from a generator-only one-stage perspective. We theoretically prove that, for any (finite Multi-)Generator-Evaluator model, there always exists a generator-only model that achieves strictly smaller approximation error to the optimal ranking policy, while also enjoying scaling laws as its size increases. Building on this result, we derive an evidence upper bound of the one-stage optimization objective, from which we find that one can leverage a reward model trained on real user feedback to construct a reference policy in a group-relative manner. This reference policy serves as a practical surrogate of the optimal policy, enabling effective training of a large generator-only ranker. Based on these insights, we propose GoalRank, a generator-only ranking framework. Extensive offline experiments on public benchmarks and large-scale online A/B tests demonstrate that GoalRank consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.
摘要 (中文)
主流的排序方法通常遵循 Generator-Evaluator 两阶段范式，其中 generator 生成候选列表，evaluator 从中选出最佳的一个。近期的工作尝试通过扩大候选列表的数量来提升性能，例如采用多 generator 设置。然而，排序的本质是从一个组合规模极大的空间中选出一个推荐列表。仅仅扩大候选集依然收效甚微，性能增益很快饱和。与此同时，近期大型推荐模型的进展表明，端到端的一阶段模型能够取得有前景的性能，并有望呈现 scaling law。受此启发，我们从一个仅有 generator 的一阶段视角重新审视排序问题。我们从理论上证明：对于任何（有限的多）Generator-Evaluator 模型，总存在一个仅有 generator 的模型，其与最优排序策略之间的近似误差严格更小，同时随着规模增大还能享有 scaling law。基于这一结果，我们推导出一阶段优化目标的一个证据上界，并由此发现可以利用一个基于真实用户反馈训练的奖励模型，以 group-relative 的方式构造一个参考策略。该参考策略作为最优策略的实用替代，能够有效训练一个大型的仅有 generator 的 ranker。基于这些洞见，我们提出了 GoalRank，一个仅有 generator 的排序框架。在公开 benchmark 上的大量离线实验和大规模线上 A/B 测试表明，GoalRank 持续优于 SOTA 方法。
LONGER: Scaling Up Long Sequence Modeling in Industrial Recommenders
字节2025推荐工业长序列建模long-sequence modeling, scaling law, global token, token merge module, industrial recommenderPDF

Modeling ultra-long user behavior sequences is critical for capturing both long- and short-term preferences in industrial recommender systems. Existing solutions typically rely on two-stage retrieval or indirect modeling paradigms, incurring upstream-downstream inconsistency and computational inefficiency. In this paper, we present LONGER, a Long-sequence Optimized traNsformer for GPU-Efficient Recommenders. 摘要 (中文)
对超长用户行为序列建模对于在工业推荐系统中捕捉长短期偏好至关重要。现有方案通常依赖两阶段检索或间接建模范式，导致上下游不一致和计算低效。本文提出 LONGER，一种面向 GPU 高效推荐器的长序列优化 Transformer（Long-sequence Optimized traNsformer for GPU-Efficient Recommenders）。LONGER 包含 (i) 一种全局 token 机制，用于稳定长上下文上的注意力；(ii) 一个 token 合并模块，结合轻量级 InnerTransformer 与混合注意力策略，以降低二次复杂度；以及 (iii) 一系列工程优化，包括混合精度训练与激活重计算、KV cache 服务，以及用于统一的基于 GPU 的稠密与稀疏参数更新的全同步模型训练与服务框架。在 ByteDance 的广告和电商服务中，LONGER 在离线指标和在线 A/B 测试上均持续优于强 baseline，验证了其稳定的有效性和工业级 scaling law。目前，LONGER 已在 ByteDance 数十个真实且有影响力的场景中完成验证并全面部署，服务数十亿用户。（∗ 同等贡献。† 通讯作者。允许出于个人或课堂教学目的，在无费用前提下制作本作品全部或部分的数字或纸质副本，前提是副本不得用于盈利或商业优势，且副本须在首页保留本声明。本作品中由非作者第三方拥有的版权组件必须予以尊重。允许带署名的摘要引用。除此以外的复制、再发表、在服务器上发布或向列表分发，均需事先获得具体许可和/或支付费用。请向 permissions@acm.org 申请许可。RecSys ’25, Prague, Czech Republic © 2025 Copyright held by the owner/author(s).
