OneRec-V2 Technical Report
快手2025推荐工业工业落地系统decoder-only architecture, preference alignment, duration-aware reward shaping, generative recommendation, scalingPDF
AuthorsOneRec Team (Kuaishou)
Abstract (EN)
Recent breakthroughs in generative AI have fundamentally transformed recommender systems by enabling end-to-end generation. OneRec, an industrial-scale generative recommendation framework, reformulates recommendation as an autoregressive generation task, allowing for direct optimization of the final objective and achieving high Model FLOPs Utilization (MFU). While OneRec-V1 has shown significant empirical success in real-world deployment, two critical challenges hinder its scalability and performance: (1) Inefficient computational allocation in encoder-decoder architecture, where 97.66% of resources are consumed by sequence encoding context encoding rather than generation, which limits model scalability; and (2) limitations in reinforcement learning that relies solely on reward models, including inefficient sampling and potential reward hacking due to proxy reward signals. To address these challenges, we propose OneRec-V2, featuring: 1. Lazy Decoder-Only Architecture: A streamlined, decoder-only design that eliminates encoder bottlenecks and simplifies cross-attention, reducing total computation by 94% and training resources by 90% (see Figure 1 right). This efficiency enables the successful scaling of the model to 8B parameters, with a consistent loss decrease observed throughout the process (see Figure 1 left). 2. Preference Alignment with Real-World User Interactions: A user feedback-driven framework incorporating (i) Duration-Aware Reward Shaping to mitigate video duration bias and (ii) Adaptive Ratio Clipping to stabilize policy optimization, effectively leveraging real-world feedback to better align with user preferences and resulting in a significant increase in App Stay Time. Extensive A/B tests on Kuaishou/Kuaishou Lite demonstrate the effectiveness of OneRec-V2, improving App Stay Time by 0.467%/0.741% while balancing multi-objective recommendations without seesaw effects. This work advances generative recommendation scalability and alignment with real-world feedback, representing a step forward in the development of end-to-end recommender systems. 0.1b 0.1b 103 Enc:Dec (1:1) Enc:Dec (1:2) 296.4 0.1b 0.1b 0.2b =-0.11 3.4 0.5b =-0.24 0.5b =-0.13 3.2 0.5/4b 1b =-0.06 2b =-0.04 0.5b =-0.23 4b =-0.03 1b =-0.06 8b =-0.01 1b =-0.07 Training FLOPs per Sample 1011 3.34 3.32 102 3.30 18.9 3.28 3.27 3.28 3.26 101 3.24 3.0 1010 Enc:Dec=1:1 (OneRec-V1) Lazy Dec-Only (OneRec-V2) Convergence Loss 3.6 Lazy Dec-Only MoE Lazy Dec-Only Naive Dec Parameters / FLOPs Model Type 3.8 Convergence Loss arXiv:2508.20900v3 [cs.IR] 16 Sep 2025 OneRec Team 100 1B 1B 3.22 Param GFLOPs Loss 3.20 Figure 1 | Left: Scaling curves for various model architectures from 0.1B to 8B parameters, among which Lazy Decoder-only models demonstrate best scaling efficiency. 摘要 (中文)
生成式 AI 的近期突破通过支持端到端生成，从根本上改变了推荐系统。OneRec 作为一个工业级生成式推荐框架，将推荐重新表述为自回归生成任务，从而可以直接优化最终目标并实现较高的 Model FLOPs Utilization（MFU）。尽管 OneRec-V1 在真实部署中已展现出显著的经验性成功，但有两个关键挑战阻碍了其可扩展性与性能：（1）encoder-decoder 架构中的计算分配低效，97.66% 的资源被用于序列编码/上下文编码而非生成，限制了模型的可扩展性；（2）仅依赖 reward model 的强化学习存在局限，包括采样效率低下以及由代理奖励信号可能导致的 reward hacking。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 OneRec-V2，其特点包括：1. Lazy Decoder-Only 架构：一种精简的 decoder-only 设计，消除了 encoder 瓶颈并简化了 cross-attention，使总计算量减少 94%、训练资源减少 90%（见图 1 右）。这种效率使模型成功扩展到 8B 参数，且整个过程观察到 loss 持续下降（见图 1 左）。2. 基于真实世界用户交互的偏好对齐：一个由用户反馈驱动的框架，包含（i）Duration-Aware Reward Shaping 以缓解视频时长偏差，以及（ii）Adaptive Ratio Clipping 以稳定策略优化，从而有效利用真实世界反馈更好地对齐用户偏好，并显著提升 App 停留时长。在快手/快手极速版上的大量 A/B 测试证明了 OneRec-V2 的有效性，将 App 停留时长分别提升 0.467%/0.741%，同时在多目标推荐中取得平衡而不产生跷跷板效应。这项工作推进了生成式推荐的可扩展性及其与真实世界反馈的对齐，代表着端到端推荐系统发展向前迈进了一步。 0.1b 0.1b 103 Enc:Dec (1:1) Enc:Dec (1:2) 296.4 0.1b 0.1b 0.2b =-0.11 3.4 0.5b =-0.24 0.5b =-0.13 3.2 0.5/4b 1b =-0.06 2b =-0.04 0.5b =-0.23 4b =-0.03 1b =-0.06 8b =-0.01 1b =-0.07 Training FLOPs per Sample 1011 3.34 3.32 102 3.30 18.9 3.28 3.27 3.28 3.26 101 3.24 3.0 1010 Enc:Dec=1:1 (OneRec-V1) Lazy Dec-Only (OneRec-V2) Convergence Loss 3.6 Lazy Dec-Only MoE Lazy Dec-Only Naive Dec Parameters / FLOPs Model Type 3.8 Convergence Loss arXiv:2508.20900v3 [cs.IR] 16 Sep 2025 OneRec Team 100 1B 1B 3.22 Param GFLOPs Loss 3.20 图 1 | 左：不同模型架构从 0.1B 到 8B 参数的 scaling 曲线，其中 Lazy Decoder-only 模型展现出最佳的扩展效率。右：1B 参数下 OneRec-V1 与 OneRec-V2 的对比。© 2025 Kuaishou. 保留所有权利 OneRec-V2 Technical Report 目录 1
Next-User Retrieval: Enhancing Cold-Start Recommendations via Generative Next-User Modeling
字节2025推荐工业工业落地系统cold-start, next-user generation, lookalike, generative modeling, transformerPDF
AuthorsYu-Ting Lan, Yang Huo, Yi Shen, Xiao Yang, Zuotao Liu
Abstract (EN)
The item cold-start problem is critical for online recommendation systems, as the success of this phase determines whether high-quality new items can transition to popular ones, receive essential feedback to inspire creators, and thus lead to the long-term retention of creators. However, modern recommendation systems still struggle to address item cold-start challenges due to the heavy reliance on item and historical interactions, which are non-trivial for cold-start items lacking sufficient exposure and feedback. Lookalike algorithms provide a promising solution by extending feedback for new items based on lookalike users. Traditional lookalike algorithms face such limitations: (1) failing to effectively model the lookalike users and further improve recommendations with the existing rule- or model-based methods; and (2) struggling to utilize the interaction signals and incorporate diverse features in modern recommendation systems. Inspired by lookalike algorithms, we propose Next-User Retrieval, a novel framework for enhancing cold-start recommendations via generative next-user modeling. Specifically, we employ a transformer-based model to capture the unidirectional relationships among recently interacted users and utilize these sequences to generate the next potential user who is most likely to interact with the item. The additional item features are also integrated as prefix prompt embeddings to assist the next-user generation. The effectiveness of Next-User Retrieval is evaluated through both offline experiments and online A/B tests. Our method achieves significant improvements with increases of 0.0142% in daily active users and +0.1144% in publications in Douyin, showcasing its practical applicability and scalability.
摘要 (中文)
物品冷启动问题对在线推荐系统至关重要，因为这一阶段的成败决定了高质量新物品能否转化为热门物品、能否获得必要的反馈以激励创作者，进而实现创作者的长期留存。然而，由于严重依赖物品信息和历史交互，而这些对于缺乏足够曝光和反馈的冷启动物品而言并不容易获得，现代推荐系统仍难以应对物品冷启动挑战。lookalike 算法提供了一种有前景的解决方案，它通过基于相似用户（lookalike users）为新物品扩展反馈。传统 lookalike 算法面临如下局限：(1) 基于规则或模型的方法无法有效建模相似用户，进而无法进一步改善推荐；(2) 难以利用交互信号并融合现代推荐系统中的多样化特征。受 lookalike 算法启发，我们提出 Next-User Retrieval，一种通过生成式 next-user 建模来增强冷启动推荐的新框架。具体而言，我们采用基于 Transformer 的模型来捕捉近期交互用户之间的单向关系，并利用这些序列生成最有可能与该物品交互的下一个潜在用户。物品的额外特征也被作为 prefix prompt embedding 整合进来，以辅助 next-user 生成。我们通过离线实验和在线 A/B test 评估了 Next-User Retrieval 的有效性。在 Douyin，我们的方法取得了显著提升，日活跃用户数提升 0.0142%，发布量提升 0.1144%，体现了其实际适用性与可扩展性。
DualGR: Generative Retrieval with Long and Short-Term Interests Modeling
USTC / 快手2025推荐工业工业落地系统generative retrieval, long-short term interests, semantic ID decoding, exposure-aware next-token prediction, dual-branch routingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsZhongchao Yi, Kai Feng, Xiaojian Ma, Yalong Wang, Yongqi Liu, Han Li, Zhengyang Zhou, Yang Wang
Abstract (EN)
In large-scale industrial recommendation systems, retrieval must produce high-quality candidates from massive corpora under strict latency. Recently, Generative Retrieval (GR) has emerged as a viable alternative to Embedding-Based Retrieval (EBR), which quantizes items into a finite token space and decodes candidates autoregressively, providing a scalable path that explicitly models target-history interactions via cross-attention. However, deploying GR in short-video feeds remains challenged by long-short interest interference, context-induced noise in hierarchical SID generation, and the lack of explicit learning from exposed-but-unclicked feedback. To address these challenges, we propose DualGR, which combines (i) a Dual-Branch Long/Short-Term Router (DBR) with selective activation, (ii) Search-based SID Decoding (S2D) that constrains fine-level decoding within the current coarse bucket for efficiency and noise control, and (iii) an Exposure-aware Next-Token Prediction Loss (ENTP-Loss) that treats unclicked exposures as coarse-level hard negatives to promote timely interest fade-out. On the large-scale Kuaishou short-video recommendation system, DualGR has achieved outstanding performance. Online A/B testing shows +0.527% video views and +0.432% watch time lifts, validating DualGR as a practical and effective paradigm for industrial generative retrieval.
摘要 (中文)
在大规模工业推荐系统中，retrieval 必须在严格的延迟约束下从海量语料中产出高质量候选。近来，Generative Retrieval（GR）已成为 Embedding-Based Retrieval（EBR）的可行替代方案，它将物品量化为有限的 token 空间并自回归解码候选，提供了一条通过 cross-attention 显式建模目标-历史交互的可扩展路径。然而，在短视频信息流中部署 GR 仍面临长短期兴趣干扰、层级式 SID 生成中上下文引入的噪声，以及缺乏对曝光未点击反馈的显式学习等挑战。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 DualGR，它结合了 (i) 带选择性激活的双分支长/短期路由（DBR），(ii) 基于搜索的 SID 解码（S2D），将细粒度解码限制在当前粗桶内以提升效率并控制噪声，以及 (iii) 曝光感知下一 token 预测损失（ENTP-Loss），将未点击曝光视为粗粒度难负样本，以促进兴趣及时衰减。在大规模快手短视频推荐系统上，DualGR 取得了出色表现。在线 A/B 测试显示视频观看量提升 +0.527%、观看时长提升 +0.432%，验证了 DualGR 是工业生成式检索的一个实用且有效的范式。
Multimodal Large Language Models with Adaptive Preference Optimization for Sequential Recommendation
合肥工业大学 / 新加坡国立2025推荐学术多模态推荐multimodal LLM, sequential recommendation, preference optimization, DPO, hardness-aware samplingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYu Wang, Yonghui Yang, Le Wu, Yi Zhang, Fei Liu, Richang Hong
Abstract (EN)
Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have opened new avenues for sequential recommendation by enabling natural language reasoning over user behavior sequences. A common approach formulates recommendation as a language modeling task, where interaction histories are transformed into prompts and user preferences are learned via supervised fine-tuning. However, these methods operate solely in the textual modality and often miss users' fine-grained interests, especially when shaped by rich visual signals such as product images or movie posters. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer a promising alternative by aligning text and vision in a shared semantic space. A prevalent training paradigm applies Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) followed by Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to model user preferences. Yet, two core challenges remain: 1) Imbalanced sample hardness, where random negative sampling causes overfitting on easy examples and under-training on hard ones; 2) Cross-modal semantic bias, where the fixed reference model in DPO prevents the policy model from correcting modality misalignments--especially over long sequences. To address these issues, we propose a Multimodal LLM framework that integrates Hardness-aware and Noise-regularized preference optimization for Recommendation (HaNoRec). Specifically, HaNoRec dynamically adjusts optimization weights based on both the estimated hardness of each training sample and the policy model's real-time responsiveness, prioritizing harder examples. It further introduces Gaussian-perturbed distribution optimization on output logits to enhance cross-modal semantic consistency and reduce modality bias inherited from the reference model.
摘要 (中文)
近年来，Large Language Models（LLM）的进展为序列推荐开辟了新途径，使其能够对用户行为序列进行自然语言推理。一种常见做法是把推荐建模为语言建模任务，将交互历史转化为 prompt，并通过监督微调学习用户偏好。然而，这些方法仅在文本模态下运作，往往会遗漏用户的细粒度兴趣，尤其是当这些兴趣由商品图片或电影海报等丰富的视觉信号塑造时。Multimodal Large Language Models（MLLM）通过在共享语义空间中对齐文本与视觉，提供了一种有前景的替代方案。一种流行的训练范式是先做 Supervised Fine-Tuning（SFT），再用 Direct Preference Optimization（DPO）来建模用户偏好。但仍有两大核心挑战：1）样本难度不均衡，随机负采样会导致在简单样本上过拟合、在困难样本上训练不足；2）跨模态语义偏差，DPO 中固定的 reference model 使 policy model 无法纠正模态错位——在长序列上尤为明显。为解决这些问题，我们提出一个将难度感知与噪声正则化偏好优化相结合的多模态 LLM 推荐框架（HaNoRec）。具体而言，HaNoRec 依据每个训练样本的估计难度以及 policy model 的实时响应性动态调整优化权重，优先优化更困难的样本。它进一步在输出 logits 上引入高斯扰动分布优化，以增强跨模态语义一致性并减少从 reference model 继承的模态偏差。
Token-Efficient Item Representation via Images for LLM Recommender Systems
Amazon2025推荐工业多模态推荐item representation, token efficiency, image-based representation, LLM recommenderPDF · arXiv
AuthorsKibum Kim, Sein Kim, Hongseok Kang, Jiwan Kim, Heewoong Noh, Yeonjun In, Kanghoon Yoon, Jinoh Oh, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as a powerful backbone for recommender systems. Existing LLM-based recommender systems take two different approaches for representing items in natural language, i.e., Attribute-based Representation and Description-based Representation. In this work, we aim to address the trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness that these two approaches encounter, when representing items consumed by users. Based on our interesting observation that there is a significant information overlap between images and descriptions associated with items, we propose a novel method, Item representation for LLM-based Recommender system (I-LLMRec). Our main idea is to leverage images as an alternative to lengthy textual descriptions for representing items, aiming at reducing token usage while preserving the rich semantic information of item descriptions. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that I-LLMRec outperforms existing methods in both efficiency and effectiveness by leveraging images. Moreover, a further appeal of I-LLMRec is its ability to reduce sensitivity to noise in descriptions, leading to more robust recommendations.
摘要 (中文)
Large Language Models（LLM）近来已成为推荐系统的强大骨干。现有基于 LLM 的推荐系统在用自然语言表示商品时采取两种不同方式，即 Attribute-based Representation 与 Description-based Representation。在本工作中，我们旨在解决这两种方式在表示用户所消费商品时面临的效率与效果之间的权衡。基于我们的一个有趣观察——商品相关的图片与描述之间存在显著的信息重叠，我们提出一种新方法，即面向 LLM 推荐系统的商品表示（I-LLMRec）。我们的核心思想是利用图片替代冗长的文本描述来表示商品，以在保留商品描述丰富语义信息的同时减少 token 使用量。通过大量实验，我们证明 I-LLMRec 借助图片在效率与效果两方面均优于现有方法。此外，I-LLMRec 还有一个额外吸引力：它能降低对描述中噪声的敏感性，从而带来更鲁棒的推荐。
Hierarchical Time-Aware Mixture of Experts for Multi-Modal Sequential Recommendation
USTC / 南航2025推荐学术多模态推荐multi-modal sequential recommendation, mixture of experts, temporal embedding, multi-task learning, contrastive learningPDF · arXiv
AuthorsShengzhe Zhang, Liyi Chen, Dazhong Shen, Chao Wang, Hui Xiong
Abstract (EN)
Multi-modal sequential recommendation (SR) leverages multi-modal data to learn more comprehensive item features and user preferences than traditional SR methods, which has become a critical topic in both academia and industry. Existing methods typically focus on enhancing multi-modal information utility through adaptive modality fusion to capture the evolving of user preference from user-item interaction sequences. However, most of them overlook the interference caused by redundant interest-irrelevant information contained in rich multi-modal data. Additionally, they primarily rely on implicit temporal information based solely on chronological ordering, neglecting explicit temporal signals that could more effectively represent dynamic user interest over time. To address these limitations, we propose a Hierarchical time-aware Mixture of experts for multi-modal Sequential Recommendation (HM4SR) with a two-level Mixture of Experts (MoE) and a multi-task learning strategy. Specifically, the first MoE, named Interactive MoE, extracts essential user interest-related information from the multi-modal data of each item. Then, the second MoE, termed Temporal MoE, captures user dynamic interests by introducing explicit temporal embeddings from timestamps in modality encoding. To further address data sparsity, we propose three auxiliary supervision tasks: sequence-level category prediction (CP) for item feature understanding, contrastive learning on ID (IDCL) to align sequence context with user interests, and placeholder contrastive learning (PCL) to integrate temporal information with modalities for dynamic interest modeling. Extensive experiments on four public datasets verify the effectiveness of HM4SR compared to several state-of-the-art approaches.
摘要 (中文)
多模态序列推荐（SR）利用多模态数据学习比传统 SR 方法更全面的 item 特征和用户偏好，已成为学术界和工业界的重要课题。现有方法通常聚焦于通过自适应模态融合增强多模态信息效用，以从用户-item 交互序列中捕捉用户偏好的演化。然而，大多数方法忽视了丰富多模态数据中冗余的、与兴趣无关的信息所造成的干扰。此外，它们主要依赖仅仅基于时间先后顺序的隐式时间信息，而忽略了能够更有效表示用户兴趣随时间动态变化的显式时间信号。为应对这些局限，我们提出面向多模态序列推荐的分层时间感知专家混合模型（HM4SR），其包含两级 Mixture of Experts（MoE）以及多任务学习策略。具体而言，第一级 MoE 称为 Interactive MoE，从每个 item 的多模态数据中提取与用户兴趣相关的关键信息。随后，第二级 MoE 称为 Temporal MoE，通过在模态编码中引入来自时间戳的显式时间 embedding 来捕捉用户的动态兴趣。为进一步应对数据稀疏问题，我们提出三个辅助监督任务：用于 item 特征理解的序列级类别预测（CP）、用于将序列上下文与用户兴趣对齐的 ID 对比学习（IDCL），以及用于将时间信息与模态融合以建模动态兴趣的 placeholder contrastive learning（PCL）。在四个公开数据集上的大量实验验证了 HM4SR 相较于多个 state-of-the-art 方法的有效性。
FindRec: Stein-Guided Entropic Flow for Multi-Modal Sequential Recommendation
香港城市大学 / 北航2025推荐学术多模态推荐multi-modal sequential recommendation, information disentanglement, Stein kernel, cross-modal expert routing, MambaPDF · arXiv
AuthorsMaolin Wang, Yutian Xiao, Binhao Wang, Sheng Zhang, Shanshan Ye, Wanyu Wang, Hongzhi Yin, Ruocheng Guo, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Modern recommendation systems face significant challenges in processing multimodal sequential data, particularly in temporal dynamics modeling and information flow coordination. Traditional approaches struggle with distribution discrepancies between heterogeneous features and noise interference in multimodal signals. We propose \textbf{FindRec}~ (\textbf{F}lexible unified \textbf{in}formation \textbf{d}isentanglement for multi-modal sequential \textbf{Rec}ommendation), introducing a novel "information flow-control-output" paradigm. The framework features two key innovations: (1) A Stein kernel-based Integrated Information Coordination Module (IICM) that theoretically guarantees distribution consistency between multimodal features and ID streams, and (2) A cross-modal expert routing mechanism that adaptively filters and combines multimodal features based on their contextual relevance. Our approach leverages multi-head subspace decomposition for routing stability and RBF-Stein gradient for unbiased distribution alignment, enhanced by linear-complexity Mamba layers for efficient temporal modeling. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate FindRec's superior performance over state-of-the-art baselines, particularly in handling long sequences and noisy multimodal inputs. Our framework achieves both improved recommendation accuracy and enhanced model interpretability through its modular design. The implementation code is available anonymously online for easy reproducibility~\footnote{https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/FindRec}.
摘要 (中文)
现代推荐系统在处理多模态序列数据时面临重大挑战，尤其是在时间动态建模和信息流协调方面。传统方法难以应对异构特征之间的分布差异以及多模态信号中的噪声干扰。我们提出 \textbf{FindRec}（\textbf{F}lexible unified \textbf{in}formation \textbf{d}isentanglement for multi-modal sequential \textbf{Rec}ommendation），引入一种新颖的"信息流-控制-输出"范式。该框架具有两项关键创新：（1）基于 Stein 核的 Integrated Information Coordination Module（IICM），它在理论上保证了多模态特征与 ID 流之间的分布一致性；（2）跨模态专家路由机制，它根据上下文相关性自适应地过滤和组合多模态特征。我们的方法利用多头子空间分解来提升路由稳定性，利用 RBF-Stein 梯度实现无偏的分布对齐，并通过线性复杂度的 Mamba 层增强高效时序建模。在三个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，FindRec 优于 state-of-the-art 的 baseline，尤其是在处理长序列和含噪多模态输入方面。我们的框架通过其模块化设计，同时实现了推荐精度的提升和模型可解释性的增强。实现代码已匿名在线提供以便复现~\footnote{https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/FindRec}。
Native Sparse Attention: Hardware-Aligned and Natively Trainable Sparse Attention
DeepSeek2025通用工业基础设施 / 基础模型sparse attention, long-context modeling, hardware-aligned optimization, hierarchical sparse strategy, end-to-end trainingPDF
AuthorsJingyang Yuan, Huazuo Gao, Damai Dai, Junyu Luo, Liang Zhao, Zhengyan Zhang, Zhenda Xie, Y. X. Wei, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Long-context modeling is crucial for next-generation language models, yet the high computational cost of standard attention mechanisms poses significant computational challenges. Sparse attention offers a promising direction for improving efficiency while maintaining model capabilities. We present NSA, a Natively trainable Sparse Attention mechanism that integrates algorithmic innovations with hardware-aligned optimizations to achieve efficient long-context modeling. NSA employs a dynamic hierarchical sparse strategy, combining coarse-grained token compression with fine-grained token selection to preserve both global context awareness and local precision. Our approach advances sparse attention design with two key innovations: (1) We achieve substantial speedups through arithmetic intensity-balanced algorithm design, with implementation optimizations for modern hardware. (2) We enable end-to-end training, reducing pretraining computation without sacrificing model performance. As shown in Figure 1, experiments show the model pretrained with NSA maintains or exceeds Full Attention models across general benchmarks, long-context tasks, and instruction-based reasoning. Meanwhile, NSA achieves substantial speedups over Full Attention on 64k-length sequences across decoding, forward propagation, and backward propagation, validating its efficiency throughout the model lifecycle.
摘要 (中文)
长上下文建模对下一代语言模型至关重要，但标准 attention 机制的高计算成本带来了显著的计算挑战。稀疏 attention 为在保持模型能力的同时提升效率提供了有前景的方向。我们提出 NSA，一种原生可训练的稀疏 attention（Natively trainable Sparse Attention）机制，它将算法创新与面向硬件的优化相结合，以实现高效的长上下文建模。NSA 采用动态分层稀疏策略，将粗粒度 token 压缩与细粒度 token 选择相结合，同时保留全局上下文感知与局部精度。我们的方法通过两项关键创新推进了稀疏 attention 的设计：（1）通过算术强度均衡的算法设计以及针对现代硬件的实现优化，实现了显著加速；（2）支持端到端训练，在不牺牲模型性能的前提下降低预训练计算量。如图 1 所示，实验表明，用 NSA 预训练的模型在通用 benchmark、长上下文任务以及基于指令的推理上均保持或超越了 Full Attention 模型。同时，在 64k 长度序列上，NSA 在 decoding、前向传播与反向传播上相较 Full Attention 都取得了显著加速，验证了其在整个模型生命周期中的高效性。
Gated Attention for Large Language Models: Non-linearity, Sparsity, and Attention-Sink-Free
阿里2025通用工业基础设施 / 基础模型gated attention, sigmoid gate, sparsity, attention sink, long-context extrapolationPDF
AuthorsZihan Qiu, Zekun Wang, Bo Zheng, Zeyu Huang, Kaiyue Wen, Songlin Yang, Rui Men, Le Yu, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Gating mechanisms have been widely utilized, from early models like LSTMs (Hochreiter & Schmidhuber, 1997) and Highway Networks (Srivastava et al., 2015) to recent state space models (Gu & Dao, 2023), linear attention (Hua et al., 2022), and also softmax attention (Lin et al., 2025). Yet, existing literature rarely examines the specific effects of gating. In this work, we conduct comprehensive experiments to systematically investigate gating-augmented softmax attention variants. Specifically, we perform a comprehensive comparison over 30 variants of 15B Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models and 1.7B dense models trained on a 3.5 trillion token dataset. Our central finding is that a simple modification—applying a head-specific sigmoid gate after the Scaled Dot-Product Attention (SDPA)—consistently improves performance. This modification also enhances training stability, tolerates larger learning rates, and improves scaling properties. By comparing various gating positions and computational variants, we attribute this effectiveness to two key factors: (1) introducing non-linearity upon the low-rank mapping in the softmax attention, and (2) applying query-dependent sparse gating scores to modulate the SDPA output. Notably, we find this sparse gating mechanism mitigates ‘attention sink’ and enhances long-context extrapolation performance, and we also release related codes and models to facilitate future research.
摘要 (中文)
门控机制已被广泛使用，从早期的 LSTM（Hochreiter & Schmidhuber, 1997）和 Highway Networks（Srivastava et al., 2015），到近期的状态空间模型（Gu & Dao, 2023）、线性注意力（Hua et al., 2022），以及 softmax 注意力（Lin et al., 2025）。然而，现有文献很少考察门控的具体作用。在本工作中，我们开展了全面的实验，系统性地研究了引入门控的 softmax 注意力变体。具体而言，我们在一个 3.5 万亿 token 的数据集上，对 15B 的 Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型和 1.7B 的稠密模型的 30 种变体进行了全面比较。我们的核心发现是：一个简单的改动——在 Scaled Dot-Product Attention（SDPA）之后施加一个按 head 独立的 sigmoid 门控——能持续提升性能。这一改动还增强了训练稳定性，允许更大的学习率，并改善了 scaling 特性。通过比较不同的门控位置和计算变体，我们将这一有效性归因于两个关键因素：(1) 在 softmax attention 的低秩映射上引入了非线性；(2) 施加依赖 query 的稀疏门控分数来调制 SDPA 的输出。值得注意的是，我们发现这种稀疏门控机制缓解了“attention sink”现象并增强了长上下文外推性能。我们同时开源了相关代码和模型以促进未来研究。
RASTP: Representation-Aware Semantic Token Pruning for Generative Recommendation with Semantic Identifiers
浙大2025通用学术基础设施 / 基础模型semantic token pruning, generative recommendation, semantic identifiers, attention centrality, token importancePDF · arXiv
AuthorsTianyu Zhan, Kairui Fu, Zheqi Lv, Shengyu Zhang
Abstract (EN)
Generative recommendation systems typically leverage Semantic Identifiers (SIDs), which represent each item as a sequence of tokens that encode semantic information. However, representing item ID with multiple SIDs significantly increases input sequence length, which is a major determinant of computational complexity and memory consumption. While existing efforts primarily focus on optimizing attention computation and KV cache, we propose RASTP (Representation-Aware Semantic Token Pruning), which directly prunes less informative tokens in the input sequence. Specifically, RASTP evaluates token importance by combining semantic saliency, measured via representation magnitude, and attention centrality, derived from cumulative attention weights. Since RASTP dynamically prunes low-information or irrelevant semantic tokens, experiments on three real-world Amazon datasets show that RASTP reduces training time by 26.7\%, while maintaining or slightly improving recommendation performance. The code has been open-sourced at https://github.com/Yuzt-zju/RASTP.
摘要 (中文)
生成式推荐系统通常利用 Semantic Identifiers（SIDs），即将每个物品表示为编码语义信息的 token 序列。然而，用多个 SID 表示物品 ID 会显著增加输入序列长度，而这是计算复杂度与内存消耗的主要决定因素。尽管现有工作主要聚焦于优化注意力计算与 KV cache，我们提出 RASTP（Representation-Aware Semantic Token Pruning），直接剪除输入序列中信息量较少的 token。具体而言，RASTP 通过结合经由表示幅值度量的语义显著性与由累积注意力权重导出的注意力中心性来评估 token 重要性。由于 RASTP 动态剪除低信息或无关的语义 token，在三个真实 Amazon 数据集上的实验表明，RASTP 将训练时间减少 26.7%，同时保持或略微提升推荐性能。代码已开源于 https://github.com/Yuzt-zju/RASTP。
BlossomRec: Block-level Fused Sparse Attention Mechanism for Sequential Recommendations
香港城市大学 / Rutgers2025通用学术基础设施 / 基础模型sparse attention, sequential recommendation, block-level fused sparse attention, long-term and short-term interests, learnable gated outputPDF · arXiv
AuthorsMengyang Ma, Xiaopeng Li, Wanyu Wang, Zhaocheng Du, Jingtong Gao, Pengyue Jia, Yuyang Ye, Yiqi Wang, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Transformer structures have been widely used in sequential recommender systems (SRS). However, as user interaction histories increase, computational time and memory requirements also grow. This is mainly caused by the standard attention mechanism. Although there exist many methods employing efficient attention and SSM-based models, these approaches struggle to effectively model long sequences and may exhibit unstable performance on short sequences. To address these challenges, we design a sparse attention mechanism, BlossomRec, which models both long-term and short-term user interests through attention computation to achieve stable performance across sequences of varying lengths. Specifically, we categorize user interests in recommendation systems into long-term and short-term interests, and compute them using two distinct sparse attention patterns, with the results combined through a learnable gated output. Theoretically, it significantly reduces the number of interactions participating in attention computation. Extensive experiments on four public datasets demonstrate that BlossomRec, when integrated with state-of-the-art Transformer-based models, achieves comparable or even superior performance while significantly reducing memory usage, providing strong evidence of BlossomRec's efficiency and effectiveness. The code is available at https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/WWW2026_BlossomRec.
摘要 (中文)
Transformer 结构已被广泛用于序列推荐系统（SRS）。然而，随着用户交互历史增长，计算时间和内存需求也随之增加。这主要是由标准注意力机制造成的。尽管已有许多方法采用高效注意力和基于 SSM 的模型，但这些方法难以有效建模长序列，并且在短序列上可能表现出不稳定的性能。为应对这些挑战，我们设计了一种稀疏注意力机制 BlossomRec，它通过注意力计算同时对用户的长期和短期兴趣建模，从而在不同长度的序列上实现稳定性能。具体而言，我们将推荐系统中的用户兴趣划分为长期兴趣和短期兴趣，并使用两种不同的稀疏注意力模式进行计算，其结果通过可学习的门控输出进行组合。在理论上，它显著减少了参与注意力计算的交互数量。在四个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，当与 SOTA 的基于 Transformer 的模型集成时，BlossomRec 在显著降低内存使用的同时取得了相当甚至更优的性能，有力地证明了 BlossomRec 的高效性与有效性。代码可在 https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/WWW2026_BlossomRec 获取。
Pre-train and Fine-tune: Recommenders as Large Models
香港中文大学 / 阿里2025推荐工业CTR 训练 / 工程fine-tuning, information bottleneck, information-aware adaptive kernel, pre-trained recommender, multi-domainPDF · arXiv
AuthorsZhenhao Jiang, Chenghao Chen, Hao Feng, Yu Yang, Jin Liu, Jie Zhang, Jia Jia, Ning Hu
Abstract (EN)
In reality, users have different interests in different periods, regions, scenes, etc. Such changes in interest are so drastic that they are difficult to be captured by recommenders. Existing multi-domain learning can alleviate this problem. However, the structure of the industrial recommendation system is complex, the amount of data is huge, and the training cost is extremely high, so it is difficult to modify the structure of the industrial recommender and re-train it. To fill this gap, we consider recommenders as large pre-trained models and fine-tune them. We first propose the theory of the information bottleneck for fine-tuning and present an explanation for the fine-tuning technique in recommenders. To tailor for recommendation, we design an information-aware adaptive kernel (IAK) technique to fine-tune the pre-trained recommender. Specifically, we define fine-tuning as two phases: knowledge compression and knowledge matching and let the training stage of IAK explicitly approximate these two phases. Our proposed approach designed from the essence of fine-tuning is well interpretable. Extensive online and offline experiments show the superiority of our proposed method. Besides, we also share unique and important lessons we learned when deploying the method in a large-scale online platform. We also present the potential issues of fine-tuning techniques in recommendation systems and the corresponding solutions. The recommender with IAK technique has been deployed on the homepage of a billion-scale online food platform for several months and has yielded considerable profits in our business.
摘要 (中文)
现实中，用户在不同时期、地区、场景等下的兴趣各不相同。这种兴趣变化如此剧烈，以至于推荐器难以捕捉。现有多域学习可以缓解这一问题。然而，工业推荐系统结构复杂、数据量庞大且训练成本极高，因此难以修改工业推荐器的结构并重新训练。为填补这一空白，我们将推荐器视为大型预训练模型并对其进行 fine-tune。我们首先提出用于 fine-tuning 的信息瓶颈理论，并对推荐器中的 fine-tuning 技术给出解释。为适配推荐任务，我们设计了一种信息感知自适应核（IAK）技术来 fine-tune 预训练推荐器。具体而言，我们将 fine-tuning 定义为两个阶段：知识压缩与知识匹配，并让 IAK 的训练过程显式逼近这两个阶段。我们提出的方法从 fine-tuning 的本质出发进行设计，具有良好的可解释性。大量线上与线下实验表明了所提方法的优越性。此外，我们还分享了将该方法部署到大规模线上平台时所获得的独特而重要的经验教训。我们还给出了推荐系统中 fine-tuning 技术的潜在问题及其相应解决方案。采用 IAK 技术的推荐器已在十亿级规模的线上外卖平台首页部署数月，并在我们的业务中带来了可观的收益。
ESANS: Effective and Semantic-Aware Negative Sampling for Large-Scale Retrieval Systems
阿里国际2025推荐工业CTR 训练 / 工程negative sampling, embedding-based retrieval, dense interpolation, semantic-aware clustering, false negativesPDF · arXiv
AuthorsHaibo Xing, Kanefumi Matsuyama, Hao Deng, Jinxin Hu, Yu Zhang, Xiaoyi Zeng
Abstract (EN)
Industrial recommendation systems typically involve a two-stage process: retrieval and ranking, which aims to match users with millions of items. In the retrieval stage, classic embedding-based retrieval (EBR) methods depend on effective negative sampling techniques to enhance both performance and efficiency. However, existing techniques often suffer from false negatives, high cost for ensuring sampling quality and semantic information deficiency. To address these limitations, we propose Effective and Semantic-Aware Negative Sampling (ESANS), which integrates two key components: Effective Dense Interpolation Strategy (EDIS) and Multimodal Semantic-Aware Clustering (MSAC). EDIS generates virtual samples within the low-dimensional embedding space to improve the diversity and density of the sampling distribution while minimizing computational costs. MSAC refines the negative sampling distribution by hierarchically clustering item representations based on multimodal information (visual, textual, behavioral), ensuring semantic consistency and reducing false negatives. Extensive offline and online experiments demonstrate the superior efficiency and performance of ESANS.
摘要 (中文)
工业推荐系统通常包含两个阶段：retrieval 与 ranking，旨在将用户与数百万 item 进行匹配。在 retrieval 阶段，经典的基于 embedding 的检索（EBR）方法依赖有效的负采样技术来同时提升性能与效率。然而，现有技术往往面临 false negatives、保证采样质量的高成本以及语义信息不足的问题。为应对这些局限，我们提出 Effective and Semantic-Aware Negative Sampling（ESANS），它整合了两个关键组件：Effective Dense Interpolation Strategy（EDIS）与 Multimodal Semantic-Aware Clustering（MSAC）。EDIS 在低维 embedding 空间内生成虚拟样本，以提升采样分布的多样性与密度，同时将计算成本降到最低。MSAC 基于多模态信息（视觉、文本、行为）对 item 表示进行层次化聚类，从而细化负采样分布，确保语义一致性并减少 false negatives。大量离线与线上实验证明了 ESANS 更优的效率与性能。
IDGenRec: LLM-RecSys Alignment with Textual ID Learning
Rutgers2024推荐学术Semantic IDgenerative recommendation, textual ID learning, LLM alignment, foundation model, zero-shotPDF · arXiv
AuthorsJuntao Tan, Yingqiang Ge, Shuyuan Xu, Wenyue Hua, Zelong Li, Yongfeng Zhang
Abstract (EN)
Generative recommendation based on Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed the traditional ranking-based recommendation style into a text-to-text generation paradigm. However, in contrast to standard NLP tasks that inherently operate on human vocabulary, current research in generative recommendations struggles to effectively encode recommendation items within the text-to-text framework using concise yet meaningful ID representations. To better align LLMs with recommendation needs, we propose IDGen, representing each item as a unique, concise, semantically rich, platform-agnostic textual ID using human language tokens. This is achieved by training a textual ID generator alongside the LLM-based recommender, enabling seamless integration of personalized recommendations into natural language generation. Notably, as user history is expressed in natural language and decoupled from the original dataset, our approach suggests the potential for a foundational generative recommendation model. Experiments show that our framework consistently surpasses existing models in sequential recommendation under standard experimental setting. Then, we explore the possibility of training a foundation recommendation model with the proposed method on data collected from 19 different datasets and tested its recommendation performance on 6 unseen datasets across different platforms under a completely zero-shot setting. The results show that the zero-shot performance of the pre-trained foundation model is comparable to or even better than some traditional recommendation models based on supervised training, showing the potential of the IDGen paradigm serving as the foundation model for generative recommendation. Code and data are open-sourced at this https URL.
摘要 (中文)
基于 Large Language Models (LLMs) 的生成式推荐将传统的基于排序的推荐范式转变为 text-to-text 生成范式。然而，与天然基于人类词表运作的标准 NLP 任务不同，当前生成式推荐研究难以在 text-to-text 框架下用简洁而有意义的 ID 表示来有效编码推荐物品。为了更好地使 LLM 与推荐需求对齐，我们提出 IDGen，使用人类语言 token 将每个物品表示为一个唯一、简洁、语义丰富且与平台无关的 textual ID。其实现方式是与基于 LLM 的推荐器一同训练一个 textual ID generator，从而将个性化推荐无缝融入自然语言生成之中。值得注意的是，由于用户历史以自然语言表达并与原始数据集解耦，我们的方法展现了构建基础生成式推荐模型的潜力。实验表明，在标准实验设置下，我们的框架在序列推荐任务上持续优于现有模型。随后，我们探索了用所提方法在从 19 个不同数据集收集的数据上训练基础推荐模型的可能性，并在来自不同平台的 6 个未见数据集上以完全 zero-shot 设置测试其推荐性能。结果表明，预训练基础模型的 zero-shot 性能与某些基于监督训练的传统推荐模型相当、甚至更优，显示了 IDGen 范式作为生成式推荐基础模型的潜力。代码和数据已开源于 this https URL。
EAGER: Two-Stream Generative Recommender with Behavior-Semantic Collaboration
浙大 / 华为2024推荐工业Semantic IDgenerative retrieval, two-stream generation, behavior-semantic collaboration, semantic tokens, contrastive learningPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYe Wang, Jiahao Xun, Minjie Hong, Jieming Zhu, Tao Jin, Wang Lin, Haoyuan Li, Linjun Li, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Generative retrieval has recently emerged as a promising approach to sequential recommendation, framing candidate item retrieval as an autoregressive sequence generation problem. However, existing generative methods typically focus solely on either behavioral or semantic aspects of item information, neglecting their complementary nature and thus resulting in limited effectiveness. To address this limitation, we introduce EAGER, a novel generative recommendation framework that seamlessly integrates both behavioral and semantic information. Specifically, we identify three key challenges in combining these two types of information: a unified generative architecture capable of handling two feature types, ensuring sufficient and independent learning for each type, and fostering subtle interactions that enhance collaborative information utilization. To achieve these goals, we propose (1) a two-stream generation architecture leveraging a shared encoder and two separate decoders to decode behavior tokens and semantic tokens with a confidence-based ranking strategy; (2) a global contrastive task with summary tokens to achieve discriminative decoding for each type of information; and (3) a semantic-guided transfer task designed to implicitly promote cross-interactions through reconstruction and estimation objectives. We validate the effectiveness of EAGER on four public benchmarks, demonstrating its superior performance compared to existing methods.
摘要 (中文)
生成式检索近来已成为序列推荐的一种有前景的方法，将候选物品检索表述为自回归序列生成问题。然而，现有生成式方法通常仅关注物品信息的行为或语义单一方面，忽视了两者的互补性，因而效果有限。为弥补这一局限，我们提出 EAGER，一个新颖的生成式推荐框架，无缝整合行为与语义信息。具体而言，我们识别出结合这两类信息时的三个关键挑战：一个能够处理两类特征的统一生成架构、确保每类信息得到充分且独立的学习，以及促进增强协同信息利用的微妙交互。为实现这些目标，我们提出 (1) 一种双流生成架构，利用共享 encoder 与两个独立 decoder，以基于置信度的排序策略解码行为 token 与语义 token；(2) 一个带 summary token 的全局对比任务，以实现对每类信息的判别式解码；以及 (3) 一个语义引导迁移任务，旨在通过重建与估计目标隐式促进跨模态交互。我们在四个公开基准上验证了 EAGER 的有效性，展示其相比现有方法的优越性能。
TokenRec: Learning to Tokenize ID for LLM-based Generative Recommendation
港理工2024推荐学术Semantic IDID tokenization, LLM-based recommendation, vector quantization, generative retrieval, collaborative filteringPDF · arXiv
AuthorsHaohao Qu, Wenqi Fan, Zihuai Zhao, Qing Li
Abstract (EN)
There is a growing interest in utilizing large-scale language models (LLMs) to advance next-generation Recommender Systems (RecSys), driven by their outstanding language understanding and in-context learning capabilities. In this scenario, tokenizing (i.e., indexing) users and items becomes essential for ensuring a seamless alignment of LLMs with recommendations. While several studies have made progress in representing users and items through textual contents or latent representations, challenges remain in efficiently capturing high-order collaborative knowledge into discrete tokens that are compatible with LLMs. Additionally, the majority of existing tokenization approaches often face difficulties in generalizing effectively to new/unseen users or items that were not in the training corpus. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework called TokenRec, which introduces not only an effective ID tokenization strategy but also an efficient retrieval paradigm for LLM-based recommendations. Specifically, our tokenization strategy, Masked Vector-Quantized (MQ) Tokenizer, involves quantizing the masked user/item representations learned from collaborative filtering into discrete tokens, thus achieving a smooth incorporation of high-order collaborative knowledge and a generalizable tokenization of users and items for LLM-based RecSys. Meanwhile, our generative retrieval paradigm is designed to efficiently recommend top-$K$ items for users to eliminate the need for the time-consuming auto-regressive decoding and beam search processes used by LLMs, thus significantly reducing inference time. Comprehensive experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods, demonstrating that TokenRec outperforms competitive benchmarks, including both traditional recommender systems and emerging LLM-based recommender systems.
摘要 (中文)
利用大规模语言模型（LLM）推进下一代推荐系统（RecSys）的兴趣日益增长，这得益于其出色的语言理解与上下文学习能力。在这一场景下，对用户与物品进行 tokenizing（即索引化）对于确保 LLM 与推荐任务的无缝对齐至关重要。尽管已有若干研究在通过文本内容或潜在表示来表示用户与物品方面取得进展，但如何高效地将高阶协同知识捕获为与 LLM 兼容的离散 token 仍存在挑战。此外，大多数现有 tokenization 方法往往难以有效泛化到训练语料中未出现的新用户或新物品。为应对这些挑战，我们提出一个名为 TokenRec 的新框架，它不仅引入了有效的 ID tokenization 策略，还提出了面向基于 LLM 推荐的高效检索范式。具体而言，我们的 tokenization 策略 Masked Vector-Quantized（MQ）Tokenizer 将从协同过滤中学到的掩码用户/物品表示量化为离散 token，从而实现高阶协同知识的平滑融入，以及面向基于 LLM 的 RecSys 的用户与物品可泛化 tokenization。同时，我们的生成式检索范式旨在高效地为用户推荐 top-K 物品，从而无需 LLM 所使用的耗时的自回归解码与 beam search 过程，显著降低推理时间。大量实验验证了所提方法的有效性，表明 TokenRec 优于竞争性基准，包括传统推荐系统与新兴的基于 LLM 的推荐系统。
MVIGER: Multi-View Variational Integration of Complementary Knowledge for Generative Recommender
Yonsei University / Korea University2024推荐学术Semantic IDgenerative recommender, prompt template, item index, variational framework, complementary knowledgePDF · arXiv
AuthorsTongyoung Kim, Soojin Yoon, SeongKu Kang, Jinyoung Yeo, Dongha Lee
Abstract (EN)
Language Models (LMs) have been widely used in recommender systems to incorporate textual information of items into item IDs, leveraging their advanced language understanding and generation capabilities. Recently, generative recommender systems have utilized the reasoning abilities of LMs to directly generate index tokens for potential items of interest based on the user's interaction history. To inject diverse item knowledge into LMs, prompt templates with detailed task descriptions and various indexing techniques derived from diverse item information have been explored. This paper focuses on the inconsistency in outputs generated by variations in input prompt templates and item index types, even with the same user's interaction history. Our in-depth quantitative analysis reveals that preference knowledge learned from diverse prompt templates and heterogeneous indices differs significantly, indicating a high potential for complementarity. To fully exploit this complementarity and provide consistent performance under varying prompts and item indices, we propose MVIGER, a unified variational framework that models selection among these information sources as a categorical latent variable with a learnable prior. During inference, this prior enables the model to adaptively select the most relevant source or aggregate predictions across multiple sources, thereby ensuring high-quality recommendation across diverse template-index combinations. We validate the effectiveness of MVIGER on three real-world datasets, demonstrating its superior performance over existing generative recommender baselines through the effective integration of complementary knowledge.
摘要 (中文)
语言模型（LM）已被广泛用于推荐系统，借助其先进的语言理解与生成能力，将物品的文本信息融入物品 ID。近来，生成式推荐系统利用 LM 的推理能力，基于用户的交互历史直接为潜在感兴趣的物品生成索引 token。为了向 LM 注入多样的物品知识，研究者探索了带有详细任务描述的提示模板，以及源自多样物品信息的各种索引技术。本文关注的是：即使用户的交互历史相同，输入提示模板和物品索引类型的变化也会导致生成输出不一致。我们深入的定量分析表明，从不同提示模板和异构索引中学到的偏好知识存在显著差异，这说明它们具有很高的互补潜力。为充分挖掘这种互补性，并在不同的提示和物品索引下提供一致的性能，我们提出了 MVIGER，一个统一的变分框架，它将对这些信息源之间的选择建模为一个带有可学习先验的类别型隐变量。在推理时，该先验使模型能够自适应地选择最相关的信息源，或在多个信息源之间聚合预测，从而在多样的模板-索引组合下确保高质量的推荐。我们在三个真实世界数据集上验证了 MVIGER 的有效性，证明其通过对互补知识的有效整合，性能优于现有的生成式推荐 baseline。
Inductive Generative Recommendation via Retrieval-based Speculation
UCSD2024推荐学术Semantic IDgenerative recommendation, inductive recommendation, retrieval-based speculation, drafter-verifier, guided re-draftingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYijie Ding, Jiacheng Li, Julian McAuley, Yupeng Hou
Abstract (EN)
Generative recommendation (GR) is an emerging paradigm that tokenizes items into discrete tokens and learns to autoregressively generate the next tokens as predictions. While this token-generation paradigm is expected to surpass traditional transductive methods, potentially generating new items directly based on semantics, we empirically show that GR models predominantly generate items seen during training and struggle to recommend unseen items. In this paper, we propose SpecGR, a plug-and-play framework that enables GR models to recommend new items in an inductive setting. SpecGR uses a drafter model with inductive capability to propose candidate items, which may include both existing items and new items. The GR model then acts as a verifier, accepting or rejecting candidates while retaining its strong ranking capabilities. We further introduce the guided re-drafting technique to make the proposed candidates more aligned with the outputs of generative recommendation models, improving the verification efficiency. We consider two variants for drafting: (1) using an auxiliary drafter model for better flexibility, or (2) leveraging the GR model's own encoder for parameter-efficient self-drafting. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that SpecGR exhibits both strong inductive recommendation ability and the best overall performance among the compared methods. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Jamesding000/SpecGR.
摘要 (中文)
生成式推荐（GR）是一种新兴范式，它将物品 token 化为离散 token，并学习自回归地生成下一个 token 作为预测。尽管这种 token 生成范式有望超越传统的 transduction 方法，甚至可能基于语义直接生成新物品，但我们通过实证表明，GR 模型主要生成训练中见过的物品，难以推荐未见过的物品。在本文中，我们提出了 SpecGR，一个即插即用的框架，使 GR 模型能够在 inductive 设置下推荐新物品。SpecGR 使用一个具备 inductive 能力的 drafter 模型来提出候选物品，这些候选既可能包括已有物品，也可能包括新物品。随后，GR 模型作为 verifier，在接受或拒绝候选的同时保留其强大的排序能力。我们进一步引入了引导式重起草（guided re-drafting）技术，使提出的候选与生成式推荐模型的输出更加对齐，从而提升验证效率。我们考虑了两种起草变体：（1）使用一个辅助 drafter 模型以获得更好的灵活性；或（2）利用 GR 模型自身的编码器实现参数高效的自起草（self-drafting）。在三个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，SpecGR 既展现出强大的 inductive 推荐能力，又在所比较的方法中取得了最佳的整体性能。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/Jamesding000/SpecGR 获取。
Actions Speak Louder than Words: Trillion-Parameter Sequential Transducers for Generative Recommendations
Meta2024推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalinggenerative recommenders, sequential transduction, HSTU, scaling law, high-cardinality featuresPDF
AuthorsJiaqi Zhai, Lucy Liao, Xing Liu, Yueming Wang, Rui Li, Xuan Cao, Leon Gao, Zhaojie Gong, Fangda Gu, Michael He, Yinghai Lu, Yu Shi
Abstract (EN)
10000 Large-scale recommendation systems are characterized by their reliance on high cardinality, heterogeneous features and the need to handle tens of billions of user actions on a daily basis. Despite being trained on huge volume of data with thousands of features, most Deep Learning Recommendation Models (DLRMs) in industry fail to scale with compute. Inspired by success achieved by Transformers in language and vision domains, we revisit fundamental design choices in recommendation systems. We reformulate recommendation problems as sequential transduction tasks within a generative modeling framework (“Generative Recommenders”), and propose a new architecture, HSTU, designed for high cardinality, non-stationary streaming recommendation data. HSTU outperforms baselines over synthetic and public datasets by up to 65.8% in NDCG, and is 5.3x to 15.2x faster than FlashAttention2-based Transformers on 8192 length sequences. HSTUbased Generative Recommenders, with 1.5 trillion parameters, improve metrics in online A/B tests by 12.4% and have been deployed on multiple surfaces of a large internet platform with billions of users. More importantly, the model quality of Generative Recommenders empirically scales as a power-law of training compute across three orders of magnitude, up to GPT-3/LLaMa-2 scale, which reduces carbon footprint needed for future model developments, and further paves the way for the first foundation models in recommendations. DLRM results are from (Mudigere et al., 2022); GRs are deployed models from this work. DLRMs/GRs are continuously trained in a streaming setting; we report compute used per year. in personalizing billions of user experiences on a daily basis. State-of-the-art approaches in recommendations have been based on Deep Learning Recommendation Models (DLRMs) (Mudigere et al., 2022) for about a decade (Covington et al., 2016; Cheng et al., 2016; Zhou et al., 2018; Tang et al., 2020; Wang et al., 2021; Xia et al., 2023). DLRMs are characterized by their usage of heterogeneous features, such as numerical features – counters and ratios, embeddings, and categorical features such as creator ids, user ids, etc. Due to new content and products being added every minute, the feature space is of extreme high cardinality, often in the range of billions (Eksombatchai et al., 2018). To leverage tens of thousands of such features, DLRMs employ various neural networks to combine features, transform intermediate representations, and compose the final outputs. Despite utilizing extensive human-engineered feature sets and training on vast amounts of data, most DLRMs in industry scale poorly with compute (Zhao et al., 2023). This limitation is noteworthy and remains unanswered.
