10000 大规模推荐系统的特点在于依赖高基数、异构的特征，并且每天需要处理数百亿次用户行为。尽管在包含数千个特征的巨量数据上进行训练，工业界大多数 Deep Learning Recommendation Models（DLRMs）仍无法随算力增长而有效扩展。受 Transformers 在语言和视觉领域取得成功的启发，我们重新审视了推荐系统中的基础设计选择。我们在生成式建模框架（“Generative Recommenders”）下将推荐问题重新表述为序列转换（sequential transduction）任务，并提出了一种面向高基数、非平稳流式推荐数据的新架构 HSTU。在合成数据集和公开数据集上，HSTU 在 NDCG 上相较 baselines 最高提升 65.8%，在长度为 8192 的序列上比基于 FlashAttention2 的 Transformers 快 5.3 倍到 15.2 倍。拥有 1.5 万亿参数的基于 HSTU 的 Generative Recommenders 将在在线 A/B 测试中的指标提升 12.4%，并已部署在一个拥有数十亿用户的大型互联网平台的多个场景中。更重要的是，Generative Recommenders 的模型质量在跨越三个数量级的范围内、直至 GPT-3/LLaMa-2 规模，都经验性地按训练算力的幂律扩展，这降低了未来模型开发所需的碳足迹，并进一步为推荐领域首批基础模型（foundation models）铺平了道路。Training PetaFLOP/s-days arXiv:2402.17152v3 [cs.LG] 6 May 2024 Xuan Cao 1 100 1 0.01 2012 2014 2016 2018 2020 2022 2024 Year 图 1. 多年来用于训练深度学习模型的总算力。DLRM 结果来自 (Mudigere et al., 2022)；GRs 是本文部署的模型。DLRMs/GRs 以流式方式持续训练；我们报告每年所用的算力。每天为数十亿用户提供个性化体验。近十年来，推荐领域 state-of-the-art 的方法一直基于 Deep Learning Recommendation Models（DLRMs）(Mudigere et al., 2022)（Covington et al., 2016; Cheng et al., 2016; Zhou et al., 2018; Tang et al., 2020; Wang et al., 2021; Xia et al., 2023）。DLRMs 的特点在于使用异构特征，例如数值特征——计数器和比率、embeddings，以及诸如创作者 id、用户 id 等类别特征。由于每分钟都有新内容和新商品加入，特征空间具有极高的基数，通常达到数十亿的量级（Eksombatchai et al., 2018）。为了利用数万个此类特征，DLRMs 采用各种神经网络来组合特征、变换中间表示并合成最终输出。尽管使用了大量人工工程特征集并在海量数据上训练，工业界大多数 DLRMs 仍难以随算力有效扩展（Zhao et al., 2023）。这一局限值得关注，且尚未得到解答。
RankMixer: Scaling Up Ranking Models in Industrial Recommenders
字节2024推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalingfeature interaction, token mixing, Sparse-MoE, MFU, scalingPDF
AuthorsJie Zhu, Zhifang Fan, Xiaoxie Zhu, Yuchen Jiang, Hangyu Wang, Xintian Han, Haoran Ding, Xinmin Wang, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Recent progress on large language models (LLMs) has spurred interest in scaling up recommendation systems, yet two practical obstacles remain. First, training and serving cost on industrial Recommenders must respect strict latency bounds and high QPS demands. Second, most human-designed feature-crossing modules in ranking models were inherited from the CPU era and fail to exploit modern GPUs, resulting in low Model Flops Utilization (MFU) and poor scalability. We introduce RankMixer, a hardware-aware model design tailored towards a unified and scalable feature-interaction architecture. RankMixer retains the transformer’s high parallelism while replacing quadratic self-attention with multi-head token mixing module for higher efficiency. Besides, RankMixer maintains both the modeling for distinct feature subspaces and cross-featurespace interactions with Per-token FFNs. We further extend it to one billion parameters with a Sparse-MoE variant for higher ROI. A dynamic routing strategy is adapted to address the inadequacy and imbalance of experts training. Experiments show RankMixer’s superior scaling abilities on a trillion-scale production dataset. By replacing previously diverse handcrafted low-MFU modules with RankMixer, we boost the model MFU from 4.5% to 45%, and scale our ranking model parameters by 100x while maintaining roughly the same inference latency. We verify RankMixer’s universality with online A/B tests across three core application scenarios (Recommendation, Advertisement and Search). Finally, we launch 1B Dense-Parameters RankMixer for full traffic serving without increasing the serving cost, which improves user active days by 0.2% and total in-app usage duration by 0.5%.
摘要 (中文)
大语言模型（LLM）的近期进展激发了人们对扩大推荐系统规模的兴趣，但仍存在两个实际障碍。第一，工业推荐系统的训练与服务成本必须遵守严格的延迟上限和高 QPS 需求。第二，排序模型中大多数人工设计的特征交叉模块继承自 CPU 时代，无法利用现代 GPU，导致 Model Flops Utilization（MFU）低下且可扩展性差。我们提出 RankMixer，一种面向统一且可扩展的特征交互架构的硬件感知模型设计。RankMixer 保留了 transformer 的高并行性，同时用多头 token mixing 模块替代二次方复杂度的 self-attention，以获得更高效率。此外，RankMixer 通过 Per-token FFN 同时保持对不同特征子空间的建模以及跨特征空间的交互。我们进一步将其扩展到十亿参数，并采用 Sparse-MoE 变体以获得更高 ROI。我们采用动态路由策略来应对专家训练不足和不均衡的问题。实验表明，RankMixer 在万亿规模的生产数据集上展现出优越的扩展能力。通过用 RankMixer 替换此前多样化的低 MFU 手工模块，我们将模型 MFU 从 4.5% 提升到 45%，并在保持推理延迟大致不变的情况下将排序模型参数扩大 100 倍。我们通过覆盖三个核心应用场景（推荐、广告和搜索）的在线 A/B 测试验证了 RankMixer 的通用性。最后，我们上线了 1B 稠密参数的 RankMixer 用于全量流量服务，且未增加服务成本，使用户活跃天数提升 0.2%、App 内总使用时长提升 0.5%。
CCFormer: Efficient Cross-Field Interaction and Hierarchical Sequence Compression for Industrial Recommendation at Tencent
腾讯2024推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalingcross-field feature interaction, hierarchical sequence compression, long-sequence modeling, industrial recommendation, scaling lawPDF
AuthorsYunlong Wang, Huizhe Zhang, Haonan Hu, Yudong Li, Bing Wen, Jianchao Tu, Chengxiang Zhuo, Zang Li
Abstract (EN)
Recent studies in industrial recommendation systems have demonstrated that sequential recommendation models built upon self-attention can benefit from predictable scaling laws by increasing sequence length and model capacity. However, practical recommender systems impose strict latency and resource constraints, making it challenging to balance computational overhead with fine-grained feature interaction. In this paper, we propose CCFormer, an efficient Transformer backbone that unifies cross-field feature interaction and compressed long-sequence modeling for industrial recommendation. Specifically, CCFormer combines feature-field separated cross attention with long-sequence subspace token mixing to exploit long-term preference signals across heterogeneous feature domains. A hierarchical sequence compression strategy with progressively expanded receptive fields enables efficient long-sequence modeling with reduced information loss. Extensive experiments on two public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate that CCFormer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Online A/B tests in a video recommendation scenario and an advertising ranking scenario at Tencent further validate its industrial practicality, yielding a 3.57% CTR gain and a 1.71% advertising revenue lift, respectively, while accelerating model training by 2.21x over the strong HSTU baseline. CCFormer has been fully deployed in Tencent's production recommendation system, serving the main traffic of both scenarios.
摘要 (中文)
近期工业推荐系统的研究表明，基于 self-attention 的序列推荐模型能够通过增加序列长度和模型容量获得可预测的 scaling law 收益。然而，实际推荐系统有着严格的延迟和资源约束，使得在计算开销与细粒度特征交互之间取得平衡颇具挑战。本文提出 CCFormer，一种高效的 Transformer backbone，将跨域（cross-field）特征交互与压缩长序列建模统一起来，用于工业推荐。具体而言，CCFormer 结合了特征域分离的 cross attention 与长序列 subspace token mixing，以挖掘异构特征域中的长期偏好信号。一种感受野逐步扩展的分层序列压缩（hierarchical sequence compression）策略，使得在降低信息损失的同时实现高效的长序列建模。在两个公开 benchmark 和一个大规模工业数据集上的大量实验表明，CCFormer 持续优于 state-of-the-art baseline。在 Tencent 的视频推荐场景和广告排序场景中的在线 A/B test 进一步验证了其工业实用性，分别带来 3.57% 的 CTR 提升和 1.71% 的广告收入增长，同时相比强 HSTU baseline 将模型训练加速 2.21 倍。CCFormer 已全面部署于 Tencent 的生产推荐系统，服务于两个场景的主要流量。
Wukong: Towards a Scaling Law for Large-Scale Recommendation
Meta2024推荐工业生成式骨干 与 Scalingscaling law, stacked factorization machines, synergistic upscaling, any-order interactions, model complexityPDF · arXiv
AuthorsBuyun Zhang, Liang Luo, Yuxin Chen, Jade Nie, Xi Liu, Daifeng Guo, Yanli Zhao, Shen Li, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Scaling laws play an instrumental role in the sustainable improvement in model quality. Unfortunately, recommendation models to date do not exhibit such laws similar to those observed in the domain of large language models, due to the inefficiencies of their upscaling mechanisms. This limitation poses significant challenges in adapting these models to increasingly more complex real-world datasets. In this paper, we propose an effective network architecture based purely on stacked factorization machines, and a synergistic upscaling strategy, collectively dubbed Wukong, to establish a scaling law in the domain of recommendation. Wukong’s unique design makes it possible to capture diverse, any-order of interactions simply through taller and wider layers. We conducted extensive evaluations on six public datasets, and our results demonstrate that Wukong consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models quality-wise. Further, we assessed Wukong’s scalability on an internal, large-scale dataset. The results show that Wukong retains its superiority in quality over state-of-the-art models, while holding the scaling law across two orders of magnitude in model complexity, extending beyond 100 GFLOP/example, where prior arts fall short.
摘要 (中文)
scaling law 对模型质量的持续提升起着关键作用。遗憾的是，由于扩容机制的低效，推荐模型至今未能像大语言模型领域那样展现出类似的 scaling law，这给模型适配日益复杂的真实数据集带来了巨大挑战。本文提出一种完全基于堆叠因子分解机（stacked factorization machines）的高效网络架构，以及一套协同扩容策略，合称 Wukong，以在推荐领域建立 scaling law。Wukong 的独特设计使得仅通过加深、加宽的层就能捕捉多样的、任意阶的特征交互。我们在六个公开数据集上做了大量评测，结果表明 Wukong 在质量上持续优于当前最优模型。进一步地，我们在一个内部大规模数据集上评估了 Wukong 的可扩展性，结果显示 Wukong 在质量上依然优于 SOTA，同时在跨越两个数量级的模型复杂度范围内（超过 100 GFLOP/example，此前方法无法企及）保持了 scaling law。
Preference Diffusion for Recommendation
华东师大 / 新加坡国立2024推荐学术生成式骨干 与 Scalingdiffusion models, preference ranking, recommender systems, BPR, direct preference optimizationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsShuo Liu, An Zhang, Guoqing Hu, Hong Qian, Tat-seng Chua
Abstract (EN)
Recommender systems predict personalized item rankings based on user preference distributions derived from historical behavior data. Recently, diffusion models (DMs) have gained attention in recommendation for their ability to model complex distributions, yet current DM-based recommenders often rely on traditional objectives like mean squared error (MSE) or recommendation objectives, which are not optimized for personalized ranking tasks or fail to fully leverage DM's generative potential. To address this, we propose PreferDiff, a tailored optimization objective for DM-based recommenders. PreferDiff transforms BPR into a log-likelihood ranking objective and integrates multiple negative samples to better capture user preferences. Specifically, we employ variational inference to handle the intractability through minimizing the variational upper bound and replaces MSE with cosine error to improve alignment with recommendation tasks. Finally, we balance learning generation and preference to enhance the training stability of DMs. PreferDiff offers three key benefits: it is the first personalized ranking loss designed specifically for DM-based recommenders and it improves ranking and faster convergence by addressing hard negatives. We also prove that it is theoretically connected to Direct Preference Optimization which indicates that it has the potential to align user preferences in DM-based recommenders via generative modeling. Extensive experiments across three benchmarks validate its superior recommendation performance and commendable general sequential recommendation capabilities. Our codes are available at https://github.com/lswhim/PreferDiff.
摘要 (中文)
推荐系统基于从历史行为数据中导出的用户偏好分布来预测个性化物品排序。近来，扩散模型（DM）因其建模复杂分布的能力在推荐中受到关注，然而当前基于 DM 的推荐器往往依赖均方误差（MSE）或推荐目标等传统目标，这些目标并未针对个性化排序任务进行优化，也未能充分利用 DM 的生成潜力。为此，我们提出 PreferDiff，一个为基于 DM 的推荐器量身定制的优化目标。PreferDiff 将 BPR 转化为对数似然排序目标，并整合多个负样本来更好地捕获用户偏好。具体而言，我们采用变分推断，通过最小化变分上界来处理不可解性，并用余弦误差替代 MSE 以改善与推荐任务的对齐。最后，我们平衡生成学习与偏好学习，以增强 DM 的训练稳定性。PreferDiff 提供三项关键益处：它是首个专为基于 DM 的推荐器设计的个性化排序损失，并通过处理难负样本改善排序并加速收敛。我们还证明它在理论上与 Direct Preference Optimization 相关联，这表明它有潜力通过生成式建模在基于 DM 的推荐器中对齐用户偏好。在三个基准上的大量实验验证了其优越的推荐性能与良好的通用序列推荐能力。我们的代码见 https://github.com/lswhim/PreferDiff。
PinRec: Outcome-Conditioned, Multi-Token Generative Retrieval for Industry-Scale Recommendation Systems
Pinterest2024推荐工业生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, outcome-conditioned generation, multi-token generation, industrial-scale recommendation, diversityPDF
AuthorsPrabhat Agarwal, Anirudhan Badrinath, Laksh Bhasin, Jaewon Yang, Edoardo Botta, Jiajing Xu, Charles Rosenberg
Abstract (EN)
Generative retrieval methods utilize generative sequential modeling techniques, such as transformers, to generate candidate items for recommender systems. These methods have demonstrated promising results in academic benchmarks, surpassing traditional retrieval models like two-tower architectures. However, current generative retrieval methods lack the scalability required for industrial recommender systems, and they are insufficiently flexible to satisfy the multiple metric requirements of modern systems. This paper introduces PinRec, a novel generative retrieval model developed for applications at Pinterest. PinRec utilizes outcomeconditioned generation, enabling modelers to specify how to balance various outcome metrics, such as the number of saves and clicks, to effectively align with business goals and user exploration. Additionally, PinRec incorporates multi-token generation to enhance output diversity while optimizing generation. Our experiments demonstrate that PinRec can successfully balance performance, diversity, and efficiency, delivering a significant positive impact such as +2% sitewide clicks and +4% search repins. This paper marks a significant milestone in generative retrieval, as it presents, to our knowledge, the first rigorous study on implementing generative retrieval at the scale of Pinterest.
摘要 (中文)
生成式检索方法利用诸如 transformer 之类的生成式序列建模技术，为推荐系统生成候选物品。这些方法在学术 benchmark 上已展现出令人鼓舞的结果，超越了双塔架构等传统检索模型。然而，当前的生成式检索方法缺乏工业级推荐系统所需的可扩展性，也不够灵活，无法满足现代系统的多项指标要求。本文提出 PinRec，一个为 Pinterest 应用开发的新型生成式检索模型。PinRec 采用 outcome-conditioned 生成，使建模者能够指定如何平衡各类结果指标（例如收藏数和点击数），以有效对齐业务目标与用户探索。此外，PinRec 引入 multi-token 生成，以在优化生成的同时增强输出多样性。我们的实验表明，PinRec 能够成功平衡性能、多样性与效率，带来显著的正向收益，例如全站点击 +2%、搜索 repin +4%。本文是生成式检索领域的一个重要里程碑，因为据我们所知，它首次对在 Pinterest 规模上落地生成式检索进行了严谨研究。
A Generative Re-ranking Model for List-level Multi-objective Optimization at Taobao
阿里2024推荐工业生成式检索 / 排序方法generative re-ranking, list-level multi-objective optimization, ordered regression, diversityPDF
AuthorsYue Meng, Cheng Guo, Yi Cao, Tong Liu, Bo Zheng
Abstract (EN)
E-commerce recommendation systems aim to generate ordered lists of items for customers, optimizing multiple business objectives, such as clicks, conversions and Gross Merchandise Volume (GMV). Traditional multi-objective optimization methods like formulas or Learning-to-rank (LTR) models take effect at item-level, neglecting dynamic user intent and contextual item interactions. List-level multi-objective optimization in the re-ranking stage can overcome this limitation, but most current re-ranking models focus more on accuracy improvement with context. In addition, re-ranking is faced with the challenges of time complexity and diversity. In light of this, we propose a novel end-to-end generative re-ranking model named Sequential Ordered Regression Transformer-Generator (SORT-Gen) for the less-studied list-level multi-objective optimization problem. Specifically, SORT-Gen is divided into two parts: 1)Sequential Ordered Regression Transformer innovatively uses Transformer and ordered regression to accurately estimate multi-objective values for variable-length sub-lists. 2)Mask-Driven Fast Generation Algorithm combines multi-objective candidate queues, efficient item selection and diversity mechanism into model inference, providing a fast online list generation method. Comprehensive online experiments demonstrate that SORT-Gen brings +4.13% CLCK and +8.10% GMV for Baiyibutie, a notable Mini-app of Taobao. Currently, SORT-Gen has been successfully deployed in multiple scenarios of Taobao App, serving for a vast number of users.
摘要 (中文)
电商推荐系统旨在为用户生成有序的商品列表，并优化点击、转化、GMV（Gross Merchandise Volume）等多个业务目标。传统的多目标优化方法（如公式法或 Learning-to-rank（LTR）模型）在 item 层面生效，忽略了动态的用户意图以及上下文中的 item 交互。在 re-ranking 阶段进行列表级多目标优化可以克服这一局限，但当前大多数 re-ranking 模型更关注结合上下文提升准确性。此外，re-ranking 还面临时间复杂度和多样性的挑战。鉴于此，我们针对这一研究相对不足的列表级多目标优化问题，提出了一种新颖的端到端生成式 re-ranking 模型 Sequential Ordered Regression Transformer-Generator（SORT-Gen）。具体而言，SORT-Gen 分为两部分：1）Sequential Ordered Regression Transformer 创新性地使用 Transformer 和 ordered regression，对变长子列表的多目标值进行准确估计；2）Mask-Driven Fast Generation Algorithm 将多目标候选队列、高效的 item 选择以及多样性机制融入模型推理，提供了一种快速的在线列表生成方法。充分的在线实验表明，SORT-Gen 为淘宝的知名小程序 Baiyibutie 带来了 +4.13% 的 CLCK 和 +8.10% 的 GMV 提升。目前，SORT-Gen 已成功部署在淘宝 App 的多个场景中，服务大量用户。
Generative Retrieval as Multi-Vector Dense Retrieval
山东大学2024搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, multi-vector dense retrieval, document identifiers, relevance alignment matrix, term matchingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsShiguang Wu, Wenda Wei, Mengqi Zhang, Zhumin Chen, Jun Ma, Zhaochun Ren, Maarten de Rijke, Pengjie Ren
Abstract (EN)
Generative retrieval generates identifiers of relevant documents in an end-to-end manner using a sequence-to-sequence architecture for a given query. The relation between generative retrieval and other retrieval methods, especially those based on matching within dense retrieval models, is not yet fully comprehended. Prior work has demonstrated that generative retrieval with atomic identifiers is equivalent to single-vector dense retrieval. Accordingly, generative retrieval exhibits behavior analogous to hierarchical search within a tree index in dense retrieval when using hierarchical semantic identifiers. However, prior work focuses solely on the retrieval stage without considering the deep interactions within the decoder of generative retrieval. In this paper, we fill this gap by demonstrating that generative retrieval and multi-vector dense retrieval share the same framework for measuring the relevance to a query of a document. Specifically, we examine the attention layer and prediction head of generative retrieval, revealing that generative retrieval can be understood as a special case of multi-vector dense retrieval. Both methods compute relevance as a sum of products of query and document vectors and an alignment matrix. We then explore how generative retrieval applies this framework, employing distinct strategies for computing document token vectors and the alignment matrix. We have conducted experiments to verify our conclusions and show that both paradigms exhibit commonalities of term matching in their alignment matrix. Our finding applies to many generative retrieval identifier designs and provides possible explanations on how generative retrieval can express query-document relevance. As multi-vector dense retrieval is the state-of-the-art dense retrieval method currently, understanding the connection between generative retrieval and multi-vector dense retrieval is crucial for shedding light on the underlying mechanisms of generative retrieval and for developing, and understanding the potential of, new retrieval models. CCS CONCEPTS • Information systems → Retrieval models and ranking.
摘要 (中文)
生成式检索（generative retrieval）针对给定 query，使用序列到序列架构以端到端方式生成相关文档的标识符。生成式检索与其他检索方法、尤其是那些基于稠密检索模型内部匹配的方法之间的关系，尚未被完全理解。已有工作证明，使用原子标识符（atomic identifier）的生成式检索等价于单向量稠密检索。相应地，当使用层级化语义标识符时，生成式检索表现出类似于稠密检索中树索引层级搜索的行为。然而，已有工作仅关注检索阶段，未考虑生成式检索 decoder 内部的深层交互。本文填补了这一空白，证明生成式检索与多向量稠密检索在衡量文档与 query 的相关性时共享同一框架。具体而言，我们考察了生成式检索的 attention 层和预测头，揭示生成式检索可被理解为多向量稠密检索的一个特例。两种方法都将相关性计算为 query 向量与文档向量之积、再与一个对齐矩阵相乘后的求和。随后我们探讨了生成式检索如何应用这一框架，即采用不同的策略来计算文档 token 向量和对齐矩阵。我们进行了实验来验证结论，并表明两种范式在其对齐矩阵中都表现出词项匹配（term matching）的共性。我们的发现适用于多种生成式检索标识符设计，并为生成式检索如何表达 query-文档相关性提供了可能的解释。由于多向量稠密检索是当前最先进的稠密检索方法，理解生成式检索与多向量稠密检索之间的联系，对于揭示生成式检索的底层机制、以及开发并理解新检索模型的潜力至关重要。CCS 概念 • 信息系统 → 检索模型与排序。
Learning to Rank in Generative Retrieval
港理工2024搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, learning-to-rank, ranking loss, passage identifiers, learning gapPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYongqi Li, Nan Yang, Liang Wang, Furu Wei, Wenjie Li
Abstract (EN)
Generative retrieval stands out as a promising new paradigm in text retrieval that aims to generate identifier strings of relevant passages as the retrieval target. This generative paradigm taps into powerful generative language models, distinct from traditional sparse or dense retrieval methods. However, only learning to generate is insufficient for generative retrieval. Generative retrieval learns to generate identifiers of relevant passages as an intermediate goal and then converts predicted identifiers into the final passage rank list. The disconnect between the learning objective of autoregressive models and the desired passage ranking target leads to a learning gap. To bridge this gap, we propose a learning-to-rank framework for generative retrieval, dubbed LTRGR. LTRGR enables generative retrieval to learn to rank passages directly, optimizing the autoregressive model toward the final passage ranking target via a rank loss. This framework only requires an additional learning-to-rank training phase to enhance current generative retrieval systems and does not add any burden to the inference stage. We conducted experiments on three public benchmarks, and the results demonstrate that LTRGR achieves state-of-the-art performance among generative retrieval methods. The code and checkpoints are released at https://github.com/liyongqi67/LTRGR.
摘要 (中文)
生成式检索（generative retrieval）是文本检索中一个颇具前景的新范式，其目标是以生成相关段落的标识符字符串作为检索目标。这一生成式范式利用了强大的生成式语言模型，与传统稀疏或稠密检索方法截然不同。然而，仅学习生成对于生成式检索是不够的。生成式检索把生成相关段落的标识符作为中间目标来学习，然后将预测的标识符转换为最终的段落排序列表。自回归模型的学习目标与所期望的段落排序目标之间存在脱节，导致学习鸿沟（learning gap）。为弥合这一鸿沟，我们提出一个面向生成式检索的 learning-to-rank 框架，称为 LTRGR。LTRGR 使生成式检索能够直接学习对段落排序，通过 rank loss 将自回归模型优化指向最终的段落排序目标。该框架只需增加一个 learning-to-rank 训练阶段即可增强现有生成式检索系统，且不增加任何推理阶段的负担。我们在三个公开基准上进行了实验，结果表明 LTRGR 在生成式检索方法中达到 state-of-the-art 性能。代码与 checkpoint 发布于 https://github.com/liyongqi67/LTRGR。
Generative Retrieval Meets Multi-Graded Relevance
中科院 / 国科大2024搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, multi-graded relevance, constrained contrastive training, document identifiers, relevance gradesPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYubao Tang, Ruqing Zhang, Jiafeng Guo, Maarten de Rijke, Wei Chen, Xueqi Cheng
Abstract (EN)
Generative retrieval represents a novel approach to information retrieval. It uses an encoder-decoder architecture to directly produce relevant document identifiers (docids) for queries. While this method offers benefits, current approaches are limited to scenarios with binary relevance data, overlooking the potential for documents to have multi-graded relevance. Extending generative retrieval to accommodate multi-graded relevance poses challenges, including the need to reconcile likelihood probabilities for docid pairs and the possibility of multiple relevant documents sharing the same identifier. To address these challenges, we introduce a framework called GRaded Generative Retrieval (GR$^2$). GR$^2$ focuses on two key components: ensuring relevant and distinct identifiers, and implementing multi-graded constrained contrastive training. First, we create identifiers that are both semantically relevant and sufficiently distinct to represent individual documents effectively. This is achieved by jointly optimizing the relevance and distinctness of docids through a combination of docid generation and autoencoder models. Second, we incorporate information about the relationship between relevance grades to guide the training process. We use a constrained contrastive training strategy to bring the representations of queries and the identifiers of their relevant documents closer together, based on their respective relevance grades. Extensive experiments on datasets with both multi-graded and binary relevance demonstrate the effectiveness of GR$^2$.
摘要 (中文)
生成式检索（generative retrieval）代表了一种新颖的信息检索方法。它采用 encoder-decoder 架构，直接为 query 生成相关的文档标识符（docids）。尽管该方法具有诸多优势，但当前方法仅限于二值相关性数据的场景，忽视了文档可能具有多级相关性的潜力。将生成式检索扩展到支持多级相关性面临诸多挑战，包括需要协调 docid 对的可能性概率（likelihood probabilities），以及多个相关文档可能共享同一标识符的问题。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一个名为 GRaded Generative Retrieval（GR$^2$）的框架。GR$^2$ 聚焦于两个关键组成部分：确保标识符既相关又具有区分性，以及实现多级约束对比训练。首先，我们创建既语义相关又足够区分以有效表示单个文档的标识符。这通过结合 docid 生成与 autoencoder 模型，联合优化 docids 的相关性和区分性来实现。其次，我们引入关于相关性等级之间关系的信息来指导训练过程。我们采用约束对比训练策略，依据 query 与其相关文档各自的相关性等级，将二者的表示拉近。在多级相关性和二值相关性数据集上的大量实验证明了 GR$^2$ 的有效性。
Planning Ahead in Generative Retrieval: Guiding Autoregressive Generation through Simultaneous Decoding
UMass Amherst / Amazon2024搜索工业生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, planning ahead, simultaneous decoding, document identifiers, autoregressive generationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsHansi Zeng, Chen Luo, Hamed Zamani
Abstract (EN)
This paper introduces PAG-a novel optimization and decoding approach that guides autoregressive generation of document identifiers in generative retrieval models through simultaneous decoding. To this aim, PAG constructs a set-based and sequential identifier for each document. Motivated by the bag-of-words assumption in information retrieval, the set-based identifier is built on lexical tokens. The sequential identifier, on the other hand, is obtained via quantizing relevance-based representations of documents. Extensive experiments on MSMARCO and TREC Deep Learning Track data reveal that PAG outperforms the state-of-the-art generative retrieval model by a large margin (e.g., 15.6% MRR improvements on MS MARCO), while achieving 22x speed up in terms of query latency.
摘要 (中文)
本文介绍了 PAG——一种新颖的优化与解码方法，它通过同时解码（simultaneous decoding）来引导生成式检索模型中文档标识符的自回归生成。为此，PAG 为每个文档构建了一个基于集合的标识符和一个序列标识符。受信息检索中 bag-of-words 假设的启发，基于集合的标识符建立在词法 token 之上。而序列标识符则通过对文档基于相关性的表示进行量化获得。在 MSMARCO 和 TREC Deep Learning Track 数据上的大量实验表明，PAG 大幅超越了当前最优的生成式检索模型（例如在 MS MARCO 上 MRR 提升 15.6%），同时在 query 延迟方面实现了 22 倍的加速。
Multi-Layer Ranking with Large Language Models for News Source Recommendation
University of Warwick / King's College London2024搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法expert recommendation, multi-layer ranking, in-context learning, NewsQuotePDF · arXiv
AuthorsWenjia Zhang, Lin Gui, Rob Procter, Yulan He
Abstract (EN)
To seek reliable information sources for news events, we introduce a novel task of expert recommendation, which aims to identify trustworthy sources based on their previously quoted statements. To achieve this, we built a novel dataset, called NewsQuote, consisting of 23,571 quote-speaker pairs sourced from a collection of news articles. We formulate the recommendation task as the retrieval of experts based on their likelihood of being associated with a given query. We also propose a multi-layer ranking framework employing Large Language Models to improve the recommendation performance. Our results show that employing an in-context learning based LLM ranker and a multi-layer ranking-based filter significantly improve both the predictive quality and behavioural quality of the recommender system.
摘要 (中文)
为给新闻事件寻找可靠的信息来源，我们引入一项新颖的专家推荐任务，旨在基于专家此前被引用的陈述来识别可信来源。为此，我们构建了一个新颖的数据集 NewsQuote，包含来自一批新闻文章的 23,571 个引语-发言人（quote-speaker）对。我们将该推荐任务形式化为依据专家与给定 query 相关联的可能性来检索专家。我们还提出一个采用 Large Language Models 的多层排序框架，以提升推荐性能。我们的结果表明，采用基于 in-context learning 的 LLM ranker 以及基于多层排序的 filter，能显著提升推荐系统的预测质量与行为质量。
ListT5: Listwise Reranking with Fusion-in-Decoder Improves Zero-shot Retrieval
Seoul National University / LG AI Research2024搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法listwise reranking, Fusion-in-Decoder, zero-shot retrieval, tournament sort, lost-in-the-middlePDF · arXiv
AuthorsSoyoung Yoon, Eunbi Choi, Jiyeon Kim, Hyeongu Yun, Yireun Kim, Seung-won Hwang
Abstract (EN)
We propose ListT5, a novel reranking approach based on Fusion-in-Decoder (FiD) that handles multiple candidate passages at both train and inference time. We also introduce an efficient inference framework for listwise ranking based on m-ary tournament sort with output caching. We evaluate and compare our model on the BEIR benchmark for zero-shot retrieval task, demonstrating that ListT5 (1) outperforms the state-of-the-art RankT5 baseline with a notable +1.3 gain in the average NDCG@10 score, (2) has an efficiency comparable to pointwise ranking models and surpasses the efficiency of previous listwise ranking models, and (3) overcomes the lost-in-the-middle problem of previous listwise rerankers. Our code, model checkpoints, and the evaluation framework are fully open-sourced at \url{https://github.com/soyoung97/ListT5}.
摘要 (中文)
我们提出 ListT5，一种基于 Fusion-in-Decoder（FiD）的新型 reranking 方法，它在训练和推理时均处理多个候选 passage。我们还引入了一个基于带输出缓存的 m 元锦标赛排序的高效 listwise ranking 推理框架。我们在 zero-shot 检索任务的 BEIR 基准上评估并比较了我们的模型，证明 ListT5：（1）优于 state-of-the-art 的 RankT5 baseline，在平均 NDCG@10 上取得显著的 +1.3 提升；（2）效率与 pointwise 排序模型相当，并超越先前 listwise 排序模型的效率；（3）克服了先前 listwise 重排器的 lost-in-the-middle 问题。我们的代码、模型 checkpoint 以及评测框架已在 \url{https://github.com/soyoung97/ListT5} 完全开源。
Listwise Generative Retrieval Models via a Sequential Learning Process
中科院 / 国科大2024搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, listwise, sequential learning process, docid, relevance calibrationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYubao Tang, Ruqing Zhang, Jiafeng Guo, Maarten de Rijke, Wei Chen, Xueqi Cheng
Abstract (EN)
Recently, a novel generative retrieval (GR) paradigm has been proposed, where a single sequence-to-sequence model is learned to directly generate a list of relevant document identifiers (docids) given a query. Existing GR models commonly employ maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) for optimization: this involves maximizing the likelihood of a single relevant docid given an input query, with the assumption that the likelihood for each docid is independent of the other docids in the list. We refer to these models as the pointwise approach in this paper. While the pointwise approach has been shown to be effective in the context of GR, it is considered sub-optimal due to its disregard for the fundamental principle that ranking involves making predictions about lists. In this paper, we address this limitation by introducing an alternative listwise approach, which empowers the GR model to optimize the relevance at the docid list level. Specifically, we view the generation of a ranked docid list as a sequence learning process: at each step we learn a subset of parameters that maximizes the corresponding generation likelihood of the $i$-th docid given the (preceding) top $i-1$ docids. To formalize the sequence learning process, we design a positional conditional probability for GR. To alleviate the potential impact of beam search on the generation quality during inference, we perform relevance calibration on the generation likelihood of model-generated docids according to relevance grades. We conduct extensive experiments on representative binary and multi-graded relevance datasets. Our empirical results demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art GR baselines in terms of retrieval performance.
摘要 (中文)
近期，一种新颖的生成式检索（GR）范式被提出，其中单个 sequence-to-sequence 模型被学习来在给定 query 时直接生成一组相关文档标识符（docid）列表。现有 GR 模型通常采用最大似然估计（MLE）进行优化：即在给定输入 query 时最大化单个相关 docid 的似然，并假设每个 docid 的似然与列表中的其他 docid 相互独立。本文中我们将这类模型称为 pointwise 方法。尽管 pointwise 方法在 GR 背景下已被证明有效，但它被认为是次优的，因为它忽视了排序本质上是对列表进行预测这一基本原则。本文中，我们通过引入另一种 listwise 方法来弥补这一局限，该方法使 GR 模型能够在 docid 列表层面优化相关性。具体而言，我们将排序 docid 列表的生成视为一个序列学习过程：每一步我们学习一部分参数，使得在给定（前序）top $i-1$ 个 docid 的条件下，第 $i$ 个 docid 的对应生成似然最大化。为形式化该序列学习过程，我们为 GR 设计了位置条件概率。为缓解推理时 beam search 对生成质量的潜在影响，我们依据相关性等级对模型生成 docid 的生成似然进行相关性校准。我们在具有代表性的二元与多级相关性数据集上进行了大量实验。实证结果表明，我们的方法在检索性能上优于 state-of-the-art 的 GR baseline。
Event GDR: Event-Centric Generative Document Retrieval
清华 / 山西大学2024搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法generative document retrieval, event-centric, event taxonomy, identifier construction, document representationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYong Guan, Dingxiao Liu, Jinchen Ma, Hao Peng, Xiaozhi Wang, Lei Hou, Ru Li
Abstract (EN)
Generative document retrieval, an emerging paradigm in information retrieval, learns to build connections between documents and identifiers within a single model, garnering significant attention. However, there are still two challenges: (1) neglecting inner-content correlation during document representation; (2) lacking explicit semantic structure during identifier construction. Nonetheless, events have enriched relations and well-defined taxonomy, which could facilitate addressing the above two challenges. Inspired by this, we propose Event GDR, an event-centric generative document retrieval model, integrating event knowledge into this task. Specifically, we utilize an exchange-then-reflection method based on multi-agents for event knowledge extraction. For document representation, we employ events and relations to model the document to guarantee the comprehensiveness and inner-content correlation. For identifier construction, we map the events to well-defined event taxonomy to construct the identifiers with explicit semantic structure. Our method achieves significant improvement over the baselines on two datasets, and also hopes to provide insights for future research.
摘要 (中文)
生成式文档检索是信息检索中的一种新兴范式，它学习在单一模型内建立文档与标识符之间的关联，已获得广泛关注。然而，仍存在两个挑战：（1）文档表示时忽视了内容内部的相关性；（2）标识符构建时缺乏显式的语义结构。尽管如此，事件具有丰富的关系和定义良好的分类体系，这有助于应对上述两个挑战。受此启发，我们提出 Event GDR，一种以事件为中心的生成式文档检索模型，将事件知识整合进该任务。具体而言，我们利用基于多 agent 的 exchange-then-reflection 方法进行事件知识抽取。在文档表示方面，我们使用事件和关系来建模文档，以保证全面性和内容内部的相关性。在标识符构建方面，我们将事件映射到定义良好的事件分类体系，以构建具有显式语义结构的标识符。我们的方法在两个数据集上相较 baseline 取得显著提升，也希望为未来研究提供启示。
Bottleneck-Minimal Indexing for Generative Document Retrieval
Waseda University2024搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法generative document retrieval, indexing, rate-distortion, mutual information, bottleneckPDF · arXiv
AuthorsXin Du, Lixin Xiu, Kumiko Tanaka-Ishii
Abstract (EN)
We apply an information-theoretic perspective to reconsider generative document retrieval (GDR), in which a document $x \in X$ is indexed by $t \in T$, and a neural autoregressive model is trained to map queries $Q$ to $T$. GDR can be considered to involve information transmission from documents $X$ to queries $Q$, with the requirement to transmit more bits via the indexes $T$. By applying Shannon's rate-distortion theory, the optimality of indexing can be analyzed in terms of the mutual information, and the design of the indexes $T$ can then be regarded as a {\em bottleneck} in GDR. After reformulating GDR from this perspective, we empirically quantify the bottleneck underlying GDR. Finally, using the NQ320K and MARCO datasets, we evaluate our proposed bottleneck-minimal indexing method in comparison with various previous indexing methods, and we show that it outperforms those methods.
摘要 (中文)
我们从信息论视角重新审视生成式文档检索（GDR），其中文档 $x \in X$ 由 $t \in T$ 索引，并训练一个神经自回归模型将 query $Q$ 映射到 $T$。GDR 可被视为涉及从文档 $X$ 到 query $Q$ 的信息传输，且要求通过索引 $T$ 传输更多比特。通过应用 Shannon 的率失真理论，可以用互信息来分析索引的最优性，索引 $T$ 的设计便可被视为 GDR 中的一个{\em 瓶颈}。在从这一视角重新表述 GDR 之后，我们实证量化了 GDR 底层的瓶颈。最后，我们使用 NQ320K 和 MARCO 数据集，将我们提出的 bottleneck-minimal indexing 方法与多种先前的索引方法进行比较评估，结果表明它优于这些方法。
Bridging Items and Language: A Transition Paradigm for Large Language Model-Based Recommendation
新加坡国立2024推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐LLM-based recommendation, item indexing, generation grounding, multi-facet identifiers, substring indexingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsXinyu Lin, Wenjie Wang, Yongqi Li, Fuli Feng, See-Kiong Ng, Tat-Seng Chua
Abstract (EN)
CCS CONCEPTS Harnessing Large Language Models (LLMs) for recommendation is rapidly emerging, which relies on two fundamental steps to bridge the recommendation item space and the language space: 1) item indexing utilizes identifiers to represent items in the language space, and 2) generation grounding associates LLMs’ generated token sequences to in-corpus items. However, previous methods exhibit inherent limitations in the two steps. Existing IDbased identifiers (e.g., numeric IDs) and description-based identifiers (e.g., titles) either lose semantics or lack adequate distinctiveness. Moreover, prior generation grounding methods might generate invalid identifiers, thus misaligning with in-corpus items. To address these issues, we propose a novel Transition paradigm for LLM-based Recommender (named TransRec) to bridge items and language. Specifically, TransRec presents multi-facet identifiers, which simultaneously incorporate ID, title, and attribute for item indexing to pursue both distinctiveness and semantics. Additionally, we introduce a specialized data structure for TransRec to ensure generating valid identifiers only and utilize substring indexing to encourage LLMs to generate from any position of identifiers. Lastly, TransRec presents an aggregated grounding module to leverage generated multi-facet identifiers to rank in-corpus items efficiently. We instantiate TransRec on two backbone models, BART-large and LLaMA-7B. Extensive results on three real-world datasets under diverse settings validate the superiority of TransRec. • Information systems → Recommender systems. ∗ Corresponding author. 摘要 (中文)
CCS CONCEPTS 利用大语言模型（LLMs）进行推荐正迅速兴起，这依赖两个基础步骤来桥接推荐 item 空间与语言空间：1）item 索引，利用标识符在语言空间中表示 item；2）generation grounding，将 LLMs 生成的 token 序列关联到语料内的 item。然而，先前的方法在这两个步骤上存在固有局限。现有的基于 ID 的标识符（如数值 ID）和基于描述的标识符（如标题）要么丢失语义，要么缺乏足够的区分度。此外，先前的 generation grounding 方法可能生成无效标识符，从而与语料内 item 错位。为解决这些问题，我们提出一种新的 Transition 范式用于基于 LLM 的推荐器（命名为 TransRec），以桥接 item 与语言。具体而言，TransRec 提出多面（multi-facet）标识符，在 item 索引时同时纳入 ID、标题和属性，以兼顾区分度与语义。此外，我们为 TransRec 引入一种专门的数据结构，确保只生成有效标识符，并利用子串索引（substring indexing）鼓励 LLMs 从标识符的任意位置开始生成。最后，TransRec 提出一个聚合落地（aggregated grounding）模块，利用生成的多面标识符对语料内 item 高效排序。我们在 BART-large 和 LLaMA-7B 两个骨干模型上实例化了 TransRec。在三种真实世界数据集、多种设置下的大量结果验证了 TransRec 的优越性。• Information systems → Recommender systems。∗ 通讯作者。本研究由 A*STAR、CISCO Systems (USA) Pte. Representation Learning with Large Language Models for Recommendation
香港大学2024推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐LLM-empowered representation learning, cross-view alignment, user/item profiling, collaborative signals, mutual information maximizationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsXubin Ren, Wei Wei, Lianghao Xia, Lixin Su, Suqi Cheng, Junfeng Wang, Dawei Yin, Chao Huang
Abstract (EN)
CCS CONCEPTS Recommender systems have seen significant advancements with the influence of deep learning and graph neural networks, particularly in capturing complex user-item relationships. However, these graph-based recommenders heavily depend on ID-based data, potentially disregarding valuable textual information associated with users and items, resulting in less informative learned representations. Moreover, the utilization of implicit feedback data introduces potential noise and bias, posing challenges for the effectiveness of user preference learning. While the integration of large language models (LLMs) into traditional ID-based recommenders has gained attention, challenges such as scalability issues, limitations in textonly reliance, and prompt input constraints need to be addressed for effective implementation in practical recommender systems. To address these challenges, we propose a model-agnostic framework RLMRec that aims to enhance existing recommenders with LLMempowered representation learning. It proposes a recommendation paradigm that integrates representation learning with LLMs to capture intricate semantic aspects of user behaviors and preferences. RLMRec incorporates auxiliary textual signals, employs LLMs for user/item profiling, and aligns the semantic space of LLMs with collaborative relational signals through cross-view alignment. This work further demonstrates the theoretical foundation of incorporating textual signals through mutual information maximization, which improves the quality of representations. Our evaluation integrates RLMRec with state-of-the-art recommender models, while also analyzing its efficiency and robustness to noise data. Implementation codes are available at https://github.com/HKUDS/RLMRec. • Information systems → Recommender systems. ∗ Chao Huang is the Corresponding Author. 摘要 (中文)
在深度学习和图神经网络的影响下，推荐系统取得了显著进展，尤其是在捕捉复杂用户-物品关系方面。然而，这些基于图的推荐器严重依赖基于 ID 的数据，可能忽略与用户和物品相关的有价值文本信息，导致所学到的表示信息量不足。此外，使用隐式反馈数据会引入潜在噪声和偏差，给用户偏好学习的有效性带来挑战。尽管将大语言模型（LLM）融入传统基于 ID 的推荐器已受到关注，但在实际推荐系统中有效落地仍需解决可扩展性问题、仅依赖文本的局限以及 prompt 输入约束等挑战。为应对这些挑战，我们提出一种模型无关的框架 RLMRec，旨在以 LLM 赋能的表示学习来增强现有推荐器。它提出一种将表示学习与 LLM 相结合的推荐范式，以捕捉用户行为和偏好中错综复杂的语义层面。RLMRec 引入辅助文本信号，利用 LLM 进行用户/物品画像，并通过跨视图对齐（cross-view alignment）使 LLM 的语义空间与协同关系信号对齐。本工作进一步通过互信息最大化展示了引入文本信号的理论基础，这提升了表示的质量。我们的评估将 RLMRec 与最先进的推荐模型相集成，同时分析了其效率和面对噪声数据的鲁棒性。实现代码见 https://github.com/HKUDS/RLMRec。
Customizing Language Models with Instance-wise LoRA for Sequential Recommendation
USTC2024推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐sequential recommendation, instance-wise LoRA, mixture-of-experts, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, negative transferPDF · arXiv
AuthorsXiaoyu Kong, Jiancan Wu, An Zhang, Leheng Sheng, Hui Lin, Xiang Wang, Xiangnan He
Abstract (EN)
Sequential recommendation systems predict the next interaction item based on users’ past interactions, aligning recommendations with individual preferences. Leveraging the strengths of Large Language Models (LLMs) in knowledge comprehension and reasoning, recent approaches are eager to apply LLMs to sequential recommendation. A common paradigm is converting user behavior sequences into instruction data, and fine-tuning the LLM with parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods like Low-Rank Adaption (LoRA). However, the uniform application of LoRA across diverse user behaviors is insufficient to capture individual variability, resulting in negative transfer between disparate sequences. To address these challenges, we propose Instance-wise LoRA (iLoRA). We innovatively treat the sequential recommendation task as a form of multi-task learning, integrating LoRA with the Mixture of Experts (MoE) framework. This approach encourages different experts to capture various aspects of user behavior. Additionally, we introduce a sequence representation guided gate function that generates customized expert participation weights for each user sequence, which allows dynamic parameter adjustment for instance-wise recommendations. In sequential recommendation, iLoRA achieves an average relative improvement of 11.4% over basic LoRA in the hit ratio metric, with less than a 1% relative increase in trainable parameters. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of iLoRA, highlighting its superior performance compared to existing methods in mitigating negative transfer and improving recommendation accuracy. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/AkaliKong/iLoRA.
摘要 (中文)
序列推荐系统根据用户过去的交互预测下一次交互的 item，使推荐契合个体偏好。借助大语言模型（LLMs）在知识理解与推理方面的优势，近期方法热衷于将 LLMs 应用于序列推荐。一种常见范式是将用户行为序列转换为指令数据，并使用 Low-Rank Adaption（LoRA）等参数高效微调（PEFT）方法对 LLM 进行微调。然而，对多样化的用户行为统一施加 LoRA 不足以刻画个体差异，导致不同序列之间的负迁移（negative transfer）。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 Instance-wise LoRA（iLoRA）。我们创新性地将序列推荐任务视为一种多任务学习，把 LoRA 与 Mixture of Experts（MoE）框架相结合。这种做法促使不同专家捕获用户行为的不同方面。此外，我们引入一个由序列表示引导的门控函数，为每个用户序列生成定制化的专家参与权重，从而实现针对实例（instance-wise）推荐的动态参数调整。在序列推荐中，iLoRA 在 hit ratio 指标上相较基础 LoRA 取得平均 11.4% 的相对提升，而可训练参数的相对增幅不足 1%。在三个基准数据集上的大量实验证明了 iLoRA 的有效性，凸显其在缓解负迁移和提升推荐准确性方面优于现有方法的性能。我们的数据和代码见 https://github.com/AkaliKong/iLoRA。

AuthorsHoang Ngo, Dat Quoc Nguyen

We present the first domain-adapted and fullytrained large language model, RecGPT-7B, and its instruction-following variant, RecGPT-7BInstruct, for text-based recommendation.
