摘要 (中文)
CCS CONCEPTS 点击率（CTR）预测是推荐系统中的核心任务。现有方法（简称 IDRec）依赖唯一标识来表示不同的用户和物品，已流行数十年。一方面，IDRec 在冷启动问题上常面临显著性能退化；另一方面，受迭代效率约束，IDRec 无法使用更长的训练数据。多数先前研究通过引入预训练知识（例如预训练用户模型或多模态 embedding）来缓解上述问题。然而，在线延迟的爆炸式增长可归因于预训练模型中的庞大参数。因此，它们大多无法在工业推荐系统中与 IDRec 采用端到端训练的统一模型，从而限制了预训练模型的潜力。为此，我们提出一种预训练插件式 CTR 模型，即 PPM。PPM 以多模态特征为输入，并利用大规模数据进行预训练。随后，PPM 被插入 IDRec 模型，以增强统一模型的性能与迭代效率。在接入 IDRec 模型后，网络中的某些中间结果被缓存，只有一部分参数参与训练与服务。因此，我们的方法可以成功部署端到端模型，而不会造成延迟的大幅增加。在京东电商的大量离线实验与在线 A/B 测试证明了 PPM 的高效性与有效性。• Information systems → Recommender systems; Personalization. ∗ 两位作者对本研究贡献相同。† 通讯作者。允许出于个人或课堂教学目的，对本作品的全部或部分内容进行数字或纸质复制，无需付费，前提是复制件不得用于营利或商业目的，且复制件须在首页保留本声明及完整引用信息。本作品中他人拥有的版权必须得到尊重。允许在注明出处的前提下摘录。以其他方式复制、重新发布、在服务器上发布或向邮件列表分发，均需事先获得特别许可和/或支付费用。许可申请请联系 permissions@acm.org。WWW ’24 Companion, May 13–17, 2024, Singapore, Singapore © 2024 版权归作者/所有者持有。出版权由 ACM 授权。ACM ISBN 979-8-4007-0172-6/24/05. . . $15.00 https://doi.org/10.1145/3589335.3648329
Multi-Epoch learning with Data Augmentation for Deep Click-Through Rate Prediction
快手2024推荐工业CTR 训练 / 工程multi-epoch learning, data augmentation, embedding overfitting, CTR prediction, catastrophic forgettingPDF
AuthorsZhongxiang Fan, Zhaocheng Liu, Jian Liang, Dongying Kong, Han Li, Peng Jiang, Shuang Li, Kun Gai
Abstract (EN)
This paper investigates the one-epoch overfitting phenomenon in Click-Through Rate (CTR) models, where performance notably declines at the start of the second epoch. Despite extensive research, the efficacy of multi-epoch training over the conventional oneepoch approach remains unclear. We identify the overfitting of the embedding layer, caused by high-dimensional data sparsity, as the primary issue. To address this, we introduce a novel and simple Multi-Epoch learning with Data Augmentation (MEDA) framework, suitable for both non-continual and continual learning scenarios, which can be seamlessly integrated into existing deep CTR models and may have potential applications to handle the “forgetting or overfitting” dilemma in the retraining and the well-known catastrophic forgetting problems. MEDA minimizes overfitting by reducing the dependency of the embedding layer on subsequent training data or the Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) layers, and achieves data augmentation through training the MLP with varied embedding spaces. Our findings confirm that pre-trained MLP layers can adapt to new embedding spaces, enhancing performance without overfitting. This adaptability underscores the MLP layers’ role in learning a matching function focused on the relative relationships among embeddings rather than their absolute positions. To our knowledge, MEDA represents the first multi-epoch training strategy tailored for deep CTR prediction models. We conduct extensive experiments on several public and business datasets, and the effectiveness of data augmentation and superiority over conventional single-epoch training are fully demonstrated. Besides, MEDA has exhibited significant benefits in a real-world online advertising system. CCS CONCEPTS • Information systems → Recommender systems.
摘要 (中文)
本文研究点击率（CTR）模型中的 one-epoch 过拟合现象，即性能在第二个 epoch 开始时明显下降。尽管已有大量研究，多 epoch 训练相比传统 one-epoch 方法的有效性仍不明确。我们将由高维数据稀疏性导致的 embedding 层过拟合认定为主要问题。为此，我们提出一种新颖且简单的基于数据增强的多 epoch 学习（MEDA）框架，适用于非持续学习与持续学习两种场景，可无缝集成到现有深度 CTR 模型中，并可能有助于处理重训练中的“遗忘或过拟合”困境以及众所周知的灾难性遗忘问题。MEDA 通过降低 embedding 层对后续训练数据或多层感知机（MLP）层的依赖来最小化过拟合，并通过在不同 embedding 空间上训练 MLP 来实现数据增强。我们的发现证实，预训练的 MLP 层能够适应新的 embedding 空间，在不发生过拟合的情况下提升性能。这种适应性凸显了 MLP 层的作用：它学习的是一个聚焦于 embedding 之间相对关系而非绝对位置的匹配函数。据我们所知，MEDA 是首个为深度 CTR 预测模型量身定制的多 epoch 训练策略。我们在多个公开数据集与业务数据集上进行了大量实验，充分证明了数据增强的有效性及其优于传统单 epoch 训练。此外，MEDA 在一个真实在线广告系统中展现出显著收益。CCS CONCEPTS • Information systems → Recommender systems.
Recommender Systems with Generative Retrieval
Google2023推荐工业Semantic IDgenerative retrieval, Semantic ID, sequence-to-sequence, codewords, cold-startPDF · arXiv
AuthorsShashank Rajput, Nikhil Mehta, Anima Singh, Raghunandan Keshavan, Trung Vu, Lukasz Heldt, Lichan Hong, Yi Tay, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Modern recommender systems perform large-scale retrieval by embedding queries and item candidates in the same unified space, followed by approximate nearest neighbor search to select top candidates given a query embedding. In this paper, we propose a novel generative retrieval approach, where the retrieval model autoregressively decodes the identifiers of the target candidates. To that end, we create semantically meaningful tuple of codewords to serve as a Semantic ID for each item. Given Semantic IDs for items in a user session, a Transformer-based sequence-to-sequence model is trained to predict the Semantic ID of the next item that the user will interact with. We show that recommender systems trained with the proposed paradigm significantly outperform the current SOTA models on various datasets. In addition, we show that incorporating Semantic IDs into the sequence-to-sequence model enhances its ability to generalize, as evidenced by the improved retrieval performance observed for items with no prior interaction history.
摘要 (中文)
现代推荐系统通过将查询和候选项嵌入到同一统一空间中进行大规模 retrieval，随后通过近似最近邻搜索，在给定查询 embedding 的情况下选出 top 候选项。本文提出一种新颖的 generative retrieval 方法，其中 retrieval 模型自回归地解码目标候选项的标识符。为此，我们为每个物品构造语义上有意义的 codeword 元组，作为其 Semantic ID。给定用户会话中物品的 Semantic ID，训练一个基于 Transformer 的 sequence-to-sequence 模型来预测用户接下来将交互的下一个物品的 Semantic ID。我们表明，用所提范式训练的推荐系统在各种数据集上显著优于当前的 SOTA 模型。此外，我们表明将 Semantic ID 融入 sequence-to-sequence 模型可增强其泛化能力，这一点可从对无历史交互记录的物品取得更好的 retrieval 性能得到印证。
Adapting Large Language Models by Integrating Collaborative Semantics for Recommendation
USTC2023推荐学术Semantic IDLLM-based recommendation, collaborative semantics, item indexing, vector quantization, alignment tuningPDF · arXiv
AuthorsBowen Zheng, Yupeng Hou, Hongyu Lu, Yu Chen, Wayne Xin Zhao, Ming Chen, Ji-Rong Wen
Abstract (EN)
Recently, large language models (LLMs) have shown great potential in recommender systems, either improving existing recommendation models or serving as the backbone. However, there exists a large semantic gap between LLMs and recommender systems, since items to be recommended are often indexed by discrete identifiers (item ID) out of the LLM’s vocabulary. In essence, LLMs capture language semantics while recommender systems imply collaborative semantics, making it difficult to sufficiently leverage the model capacity of LLMs for recommendation. To address this challenge, in this paper, we propose a new LLMbased recommendation model called LC-Rec, which can better integrate language and collaborative semantics for recommender systems. Our approach can directly generate items from the entire item set for recommendation, without relying on candidate items. Specifically, we make two major contributions in our approach. For item indexing, we design a learning-based vector quantization method with uniform semantic mapping, which can assign meaningful and non-conflicting IDs (called item indices) for items. For alignment tuning, we propose a series of specially designed tuning tasks to enhance the integration of collaborative semantics in LLMs. Our fine-tuning tasks enforce LLMs to deeply integrate language and collaborative semantics (characterized by the learned item indices), so as to achieve an effective adaptation to recommender systems. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, showing that our approach can outperform a number of competitive baselines including traditional recommenders and existing LLM-based recommenders. Our code is available at https://github.com/RUCAIBox/LC-Rec/.
摘要 (中文)
近期，大语言模型（LLMs）在推荐系统中展现出巨大潜力，既能改进现有推荐模型，也可作为骨干模型。然而，LLMs 与推荐系统之间存在巨大的语义鸿沟，因为待推荐的 item 通常由不在 LLM 词表内的离散标识符（item ID）来索引。本质上，LLMs 捕获的是语言语义，而推荐系统蕴含的是协同语义，这使得难以充分利用 LLMs 的模型能力来完成推荐。为应对这一挑战，本文提出了一种新的基于 LLM 的推荐模型 LC-Rec，它能够更好地融合推荐系统中的语言语义与协同语义。我们的方法可以直接从整个 item 集合中生成待推荐的 item，而不依赖候选 item。具体而言，我们的方法有两大贡献。在 item 索引方面，我们设计了一种基于学习的向量量化方法，并配以统一的语义映射，能够为 item 分配有意义且互不冲突的 ID（称为 item indices）。在对齐微调方面，我们提出了一系列专门设计的调优任务，以增强协同语义在 LLMs 中的融合。我们的微调任务促使 LLMs 深度融合语言语义与协同语义（由学到的 item indices 刻画），从而实现对推荐系统的有效适配。大量实验证明了我们方法的有效性，表明其性能优于包括传统推荐器和现有基于 LLM 的推荐器在内的多个有竞争力的 baselines。我们的代码见 https://github.com/RUCAIBox/LC-Rec/。
Semantic-Enhanced Differentiable Search Index Inspired by Learning Strategies
中科院 / 国科大2023搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法differentiable search index, document identifiers, semantic enhancement, learning strategies, document retrievalPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYubao Tang, Ruqing Zhang, Jiafeng Guo, Jiangui Chen, Zuowei Zhu, Shuaiqiang Wang, Dawei Yin, Xueqi Cheng
Abstract (EN)
Recently, a new paradigm called Differentiable Search Index (DSI) has been proposed for document retrieval, wherein a sequence-to-sequence model is learned to directly map queries to relevant document identifiers. The key idea behind DSI is to fully parameterize traditional ``index-retrieve'' pipelines within a single neural model, by encoding all documents in the corpus into the model parameters. In essence, DSI needs to resolve two major questions: (1) how to assign an identifier to each document, and (2) how to learn the associations between a document and its identifier. In this work, we propose a Semantic-Enhanced DSI model (SE-DSI) motivated by Learning Strategies in the area of Cognitive Psychology. Our approach advances original DSI in two ways: (1) For the document identifier, we take inspiration from Elaboration Strategies in human learning. Specifically, we assign each document an Elaborative Description based on the query generation technique, which is more meaningful than a string of integers in the original DSI; and (2) For the associations between a document and its identifier, we take inspiration from Rehearsal Strategies in human learning. Specifically, we select fine-grained semantic features from a document as Rehearsal Contents to improve document memorization. Both the offline and online experiments show improved retrieval performance over prevailing baselines.
摘要 (中文)
近来，一种名为 Differentiable Search Index（DSI）的新范式被提出用于文档检索，其中学习一个序列到序列模型以直接将 query 映射到相关文档标识符。DSI 背后的核心思想是将传统的“索引-检索”流水线完全参数化到单个神经模型中，通过将语料中的所有文档编码进模型参数。本质上，DSI 需要解决两个主要问题：(1) 如何为每个文档分配标识符，(2) 如何学习文档与其标识符之间的关联。在本工作中，我们受认知心理学领域学习策略的启发，提出 Semantic-Enhanced DSI 模型（SE-DSI）。我们的方法在两方面推进了原始 DSI：(1) 对于文档标识符，我们受人类学习中精加工策略（Elaboration Strategies）的启发。具体而言，我们基于 query 生成技术为每个文档分配一个 Elaborative Description，它比原始 DSI 中的一串整数更有意义；(2) 对于文档与其标识符之间的关联，我们受人类学习中复述策略（Rehearsal Strategies）的启发。具体而言，我们从文档中选取细粒度语义特征作为 Rehearsal Contents 以改善文档记忆。离线与在线实验均显示出相比主流 baseline 的检索性能提升。
TOME: A Two-stage Approach for Model-based Retrieval
人大 / 百度2023搜索工业生成式检索 / 排序方法model-based retrieval, document identifiers, tokenized URLs, two-stage generation, scaling lawsPDF · arXiv
AuthorsRuiyang Ren, Wayne Xin Zhao, Jing Liu, Hua Wu, Ji-Rong Wen, Haifeng Wang
Abstract (EN)
Recently, model-based retrieval has emerged as a new paradigm in text retrieval that discards the index in the traditional retrieval model and instead memorizes the candidate corpora using model parameters. This design employs a sequence-to-sequence paradigm to generate document identifiers, which enables the complete capture of the relevance between queries and documents and simplifies the classic indexretrieval-rerank pipeline. Despite its attractive qualities, there remain several major challenges in model-based retrieval, including the discrepancy between pre-training and fine-tuning, and the discrepancy between training and inference. To deal with the above challenges, we propose a novel two-stage model-based retrieval approach called TOME, which makes two major technical contributions, including the utilization of tokenized URLs as identifiers and the design of a two-stage generation architecture. We also propose a number of training strategies to deal with the training difficulty as the corpus size increases. Extensive experiments and analysis on MS MARCO and Natural Questions demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed approach, and we investigate the scaling laws of TOME by examining various influencing factors.
摘要 (中文)
近年来，基于模型的检索（model-based retrieval）作为文本检索的一种新范式出现，它摒弃了传统检索模型中的索引，转而使用模型参数来记忆候选语料。该设计采用 sequence-to-sequence 范式生成文档标识符，从而能够完整捕捉 query 与文档之间的相关性，并简化经典的 index-retrieval-rerank 流水线。尽管具有这些吸引人的特性，基于模型的检索仍存在若干重大挑战，包括预训练与微调之间的差异，以及训练与推理之间的差异。为应对上述挑战，我们提出了一种新颖的两阶段基于模型的检索方法 TOME，其做出了两项主要技术贡献：使用 tokenized URL 作为标识符，以及设计了两阶段生成架构。我们还提出了若干训练策略，以应对语料规模增大带来的训练困难。在 MS MARCO 和 Natural Questions 上的大量实验与分析证明了我们方法的有效性，并且我们通过考察各种影响因素研究了 TOME 的 scaling law。
Evaluating Generative Ad Hoc Information Retrieval
Leipzig University / The University of Queensland2023搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, evaluation methodology, ad hoc retrieval, user model, generated responsesPDF · arXiv
AuthorsLukas Gienapp, Harrisen Scells, Niklas Deckers, Janek Bevendorff, Shuai Wang, Johannes Kiesel, Shahbaz Syed, Maik Fröbe, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Recent advances in large language models have enabled the development of viable generative retrieval systems. Instead of a traditional document ranking, generative retrieval systems often directly return a grounded generated text as a response to a query. Quantifying the utility of the textual responses is essential for appropriately evaluating such generative ad hoc retrieval. Yet, the established evaluation methodology for ranking-based ad hoc retrieval is not suited for the reliable and reproducible evaluation of generated responses. To lay a foundation for developing new evaluation methods for generative retrieval systems, we survey the relevant literature from the fields of information retrieval and natural language processing, identify search tasks and system architectures in generative retrieval, develop a new user model, and study its operationalization.
摘要 (中文)
大语言模型的近期进展使得构建可行的生成式检索系统成为可能。与传统的文档排序不同，生成式检索系统通常直接返回一段有依据的生成文本作为对 query 的响应。量化这些文本响应的效用对于恰当评估此类生成式 ad hoc 检索至关重要。然而，为基于排序的 ad hoc 检索所建立的评测方法并不适用于对生成响应进行可靠且可复现的评估。为给生成式检索系统的新评测方法奠定基础，我们综述了信息检索和自然语言处理领域的相关文献，梳理了生成式检索中的搜索任务与系统架构，构建了一个新的用户模型，并研究了其可操作化方式。
T2Ranking: A large-scale Chinese Benchmark for Passage Ranking
清华 / 中关村实验室2023搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法passage ranking, Chinese benchmark, graded relevance, passage retrieval, re-rankingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsXiaohui Xie, Qian Dong, Bingning Wang, Feiyang Lv, Ting Yao, Weinan Gan, Zhijing Wu, Xiangsheng Li, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Passage ranking involves two stages: passage retrieval and passage re-ranking, which are important and challenging topics for both academics and industries in the area of Information Retrieval (IR). However, the commonly-used datasets for passage ranking usually focus on the English language. For non-English scenarios, such as Chinese, the existing datasets are limited in terms of data scale, fine-grained relevance annotation and false negative issues. To address this problem, we introduce T2Ranking, a large-scale Chinese benchmark for passage ranking. T2Ranking comprises more than 300K queries and over 2M unique passages from real-world search engines. Expert annotators are recruited to provide 4-level graded relevance scores (fine-grained) for query-passage pairs instead of binary relevance judgments (coarse-grained). To ease the false negative issues, more passages with higher diversities are considered when performing relevance annotations, especially in the test set, to ensure a more accurate evaluation. Apart from the textual query and passage data, other auxiliary resources are also provided, such as query types and XML files of documents which passages are generated from, to facilitate further studies. To evaluate the dataset, commonly used ranking models are implemented and tested on T2Ranking as baselines. The experimental results show that T2Ranking is challenging and there is still scope for improvement. The full data and all codes are available at https://github.com/THUIR/T2Ranking/
摘要 (中文)
Passage ranking 包含两个阶段：passage retrieval 与 passage re-ranking，这在信息检索（IR）领域对学术界和工业界而言都是重要且具有挑战性的课题。然而，常用于 passage ranking 的数据集通常聚焦于英语。对于非英语场景（如中文），现有数据集在数据规模、细粒度相关性标注以及 false negative 问题上存在局限。为解决这一问题，我们提出 T2Ranking，一个大规模的 passage ranking 中文基准。T2Ranking 包含来自真实搜索引擎的 30 万余条 query 和 200 万余个唯一 passage。我们招募专家标注员为 query-passage 对提供 4 级分级相关性分数（细粒度），而非二元相关性判断（粗粒度）。为缓解 false negative 问题，在进行相关性标注时考虑了更多、更多样化的 passage，尤其是在测试集中，以确保更准确的评测。除文本 query 和 passage 数据外，还提供了其他辅助资源，如 query 类型以及 passage 所来源文档的 XML 文件，以促进进一步研究。为评估该数据集，我们在 T2Ranking 上实现并测试了常用排序模型作为 baseline。实验结果表明 T2Ranking 具有挑战性，仍有提升空间。完整数据与全部代码见 https://github.com/THUIR/T2Ranking/
A Unified Generative Retriever for Knowledge-Intensive Language Tasks via Prompt Learning
中科院 / 国科大2023搜索学术生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retriever, knowledge-intensive language tasks, prompt learning, n-gram identifier, multi-taskPDF · arXiv
AuthorsJiangui Chen, Ruqing Zhang, Jiafeng Guo, Maarten de Rijke, Yiqun Liu, Yixing Fan, Xueqi Cheng
Abstract (EN)
Knowledge-intensive language tasks (KILTs) benefit from retrieving high-quality relevant contexts from large external knowledge corpora. Learning task-specific retrievers that return relevant contexts at an appropriate level of semantic granularity, such as a document retriever, passage retriever, sentence retriever, and entity retriever, may help to achieve better performance on the end-to-end task. But a task-specific retriever usually has poor generalization ability to new domains and tasks, and it may be costly to deploy a variety of specialised retrievers in practice. We propose a unified generative retriever (UGR) that combines task-specific effectiveness with robust performance over different retrieval tasks in KILTs. To achieve this goal, we make two major contributions: (i) To unify different retrieval tasks into a single generative form, we introduce an n-gram-based identifier for relevant contexts at different levels of granularity in KILTs. And (ii) to address different retrieval tasks with a single model, we employ a prompt learning strategy and investigate three methods to design prompt tokens for each task. In this way, the proposed UGR model can not only share common knowledge across tasks for better generalization, but also perform different retrieval tasks effectively by distinguishing task-specific characteristics. We train UGR on a heterogeneous set of retrieval corpora with well-designed prompts in a supervised and multi-task fashion. Experimental results on the KILT benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of UGR on in-domain datasets, out-of-domain datasets, and unseen tasks.
摘要 (中文)
知识密集型语言任务（KILT）受益于从大型外部知识语料中检索高质量的相关上下文。学习任务特定的检索器，在适当的语义粒度层级上返回相关上下文，例如文档检索器、passage 检索器、句子检索器和实体检索器，可能有助于在端到端任务上取得更好性能。但任务特定的检索器通常对新领域和新任务的泛化能力较差，且在实践中部署多种专用检索器可能成本高昂。我们提出统一生成式检索器（UGR），它将任务特定的有效性与跨 KILT 中不同检索任务的稳健性能结合起来。为实现这一目标，我们做出两项主要贡献：（i）为将不同检索任务统一为单一生成形式，我们为 KILT 中不同粒度层级的相关上下文引入了基于 n-gram 的标识符。（ii）为用单一模型应对不同检索任务，我们采用 prompt learning 策略，并研究了三种为各任务设计 prompt token 的方法。通过这种方式，所提出的 UGR 模型不仅能跨任务共享通用知识以获得更好的泛化能力，还能通过区分任务特定特征来有效执行不同的检索任务。我们以监督式、多任务的方式在精心设计 prompt 的异构检索语料集合上训练 UGR。在 KILT 基准上的实验结果证明了 UGR 在域内数据集、域外数据集以及未见任务上的有效性。
Multiview Identifiers Enhanced Generative Retrieval
港理工 / Microsoft2023搜索工业生成式检索 / 排序方法generative retrieval, multiview identifiers, synthetic identifiers, docid, passage rankingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYongqi Li, Nan Yang, Liang Wang, Furu Wei, Wenjie Li
Abstract (EN)
Instead of simply matching a query to pre-existing passages, generative retrieval generates identifier strings of passages as the retrieval target. At a cost, the identifier must be distinctive enough to represent a passage. Current approaches use either a numeric ID or a text piece (such as a title or substrings) as the identifier. However, these identifiers cannot cover a passage's content well. As such, we are motivated to propose a new type of identifier, synthetic identifiers, that are generated based on the content of a passage and could integrate contextualized information that text pieces lack. Furthermore, we simultaneously consider multiview identifiers, including synthetic identifiers, titles, and substrings. These views of identifiers complement each other and facilitate the holistic ranking of passages from multiple perspectives. We conduct a series of experiments on three public datasets, and the results indicate that our proposed approach performs the best in generative retrieval, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness.
摘要 (中文)
生成式检索并非简单地将 query 与已有 passage 进行匹配，而是生成 passage 的标识符字符串作为检索目标。代价是，标识符必须足够具有区分度以代表一个 passage。当前方法要么使用数字 ID，要么使用文本片段（如标题或子串）作为标识符。然而，这些标识符无法很好地覆盖 passage 的内容。因此，我们受到启发提出一种新型标识符——合成标识符（synthetic identifiers），它基于 passage 的内容生成，能够整合文本片段所缺乏的上下文信息。此外，我们同时考虑多视角标识符，包括合成标识符、标题和子串。这些标识符视角相互补充，有助于从多个角度对 passage 进行整体排序。我们在三个公开数据集上进行了一系列实验，结果表明我们所提方法在生成式检索中表现最佳，证明了其有效性与稳健性。
GPT4Rec: A Generative Framework for Personalized Recommendation and User Interests Interpretation
Amazon2023推荐工业LLM4Rec 与对齐generative recommendation, search query generation, multi-query beam search, cold-start, interpretabilityPDF · arXiv
AuthorsJinming Li, Wentao Zhang, Tian Wang, Guanglei Xiong, Alan Lu, Gerard Medioni
Abstract (EN)
Recent advancements in Natural Language Processing (NLP) have led to the development of NLP-based recommender systems that have shown superior performance. However, current models commonly treat items as mere IDs and adopt discriminative modeling, resulting in limitations of (1) fully leveraging the content information of items and the language modeling capabilities of NLP models; (2) interpreting user interests to improve relevance and diversity; and (3) adapting practical circumstances such as growing item inventories. To address these limitations, we present GPT4Rec, a novel and flexible generative framework inspired by search engines. It first generates hypothetical "search queries" given item titles in a user’s history, and then retrieves items for recommendation by searching these queries. The framework overcomes previous limitations by learning both user and item embeddings in the language space. To well-capture user interests with different aspects and granularity for improving relevance and diversity, we propose a multi-query generation technique with beam search. The generated queries naturally serve as interpretable representations of user interests and can be searched to recommend cold-start items. With GPT-2 language model and BM25 search engine, our framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods by 75.7% and 22.2% in Recall@K on two public datasets. Experiments further revealed that multiquery generation with beam search improves both the diversity of retrieved items and the coverage of a user’s multi-interests. The adaptiveness and interpretability of generated queries are discussed with qualitative case studies. CCS CONCEPTS • Information systems → Recommender systems.
摘要 (中文)
自然语言处理（NLP）的近期进展催生了基于 NLP 的推荐系统，并展现出优越的性能。然而，当前模型通常仅将物品视为 ID 并采用判别式建模，从而导致以下局限：(1) 无法充分利用物品的内容信息以及 NLP 模型的语言建模能力；(2) 难以解释用户兴趣以提升相关性和多样性；(3) 难以适应不断增长的商品库等实际情形。为解决这些局限，我们提出 GPT4Rec，一个受搜索引擎启发的新颖且灵活的生成式框架。它首先根据用户历史中的物品标题生成假设性的“搜索查询”，然后通过检索这些查询来获取用于推荐的物品。该框架在语言空间中同时学习用户和物品 embedding，从而克服了前述局限。为了从不同方面和粒度很好地捕捉用户兴趣以提升相关性和多样性，我们提出带 beam search 的多查询生成技术。生成的查询天然地作为用户兴趣的可解释表示，并可通过检索来推荐冷启动物品。在 GPT-2 语言模型和 BM25 搜索引擎上，我们的框架在两个公开数据集上于 Recall@K 指标上分别超出 state-of-the-art 方法 75.7% 和 22.2%。实验进一步表明，带 beam search 的多查询生成同时提升了检索物品的多样性和对用户多重兴趣的覆盖度。我们通过定性案例研究讨论了生成查询的自适应性和可解释性。CCS 概念 • 信息系统 → 推荐系统。
TALLRec: An Effective and Efficient Tuning Framework to Align Large Language Model with Recommendation
USTC2023推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐instruction tuning, LLM alignment, recommendation, cross-domain generalization, parameter efficiencyPDF · arXiv
AuthorsKeqin Bao, Jizhi Zhang, Yang Zhang, Wenjie Wang, Fuli Feng, Xiangnan He
Abstract (EN)
Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across diverse domains, thereby prompting researchers to explore their potential for use in recommendation systems. Initial attempts have leveraged the exceptional capabilities of LLMs, such as rich knowledge and strong generalization through In-context Learning, which involves phrasing the recommendation task as prompts. Nevertheless, the performance of LLMs in recommendation tasks remains suboptimal due to a substantial disparity between the training tasks for LLMs and recommendation tasks, as well as inadequate recommendation data during pre-training. To bridge the gap, we consider building a Large Recommendation Language Model by tunning LLMs with recommendation data. To this end, we propose an efficient and effective Tuning framework for Aligning LLMs with Recommendations, namely TALLRec. We have demonstrated that the proposed TALLRec framework can significantly enhance the recommendation capabilities of LLMs in the movie and book domains, even with a limited dataset of fewer than 100 samples. Additionally, the proposed framework is highly efficient and can be executed on a single RTX 3090 with LLaMA-7B. Furthermore, the fine-tuned LLM exhibits robust cross-domain generalization. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/SAI990323/TALLRec. CCS CONCEPTS • Information systems → Recommender systems.
摘要 (中文)
大语言模型（LLM）在多个领域展现出卓越性能，促使研究者探索其在推荐系统中的潜力。最初的尝试利用 LLM 的出色能力，如丰富的知识和通过 In-context Learning 实现的强泛化能力，将推荐任务表述为 prompt。然而，由于 LLM 的训练任务与推荐任务之间存在巨大差异，且预训练期间缺乏足够的推荐数据，LLM 在推荐任务上的表现仍不尽如人意。为弥合这一差距，我们考虑用推荐数据微调 LLM 来构建大型推荐语言模型。为此，我们提出一个高效且有效的调优框架，用于将 LLM 与推荐对齐，即 TALLRec。我们证明，所提出的 TALLRec 框架即使在样本量不足 100 的有限数据集上，也能显著增强 LLM 在电影和图书领域的推荐能力。此外，该框架效率极高，可在单张 RTX 3090 上以 LLaMA-7B 运行。而且，微调后的 LLM 展现出稳健的跨域泛化能力。代码和数据见 https://github.com/SAI990323/TALLRec。
LLaRA: Large Language-Recommendation Assistant
USTC2023推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐sequential recommendation, hybrid prompting, ID embeddings, curriculum learning, LLMPDF · arXiv
AuthorsJiayi Liao, Sihang Li, Zhengyi Yang, Jiancan Wu, Yancheng Yuan, Xiang Wang, Xiangnan He
Abstract (EN)
Sequential recommendation aims to predict users’ next interaction with items based on their past engagement sequence. Recently, the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs) has sparked interest in leveraging them for sequential recommendation, viewing it as language modeling. Previous studies represent items within LLMs’ input prompts as either ID indices or textual metadata. However, these approaches often fail to either encapsulate comprehensive world knowledge or exhibit sufficient behavioral understanding. To combine the complementary strengths of conventional recommenders in capturing behavioral patterns of users and LLMs in encoding world knowledge about items, we introduce Large Language-Recommendation Assistant (LLaRA). Specifically, it uses a novel hybrid prompting method that integrates ID-based item embeddings learned by traditional recommendation models with textual item features. Treating the “sequential behaviors of users” as a distinct modality beyond texts, we employ a projector to align the traditional recommender’s ID embeddings with the LLM’s input space. Moreover, rather than directly exposing the hybrid prompt to LLMs, a curriculum learning strategy is adopted to gradually ramp up training complexity. Initially, we warm up the LLM using text-only prompts, which better suit its inherent language modeling ability. Subsequently, we progressively transition to the hybrid prompts, training the model to seamlessly ∗ Corresponding authors, and they are also affiliated with Institute of Dataspace, Hefei Comprehensive National Science Center. 摘要 (中文)
序列推荐旨在基于用户过去的参与序列，预测其下一次与物品的交互。近来，大语言模型（LLM）的出现激发了将其用于序列推荐、并视之为语言建模的兴趣。此前的研究将 LLM 输入 prompt 中的物品表示为 ID 索引或文本元数据。然而，这些方法往往无法要么封装全面的世界知识，要么展现足够的行为理解。为了结合传统推荐器在捕捉用户行为模式方面的优势与 LLM 在编码物品世界知识方面的优势，我们提出 Large Language-Recommendation Assistant（LLaRA）。具体而言，它采用一种新颖的混合 prompt 方法，将传统推荐模型学到的基于 ID 的物品 embedding 与文本物品特征相结合。我们将“用户序列行为”视为文本之外的一种独立模态，采用一个 projector 将传统推荐器的 ID embedding 与 LLM 的输入空间对齐。此外，我们并不直接将混合 prompt 暴露给 LLM，而是采用课程学习（curriculum learning）策略，逐步提升训练复杂度。起初，我们用纯文本 prompt 对 LLM 进行预热，这更契合其固有的语言建模能力。随后，我们逐步过渡到混合 prompt，训练模型将从传统序列推荐器获得的行为知识无缝融入 LLM。实证结果验证了所提框架的有效性。代码开源于 https://github.com/ljy0ustc/LLaRA。CCS 概念 • 信息系统 → 推荐系统。（∗ 通讯作者，同时隶属合肥综合性国家科学中心数据空间研究院。允许出于个人或课堂教学目的，在无费用前提下制作本作品全部或部分的数字或纸质副本，前提是副本不得用于盈利或商业优势，且副本须在首页保留本声明。本作品中由非作者第三方拥有的版权组件必须予以尊重。允许带署名的摘要引用。除此以外的复制、再发表、在服务器上发布或向列表分发，均需事先获得具体许可和/或支付费用。请向 permissions@acm.org 申请许可。SIGIR ’24, July 14–18, 2024, Washington, DC, USA © 2024 Copyright held by the owner/author(s). Text Is All You Need: Learning Language Representations for Sequential Recommendation
UCSD / Amazon2023推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐language representations, sequential recommendation, bi-directional Transformer, cold-start, item attributesPDF · arXiv
AuthorsJiacheng Li, Ming Wang, Jin Li, Jinmiao Fu, Xin Shen, Jingbo Shang, Julian McAuley
Abstract (EN)
Sequential recommendation aims to model dynamic user behavior from historical interactions. Existing methods rely on either explicit item IDs or general textual features for sequence modeling to understand user preferences. While promising, these approaches still struggle to model cold-start items or transfer knowledge to new datasets. In this paper, we propose to model user preferences and item features as language representations that can be generalized to new items and datasets. To this end, we present a novel framework, named Recformer, which effectively learns language representations for sequential recommendation. Specifically, we propose to formulate an item as a “sentence” (word sequence) by flattening item key-value attributes described by text so that an item sequence for a user becomes a sequence of sentences. For recommendation, Recformer is trained to understand the “sentence” sequence and retrieve the next “sentence”. To encode item sequences, we design a bi-directional Transformer similar to the model Longformer but with different embedding layers for sequential recommendation. For effective representation learning, we propose novel pretraining and finetuning methods which combine language understanding and recommendation tasks. Therefore, Recformer can effectively recommend the next item based on language representations. Extensive experiments conducted on six datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of Recformer for sequential recommendation, especially in low-resource and cold-start settings. CCS CONCEPTS • Information systems → Recommender systems.
摘要 (中文)
序列推荐旨在从历史交互中建模动态用户行为。现有方法依赖显式的物品 ID 或通用文本特征来进行序列建模以理解用户偏好。尽管有一定前景，这些方法仍难以建模冷启动物品或将知识迁移到新数据集。本文提出将用户偏好和物品特征建模为可泛化到新物品和新数据集的语言表示。为此，我们提出一个新颖框架 Recformer，它能为序列推荐有效学习语言表示。具体而言，我们提出通过展平由文本描述的物品键值属性，将物品表述为一个句子（词序列），从而用户的物品序列就成为一个句子序列。在推荐时，Recformer 被训练来理解该句子序列并检索下一个句子。为编码物品序列，我们设计了一个类似 Longformer 的双向 Transformer，但为序列推荐采用了不同的 embedding 层。为实现有效的表示学习，我们提出了结合语言理解与推荐任务的新颖预训练和微调方法。因此，Recformer 能够基于语言表示有效推荐下一个物品。在六个数据集上进行的大量实验证明了 Recformer 在序列推荐中的有效性，尤其是在低资源和冷启动场景下。
E4SRec: An Elegant Effective Efficient Extensible Solution of Large Language Models for Sequential Recommendation
清华2023推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐LLM-based recommendation, sequential recommendation, ID-based recommendation, pluggable parameters, frozen LLMPDF · arXiv
AuthorsXinhang Li, Chong Chen, Xiangyu Zhao, Yong Zhang, Chunxiao Xing
Abstract (EN)
The recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have sparked interest in harnessing their potential within recommender systems. Since LLMs are designed for natural language tasks, existing recommendation approaches have predominantly transformed recommendation tasks into open-domain natural language generation tasks. However, this approach necessitates items to possess rich semantic information, often generates out-of-range results, and suffers from notably low efficiency and limited extensibility. Furthermore, practical ID-based recommendation strategies, reliant on a huge number of unique identities (IDs) to represent users and items, have gained prominence in real-world recommender systems due to their effectiveness and efficiency. Nevertheless, the incapacity of LLMs to model IDs presents a formidable challenge when seeking to leverage LLMs for personalized recommendations. In this paper, we introduce an Elegant Effective Efficient Extensible solution for large language models for Sequential Recommendation (E4SRec), which seamlessly integrates LLMs with traditional recommender systems that exclusively utilize IDs to represent items. Specifically, E4SRec takes ID sequences as inputs, ensuring that the generated outputs fall within the candidate lists. Furthermore, E4SRec possesses the capability to generate the entire ranking list in a single forward process, and demands only a minimal set of pluggable parameters, which are trained for each dataset while keeping the entire LLM frozen. We substantiate the effectiveness, efficiency, and extensibility of our proposed E4SRec through comprehensive experiments conducted on four widely-used real-world datasets. The implementation code is accessible at this https URL.
摘要 (中文)
近年来 Large Language Models (LLMs) 的进展激发了人们挖掘其在推荐系统中潜力的兴趣。由于 LLM 是为自然语言任务设计的，现有推荐方法大多将推荐任务转化为开放域自然语言生成任务。然而，这种方式要求物品具备丰富的语义信息，常常生成超出范围（out-of-range）的结果，并且效率明显偏低、可扩展性有限。此外，依赖大量唯一标识（IDs）来表示用户和物品的实用 ID-based 推荐策略，因其有效性和高效性而在真实推荐系统中占据重要地位。但 LLM 无法建模 ID，这为利用 LLM 进行个性化推荐带来了巨大挑战。本文提出 E4SRec（Elegant Effective Efficient Extensible solution for large language models for Sequential Recommendation），将 LLM 与仅使用 ID 表示物品的传统推荐系统无缝结合。具体而言，E4SRec 以 ID 序列作为输入，确保生成的输出落在候选列表范围内。此外，E4SRec 能够在单次前向过程中生成完整的排序列表，且仅需极少量可插拔（pluggable）参数，这些参数针对每个数据集单独训练，同时整个 LLM 保持冻结。我们在四个广泛使用的真实数据集上进行了全面实验，验证了所提 E4SRec 的有效性、高效性和可扩展性。实现代码见 this https URL。
OpenP5: An Open-Source Platform for Developing, Training, and Evaluating LLM-based Recommender Systems
Rutgers2023推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐LLM-based recommendation, open-source platform, item indexing, sequential recommendation, generative recommender systemsPDF · arXiv
AuthorsShuyuan Xu, Wenyue Hua, Yongfeng Zhang
Abstract (EN)
In recent years, the integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into recommender systems has garnered interest among both practitioners and researchers. Despite this interest, the field is still emerging, and the lack of open-source R&D platforms may impede the exploration of LLM-based recommendations. This paper introduces OpenP5, an open-source platform designed as a resource to facilitate the development, training, and evaluation of LLM-based generative recommender systems for research purposes. The platform is implemented using encoder-decoder LLMs (e.g., T5) and decoder-only LLMs (e.g., Llama-2) across 10 widely recognized public datasets, catering to two fundamental recommendation tasks: sequential and straightforward recommendations. Recognizing the crucial role of item IDs in LLM-based recommendations, we have also incorporated three item indexing methods within the OpenP5 platform: random indexing, sequential indexing and collaborative indexing. Built on the Transformers library, the platform facilitates easy customization of LLM-based recommendations for users. OpenP5 boasts a range of features including extensible data processing, task-centric optimization, comprehensive datasets and checkpoints, efficient acceleration, and standardized evaluations, making it a valuable tool for the implementation and evaluation of LLM-based recommender systems. The open-source code and pre-trained checkpoints for the OpenP5 library are publicly available at https://github.com/agiresearch/OpenP5.
摘要 (中文)
近年来，将大语言模型（LLM）集成到推荐系统中引起了从业者与研究者的兴趣。尽管有这种兴趣，该领域仍处于新兴阶段，缺乏开源的研发平台可能阻碍对基于 LLM 推荐的探索。本文介绍 OpenP5，一个开源平台，旨在作为促进面向研究用途的基于 LLM 的生成式推荐系统的开发、训练与评估的资源。该平台基于 encoder-decoder LLM（如 T5）与 decoder-only LLM（如 Llama-2）实现，覆盖 10 个广泛认可的公开数据集，面向两类基本推荐任务：序列推荐与简单推荐。鉴于物品 ID 在基于 LLM 推荐中的关键作用，我们还在 OpenP5 平台中纳入了三种物品索引方法：随机索引、序列索引与协同索引。该平台构建于 Transformers 库之上，便于用户轻松定制基于 LLM 的推荐。OpenP5 具有一系列特性，包括可扩展的数据处理、以任务为中心的优化、全面的数据集与 checkpoint、高效加速以及标准化评估，使其成为实现与评估基于 LLM 的推荐系统的宝贵工具。OpenP5 库的开源代码与预训练 checkpoint 公开于 https://github.com/agiresearch/OpenP5。
CoLLM: Integrating Collaborative Embeddings into Large Language Models for Recommendation
USTC2023推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐collaborative embeddings, LLMRec, cold-start, warm-start, token embedding spacePDF · arXiv
AuthorsYang Zhang, Fuli Feng, Jizhi Zhang, Keqin Bao, Qifan Wang, Xiangnan He
Abstract (EN)
Leveraging Large Language Models as Recommenders (LLMRec) has gained significant attention and introduced fresh perspectives in user preference modeling. Existing LLMRec approaches prioritize text semantics, usually neglecting the valuable collaborative information from user-item interactions in recommendations. While these text-emphasizing approaches excel in cold-start scenarios, they may yield sub-optimal performance in warm-start situations. In pursuit of superior recommendations for both cold and warm start scenarios, we introduce CoLLM, an innovative LLMRec methodology that seamlessly incorporates collaborative information into LLMs for recommendation. CoLLM captures collaborative information through an external traditional model and maps it to the input token embedding space of LLM, forming collaborative embeddings for LLM usage. Through this external integration of collaborative information, CoLLM ensures effective modeling of collaborative information without modifying the LLM itself, providing the flexibility to employ various collaborative information modeling techniques. Extensive experiments validate that CoLLM adeptly integrates collaborative information into LLMs, resulting in enhanced recommendation performance. We release the code and data at https://github.com/zyang1580/CoLLM.
摘要 (中文)
将 Large Language Models 用作推荐器（LLMRec）已引起广泛关注，并为用户偏好建模引入了新视角。现有 LLMRec 方法优先考虑文本语义，通常忽略了推荐中来自用户-商品交互的宝贵协同信息。这些强调文本的方法在冷启动场景中表现出色，但在暖启动情形下可能产生次优性能。为了在冷、暖启动场景下都获得更优推荐，我们提出 CoLLM，一种创新的 LLMRec 方法，将协同信息无缝融入 LLM 以用于推荐。CoLLM 通过一个外部传统模型捕捉协同信息，并将其映射到 LLM 的输入 token embedding 空间，形成供 LLM 使用的协同 embedding。通过这种外部化的协同信息集成，CoLLM 在不对 LLM 本身做修改的前提下保证了对协同信息的有效建模，并可灵活采用各种协同信息建模技术。大量实验验证了 CoLLM 能巧妙地将协同信息集成进 LLM，从而带来推荐性能的提升。代码与数据见 https://github.com/zyang1580/CoLLM。
On Generative Agents in Recommendation
新加坡国立 / 清华2023推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐user simulator, generative agents, LLM-empowered agents, filter bubble, recommendation simulatorPDF · arXiv
AuthorsAn Zhang, Yuxin Chen, Leheng Sheng, Xiang Wang, Tat-Seng Chua
Abstract (EN)
Recommender systems are the cornerstone of today's information dissemination, yet a disconnect between offline metrics and online performance greatly hinders their development. Addressing this challenge, we envision a recommendation simulator, capitalizing on recent breakthroughs in human-level intelligence exhibited by Large Language Models (LLMs). We propose Agent4Rec, a user simulator in recommendation, leveraging LLM-empowered generative agents equipped with user profile, memory, and actions modules specifically tailored for the recommender system. In particular, these agents' profile modules are initialized using real-world datasets (e.g. MovieLens, Steam, Amazon-Book), capturing users' unique tastes and social traits; memory modules log both factual and emotional memories and are integrated with an emotion-driven reflection mechanism; action modules support a wide variety of behaviors, spanning both taste-driven and emotion-driven actions. Each agent interacts with personalized recommender models in a page-by-page manner, relying on a pre-implemented collaborative filtering-based recommendation algorithm. We delve into both the capabilities and limitations of Agent4Rec, aiming to explore an essential research question: ``To what extent can LLM-empowered generative agents faithfully simulate the behavior of real, autonomous humans in recommender systems?'' Extensive and multi-faceted evaluations of Agent4Rec highlight both the alignment and deviation between agents and user-personalized preferences. Beyond mere performance comparison, we explore insightful experiments, such as emulating the filter bubble effect and discovering the underlying causal relationships in recommendation tasks. Our codes are available at https://github.com/LehengTHU/Agent4Rec.
摘要 (中文)
推荐系统是当今信息传播的基石，然而离线指标与线上表现之间的脱节极大地阻碍了其发展。为应对这一挑战，我们设想了一个推荐模拟器，充分利用大语言模型（LLM）近期在类人智能方面所展现的突破。我们提出了 Agent4Rec，一个推荐场景下的用户模拟器，它利用由 LLM 赋能的生成式智能体（generative agents），这些智能体配备了专为推荐系统量身定制的用户画像、记忆和动作模块。具体而言，这些智能体的画像模块使用真实世界数据集（如 MovieLens、Steam、Amazon-Book）进行初始化，以捕捉用户独特的品味和社会特征；记忆模块记录事实性记忆与情感性记忆，并与情感驱动的反思机制相结合；动作模块支持多种多样的行为，涵盖品味驱动和情感驱动的动作。每个智能体以逐页（page-by-page）的方式与个性化推荐模型进行交互，并依赖一个预先实现的基于协同过滤的推荐算法。我们深入探究了 Agent4Rec 的能力与局限，旨在探索一个关键的研究问题：“由 LLM 赋能的生成式智能体能够在多大程度上忠实地模拟推荐系统中真实、自主的人类行为？”对 Agent4Rec 进行的大量多维度评估，既揭示了智能体与用户个性化偏好之间的一致之处，也揭示了偏差之处。除了单纯的性能比较，我们还开展了富有洞见的实验，例如模拟信息茧房（filter bubble）效应，以及发现推荐任务中潜在的因果关系。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/LehengTHU/Agent4Rec 获取。
One Model for All: Large Language Models are Domain-Agnostic Recommendation Systems
武大2023推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐domain-agnostic recommendation, LLM, multi-domain, cold-start, item representationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsZuoli Tang, Zhaoxin Huan, Zihao Li, Xiaolu Zhang, Jun Hu, Chilin Fu, Jun Zhou, Lixin Zou, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Sequential recommendation systems aim to predict users' next likely interaction based on their history. However, these systems face data sparsity and cold-start problems. Utilizing data from other domains, known as multi-domain methods, is useful for alleviating these problems. However, traditional multi-domain methods rely on meaningless ID-based item representation, which makes it difficult to align items with similar meanings from different domains, yielding sup-optimal knowledge transfer. This paper introduces LLM-Rec, a framework that utilizes pre-trained large language models (LLMs) for domain-agnostic recommendation. Specifically, we mix user's behaviors from multiple domains and concatenate item titles into a sentence, then use LLMs for generating user and item representations. By mixing behaviors across different domains, we can exploit the knowledge encoded in LLMs to bridge the semantic across over multi-domain behaviors, thus obtaining semantically rich representations and improving performance in all domains. Furthermore, we explore the underlying reasons why LLMs are effective and investigate whether LLMs can understand the semantic correlations as the recommendation model, and if advanced techniques like scaling laws in NLP also work in recommendations. We conduct extensive experiments with LLMs ranging from 40M to 6.7B to answer the above questions and to verify the effectiveness of LLM-Rec in multi-domain recommendation.
摘要 (中文)
序列推荐系统旨在根据用户历史预测其下一次可能的交互。然而，这类系统面临数据稀疏和冷启动问题。利用来自其他领域的数据，即多域方法，有助于缓解这些问题。但传统的多域方法依赖无意义的基于 ID 的 item 表示，使得难以对齐来自不同领域的含义相似的 item，导致知识迁移次优。本文提出 LLM-Rec，一个利用预训练大语言模型（LLM）进行领域无关推荐的框架。具体而言，我们将用户来自多个领域的行为混合，并将 item 标题拼接成句子，然后使用 LLM 生成用户和 item 表示。通过混合跨不同领域的行为，我们能够利用 LLM 中编码的知识来桥接多域行为之间的语义，从而获得语义丰富的表示并提升所有领域的性能。此外，我们探究了 LLM 为何有效的深层原因，并研究了 LLM 能否像推荐模型那样理解语义相关性，以及 NLP 中的 scaling laws 等先进技术是否也适用于推荐。我们使用从 40M 到 6.7B 的 LLM 进行了大量实验，以回答上述问题并验证 LLM-Rec 在多域推荐中的有效性。
GPT-4 Technical Report
OpenAI2023通用工业基础设施 / 基础模型GPT-4, multimodal, post-training alignment, predictable scaling, TransformerPDF
AuthorsOpenAI
Abstract (EN)
We report the development of GPT-4, a large-scale, multimodal model which can accept image and text inputs and produce text outputs. While less capable than humans in many real-world scenarios, GPT-4 exhibits human-level performance on various professional and academic benchmarks, including passing a simulated bar exam with a score around the top 10% of test takers. GPT-4 is a Transformerbased model pre-trained to predict the next token in a document. The post-training alignment process results in improved performance on measures of factuality and adherence to desired behavior. A core component of this project was developing infrastructure and optimization methods that behave predictably across a wide range of scales. This allowed us to accurately predict some aspects of GPT-4’s performance based on models trained with no more than 1/1,000th the compute of GPT-4.
摘要 (中文)
我们报告 GPT-4 的研发工作。GPT-4 是一个大规模、多模态的模型，可接受图像和文本输入并产生文本输出。尽管在许多真实场景中不如人类，GPT-4 在多种专业和学术基准上展现出人类水平的表现，包括在模拟律师考试中取得约处于考生前 10% 的成绩。GPT-4 是一个基于 Transformer 的模型，经预训练以预测文档中的下一个 token。后训练对齐过程提升了其在事实性和遵循期望行为等指标上的表现。本项目的一个核心组成部分是开发能够在广泛规模范围内表现可预测的基础设施与优化方法。这使我们能够仅基于算力不超过 GPT-4 的 1/1,000 的模型，准确预测 GPT-4 性能的某些方面。
Multi-Epoch Learning for Deep Click-Through Rate Prediction Models
快手2023推荐工业CTR 训练 / 工程multi-epoch learning, data augmentation, embedding overfitting, CTR prediction, MEDAPDF
AuthorsZhaocheng Liu, Zhongxiang Fan, Jian Liang, Dongying Kong, Han Li
Abstract (EN)
The one-epoch overfitting phenomenon has been widely observed in industrial Click-Through Rate (CTR) applications, where the model performance experiences a significant degradation at the beginning of the second epoch. Recent advances try to understand the underlying factors behind this phenomenon through extensive experiments. However, it is still unknown whether a multi-epoch training paradigm could achieve better results, as the best performance is usually achieved by one-epoch training. In this paper, we hypothesize that the emergence of this phenomenon may be attributed to the susceptibility of the embedding layer to overfitting, which can stem from the high-dimensional sparsity of data. To maintain feature sparsity while simultaneously avoiding overfitting of embeddings, we propose a novel Multi-Epoch learning with Data Augmentation (MEDA), which can be directly applied to most deep CTR models. MEDA achieves data augmentation by reinitializing the embedding layer in each epoch, thereby avoiding embedding overfitting and simultaneously improving convergence. To our best knowledge, MEDA is the first multi-epoch training paradigm designed for deep CTR prediction models. We conduct extensive experiments on several public datasets, and the effectiveness of our proposed MEDA is fully verified. Notably, the results show that MEDA can significantly outperform the conventional one-epoch training. Besides, MEDA has exhibited significant benefits in a real-world scene on Kuaishou. CCS CONCEPTS • Information systems → Recommender systems.
摘要 (中文)
在工业点击率（CTR）应用中，one-epoch 过拟合现象被广泛观察到，即模型性能在第二个 epoch 开始时出现显著退化。近期进展通过大量实验试图理解该现象背后的成因。然而，由于最佳性能通常由 one-epoch 训练取得，多 epoch 训练范式能否取得更好结果仍属未知。本文中，我们假设该现象的出现可能归因于 embedding 层易于过拟合，而这源于数据的高维稀疏性。为在保持特征稀疏性的同时避免 embedding 过拟合，我们提出一种新颖的基于数据增强的多 epoch 学习（MEDA），可直接应用于大多数深度 CTR 模型。MEDA 通过在每个 epoch 重新初始化 embedding 层来实现数据增强，从而避免 embedding 过拟合并同时改善收敛。据我们所知，MEDA 是首个为深度 CTR 预测模型设计的多 epoch 训练范式。我们在多个公开数据集上进行了大量实验，充分验证了所提 MEDA 的有效性。值得注意的是，结果表明 MEDA 能显著优于传统的 one-epoch 训练。此外，MEDA 在快手的一个真实场景中展现出显著收益。CCS CONCEPTS • Information systems → Recommender systems.
Learning Vector-Quantized Item Representation for Transferable Sequential Recommenders
人大2022推荐学术Semantic IDtransferable sequential recommender, vector-quantized item representation, item code, contrastive pre-training, cross-domain fine-tuningPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYupeng Hou, Zhankui He, Julian McAuley, Wayne Xin Zhao
Abstract (EN)
Recently, the generality of natural language text has been leveraged to develop transferable recommender systems. The basic idea is to employ pre-trained language models (PLM) to encode item text into item representations. Despite the promising transferability, the binding between item text and item representations might be too tight, leading to potential problems such as over-emphasizing the effect of text features and exaggerating the negative impact of domain gap. To address this issue, this paper proposes VQ-Rec, a novel approach to learning Vector-Quantized item representations for transferable sequential Recommenders. The main novelty of our approach lies in the new item representation scheme: it first maps item text into a vector of discrete indices (called item code), and then employs these indices to lookup the code embedding table for deriving item representations. Such a scheme can be denoted as “text =⇒ code =⇒ representation”. Based on this representation scheme, we further propose an enhanced contrastive pre-training approach, using semi-synthetic and mixed-domain code representations as hard negatives. Furthermore, we design a new cross-domain fine-tuning method based on a differentiable permutation-based network. Extensive experiments conducted on six public benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, in both cross-domain and cross-platform settings. Code and pre-trained model are available at: https://github.com/RUCAIBox/VQ-Rec. 摘要 (中文)
近来，自然语言文本的通用性被用于构建可迁移的推荐系统。其基本思想是利用预训练语言模型（PLM）将物品文本编码为物品表示。尽管具备有前景的可迁移性，物品文本与物品表示之间的绑定可能过于紧密，从而导致诸如过度强调文本特征的作用、放大领域差距（domain gap）负面影响等潜在问题。为解决这一问题，本文提出 VQ-Rec，一种学习向量量化（Vector-Quantized）物品表示以用于可迁移序列推荐器的新方法。我们方法的主要创新在于新的物品表示方案：它先将物品文本映射为一个离散索引向量（称为 item code），然后利用这些索引查表 code embedding table，从而导得物品表示。这一方案可记作“text =⇒ code =⇒ representation”。基于该表示方案，我们进一步提出一种增强的对比预训练方法，使用半合成和混合领域的 code 表示作为 hard negative。此外，我们设计了一种基于可微排列网络（differentiable permutation-based network）的新的跨域微调方法。在六个公开基准上的大量实验证明了所提方法在跨域和跨平台设定下的有效性。代码与预训练模型见：https://github.com/RUCAIBox/VQ-Rec。CCS 概念 • 信息系统 → 推荐系统。† 大数据管理与分析方法北京市重点实验室。B 通讯作者。（允许出于个人或课堂教学目的，在无费用前提下制作本作品全部或部分的数字或纸质副本，前提是副本不得用于盈利或商业优势，且副本须在首页保留本声明。本作品中由非作者第三方拥有的版权组件必须予以尊重。允许带署名的摘要引用。除此以外的复制、再发表、在服务器上发布或向列表分发，均需事先获得具体许可和/或支付费用。请向 permissions@acm.org 申请许可。WWW ’23, May 1–5, 2023, Austin, TX, USA © 2023 Copyright held by the owner/author(s). A Neural Corpus Indexer for Document Retrieval
Microsoft2022搜索工业生成式检索 / 排序方法document retrieval, neural corpus indexer, sequence-to-sequence, semantic document identifiers, prefix-aware weight-adaptive decoderPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYujing Wang, Yingyan Hou, Haonan Wang, Ziming Miao, Shibin Wu, Hao Sun, Qi Chen, Yuqing Xia, et al.
Abstract (EN)
Current state-of-the-art document retrieval solutions mainly follow an indexretrieve paradigm, where the index is hard to be directly optimized for the final retrieval target. In this paper, we aim to show that an end-to-end deep neural network unifying training and indexing stages can significantly improve the recall performance of traditional methods. To this end, we propose Neural Corpus Indexer (NCI), a sequence-to-sequence network that generates relevant document identifiers directly for a designated query. To optimize the recall performance of NCI, we invent a prefix-aware weight-adaptive decoder architecture, and leverage tailored techniques including query generation, semantic document identifiers, and consistency-based regularization. Empirical studies demonstrated the superiority of NCI on two commonly used academic benchmarks, achieving +21.4% and +16.8% relative enhancement for Recall@1 on NQ320k dataset and R-Precision on TriviaQA dataset, respectively, compared to the best baseline method.
摘要 (中文)
当前最先进的文档检索方案主要遵循 index-retrieve 范式，其中索引难以针对最终检索目标进行直接优化。本文旨在表明，一种统一训练与索引阶段的端到端深度神经网络能够显著提升传统方法的召回性能。为此，我们提出 Neural Corpus Indexer（NCI），一种序列到序列网络，可直接为给定查询生成相关文档标识符。为优化 NCI 的召回性能，我们发明了前缀感知的权重自适应解码器架构，并采用了查询生成、语义文档标识符以及基于一致性的正则化等一系列定制技术。实证研究表明，NCI 在两个常用学术基准上具有优越性：与最佳 baseline 方法相比，在 NQ320k 数据集上的 Recall@1 和在 TriviaQA 数据集上的 R-Precision 分别实现了 +21.4% 和 +16.8% 的相对提升。
Transformer Memory as a Differentiable Search Index
Google2022搜索工业生成式检索 / 排序方法differentiable search index, document identifiers, transformer memory, text-to-text retrieval, zero-shot generalizationPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYi Tay, Vinh Q. Tran, Mostafa Dehghani, Jianmo Ni, Dara Bahri, Harsh Mehta, Zhen Qin, Kai Hui, et al.
Abstract (EN)
In this paper, we demonstrate that information retrieval can be accomplished with a single Transformer, in which all information about the corpus is encoded in the parameters of the model. To this end, we introduce the Differentiable Search Index (DSI), a new paradigm that learns a text-to-text model that maps string queries directly to relevant docids; in other words, a DSI model answers queries directly using only its parameters, dramatically simplifying the whole retrieval process. We study variations in how documents and their identifiers are represented, variations in training procedures, and the interplay between models and corpus sizes. Experiments demonstrate that given appropriate design choices, DSI significantly outperforms strong baselines such as dual encoder models. Moreover, DSI demonstrates strong generalization capabilities, outperforming a BM25 baseline in a zero-shot setup.
摘要 (中文)
在本文中，我们证明信息检索可以用单个 Transformer 完成，其中关于语料的所有信息都编码在模型参数中。为此，我们引入 Differentiable Search Index（DSI），一种新范式，它学习一个 text-to-text 模型，将字符串 query 直接映射到相关 docid；换言之，DSI 模型仅用其参数直接回答 query，极大地简化了整个检索过程。我们研究了文档及其标识符表示方式的变化、训练流程的变化，以及模型与语料规模之间的相互作用。实验表明，在合适的设计选择下，DSI 显著优于双 encoder 模型等强 baseline。此外，DSI 展现出强大的泛化能力，在零样本设置下优于 BM25 baseline。
Recommendation as Language Processing (RLP): A Unified Pretrain, Personalized Prompt & Predict Paradigm (P5)
Rutgers2022推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐text-to-text paradigm, personalized prompt, pretraining, unified recommendation, language groundingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsShijie Geng, Shuchang Liu, Zuohui Fu, Yingqiang Ge, Yongfeng Zhang
Abstract (EN)
For a long time, different recommendation tasks typically require designing task-specific architectures and training objectives. As a result, it is hard to transfer the learned knowledge and representations from one task to another, thus restricting the generalization ability of existing recommendation approaches, e.g., a sequential recommendation model can hardly be applied or transferred to a review generation method. To deal with such issues, considering that language can describe almost anything and language grounding is a powerful medium to represent various problems or tasks, we present a flexible and unified text-to-text paradigm called "Pretrain, Personalized Prompt, and Predict Paradigm" (P5) for recommendation, which unifies various recommendation tasks in a shared framework. In P5, all data such as user-item interactions, user descriptions, item metadata, and user reviews are converted to a common format -- natural language sequences. The rich information from natural language assists P5 to capture deeper semantics for personalization and recommendation. Specifically, P5 learns different tasks with the same language modeling objective during pretraining. Thus, it serves as the foundation model for various downstream recommendation tasks, allows easy integration with other modalities, and enables instruction-based recommendation based on prompts. P5 advances recommender systems from shallow model to deep model to big model, and will revolutionize the technical form of recommender systems towards universal recommendation engine. With adaptive personalized prompt for different users, P5 is able to make predictions in a zero-shot or few-shot manner and largely reduces the necessity for extensive fine-tuning. On several recommendation benchmarks, we conduct experiments to show the effectiveness of P5. We release the source code at this https URL.
摘要 (中文)
长期以来，不同的推荐任务通常需要设计针对特定任务的架构和训练目标。其结果是，难以将已学到的知识和表示从一个任务迁移到另一个任务，从而限制了现有推荐方法的泛化能力，例如序列推荐模型几乎无法应用或迁移到评论生成方法上。为解决这些问题，考虑到语言几乎可以描述任何事物、且 language grounding 是表示各种问题或任务的有力媒介，我们提出了一种灵活统一的 text-to-text 范式——“Pretrain, Personalized Prompt, and Predict Paradigm” (P5)，用于推荐，将多种推荐任务统一到一个共享框架中。在 P5 中，用户-物品交互、用户描述、物品元数据、用户评论等所有数据都被转换为一种通用格式——自然语言序列。来自自然语言的丰富信息帮助 P5 捕捉更深的语义以用于个性化和推荐。具体而言，P5 在预训练期间用相同的语言建模目标学习不同任务。因此，它可作为各类下游推荐任务的基础模型，便于与其他模态集成，并支持基于 prompt 的指令式推荐。P5 推动推荐系统从浅层模型走向深度模型再走向大模型，并将革新推荐系统的技术形态，迈向通用推荐引擎。借助针对不同用户的自适应个性化 prompt，P5 能够以 zero-shot 或 few-shot 方式进行预测，大幅减少对大规模微调的需求。我们在多个推荐 benchmark 上开展实验，展示了 P5 的有效性。我们在 this https URL 发布了源代码。
Towards Universal Sequence Representation Learning for Recommender Systems
人大2022推荐学术LLM4Rec 与对齐universal sequence representation learning, transferable representations, parametric whitening, mixture-of-experts adaptor, contrastive pre-trainingPDF · arXiv
AuthorsYupeng Hou, Shanlei Mu, Wayne Xin Zhao, Yaliang Li, Bolin Ding, Ji-Rong Wen
Abstract (EN)
CCS CONCEPTS In order to develop effective sequential recommenders, a series of sequence representation learning (SRL) methods are proposed to model historical user behaviors. Most existing SRL methods rely on explicit item IDs for developing the sequence models to better capture user preference. Though effective to some extent, these methods are difficult to be transferred to new recommendation scenarios, due to the limitation by explicitly modeling item IDs. To tackle this issue, we present a novel universal sequence representation learning approach, named UniSRec. The proposed approach utilizes the associated description text of items to learn transferable representations across different recommendation scenarios. For learning universal item representations, we design a lightweight item encoding architecture based on parametric whitening and mixture-of-experts enhanced adaptor. For learning universal sequence representations, we introduce two contrastive pre-training tasks by sampling multi-domain negatives. With the pre-trained universal sequence representation model, our approach can be effectively transferred to new recommendation domains or platforms in a parameter-efficient way, under either inductive or transductive settings. Extensive experiments conducted on real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. Especially, our approach also leads to a performance improvement in a crossplatform setting, showing the strong transferability of the proposed universal SRL method. The code and pre-trained model are available at: https://github.com/RUCAIBox/UniSRec. • Information systems → Recommender systems. ∗ Equal contribution. † Beijing Key Laboratory of Big Data Management and Analysis Methods. ‡ Beijing Academy of Artificial Intelligence, Beijing, 100084, China. 摘要 (中文)
为构建有效的序列推荐器，一系列序列表示学习（SRL）方法被提出来建模历史用户行为。大多数现有 SRL 方法依赖显式的物品 ID 来构建序列模型，以更好地捕捉用户偏好。尽管在某种程度上有效，但由于受显式建模物品 ID 的限制，这些方法难以迁移到新的推荐场景。为解决这一问题，我们提出一种新颖的通用序列表示学习方法，名为 UniSRec。所提方法利用物品相关的描述文本，学习跨不同推荐场景的可迁移表示。为学习通用物品表示，我们设计了一种基于参数化白化（parametric whitening）和 mixture-of-experts 增强适配器的轻量级物品编码架构。为学习通用序列表示，我们通过采样多域负样本引入两个对比预训练任务。借助预训练的通用序列表示模型，我们的方法能够以参数高效的方式，在 inductive 或 transductive 设置下有效迁移到新的推荐领域或平台。在真实数据集上进行的大量实验证明了所提方法的有效性。特别地，我们的方法在跨平台设置下也带来了性能提升，显示出所提通用 SRL 方法的强迁移能力。代码和预训练模型见 https://github.com/RUCAIBox/UniSRec。
